# SSOT — XAUUSD AI Trading Assistant (canonical, current state)

This is the single source of truth for the system's topology, ownership and deployment. If any other document disagrees with this one, this one wins and the other document is historical. Last reconciled: 2026-11-22 (the README's document index and status lines; each section carries the date of its own owner decision). Change log: docs/CHANGELOG.md. `AGENTS.md` binds every agent on every machine: cloud sessions, local checkouts and the owner's trading VPS.

## 1. Production topology: exactly five Workers
| Worker | Source | Deployed by (only on push to `main`) | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| `xauusd-ai-trading-assistant` | `src/`, `wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-worker.yml` | Webhooks, evidence fusion, autonomous parity registry, the H1 decision (the Reasoner, after the 1H War Room's Consensus Discussion), the dashboard read API (`/api/*`) and Telegram |
| `xauusd-fundamental-news-ingestion` | `fundamental-news-ingestion/` | `deploy-fundamental-news-ingestion.yml` (Workers Builds also builds it) | Owns the single TradingView MCP OAuth grant at `news.cryptophys.work/oauth/start`. Serves news (`/pull`, cron `:05`, RPC `NewsIngestionService`) and OHLCV (RPC `TvGateway`) |
| `xauusd-tradingview-pull-adapter` | `market-data-ingestion/`, `local-engines/` | `deploy-market-data-ingestion.yml` | The only owner of local computation for MizanLib, Prophet and GMI (see §3). Serves chart data (RPC `MarketDataReader`) |
| `xauusd-nexus-dashboard` | `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/` | `deploy-nexus-dashboard.yml` | `nexus.cryptophys.work` serves the UI. `/api/ohlcv` comes from `MarketDataReader` and `/api/aether/overview` from Aether's `AetherReader` (the Aether page at `/aether`); `/omega/*` is forwarded to `xauusd-omega` (`OMEGA_PAGE`); `/api/stream` is the push hub's WebSocket (Durable Object `PushHub`, ADR-0015 phase 1: each topic's latest snapshot, sent as the cell publishes it; topics `war-room`, `threads`, `decision`; the Omega overview's topic only through Omega's `/omega/api/push`); every other `/api/*` request is proxied to the AI Worker. **ADR-0015 phase 4 (Market Feed and Omega Bridge as Durable Object cells) is deferred** (2026-11-14): D1 and KV are inside their allowances after the resource audit, so the cells would save nothing billed (their Durable Object requests would likely stay inside the included million a month), and they would add change on the trading path (pull adapter, Omega). What phase 4 was for is done narrowly: the Omega page's live prices from each bridge sync (#349) and the atomic TradingView budget (lane 3). Re-evaluate when D1 or KV use passes half its allowance, or when minute-exact timing needs alarms. **ADR-0015 phase 2 (one document per room run, incremental aggregates) is deferred too** (owner 2026-11-14: "setuju tunda"): measured that day, the rooms wrote about 3,200 message rows (about 13,000 row writes with indexes, about 0.4 million a month of the 50 million included), and the hot reads were already about one row (the latest War Room, #331; scorecards and reputation from `scorecard_snapshots`, #332), with the database at 66 MB; the change would rewrite the storage every room reader depends on for no billed or latency gain. Re-evaluate when D1 writes or size pass half the allowance, or a hot read goes back above about 1,000 rows **ADR-0015 phases 3 and 5 are deferred as well** (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjutkan semua", measured that day, read-only). Phase 3 (telemetry to Analytics Engine): the telemetry-like writes are the parity comparisons (703 rows on 2026-11-13), the compliance checks (60) and the usage meter's day rows (one upsert per model call), about 4,000 row writes a day, well under 1% of the 50 million included a month; and each of them is read as a record of truth (parity gates a chart, `ai_usage_daily` drives the shadow kill switch), which Analytics Engine, sampled and short-lived, may not hold. Phase 5 (retention with an R2 archive): the database grew to 66 MB in 11 days (about 6 MB a day, room message bodies 14 MB and decision records 5 MB of it), so about 2 GB a year against the 5 GB included; the hot reads use indexes, and the shadow arms already drop their rows after 60 or 90 days. Re-evaluate phase 3 when a telemetry stream is needed that no D1 reader uses as a fact, and phase 5 when the database passes 1 GB or a hot read scans more than 10,000 rows. |
| `xauusd-omega` | `workers/xauusd-omega/` | `deploy-omega.yml` | `omega.cryptophys.work` and `nexus.cryptophys.work/omega`, the Omega Execution Engine (owner decisions 2026-11-09): shows the MT5 EA and sets its control (active, paused, killed); runs the broker engine's settings and connection and the bridge sync (see "Omega broker engine"), all through the AI Worker's `OmegaGateway` RPC entrypoint; verifies the Access JWT itself |

**The content engine lives in its own repository** (owner 2026-11-15: "pindah content queue ke cta, nexus hanya khusus war room"): `cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-cta`, the Worker `cryptophys-cta` at `cta.cryptophys.work` (content studio behind Access, public CTA page; its own `docs/SSOT.md`). It reads this repository only through the AI Worker's read-only `PublicProjection` entrypoint (`recaps`: decisions older than the public delay `PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS`, no level, plan or account; since RC1 (2026-11-26) its row limit covers the window, the caller's `limit` or `days` x 96 + 1, at most 200 (`recapsLimit`), where it was LIMIT 50, about 12.5 hours of 15m decisions with the delay at 0; `/api/public-projection` keeps 50; `analysis`: the Key Level Agent's zones with the names of their metrics (`metrics`, at most three, e.g. "4H Camarilla H4", "daily pivot"; never a price or a trade word; owner 2026-11-15: "cukup nama metrics sumbernya"), H1/H4/D1 patterns, structure; since KM1 (owner 2026-11-25: "Agent harus punya kemampuan scheduling misal pull keymap dari xauusd per 1h dan 4h dan 1D … Jika waktunya sama berarti digabung") also, after those keys and under the same `version`, `zones_by_chart` `{ H1, H4, D1 }` (each chart's own key-level map: every zone of the Key Level Agent's clustering over only that chart's levels with at least two families, or a candidate, in the `zones[]` shape (`role`, `low`, `high`, `strength`, `sources`, `metrics`) plus `candidate` (the agent's 3-family, medium-strength rule and its cap of two per side, on that chart alone), highest first; see "Per-chart key-level maps (KM1)" under the Key Level Agent) and `closed` `{ H1, H4, D1 }` (the ISO bar-close time of the latest closed bar of that chart's own series, or null; nothing else). The CTA posts one key-level map per chart at each H1, H4 and D1 close and merges the maps into one post when closes coincide (a D1 close is also an H4 and an H1 close); `closed` tells it which charts closed. The existing keys are byte for byte what they were; `analysis()` reads what it read before (`readStrategyWithCharts`: readStrategy's inputs, once, and `readLatestDecision`), no new D1 query or KV read; `warroom({ source })`, owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Real-time analysis": the latest reading of the 15m War Room or the 1H, 4H or 1D room (bias, at most three reasons, at most four metric names, the number of analysts heard) or of the 24-hour news digest (bias, summary, reasons, counts, at most six headlines with their effect on gold), never the room's decision, its confidence, a price or order wording, `src/public-warroom.mjs`; `warroomScene()`, owner 2026-11-19 for the CTA's holo war room homepage: the latest scene of the 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D rooms, each with its roster in speaking order and per analyst its bias, at most three metric names, at most two sentences of its opening claim and of its rebuttal and the analysts it rebutted, with the Moderator's public reasons; the statements are read from the run document's steps (`room_runs.body_json.steps`, where the 1H, 4H and 1D rooms keep them), `agent_thread_messages` only for a document without steps. The filter checks whole sentences and drops, never clips, one over its length; it also refuses positional wording (flat, waiting, ready to trade, "stable enough to trade", the sidelines, "#1"), shows the Decision Matrix as "Matrix" and leaves ratios under 1 such as 0.618 out of the price check). `trackRecord()`, owner 2026-11-20 (confirmed: "Yes, build it") for the CTA's Performance page and the home's average box (`src/public-track-record.mjs`): per exit scenario (Exit at TP1 from `signal_ledger.r_tp1`, Exit at TP2 from `r_tp2`, Managed exit from the Signal Ledger's own policy, `signal_ledger.r_omega` sized by `omega_size` and closed at `omega_closed_at`, by default; owner 2026-11-23: "yang ditampilkan di cta public harusnya omega (v3) bukan yang live omega v4"; switch `PUBLIC_MANAGED_SOURCE` `v3` | `v4`, `v4` the v4 replay `ledger_omega4`) the count, won, lost and even, the win rate, the expectancy, the net R with the average win and loss, and the profit factor, in R only and aggregated, from closed results only (a closed timestamp and an R; the managed exit only `omega_status = 'closed'`), closed more than the public delay ago (`PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS`); with `v3` the managed exit has no parity gate (`replay_check` `{ status: "not_required", shown: true }`, the same shape); with `v4` it carries no number until the v4 parity check passes (`replay_check.status`); no forbidden public key at any depth, kept 1 minute per isolate. **The public delay is 0: realtime** (owner 2026-11-25: "harus realtime" and "kamu saja yang kerjakan karena xauusd sedang overload"; shown the risk word for word, "Audit menemukan masalah: recap keputusan publik (CTA, 3 hari terakhir) hanya dilindungi jeda 24 jam. Kalau jeda dibuat 0, keputusan 15m terbaru (BUY/SELL/WAIT, confidence, alasan) langsung tampil publik saat posisi EA/Omega masih berjalan. Harga, entry, SL dan TP tetap tidak tampil. Track record (Performance) aman karena hanya menghitung trade yang sudah ditutup.", the owner answered "Semua sekarang"; relayed by the Orchestrator). `PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS` is `"0"` in `wrangler.jsonc`, for `recaps` and `trackRecord()` alike (`publicDelayHours`, src/public-projection.mjs: 0 to 14 days; unset, null, empty or invalid is 24). The accepted risk: the side, confidence and reasons of a decision whose position may still be running go public at once; never a price, entry, SL or TP (the forbidden keys and `recapLeaks` stay in force). Rollback: set `PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS` to `"24"` in `wrangler.jsonc`, no code change. The `xauusd-content` Worker that held it here is retired and **never to be redeployed**: its code removed in #359, the deployed Worker deleted by the one-off workflow `retire-content-worker.yml` once Omega no longer bound it. Nexus is the War Room only: no content page.

Removed on 2026-11-04 and **never to be redeployed**:
- `xauusd-mizan-engine`, `xauusd-prophet-engine` and `xauusd-gmi-engine`: stubs that reported `production_ready: true` without computing anything.
- `xauusd-market-data-normalizer` and `xauusd-pull-orchestrator`.
- `xauusd-confluence-controller`: its `/v1/fuse` endpoint was unauthenticated and signed whatever "verified" evidence the caller sent.

The earlier "8 + 1 Workers" plan in older documents is **cancelled**.

`test/ssot-topology.test.mjs` fails if a Wrangler config in this repository declares any other Worker name.

**Public surface (owner, 2026-11-07).** The AI Worker and Nexus have no `workers.dev` address and no preview URL (`workers_dev: false`, `preview_urls: false`):
- **AI Worker:** reached only at `ai.cryptophys.work`. `POST /webhook` is public and protected by `WEBHOOK_SECRET`; `/api/*` sits behind Cloudflare Access. That is the path destination `ai.cryptophys.work/api/*` of the platform's one Access application, managed by platform `provision-access.yml` (owner option (a), 2026-11-08); never edit it in the dashboard. `/health` and `/ready` stay public. Other Workers reach it through Service Bindings.
- **Nexus:** reached only at `nexus.cryptophys.work`, behind Cloudflare Access.
- **Checks:** the deploy smoke tests check the Access lock with the CI service token (`CF_ACCESS_CLIENT_ID`, `CF_ACCESS_CLIENT_SECRET`). The topology test guards both settings.

## 2. Inter-Worker calls (RPC Service Bindings, no shared secrets)
- The AI Worker calls `NEWS_INGESTION` → news Worker `NewsIngestionService.pull()`.
- The pull adapter calls:
  - `TV_GATEWAY` → news Worker `TvGateway.ohlcv()`;
  - `AI_WORKER` → AI Worker `LocalComputationIngress.submit()`.
- The dashboard calls:
  - `MARKET_DATA` → pull adapter `MarketDataReader.candles()`;
  - `AI_WORKER` → the AI Worker's default `fetch` for its read-only `GET /api/*` (owner go 2026-11-07). It replaced a public fetch of `https://ai.cryptophys.work`, so the AI Worker's `/api` can sit behind Cloudflare Access while Nexus keeps working. Only GET and HEAD are forwarded.
  - `OMEGA_PAGE` → the `xauusd-omega` Worker's default `fetch` (owner decision 2026-11-09: "halaman omega masih di nexus.cryptophys.work/omega"). `/omega` and `/omega/*` are forwarded unchanged (method, headers with the Access JWT, body); Omega verifies the JWT itself.
- Omega calls `OMEGA` → the AI Worker's `OmegaGateway` (its only data path).
- The AI Worker calls `AGENTS` → Cryptophys Platform `cryptophys-ai-kernel` `AgentRpc` (owner approval 2026-11-05, RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P4). This is the only binding to a Worker outside this repository, and it adds no Worker to the topology above.
  - **Identity:** the binding pins `props.host_worker = "xauusd-ai-trading-assistant"`, and there is no secret or token. The kernel lets this Worker act only as the 21 XAUUSD agents registered with that `host_worker` (platform migration 0016), each with `tier_max = 1`. No other Worker, and not the preview config, may carry this binding. `host_worker` never takes another value. `test/ssot-topology.test.mjs` guards both rules.
  - **What is sent** (`src/platform-reports.mjs`, in the parity sweep cron): one advisory report per meaningful event, never per tick.
    - `xauusd-reasoner` (`xauusd_digest`): every new H1 chain decision. A BUY↔SELL flip is `warning`.
    - `xauusd-moderator` (`xauusd_digest`): the War Room 15m conclusion when its stance changes, otherwise at most once an hour.
    - `hud-*` (`hud_digest`): one report per HUD agent per 15-minute sweep at most (the kernel allows 4 an hour), over the 15m War Room and the 1H, 4H and 1D rooms: when a stance moves into or out of BUY/SELL or the directional bias flips (BULLISH↔BEARISH) on any timeframe, otherwise a heartbeat with the agent's reading on every timeframe when it has published nothing for 6 hours (owner 2026-11-12: "seharusnya tidak idle dan berkontribusi ke semua TimeFrame"). Cursors per agent and timeframe in D1 `platform_hud_cursors` (migration `0019`).
    - `xauusd-risk-manager` (`xauusd_digest`, `src/health-alerts.mjs`, owner decision 2026-11-06): system health alerts.
      - **Detected problems:** a revoked parity identity, an input bar that differs from TradingView, a missing local H1 record, a timeframe room without a conclusion 45 minutes after the close, 10+ calls lost to the Workers AI rate limit in an hour, a stale News Daily, a stale War Room 15m, and TradingView alerts rejected by POST /webhook in the last hour (`webhook_rejected`: the reason from D1 `webhook_rejections`, `src/webhook-rejections.mjs`; lengths only, never the secret). Since 2026-11-11, **market data stopped** (`market_data_stale`, `marketDataIssue`): the latest XAUUSD 5m bar is more than 30 minutes old while the market trades (Sunday 23:00 to Friday 21:00 UTC, outside the 21:00-23:00 UTC daily break); the subject names the cause the gateway reports (`NEWS_INGESTION.gatewayStatus()`: the quota cooldown and its end, or OAuth not authorized). Its opening and its resolution also go to the owner's Telegram once. Production 2026-11-11 13:05 UTC: the TradingView provider quota ran out until 00:00 UTC; prices, rooms, news and parity stood still and no alert said why (the stale War Room check waits for 5m bars that keep closing). While the MT5 fallback runs, **`market_data_fallback`** (`marketDataFallbackIssue`) is open instead: the 1m series holds the broker's bars since a time (no price in the subject), with the broker source, the real cause (`src/feed.mjs` `fallbackCause`: quota used up, rate limit or pause, the chart's daily budget, OAuth, or a real outage, the only case worded "down") and when TradingView is expected back (since 2026-11-17); its opening and its end also go to Telegram. Since 2026-11-17 (PR #405), **`omega_symbol`** (`omegaSymbolIssue`): the Omega terminal's trade symbol is not verified USD gold (`src/gold-symbol.mjs`: profit currency USD, contract size 1-100 oz, no `symbol_error` from OmegaExecutor 1.14+; since 2026-11-19 no name list), or the bridge's chart-symbol search ended `exhausted` or `stopped` while no EA runs; Omega opens nothing meanwhile (`symbol_unverified`). Since 2026-11-20, **`aether_execution_rejected`** (`executionReportAlert` in `src/aether-push.mjs`): the latest two (or more) pushes of the execution report to Aether were refused, or the latest was refused and nothing was accepted for over 24 hours (a push happens only when the report changes, so two can take hours; orchestrator review of #503) (D1 `aether_execution_report_pushes.rejected_streak`, `rejected_since`). It carries Aether's validator codes only (`rejectionCodes`: "modes_invalid, trade_invalid:77"; any token that is not a code is dropped, so never a price, an account number or an email). Its key is the run's first refusal and the codes, and it is `report_once`: one report per distinct rejection until a push is accepted; a code list already sent in the same run reopens without a second report, and the resolution sends none. Production 2026-11-18 to 11-20: every push was refused and nothing said so.
      - **Subjects:** an engine is named canonically with its timeframe and registry id (`src/engine-names.mjs`, e.g. "GMI v12.70 15m (gmi_m15)"); a room by its name ("the 1H War Room").
      - **Reports:** a `warning` when an alert opens, and an `info` when a lasting alert resolves (not for one-shot or `report_once` alerts). At most 3 reports an hour.
      - **Where they live:** D1 `health_alerts` (migration 0009). Nexus shows them as its Priority Queue (`GET /api/health/alerts`).
      - An alert never changes a decision, a room or a parity status.
  - **Rules:**
    - Observations only: decision and confidence, never size, lots, levels or order wording.
    - `critical` is never sent, because the platform opens a GitHub issue for it.
    - A denial (rate limit, kill switch, suspension) or a kernel rejection is recorded and not retried. Only a transport failure is retried, once, by the next sweep.
    - **The kernel's limits, kept here** (platform `control-plane-shared/src/capabilities.ts` and `ai-kernel/src/supervisor.ts`, read 2026-11-19): `reports.publish` allows 4 consumed calls per agent in any rolling hour, else `rate_limited`; an agent outside the kernel with 5 or more denied or failed calls in an hour is proposed for suspension by consensus (at most once per agent every 6 hours), and stays suspended until someone resumes it. Five callers share `xauusd-risk-manager`, so `publishToPlatform` counts each agent's published reports of the rolling hour (D1 `platform_publish_log`) and holds the one past 4 (`failed`, reason `local_budget`; every caller retries a failed report at its next cycle). A held report never reaches the kernel, so it cannot count toward a suspension.
    - **Suspension backoff** (platform audit 2026-11-19: `xauusd-risk-manager`, suspended by consensus on 18 Nov, kept being denied `failed: agent_enabled` every 10 to 20 minutes). A denial that says the agent itself is off (`agent_enabled`, `agent_suspended`, `agent_disabled`) puts that agent in D1 `platform_backoff` for an hour (`PLATFORM_BACKOFF_MS`): no publish until then; after it, one `whoami` read decides (`enabled` publishes again and clears the backoff, anything else waits another hour). A rate limit or the global kill switch is not a suspension. `GET /api/platform/status` lists the agents waiting (`backoff`). Resuming an agent is the owner's call on the platform's Agents page.
    - Every failure is non-fatal: the chain runs whether the kernel is up or not.
  - **Cursors and status:** cursors live in D1 `platform_publications` (migration `0007`). `GET /api/platform/status` shows `whoami("xauusd-moderator")`, cached for 5 minutes, and the publications.
  - The platform reads this Worker only through `GET /api/decisions`, and it never writes here.
- **Inbound from the platform (owner 2026-11-11: "buat notif telegram jika ada yang perlu approve owner di semua cerebrum"):** the Cerebrum kernel (`cryptophys-ai-kernel`, binding `OWNER_TELEGRAM`) calls this Worker's RPC entrypoint `OwnerNotifier.notify` when something waits for the owner's approval in Cerebrum (knowledge proposals, critical consensus actions, recovery plans), and when it is resolved. The text is built here from bounded fields (`src/owner-notify.mjs`: plain text, links only to the Cerebrum Console, at most 12 an hour) and sent with this Worker's own Telegram bot; the platform holds no Telegram credential. Nothing else is reachable through that entrypoint.
- **Grid auto-adapt (owner 2026-11-17: the strategy agent council adapts entry and stop by itself; confirmed "Setuju, bangun begitu").** `src/grid-adapt.mjs` (`GRID_ADAPT_MODE` "live", KV `grid-adapt:v1`) evaluates once a UTC day, per strategy agent x 1H regime (`plan_setups`), every grid cell against the plan in R of the plan's stop on the same setups. A cell is used only with >= 40 fills, gain > +0.05R per setup with a bootstrap 2.5% bound > 0, a positive latest-25% holdout (>= 10 setups) and agreeing neighbours; switching needs +0.05R; a slice whose last 10 live grid trades (Omega R) average below 0 reverts to the plan and is held 7 days. `eaSignal` applies the slice's cell to the signal (entry up to 0.3 1H ATR deeper as a limit, stop x0.5 to x1.25 of the plan's distance, target unchanged); the Decision Engine's record keeps the plan, so the learner never learns on its own output. Omega's sizing (risk_pct), stop floors, max_positions, the strategy arm gates and the Position Manager apply as to any entry; the open intent records `grid_cell`, `grid_slice` and `stop_source` `grid:<cell>`. Pre-entry only (platform, ADR-0020 Amendment 1): the cell sets a new order's entry and stop and never an open position's (Omega's management never reads the signal's entry or stop; test GA5). One Nexus Telegram line per adoption or revert; Nexus shows the active cells under the grid (`/api/plan-learning` `grid_adapt`).
- **Strategy-agent veto rule (SV1b, owner 2026-11-24, relayed: "Build A6+A7, switch on, auto-revert").** `src/agent-veto.mjs` (`AGENT_VETO_RULE` "two_sided"; code default "classic"): an agent that speaks on both sides no longer counts against the side it also speaks for, in the lean veto (A6) and the arm veto (A7); a trade that passes only by them is tagged `veto_softened` (decision, Omega intent, ledger row); back to classic for 7 days by itself once its latest 15 closed tagged trades average below 0R (KV `agent-veto:auto:v1`); the owner's force wins (KV `agent-veto:owner:v1`). Details under "Execution only per the strategy agents".
- **Room breakout waiver (RM1b, owner 2026-11-25, relayed: "Turn variant (d) on now").** `src/room-waive.mjs` (`ROOM_BREAKOUT_WAIVE` "on"; code default "off"): a side's room check that alone holds it back passes when the 15m close is beyond the facing edge of a corridor recorded in the last hour and TREND_TF is with the side; a trade that passes by it is tagged `room_waived` (decision, Omega intent, ledger row); off by itself for 7 days once its latest 15 closed tagged trades average below 0R (KV `room-waive:auto:v1`); the owner's FS1 override wins. Details under "Room breakout waiver (RM1b)" beside the room guardrail.
- **EA health to the Cerebrum chat (owner 2026-11-16: "info terkait HeartBeat EA di Telegram jangan masuk ke Cryptophys-Nexus, tapi ke Cryptophys-Cerebrum"; "pakai cara 2 lewat kernel"; "pindahkan yang nomor 1 saja").** The Omega self-healing step (`src/omega-broker.mjs`: restart the MT5 terminal, turn Algo Trading back on) records each recovery it dispatches in D1 `omega_events` (`agent:cerebrum`, `autonomous_recovery`) and no longer sends it to this Worker's Telegram chat (Nexus). `src/ea-heartbeat-relay.mjs` reads the recoveries since its cursor (KV `ea_heartbeat:relay`) on the 5-minute ledger cron and the hourly health step, and publishes them as one kernel report from `xauusd-risk-manager` (`reports.publish`, kind `xauusd_digest`, severity `warning`, `body.telegram_topic` `ea_heartbeat`, `body.text` plain text, at most 1800 characters, newest kept). The kernel forwards it at its next tick to every alert channel subscribed to the topic ("Cerebrum alerts"; the owner ticks "XAUUSD EA heartbeat" under Console → Alerts → Topics). The kernel allows 4 reports an hour per agent and the health alerts use the same agent, so the relay sends at most one report per 30 minutes (15 minutes after a refusal) and skips recoveries older than 100 minutes (the kernel drops a report older than 2 hours). The Decision Engine's hourly "still <stance>" heartbeat uses the same topic, published as `xauusd-reasoner` (owner 2026-11-17: "hanya heartbeat 'still …' yang pindah ke Cerebrum"; `src/decision-engine.mjs` `heartbeatReport`); a stance change stays in Nexus. Every other Telegram message (orders, closes, rejected intents, market data, ledger, breakers, policy, Decision Engine stance changes) stays in the Nexus chat. **Recovery backoff (orchestrator 2026-11-27, after 20 terminal restarts in an hour on a server name the terminal did not know).** `src/recovery-hold.mjs`: after `RECOVERY_RESTART_CAP` (3) consecutive `restart_terminal` dispatches on the same connection version (each dispatch now carries `conn_version`) with no connected terminal since, the watchdog restarts no more. It records one `autonomous_recovery` event with `status: "held"` (directive, reason, `conn_version`, `dispatches`, and a message to check the server name exactly; no server name, no login), which the relay sends once ("EA self-healing held") and the Omega overview shows as `last_recovery`. Every sync then answers the directive `hold`: bridge 1.7.0+ hands it to its recovery handler, which throttles it (once per 3 minutes: an Escape key on the display and the ini's trading switches) and restarts nothing; and a sync that carries a directive skips the bridge's own local restart (pinned by the bridge test RHB-1). It re-arms on a new connection version or on a healthy state after a recovery: connected with a quote at most 60 s old, newer than the last recovery row (`bridge` event `recovery_rearmed`, once per recovery episode; checked before the light path at most once per 5 minutes per isolate while healthy, two small reads), so a stale-quote restart that works (connected all along) re-arms too. Only a tight run counts: two dispatches more than 15 minutes apart, or a last one that old, start a new streak; a recorded hold stays until a re-arm or a new connection version; a `remediate_algo_trading` dispatch is skipped in the run (it neither breaks a run nor lifts a hold). **Wrong server name (brief A, 2026-11-27).** `src/server-check.mjs`: the terminal echoes the configured server name in its state even while disconnected, so only `connected === true` with the exact stored name counts as a login. Latched per connection version (review of #648): the first sync logged in on the exact name after `connection_set` writes one `server_seen` event (`bridge`, once per version), and from then a disconnect is `disconnected`, never `not_seen` (a broker outage is not a wrong name); a new version resets the latch. Five minutes after `connection_set` with no such login yet, `serverCheck` is `not_seen`: one `server_not_seen` event (`agent:cerebrum`, once per connection version; the message names only the server the owner typed) that the relay sends to the Cerebrum Telegram, a held recovery that names this cause, and the Omega overview's `server_check` (`ok` | `disconnected` | `pending` | `not_seen`). It clears on the same condition. A near-match hint folds case, spaces, hyphens and underscores only and compares with names the terminal reported before (`omega_accounts.server`, the connected state): `setConnection` saves as entered and returns `warning.code` `server_name_near_match` with `did_you_mean`; the overview's `server_check.did_you_mean` shows it on the Omega page only, never in Telegram (OS1: a server check line in the Broker connection card, the Save's warning inline under its success line, and a "recovery held" chip with the hold's message, dispatches and connection version in the watchdog's Last recovery card). `remediate_algo_trading` is not capped. The bridge's symbol trial (no EA state on the applied server) restarts the terminal on its own path and is not held by the server.
- **The strategy arm (owner 2026-11-17, live directly; docs/RFC_STRATEGY_RECOMMENDATION.md, platform ADR-0020).** The owner arms at most 3 strategies, one click each in Nexus (`POST /omega/api/strategy-arm`, `src/strategy-arm.mjs`, D1 `strategy_arms` and the append-only `strategy_arm_events`, KV `strategy-arms:v1`). With 1 to 3 armed only they open (and close by their own confidence-drop rule) positions; with none armed the mode is AUTO: every live strategy, but each entry is ONE, the best by `confidence × confluence` (`pickStrategy`: the agent's measured confidence on the side, at least 0.40, times the mean of its entry at a key level, the quorum's share on the side and the strategy engine's alignment on the side; ties to the better R). The decision keeps `selection` (the pick and the candidates it beat) and the Telegram line names them. At signal time a strategy disarmed since the decision has no side (`strategy_disarmed`); an unreadable arm state holds the entry. Auto-disarm on the kill switch and the daily loss floor. Arming never moves the quorum; the Decision Engine's veto and the Position Manager stay. `STRATEGY_ARM_MODE` "off" restores the previous choice.
- **The 5-minute Position Manager (owner 2026-11-17, confirmed in the session: "Ya, benar semua", "Setuju semua", USD 5 a day; live directly, no shadow phase).** `src/position-manager.mjs` runs on the 5-minute ledger cron for each open Omega position, once per 5-minute bar. The Position Council (`position_council`, a model call metered as its own source with its own daily cap `POSITION_COUNCIL_DAILY_USD`, default 5) reads measured inputs only (the strategy agent's confidence on the position's side now and 15 minutes before, the quorum's share on its side now and the bar before, the Decision Engine's stance, the broker's 5m structure, the key levels; never the council's own confidence) and answers momentum and thesis. Fixed rules, 1 pip = 0.10: +30 pips and momentum weakening, close (since CH1, 2026-11-23, only with a measured reason: the change thresholds below); +30 and still strong, stop to break-even plus the spread; 30 pips beyond TP1 (the plan's target, watched), stop to TP1; the take-profit at TP1 moves out to TP2 (the next key level) once, **since 2026-11-19 (PM PR 2)** only when the council answered with the momentum not weakening and the thesis intact and, under the TP ladder, once the stop locks the ladder's TP1 (`ladder_hold`, `ladderTp1Locked`); otherwise the target holds (`tp2_waits_council`, `tp2_waits_ladder`) and no action is stored, so nothing is said; under platform ADR-0020 Amendment 6 (owner 2026-11-19: "Merge #490 + kerjakan opsi BE") TP2 may also go out before the ladder's TP1 lock, in profit, with the stop at break-even plus the spread or better in the same modification (`tp2_breakeven`, one `modify_sltp`, both or neither, OmegaExecutor 1.18+; the stop never looser than the broker's and never below the entry), so the worst case is about zero instead of the TP1 profit; -30 pips, a measured reason (the decision on the other side, or the agent's confidence or the quorum's share on the position's side dropped; since CH1 the stance on 2 consecutive closed decisions and the confidence by at least 0.05) and the council's thesis weakening or broken, cut; otherwise hold to the original stop. **Since 2026-11-19** (owner, relayed: "WAIT bisa jadi HOLD, harus divalidasi jangan langsung diexit oleh decision"; a SELL was cut on a WAIT alone that day and holding would have made about +5.63): a WAIT decision is never a reason to cut on its own (it is said beside a measured one), the council's thesis alone never cuts, and without the council's answer nothing is cut (`council_unavailable`, the stop stays); a measured reason with the thesis intact holds (`hold_thesis_intact`). **In R since 2026-11-18** (owner, confirmed: "Per mode"; `PM_R_BAND`, `pmBand`): the ±30 pips of the profit and loss rules are the position's stop distance at entry (its open intent's `r_dist`, else |entry − sl|) times its mode's band: scalp 0.3R, intraday and range 0.5R, swing 0.7R (the intent's mode, else the decision's, else intraday); a position without a known stop distance keeps 30 pips; the 30 pips beyond TP1 of the TP1 lock stay in pips. Each line gives the pips, the R and the band. Risk-reducing only: Omega (`positionManagerIntents`) turns a stored action (D1 `position_actions`, `src/position-actions.mjs`) into a close, a stop only in the position's favour with the broker's stop distance, or a target only outward, checked against the live price at its next sync; never an open, an add, a reversal or a wider stop (except the council's bounded loosening of ADR-0020 Amendment 8 below, behind its switch). A missing input, a council failure or the cap means no action (the broker's stop and target stay; the cap is said once a day on Telegram), except the stop locks: **since 2026-11-19** (owner: "Lewat council juga", then "ya" to the fallback) break-even and the TP1 lock go through the council, which can turn the TP1 lock into a close when it reads the momentum weakening, and apply as the mechanical fallback when it does not answer (a lock only reduces risk). The TP ladder's stop locks in Omega stay mechanical until the exit paths come under the Position Manager (PM PR 3). `POSITION_MANAGER_MODE` "live" runs it, "off" stops it. Each action is one Telegram line with its rule, pips and the council's reading, sent by the Omega sync when the broker answers ("applied at the broker", or "NOT applied: the broker refused it (...)"), or when Omega drops it, with the reason in plain words (`positionManagerIntents` and the plan's `pm_dropped`: the stop already there, no room at the live price, the TP ladder holding the target until the stop locks TP1, the position gone, another close first, the action older than 6 minutes, the terminal not answering in 2); never at the moment it is decided (owner 2026-11-19: the line says what reached the broker). The same "not applied" reason for the same ticket and rule is said once an hour (`PM_NOT_APPLIED_REPEAT_MS`, a `pm:na:` key in SIGNAL_CACHE). Every Omega or delegated position that leaves the book is said once, from its first-stored exit deal closed in the last 30 minutes with the position no longer open (`src/position-exit.mjs`, at most 5 a sync): the reason (the broker's take-profit, stop, stop in profit, stop-out, a hand close, or Omega's close intent reason in words), entry, exit, pips, the result in USD over all the position's exits, and one `omega_events` row `position_closed` (no account number); a full Omega close no longer sends its own "Closed" line beside it, a partial close keeps its "Scaled Out" line; after 21:00 UTC a daily report scores each close against holding to the original stop and target. `GET /api/omega/position-manager`: the recent actions, the mode, the cap and today's spend. Beside it, every closed broker minute, the 1m fast-protect layer (PM-1M, below) may lock profit, bring the target nearer, tighten the stop or close early in the loss branch; its rows are in the same table and on the same page.
- **The council's bounded loosening and two-way target (platform ADR-0020 Amendment 8, owner 2026-11-20: "setuju", "keduanya"; docs/RFC_PM_A8.md).** Behind the Omega setting `pm_loosen_enabled` (default false, owner only on the Omega page; Aether's policy never writes it). Off, the Position Manager and Omega are byte for byte as before (test A8-2). On, for open positions only:
  - **Who.** The Position Council's answer adds `stop` (keep or loosen) and `target` (keep, nearer, further), each with one measured reason. Fixed rules check the reason and set the price. A silent council changes nothing, and nothing loosens mechanically. It replaces only a hold (`inside_band`, `protected`, `tp2_waits_*`), one change per position and bar, the stop first.
  - **The stop.** Only a stop tightened since entry moves back toward the original entry stop (the open or `place_limit` intent's `sl`), by at most `back_share` of the way, never past it. From break-even or better it never goes back into loss. It needs the thesis intact and the momentum not weakening, and one reason that holds: ATR(14, 15m) ≥ `atr_expand` × the ATR at the last tighten (else at entry), a key level just outside the stop, or a High-impact USD release in the calendar gate's window. Once per position: the `modify_sl` intent with `loosen: true` is the record (a broker refusal does not count).
  - **Omega's check (`a8Intent`).** Every bound again. The daily loss budget must hold with the position's risk at the new stop, and the profile's hard cap per trade and total open risk must hold. Refused against a veto of the side, while paused, on a real account not armed, with the tick value or money unverified, and on delegated positions.
  - **After a loosening.** The position counts as unprotected (`isProtected`, `same_side_unprotected`). No mechanical stop or target move is planned in that sync. Every later mechanical lock and Position Manager lock waits until it is tighter than the stop before the loosening.
  - **The target.** Nearer (the quorum's share fell, the agent's confidence dropped, a key level before it): the nearest key level ahead of price, else TP1. Further (a key level beyond, or ATR expansion with the confidence rising): the next level beyond, waiting for the ladder's TP1 lock (Amendment 4). Never on the wrong side of price, and never with a looser stop.
  - **Knobs.** `pmLoosenKnobs` (`src/pm-loosen-knobs.mjs`), start values per mode within the bounds proposed as `omega.pm_loosen_atr_expand_<mode>` (1.6/1.5/1.5/1.8, bound 1.2–2.5) and `omega.pm_loosen_back_share_<mode>` (0.4/0.5/0.6/0.4, bound 0.25–1.0). No platform reader yet.
  - **Lines, events, page.** "Stop loosened" and "TP moved" go to the owner's private chat when the broker answers, with the reason, the mode and R. `omega_events` `pm_stop_loosened` and `pm_tp_moved` carry R and the rule only. Nexus has the toggle and a "LOOSENED ONCE" badge (overview `pm_loosen`). The v4 replay never loosens.
- **One profit-lock gate (SLG1, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "one shared gate"; `src/profit-lock-gate.mjs`).** Behind the Omega setting `profit_lock_gate`, `"on"` (default) or `"legacy"`, the owner's switch only (Aether's policy and its rollback never write it); it is in `ERA_SWITCHES`.
  - **The rule.** No stop moves to or beyond the entry (Omega's break-even with `trailing_stop_r`, the ladder's `ladder_lock_*`, key-level SL+, the 5m trail into profit, the Position Manager's `breakeven`, `lock_tp1` and `tp2_breakeven`) until the position's MFE reaches its mode's threshold on a CLOSED bar of its mode's timeframe: scalp max(1.0R, 1.0 × ATR(14, M15)) on M5; intraday max(1.0R, 1.5 × ATR(14, M15)) on M15; swing max(1.0R, 1.0 × ATR(H1)) on H1; range max(0.75 × the plan's TP distance, 1.0 × ATR(14, M15)) on M15. R is the stop distance placed at entry (the open intent's `r_dist`); unknown, only the ATR term counts. Another mode counts as intraday. ATR(14, M15) is the terminal's (`atr_value`); ATR(H1) is the latest decision's `strategy.atr_h1`.
  - **At the gate.** The first lock is break-even plus the spread plus 0.1 × ATR(M5) (the broker's M5 ATR, else ATR(14, M15)/√3): the PM's `breakeven` and `tp2_breakeven` stop, the ladder's TP0 and the floor of key-level SL+; the ladder (TPx) and SL+ go further only after it; a PM stop already past the entry is not moved again to it. TP2 (`tp2_target`, `tp2_breakeven`) waits for the gate too, and `tp2_breakeven`'s any-profit trigger inside the band is gone: the gate is its trigger. **The gate is the break-even's trigger** (orchestrator review of #535): once it is open and the stop is not yet past the entry, the PM's `breakeven` to the gate's first lock goes in at any price inside the band as well, not only at the band (a peak past the gate that pulls back inside the band before the next 5-minute run still locks), without the council (a lock only reduces risk, as the silent fallback); `take_profit_weakening` at the band, the `lock_tp1` branch and `cut_loss` at the cut band come first, and inside the band TP2 with its break-even stop (`tp2_breakeven`, one modification) before it. With the price under the gate's break-even plus the spread it is the hold `lock_waits_room`, tried again each run while the gate stays open. A waiting lock is a PM hold (`lock_waits_gate`, `tp2_waits_gate`, `lock_waits_room`) or a dropped PM action with the gate's reading; each lock made records `mfe_r` and `mfe_atr` on its intent (and in the PM action's reason). Unchanged by the gate: the initial stop, `cut_loss`, `take_profit_weakening` (its measured reason is CH1's, below), the A8 loosening, `lock_tp1`'s level (TP1) after the gate, TP1 taking the whole position, a stop tightened while still in loss, partial closes and closes.
  - **MFE.** The favourable extreme (the wick high of a BUY, the low of a SELL) of the closed bars of the mode's timeframe since the fill, from the broker's M1 bars (the minute of the fill itself left out unless the fill is on the minute), so it moves only when such a bar closes. Live, Omega's sync and the PM runner read the same facts (`profitLockGates`, `gateBars`: the corpus since the earliest fill, at most `GATE_LOOKBACK_MIN` = `CORPUS_READ_MAX` minutes, about a day, the connected terminal's server and symbol only, once a minute per isolate); a position older than that is measured over the minutes available. v4 (`replayOmega4`) computes them with the same `gatesFor` over its 1m bars and passes them to the PM rules and `planIntents` as live does. **Missing facts mean closed:** no closed bar since the fill, no ATR, no fill time, or no corpus (`CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE` "off") keeps every stop out of profit; set `"legacy"` then.
  - **"legacy".** Today's engine byte for byte: no gate is read or applied (the fixtures with `"legacy"` on every input give the previous `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` b58968b2…, test/profit-lock-gate.test.mjs SLG1-15). The gate on moved `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (a recorded exception, a new parity era at the deploy).
  - **The brake** (read-only plus the one switch; `runProfitLockBrake` with the hourly parity check). The gate's era starts the first time the step sees `"on"` (KV `omega:plg:since:v1`; `"legacy"` clears it). Once at least 30 live closes of positions opened in the era are in, and their mean R per trade is more than 0.15R below the mean of the last 30 closes opened and closed before it, one Telegram line asks the owner to consider setting `profit_lock_gate` to `"legacy"`. At most one line per era (KV `omega:plg:brake:v1:<since>`); it never changes the setting.
- **Change thresholds (CH1, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "supaya tidak terlalu dini, perubahan confluence dan confidence perlu threshold atau ambang perubahan"; the option chosen: "Live semua (Rekomendasi)"; `src/change-thresholds.mjs`).** Behind the Omega setting `change_thresholds`, a token list of `conf`, `tpw`, `limit`, `skip` and `stance`, every token on by default (`"off"` for none; validated in `settingsFrom`, canonical order), the owner's switch only (Aether's policy and its rollback never write it), labelled in the change audit, in `ERA_SWITCHES` (`change_thresholds=<value>` starts a new era). A token off is today's rule for its trigger byte for byte; all off gives the previous `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` 94e6f50c… (test/change-thresholds.test.mjs CH1-12). The setting reaches each consumer as the other behaviour switches do: `planIntents` reads it from its settings (live and v4), the Position Manager's runner and the limit council read it from `omega_settings` (never stored: the default), v4 passes it to the PM rules from the replayed settings.
  - **`conf`.** `confidenceTrend` with `CONFIDENCE_CHANGE_MIN` 0.05: a drop or a rise needs a change of at least 0.05 (0.049 is stable), in every mode, where the trend is read: cut_loss's confidence reason, A8's `confidence_drop` and `confidence_rising`, Omega's `confidence_drop` close and its extend gate, the limit council's confidence reasons and `agent_trend`. For the extend gate (the trailing TP and the extended target) an `unknown` trend reads as the last known one, the previous decision row's own trend (its `agent_confidence` against its `agent_confidence_prev`, the signal's `agent_conf_last` and `agent_conf_last_prev`), so a missing reading no longer blocks; with no known state at all it stays unknown. Off: the ±0.02 band (`CONFIDENCE_STABLE_BAND`).
  - **`tpw`.** `take_profit_weakening` (beyond TP1 and at the band) closes only with a measured reason (`measuredFall`, `weakeningReason`): scalp, the quorum's share on the side fell at least 0.10 since the decision before, or the agent's confidence dropped at least 0.05; intraday and range, the same confirmed on 2 consecutive closed 15m decisions (both the decision before and this one at least that far under the level two closes back); swing, the share fell at least 0.15 on 2 decisions (no confidence reason). The close's `why` names it. Without one the rules below it run (under SLG1 the gate, `lock_tp1`, the break-even, `lock_waits_gate` or `lock_waits_room`), and the action's `why` says "change threshold tpw held the close back".
  - **`limit`.** A resting limit's `quorum_share_drop` (`pendingDeleteReason`, and the ledger's counterfactual `limitCancelReason` the same way): a fall of at least `cancel_share_drop` from the placing decision, scalp 0.10, intraday and range 0.10 and swing 0.15 (the knobs' start values with the token on, recorded at placement), outside scalp on the decision before this one too (closed after the placing decision, 15 minutes before this one). The knob's bound is 0.03–0.20 (it was 0.03–0.10).
  - **`skip`.** The entry council's skip needs a measured skip reason that holds under `"limit"` as under `"council"` (`htf_against`, `confidence_drop`, `quorum_share_drop`, `liquidity_sweep_against`); `confidence_drop` and `quorum_share_drop` hold by `measuredFall`'s thresholds of the request's mode. Under `"limit"` a skip whose reason does not hold places the limit (rule `skip_no_measured_reason`; a skip that holds is `council_skip:<reasons>`); under `"council"` it is today's market entry.
  - **`stance`.** "The decision is <the other side>" is a reason only on 2 consecutive closed decisions: cut_loss's (its why says "on 2 decisions") and the pending council's move and cancel (`pendingFacts`). A flip that reverts the next decision does not act.
  - **Unchanged.** cut_loss's share reason (`SHARE_DROP` 0.05, more than 0.05 since the bar before, one bar, no minimum age), `lean_flip` and `powerBand`, the v3 Signal Ledger (`strategyExits` without the tokens), the EA.
  - **Persistence per decision.** The history is the decision rows before the latest (`decisionHistory`: the decision 15 minutes before with its stance and shares, the one before that with its shares, and the previous row's confidences), never the 5-minute runs: every run on the same decision reads the same history. Live, `latestDecisions` reads the latest three rows (the Position Manager and the limit council) and the signal reads the two rows before the decision once per decision per isolate (`decisionRowsBefore`; flat fields `quorum_long_share_prev`, `quorum_short_share_prev`, `decision_prev_ms`, `agent_conf_last`, `agent_conf_last_prev`); v4 builds the same history from its decision rows. No new table.
  - **The brake** (read-only; `runChangeThresholdBrake` with the hourly parity check). Each token's era starts the first time the step sees it on (KV `omega:ch1:since:v1:<token>`; off clears it). Once at least 20 cases of a token (firings plus suppressions, one per ticket and kind) are scored in its era and the suppressed ones did worse held than acting would have (a positive mean of acting R minus holding R), one Telegram line asks the owner to consider turning the token off; at most one line per token and era (KV `omega:ch1:brake:v1:<token>:<since>`); it never changes the setting. What it measures: `tpw`, `stance` and `conf` from `position_actions` (a firing is an applied close the token let through; a suppression a row whose why says the token held a close or cut back; acting is the row's pips, holding is the daily report's measure, the stop or target the position had then touched first on the ledger's 1m bars, else the last close; both in R of the open intent's `r_dist`; for `conf` only cut_loss's confidence reason, Omega's own confidence close leaves no row); `skip` from `limit_council` entry rows (a held-back skip scored at its live trade's R, 0R when none filled within 6 hours, against 0R); `limit` is not measured (a kept order leaves no row; the step says `unmeasured`).
- **The 1m fast-protect layer (PM-1M, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "deploy semuanya live. Tf 1m diperlukan position manager untuk antisipasi TP lebih cepat dan mengurangi kerugian. Meski concern dengan noise perlu penanganan."; `src/pm-fast.mjs`; docs/RFC_PM_A8.md §8).** Behind the Omega setting `pm_fast_1m`, `"on"` (default) or `"off"`, the owner's switch only (validated in `settingsFrom`; Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; labelled in the change audit; in `ERA_SWITCHES`, `pm_fast_1m=off` starts a new era). There is no environment variable for it. `"off"` is today's engine byte for byte: the sync reads no stream and writes no fast record, and the fixtures with `"off"` on every input give the previous `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` 169243f2… (test/pm-fast.test.mjs F10). A layer beside the 5-minute Position Manager, not a re-base: A8, `pmBand` and Omega's buffers keep their calibrations and their ATRs, the Position Council still runs every 5 minutes, and no council runs here.
  - **When.** Once per newly closed broker minute, on Omega's bridge sync (the full plan runs on every sync while a position is open, about twice a second), never on a cron of its own: the crons are unchanged. The step (`runFastProtect`) runs before the plan reads the pending Position Manager actions, only with the engine on (`OMEGA_ENGINE_ENABLED`, Omega enabled), the terminal connected and no kill; it reads the closed minute's bar once it has arrived (about 1.3 s after the close), looks again every 5 s until it does, and evaluates that minute once per isolate (a second isolate writes the same ids, INSERT OR IGNORE). An action it records is planned in the same sync.
  - **The stream.** D1 `broker_bars_1m`, the last 90 minutes, pinned by the account's `account_ref`, the source (`mt5:<server>:<SYMBOL>` of the connected terminal) and the symbol's digits, as the liquidity lens's baseline key is; never by `connection_version` (a reconnect's versions 90 to 92 are one source). A SELL's bars are the bid: each bar's spread is added back (its exit price is the ask).
  - **TP anticipation (`pm_fast_tp`).** The price within the mode's near zone of TP1 or the broker's target (0.25R scalp, 0.3R intraday and range, 0.4R swing, of the stop distance at entry), the position in profit, and 2 of the last 3 closed 1m bars closed against it with a body of at least 0.5 × ATR1m(14). With the SLG1 profit-lock gate open (or `"legacy"`): the stop to protect `lock_r` of the open profit (0.6 scalp, 0.5 intraday and range, 0.4 swing), at least break-even plus the spread. With the gate closed no stop moves into profit: the target comes nearer instead, to the recent favourable extreme (the last 15 bars since the fill) less the spread, which is not a stop into profit and so not gated. Neither possible: recorded as held.
  - **Loss reduction (`pm_fast_cut`).** A 1m impulse against the position, its true range at least 2.0 × ATR1m (`impulse_k`, per mode), closing beyond the last 5m swing (the extreme of the last 3 complete 5m bars before it) and confirmed by the next closed bar not reclaiming it. At or below -0.5R the position closes early; otherwise the stop tightens to the 1m swing (the two bars' extreme) plus max(2 × spread, 0.25 × ATR1m), only tighter than the broker's stop and never beyond the original stop. A tightened stop that would sit in profit waits for the gate. ATR1m is the mean true range of the 14 bars before the last two (the impulse and its confirmation never inflate it).
  - **Noise.** Inert (no evaluation, no record): the rollover 21:55-22:15 UTC, the reopen 23:00-23:05 UTC and the first 5 minutes after any daily or weekly reopen, the market paused, the first 3 minutes after a reconnect batch (a bar received more than 60 s after its close), a latest closed bar older than 90 s, fewer than 20 contiguous bars since the last missing minute (the ATR1m warm-up). Held and recorded: the liquidity lens's spread-spike deferral (while Omega would defer a modification, up to 10 minutes from the spike's start; a fast close is held too), at most one fast action per position per 3 minutes, no fast action of the other trigger within 10 minutes of one (hysteresis), and a position the 5-minute Position Manager acted on in this 5m bar. Every threshold is one per-mode table, `PM_FAST_1M`.
  - **Fallback.** No stream, a stale or missing bar, another server, symbol or digits, `CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE` "off": the layer is inert and today's 5-minute behaviour is unchanged.
  - **Records and lines.** D1 `position_actions`, id `pm1:<ticket>:<bar minute>` (the closed bar's open in minutes, also its `bar`), rule `pm_fast_tp` or `pm_fast_cut`, the reason with ATR1m and its source (`broker_m1`; v4 `feed_m1`), `council` null; an action `pending`, a held trigger `noted`. Omega's intent carries the same id (`fastIntent` in `positionManagerIntents`: a close; a stop only tighter, with the broker's stop distance, and into profit only past the gate; a target only nearer and on the right side of price, never with a stop); the A8 hold after a loosening and the TP ladder apply as to the Position Manager's actions. One Telegram line per action, the Position Manager's, when the broker answers or Omega drops it ("1m lock: stop to …", "1m stop tightened to …", "1m TP nearer to …", "1m early close"); no account number. The 10-minute hysteresis binds the layer's own actions; the 5-minute Position Manager and Omega's routine management keep their rules (under the TP ladder an outward target still waits for the TP1 lock). Only Omega's own positions; a delegated manual position is not the layer's.
  - **v4.** `replayOmega4` runs `fastProtect` at every minute with a position, after the Position Manager of that minute, on its 1m bars (the feed's, with v4's fixed spread), and passes the actions to `planIntents` as live does; its history (the rate limit and the hysteresis) and the 5m-bar hold come from the intents it planned. v4 has no receipt time and no live spread, so the reconnect quarantine and the spread-spike hold never apply there: an expected difference in `omegaParity`. The layer on moved `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (a recorded exception, a new parity era at the deploy).
- **The 1m re-entry after a profit-lock stop (RE1, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Live: gerbang + re-entry (Rekomendasi)"; SLG1, the gate, went first; `src/reentry-1m.mjs`; docs/RFC_PM_A8.md §9).** Behind the Omega setting `reentry_1m`, `"on"` (default) or `"off"`, the owner's switch only (validated in `settingsFrom`; Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; labelled in the change audit; in `ERA_SWITCHES`, `reentry_1m=off` starts a new era). There is no environment variable for it. `"off"` is today's engine byte for byte: the sync reads no exit or stream for it and writes nothing, `planIntents` ignores a trigger, and the fixtures with `"off"` on every input give the previous `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` d0def5bb… (test/reentry-1m.test.mjs R13). A re-entry is a NEW position, an open like any other: it never bypasses an opening check.
  - **When.** Once per newly closed broker minute, on Omega's bridge sync (`runReentry`, before the plan; the minute's trigger is handed to every sync of that minute), only with the engine on (`OMEGA_ENGINE_ENABLED`, Omega enabled), the terminal connected and no kill. No cron of its own: the crons are unchanged. While flat the bridge syncs at least every 5 s (the light path and the calm stream mode), so a trigger is planned within seconds of its bar's close.
  - **Eligible (the setup).** The latest exit of an Omega position (the newest Omega exit deal of the account, `account_ref`, in the last 240 minutes whose position is no longer open), when the broker's stop closed it (deal reason `sl`), it was an original entry (its open intent `open:…`; a re-entry's own exit and a delegated position never are), and the last stop Omega applied to it (the newest `done` `modify_sl` or `modify_sltp` intent of the ticket) sat at or beyond its entry and was set by a profit-lock rule: `break_even`, `ladder_lock_tp<n>`, `sl_key_level_<band>` (SL+), `pm_breakeven`, `pm_tp2_breakeven`, `pm_lock_tp1` or `pm_fast_tp` (`REENTRY_LOCK_PATTERN`). The initial stop, a stop in loss or trailed (`trail_5m`, `pm_fast_cut`, a loosening) and every close by Omega (`pm_cut_loss`, `pm_take_profit_weakening`, …) are never one; the latter keep `reentryGate`, unchanged (it applies to a re-entry too).
  - **Valid while all hold.** The latest decision on the same side (the signal's `side`); the original stop not touched and TP1 not reached since the exit (the bars after the exit's minute, on the exit price: a BUY's bid, a SELL's ask); within 240 minutes of the plan's issue (`signal_issued_ms`; the decisions carry no `valid_until`); no re-entry of this setup yet (an `re:<ticket>:…` intent not refused) and no other Omega entry since the exit.
  - **Trigger.** The latest closed broker 1m bar, at least 5 bars after the exit's bar, closes back beyond the lock level (a SELL's ask below it, a BUY's bid above it). The stream, its identity (`account_ref`, source, digits), freshness (90 s), warm-up (20 contiguous bars), ATR1m(14) and quarantines are PM-1M's.
  - **Stop and target.** The stop at the pullback's extreme since the exit (a SELL's ask high, a BUY's bid low) plus max(2 × the live spread, 0.1 × ATR1m(14)); skipped when its distance from the entry is wider than the original stop's (`r_dist`). The target is the original TP1 (the open intent's `tp`), which must pay at least 1R at that stop (TP1-KZ's `TP1_MIN_R`), else skipped. `planIntents` checks both again at the live price (`reentry_stop_wide`, `reentry_reward`, `reentry_stops_level`) and opens at market (also under `entry_order` "limit" or "council"); no 5m stop, no adaptive stop. A re-entry the broker refuses does not use the setup's one, but every attempt counts: at most 3 open intents per setup whatever their status (then invalid, `attempts`, noted once), at least 5 minutes apart (orchestrator review of #543).
  - **Risk.** The money at risk at most min(the profile's cap, the original trade's money risk: its `r_dist` × volume × tick value per price), and the setup's total loss at most 1.0R of the original (the original's net loss is taken off): `sizeEntry`'s `maxRisk` (`lot_capped_reentry`, else `reentry_risk_cap` when even the minimum lot is over it). Every other check of an entry holds as for any open: the engine switch, kill and pause, the live arm, the symbol, price and money checks, `tick_value_mismatch`, the spec and profile review, the spread gate, the liquidity spike gate, the rollover quarantine, the news blackout, `max_positions`, `PLATFORM_MAX_OPEN` (3, also outside the limit modes), no stacking, the strategy arm's rules, `reentryGate`, the daily loss limit and the daily risk budget, the profile's risk, hard cap and total open risk, `volume_below_min`. At most one re-entry per setup.
  - **Blocked.** The rollover 21:55-22:15 UTC, the reopen 23:00-23:15 UTC and the first 15 minutes after any daily or weekly reopen, the market paused, a reconnect batch, a stale stream or the warm-up (inert, no record); the live spread above 60 points or above 2 × the median bar spread of the last 60 minutes (at least 10 bars, else blocked too), the high-impact release window (the signal's `blackout`, also the plan's own gate): blocked, noted once per setup and reason.
  - **Records and lines.** Omega's open intent `re:<the original's ticket>:<the trigger bar's minute>` (reason `pm_reentry`, `stop_source` `reentry_1m`, `reentry_of`, `reentry_lock`, `reentry_rule`, the original's `signal_issued_ms`, mode and strategy). D1 `position_actions` (same id, the original's ticket, rule `pm_reentry`, action `open`): `applied` when the plan made the intent, `skipped` with the plan's reason when it did not, `noted` for a trigger blocked or skipped before the plan; never `pending`, so the Position Manager's step never reads it. One Telegram line per re-entry when the broker answers its open ("XAUUSD RE-ENTRY · #<ticket> <side> · re-entered … · applied at the broker" or "NOT applied: …"), in place of the generic executed line; no account number. The re-entry position is managed as Omega's own (`isEntryIntentId`: the Position Manager, PM-1M, the ladder, SLG1, A8 read `re:` intents as they read `open:` ones).
  - **v4.** `replayOmega4` keeps the latest exit's setup from its own exits and the stop rule it applied last, runs `reentryCheck` at every minute after it on its 1m bars (v4's fixed spread), hands a trigger to `planIntents` as `reentry1m` (planned even while flat) and records the re-entry as a trade of its own (key `re:<the setup's decision key>`, its own ticket). `omegaParity` pairs a live trade whose open re-entered (`reentry_of`) with v4's `re:` row. v4 has no receipt times and no live spread: no reconnect quarantine, and the spread blocks never apply there (an expected difference). The re-entry on moved `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (a recorded exception, a new parity era at the deploy).
- **Limit-first for pullback strategies (E1, owner 2026-11-27 via the orchestrator: "harus mengikuti strateginya, jika memang strategi fallback harusnya tunggu di limit dan berada di zone, supply and demmand juga harus menjadi concern"; `src/limit-first.mjs`).** The 14-day data: 39 of 41 system trades were market entries of at-level plans (price within 0.1 ATR(1H) of the plan's entry); the council chose market in none, the at-level band and the entry council's `price_at_level`/`no_measured_reason` defaults did. **Who:** the pullback strategies `LIMIT_FIRST_AGENTS` (zone_pullback, the default agent, and breakout_retest) always rest a limit; sweep_reversal (market by design), range_fade, pattern_inside, orb_breakout and the pattern families are unchanged byte for byte. **Placement** (`limitFirstPlacement`, Omega's planner, broker scale): the plan's entry moved 0.1 x ATR(1H) toward the stop; when an own-type zone holds the entry (a BUY in demand, a SELL in supply, medium or strong; the decision's typed key levels and supply/demand boxes) the limit stays inside it, never past its far edge, and rests at its near edge when that edge already stands at least 0.1 ATR from price (`entry_basis` `zone_edge`, else `limit_first`); the plan's stop and targets, the fill window (`max_pending_life`), the placement band and zoneLimit's guards (at least 0.5 of the plan's risk, 1R to the target) as before. **The path:** with `entry_order` "limit" or "council" the council's skip stays a skip; its `enter_market`, its `place_limit` and a silent council all rest that limit (note `limit_first`), or nothing when it cannot rest (no market fallback); a resting limit-first order is never converted to market (the pending council's convert is dropped). With `entry_order` "market" nothing changes. **The signal** (`src/ea-signal.mjs`, flat, only on pullback trades, never read by the EA): `lf_atr` (the 1H ATR), `lf_type` (the type of the zone at the entry: demand, supply, mixed or untyped), `lf_gate` and the own-type zone `lf_zone_low`/`lf_zone_high` (feed scale). **Recorded:** the place_limit intent carries `limit_first`, `entry_basis`, `entry_zone_type`, `zone_type_gate` (pass when the zone at the entry is the side's own type, any strength, else block: what a gate "a pullback needs an own-type zone" would have done; no live effect; the owner 2026-11-27, "filter zona a": record first), `own_zone`, `decision_key`, `sl_plan` and `r_dist`; the `limit_placed` event carries them; the Telegram line adds "Entry: limit (limit-first, demand zone edge)". Every zone_pullback plan setup has the same gate from its `entry_zone_type` and side (`setupZoneTypeGate`). **Status:** `GET /api/limit-first` (`limitFirstStatus`): the stored switch, the limit-first orders placed and filled, the revert's state, and the gate's evidence over the zone_pullback plan setups of the last 30 days (pass and block: n, mean R on the control arm, fill rate; the edge) with the owner's promotion criterion (`ZONE_TYPE_GATE_PROMOTION`: at least 30 in each group and pass beating block by at least +0.15R; `met`). No price. **Switch:** the Omega setting `limit_first` ("on" default, the owner's word | "off"; FS1 group omega, an era switch: "off" writes `limit_first=off` and starts a new parity era). It acts only on a signal carrying `lf_atr`, so the fingerprint's fixtures replay as before: `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` stays ecfe3f90, only `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` moves (4dcd1ac9). Aether's policy and its rollback never write it. **Auto-revert** (`limitFirstRevert`, the 5-minute ledger step beside cd_gate's): from when limit_first was last set (key_meta, else the deploy), the first 30 closed limit-first trades; when their mean broker R (exits against the fill, over `r_dist`) is below the plan replay's e0 R of the same setups (plan_setups by decision, side and agent), `limit_first` is set to "off" once (writeSettings, actor `agent:limit-first`) with one Omega event (`limit_first_reverted`, else `limit_first_kept`) and one Telegram line (R only). Record-only and unchanged: the 15m-pattern parts P1-P4, the loss cooldown E2, Z2b; the Z5 hold is not touched.
- **The confidence-drop gate (CDG, owner 2026-11-25, relayed by the orchestrator: "Learned gate, hold otherwise (Recommended)"; `src/cd-gate.mjs`).** Behind the Omega setting `cd_gate`, `"on"` (default, live at the deploy) or `"off"`, the owner's switch only (validated in `settingsFrom`; Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; labelled in the change audit; an FS1 row; in `ERA_SWITCHES`, `cd_gate=off` starts a new era). `"off"` is the rule before CDG byte for byte.
  - **P0, the hold.** In `strategyExits` a `confidence_drop` close (a winner whose agent's confidence for its side dropped, as before) is emitted only when the learned gate passes for that agent × side × drop bucket. Otherwise nothing happens: no close, no stop move, no break-even; the position follows its plan's stop and target, SLG1 and the Position Manager like any position without a drop (test CDG-5: the held plan equals the plan with no drop). `lean_flip` and the extend gate are unchanged.
  - **The drop and the buckets.** prev − now of the agent's confidence for the position's side on the signal's decision (`agent_conf`) and the one 15 minutes before (`agent_conf_prev`), the pair `confidenceTrend` reads. Buckets `0.15-0.25` and `>=0.25`; a drop below 0.15 has none and is always held. The measurement before the ruling: the drops come in steps of agentConfidence's setup term (0.35 × {1, 0.5, 0}): 0.175 when the agent's `at_level` flips, 0.35 when it switches sides; holding to the plan beat the actual exits.
  - **The learned gate, by lift (`gateCells`).** Over the decisions of the last 30 days (or as many as exist): each decision with both confidences gives one sample per agent and side whose drop falls in a bucket (cell `agent|side|bucket`). Adverse: the broker's 1m close 1 hour after the decision (the bar at or before decided minute + 59) is against the side, from the close of the minute before the decided minute (each bar at most 5 minutes old, at least 50 minutes apart; the broker bars of `broker_bars_1m`, the corpus source with the most minutes in the window). `base_rate(side)`: the share of all measured decisions of the window where price 1 hour later was against that side. A cell passes when `n ≥ 30` and `adverse_rate − base_rate ≥ 0.10`.
  - **The daily job (`runCdGate`, the 5-minute ledger step beside RM1b's revert, once a UTC day; no cron of its own).** KV `cd-gate:v1` = `{ computed_at, day, window: { from, to, from_ms, to_ms, decisions, measured, days }, base: { long|short: { n, adverse, rate } }, cells: { "agent|side|bucket": { n, adverse, adverse_rate, base_rate, lift, pass } }, rule }`; each pass/fail change one `omega_events` row (actor `agent:cd-gate`, action `cd_gate_cell`, `{ cell, pass, n, lift }`) and one Telegram line ("XAUUSD OMEGA · Confidence-drop gate (CDG): …"); KV `cd-gate:history:v1` = `[{ at_ms, pass: [keys] }]`, one entry at the first computation and at each change of the passing set (at most 400). The same evidence again writes no change. The sync reads `cd-gate:v1` at most every 5 minutes per isolate; missing or unreadable, every drop is held.
  - **The held-drop log (D1 `omega_cd_holds`, CREATE IF NOT EXISTS, PRIMARY KEY (ticket, bar_ms)).** Every confidence drop the gate holds (`action` held) and every one it lets close (closed), once per ticket and decision bar (`bar_ms` the decision's close in minutes): `intent_id, agent, side, drop_conf, bucket, cell_n, cell_lift, cell_pass, at_level_flip` (the agent's `at_level` for the side in the two decisions' `strategy_plans`, 1/0, null when one is missing), `price` (the close price then: a BUY's bid, a SELL's ask), `open_price, volume, r_dist, cf_r` (the old rule's exit R there), `at_ms`. A gated close replaces a hold of the same bar, never the other way.
  - **The named auto-revert (`cdGateRevert`, on every ledger step until its window has a verdict).** The window opens when `cd_gate` went live (its `key_meta` time when last set, else the deploy: the log has rows only since then). The first 15 positions with an event (by their first event): a held position's outcome is the R it realised on the volume held from its first event on (`ea_deals`), a gated close's its `cf_r`. When their mean is below the mean `cf_r` of the same positions, `cd_gate` is set to `"off"` (`writeSettings`, actor `agent:cd-gate`, which does not clear a limit fault), with one `omega_events` row (`cd_gate_reverted`) and one Telegram line; a better mean is recorded `kept` (`cd_gate_kept`). Once per window (KV `cd-gate:revert:v1`); the owner turning it on again opens a new one.
  - **v4.** `loadOmega4Context` reads the history while `cd_gate` is on; `replayOmega4` passes planIntents the gate in force at each minute (`gateAt`: the latest entry at or before it). A minute before the history's first entry has no gate (the rule before CDG). Gap: between the deploy and the first job run (one ledger step, about 5 minutes) live holds every drop while v4 replays the old rule.
  - **Read.** `GET /api/strategy-agents` `cd_gate`: `{ computed_at, window, base, cells, passing: [keys], history: { entries, last_change_at }, log: { held, closed } | null, revert: { status, n, mean_r, mean_cf_r, at } | null, rule: { min_n, min_lift, buckets, window_days, revert_outcomes } }`. SELECT and KV reads only; no UI change.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (ruled): the gate is an input of planIntents (`cdGate`) and the fingerprint's fixtures carry none, so they give today's rule as before; the era is carried over at the deploy (the same behaviour hash, a new code hash), and v4 replays the gate from its history inside it. `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` c4aa5772… → 6c0f8142… (`strategyExits`, `planIntents`, `settingsFrom`, `OMEGA_DEFAULTS` and the new `cd-gate` module in `ENGINE_CODE`).
  - **Measurement only: `atr_range_ratio`** (orchestrator ruling with CDG, for CT2's re-run). Each decision record carries `atr_range_ratio`: ATR(14) of the closed 15m bars of the decision's own feed series (`pineAtr` of `src/prophet-r37-oscillator.mjs`, the Pine RMA) ÷ (high − low of the last 16 closed 15m bars), 3 places (`atrRangeRatio` in `src/decision-engine.mjs`); absent with fewer than 16 closed bars. A number, never a price; nothing reads it for a decision, the signal or Omega.
- **The Position Manager lines, each behind a breaker (B5; Track B v2 B5 with erratum E7; docs/RFC_INDICATOR_ADOPTION.md, "The Position Manager line breaker"; `src/pm-line-breaker.mjs`).** Track B is "live, no shadow" (owner 2026-11-22: "tidak perlu shadow, langsung integrasikan …"): each Position Manager rule that sends a broker action is a line with an id (its `position_actions.rule`) and an int version (`PM_LINES`), behind its own breaker instead of a shadow arm.
  - **The lines.** The fixed rules `take_profit_weakening`, `lock_tp1`, `breakeven`, `tp2_target`, `tp2_breakeven`, `cut_loss`; A8's `loosen_atr_expansion`, `loosen_key_level_outside`, `loosen_high_impact_release`, `tp_nearer_momentum_fading`, `tp_nearer_key_level_before`, `tp_nearer_confidence_drop`, `tp_further_atr_expansion_trend`, `tp_further_key_level_beyond`; `liquidity` (lens B's management reason: cut_loss's `liquidity_sweep_against` reason and A8's `tp_nearer_liquidity_sweep_against`, which is this line rather than a line of its own); PM-1M's `pm_fast_tp`, `pm_fast_cut`. All at version 1. Not lines: `pm_reentry` (RE1 opens a new position, it manages none), and every hold, wait or diagnostic outcome (`hold_*`, `*_waits_*`, `inputs_missing`, `council_unavailable`, `protected`, `inside_band`). The lines of later PRs (A13 setup, B25, B28) are registered when they land.
  - **Records.** D1 `position_actions.lines_json` (added by `ensurePositionSchema`, `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN` in try/catch) = `{ present, cause }`, tags `"id@version"`: `present` the on lines of the layer whose input the evaluation had (the fixed rules with facts, A8's with `pm_loosen_enabled`, `liquidity` with a lens reading; the 1m layer's two), `cause` the line whose reason or level set a close, stop or target (`liquidity` when the same evaluation without lens B's reading would differ, else the rule itself), null otherwise. Off lines are in neither. Rows from before B5 have none and never count.
  - **Lost acks.** A close intent not done (pending or expired; a failed one closed nothing) created within 5 s before the deal's close defers the ticket: no credit and not seen, until the intent reads done (CA1), at most 30 minutes after the close; still unacked then, the ticket is seen and no line is credited (one `pm_line_skip` event, `{ ticket, why: "unacked_close" }`).
  - **What counts.** A close counts for one line: the line that sent it (the position's final `close` intent maps to its action), else the line of the position's last applied (`done`) stop or target change before the close, whoever closed it (the broker's stop or target, or another exit). Only actions at the line's current version and inside its window count. R = the position's `ea_deals` net (`DEAL_NET_SQL`) / `risk_usd`: the open intent's own `risk_usd` (probation opens), else the stop's money as the live parity counts it (`r_dist` / 0.01 × `LIVE_TICK_VALUE_PER_001` × the open volume). A position still on the terminal, or with exits short of its volume, waits. `r_vs_hold` = R minus the R of holding to the plan's SL/TP (the open intent's `sl`, `tp`) from the line's action on, on `broker_bars_1m` in the exit price (`fastBars`), by the daily report's first-touch rule (`heldExitPrice`, which `positionReport` reads); settled once touched, else marked at the last close after 24 h. Recorded only, never a trigger.
  - **The breaker (`pmLineBreakerTick`, awaited beside `probationTick` on the 5-minute ledger step; no cron change).** A line goes off when its last 15 closes average below 0R (`lineBreakerVerdict(closes, { n: 15, floor: 0 })`): KV `pm:line-breaker:v1`, one `omega_events` row (actor `agent:pm-line-breaker`, action `pm_line_off`, `{ line, version, closes, mean_r, off }`) and one Telegram line on the Omega path ("XAUUSD SYSTEM · Position Manager line <id>@<v> off: …"), once per trip.
  - **Skipping.** An off line is not acted on: `positionAction(f, council, { off })` and `fastProtect({ …, off })` read its condition as false, so the rule below it runs exactly as if it had not fired (`take_profit_weakening` off: the TP1 lock or the band's rules; `cut_loss` off: `hold_to_stop`; `liquidity` off: the lens reading absent from the rules). PM-1M skips only `pm_fast_tp` and `pm_fast_cut`. With no line off every output is today's byte for byte (V14).
  - **Back on.** Only by a version bump in `PM_LINES` or by the owner: `POST /omega/api/pm-lines` on the Omega Worker (`/api/probation`'s checks: a person, same origin, JSON at most 256 bytes, `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB`), body `{ line, enabled: true }`, reply `{ ok }` or `{ ok: false, error }`; turning a line on is the only action (`enabled: false` and a line not in `PM_LINES` are refused; other methods 405) → `OmegaGateway.setPmLine` (an `omega_events` `pm_line_on` row, one Telegram line). Either opens a fresh window: closes 0, the last-15 window empty, counted from then on. A line `PM_LINES_OFF` names stays off (`off_by_PM_LINES_OFF`).
  - **`PM_LINES_OFF`** (AI Worker variable, not in wrangler.jsonc; an FS1 token row in plans): comma- or space-separated line ids switched off by hand; each change the tick sees is an `omega_events` `pm_lines_env` row.
  - **Read.** `GET /api/omega/position-manager` (`readPositionManager`) adds `lines: [{ id, version, enabled, off_since, closes, mean_r_last15, r_vs_hold }]` (the C5 mockup's shape) from KV `pm:line-breaker:v1` and `PM_LINES_OFF`: `off_since` ms or null, `closes` the window's count, `mean_r_last15` over its last 15, `r_vs_hold` the mean over the settled holds; numbers or null. The Omega page shows them (C5): Setup › Engine, a block in the Broker engine panel under Reversal probation (`components/PmLines.tsx`, `lib/pmLines.ts`), GET only from its allow-list (`PM_LINES_READS`: this route with `?limit=1`, every 60 s while shown); on ok cyan, off fault red, R neutral; a line off by its breaker shows its `off_since` (UTC · GMT+7) and, for `viewer.can_control`, Turn back on (a confirm box, then the panel's one write, `POST /omega/api/pm-lines`); a line off by `PM_LINES_OFF` alone (`enabled` false, `off_since` null) reads "off by the switch" with the way to Feature switches and no button; a line off by both shows the button and its refusal `off_by_PM_LINES_OFF` as "also off by the switch". Contract: `test/fixtures/nexus-pm-lines/*.json` (the seed's real answers, test/support/nexus-pm-lines-seed.mjs).
