  - **v4.** A KV state cannot be replayed, so every change of the set of off lines (a trip, the owner's turn-on, a bump, `PM_LINES_OFF`) is an `omega_events` row carrying the whole set (`off`); `loadOmega4Context` reads them (`readPmLineHistory`) and `replayOmega4` passes the set in force at each minute (`linesOffAt`) to `positionAction` and `fastProtect`. No history, or none off: the replay byte for byte as before. Gap: a `PM_LINES_OFF` change reaches v4 at the next tick (at most 5 minutes).
  - **Hashes and eras.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (V14: the fixtures carry no line off). `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 38daf894… → 70930517… (`positionAction`, `ruleAction`, `ruleCore`, `a8Action`, `runPositionManager`, `fastProtect`, `runFastProtect` and the new `pm-line-breaker` module in `ENGINE_CODE`); the era carries over at the deploy. No `ERA_SWITCHES` change: the lines are not an Omega setting, and v4 replays the off set from its history inside the era, as CDG's gate.
- **Omega health for the platform's AI Mesh (owner 2026-11-12: "bagaimana kita juga ingin menampilkan remote vps atau omega engine dengan EA nya bisa termonitor di Ai Mesh"):** the kernel (`cryptophys-ai-kernel`, binding `XAUUSD_OMEGA_HEALTH`) calls this Worker's RPC entrypoint `OmegaHealth.health()` (`src/omega-health.mjs`, schema `omega-health.v1`). It reads the EA heartbeat (KV `ea:heartbeat:last`: age, fresh within 15 minutes, version, mode, trading allowed, open positions), the Omega control mode, and the VPS bridge's last sync (D1 `omega_bridge`: age, fresh within 2 minutes, bridge version, and the terminal's connected and trade-allowed while its state is under a minute old and the credentials are stored). The `ea` block is the fresher of the legacy EA's heartbeat (KV `ea:heartbeat:last`, fresh within 15 minutes) and OmegaExecutor's state relayed by the bridge (D1 `omega_bridge.state.received_ms`, fresh within 2 minutes; `source` says which; since OmegaExecutor 1.12 its `version`, and `mode` `omega_only` or `local_stops` from its `executor` report), because OmegaExecutor sends no HTTP heartbeat. It writes nothing, returns no account figure, server name or credential, and an unmeasured value is null. Nothing else is reachable through that entrypoint.
- **The organs' DNA for the platform census (owner 2026-11-12: "DNA untuk XAUUSD dan Omega dengan auto-register"):** `organism.json` (schema `xauusd-organism.v1`) declares the five Workers, the 16 agents that report to the platform kernel (`AGENTS` binding: `xauusd-reasoner`, `xauusd-moderator`, `xauusd-risk-manager`, `nexus-teacher`, `shadow-knowledge` (KA2, 2026-11-24), `xauusd-model-router` (SB1, 2026-11-25) and the ten `hud-*` agents of `HUD_PLATFORM_IDS`), one declared-idle gene (`hud-mizan-oracle`: registered by platform PF1, migration 0087, with the same four capabilities at tier 1; nothing reports as it until B27, and `DECLARED_IDLE` in the test is empty after B27) and every AI task the code runs (each `schemaName` and `aiRunAs` label, the names the AI usage meter records). `test/organism-dna.test.mjs` keeps it equal to the code. The platform Forge (binding `EXT_ORGANISM_XAUUSD`) reads it through the RPC entrypoint `OrganismDna.dna()` (`src/organism.mjs`) and takes it into its organism census; a new agent or AI task here is then registered on the platform by consensus. External organs stay within the platform's ceiling: tier 1, no council seat, only `signals.observe`, `reports.publish`, `memory.recall` and `memory.remember`, except what `XAUUSD_CEILING` names per agent: `xauusd-moderator` also `council.decisions.read`, `nexus-teacher` only `corrections.record` (platform HAIC H3e), `shadow-knowledge` only `knowledge.verdict` (platform PF3, migration 0088; the census proposes its `register_agent`, which the platform side carries out), and `xauusd-model-router` only `model_router.status` and `model_router.report_paid` (platform AIR1, 982971c, the ADR-0007 amendment; the census proposes its `register_agent`). Every declared capability id matches the platform's rule `/^[a-z][a-z_]*\.[a-z_]+(\.[a-z_]+)?$/` (test D2). Nothing else is reachable through that entrypoint.
  - **The owner's corrections (HAIC H3f; platform docs/design/HAIC-H3.md §4, §7.2; the full H3f: owner "2. yes", 2026-11-25).** `src/owner-corrections.mjs` holds the `correction.v1` contract (a port of the platform's `correction.ts`, checked against the shared fixtures in `test/fixtures/correction-v1`), the local D1 table `owner_corrections` (the original, prices included, stays here; the masked record is what leaves) and the sender: every sweep, `nexus-teacher` sends pending records through `AGENTS` as `corrections.record`, oldest first, each call within 15 seconds. A thrown error or a timeout stops the sweep and the record waits for the next one (at most 48 attempts); the kernel's own `failed` is final for the record (`rejected`, never retried) and stops the sweep; at most 3 denied, failed or timed-out calls in any hour (the kernel proposes a suspension at 5); a denial (the agent's or the system's state) stops the sweep with every record still pending and waits an hour, then one `whoami` decides (enabled and holding `corrections.record`). A payload that would carry a secret anywhere, or a price-like number outside its ref and id fields, is never sent; the raw category goes through the same mask as the text and is then cut to 60 characters (the platform's order), `supersedes` is sent null, and secrets are redacted in the local copy too (prices stay). Behind `OWNER_CORRECTIONS` (FS1 row, group knowledge, off unless "on"; not set in wrangler.jsonc): **off until the owner turns it on in FS1**; off, nothing is recorded or sent and D1 is not touched (`disabled`). A correction never writes a parameter, a weight, a profile or a gate, never arms a strategy and never enters `outcome_scores`.
    - **The route** (owner "harus dibuat jalurnya untuk feedback loop"): `POST /omega/api/correction` on the Omega Worker (through Nexus' `/omega/*` forward), the guard chain of `POST /omega/api/feature-switches` (a person, same origin, JSON at most 4096 bytes, `sec-fetch-site` same-origin, `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB` set and equal to the Access user id, else 403 `owner_only`). Body, shape-checked there (`correctionRequest`; anything else is 400 with its code): `source` (`nexus_card`, `nexus_room`, `nexus_engine`, `nexus_council`), `label` (`owner_rating` with `rating` `up`/`down`, or `owner_correction` with a `category` or `text`), `rating`, `category` (one of `CORRECTION_CATEGORIES`), `target` `{ ref (ev:…), subject (agent:|model:|seat:…), as_of }`, `text` (at most 500 characters), `evidence_refs` (at most 10 ev: refs). → `OmegaGateway.recordCorrection` (FS1-resolved env; the owner checked again; the actor `owner:<Access email>`) → `recordOwnerCorrection`. Reply `{ ok, correction_id, redactions, deduplicated }`, never the text; HTTP 429 `rate_limited`, 409 `disabled`, 403 `owner_only`, 400 any other refusal, 502 `correction_failed`.
    - **Dedup and cap.** The same correction again (its `dedup_sha256`) answers the stored id with `deduplicated: true` and stores nothing; the insert is `INSERT OR IGNORE` on the unique index `idx_owner_corrections_dedup` with the hour's cap in the same statement, so simultaneous POSTs store one row. At most `CORRECTIONS_PER_HOUR` (30) corrections in any rolling hour, then `rate_limited`. `test/owner-corrections-route-h3f.test.mjs` pins the route, the masking in the reply and the sent payload, the dedup, the race, the cap and the switch.
    - **The refs the control cites (H3f-refs, 2026-11-25).** Each owner-judgeable output Nexus reads carries `ev_ref`, built on read by `evRef` (`src/cfc-pack.mjs`, `REF_RE`); read-time only (no D1 or KV write; the rooms, councils, prompts and agents' words unchanged; every other key unchanged). `GET /api/war-room/latest` `room.openings[]`, `room.debate[]`, `room.conclusion` and `GET /api/threads/timeframe` `thread.openings[]`, `thread.debate[]`, `thread.conclusion`: `ev:xauusd/agent_thread_messages/<row id>` (the message row's integer id, from the row or the run document's step; null for a step without one; never the run id; shadow openings none). `GET /api/council/sessions` `sessions[]` and `GET /api/council/session` `session`: `ev:xauusd/council_sessions/<id>`; `session.messages[]`: `ev:xauusd/council_messages/<id>`; `session.options.options[]`: `ev:xauusd/council_sessions/<id>#options.<option_id>` (only when the options are the parsed object with an options array; null when the correction route would refuse it: a `-` or `:`, a price word last, or a 3+ digit number in the model-written option_id (`optionRef`)). `GET /api/council/proposals` `proposals[]`: `ev:xauusd/council_proposals/<id>`. `GET /api/strategy-agents` `agents[].verdicts`: `ev:xauusd/strategy_council_runs/<day>/<strategy id>#proposal` (from the KV document's day; null without it). The decision cards, kernel reports and the Reasoner's input pick their fields and carry none. `test/ev-refs-h3f.test.mjs`.
    - **The Nexus control** (H3f UI, 2026-11-25; `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui` `components/Correction.tsx`, `lib/corrections.ts`): a "Rate / correct" button on the Decision Engine card, the room conclusion cards, openings and rebuttals, Call for Councils opinions and options, and the strategy council verdicts, only for the owner (`viewer.can_control`) while `OWNER_CORRECTIONS` is on (one shared read of `GET /omega/api/feature-switches`), and only where the output carries an ev: ref (the engine decision's `engine_decisions/<decision_key>`, else its `ev_ref`) valid by `REF_RE` and the contract's form; the decision audit has none. Its one write is `POST /omega/api/correction`.
    - **Into the Call for Councils packs** (HAIC H3g, 2026-11-25; the owner's H3f "2. yes", "harus dibuat jalurnya untuk feedback loop"): `src/cfc-pack.mjs` reads the owner's corrections (any send status but `rejected`: a record the platform's contract refused is left out) into the seat pack (`seat:<id>`: `target.subject` `agent:<seat>` or `seat:<seat>`) and the learned-item pack (`knowledge:<item>`: `target.ref` `ev:xauusd/learned_live/<item>` ending there or at `/`, `@`, `#`, or a subject `agent:`, `model:` or `seat:<item>`) as the section `owner_corrections` `{ n, items: [{ correction_id, label, rating, category, text, subject, target_ref, created_at, redactions }], note }`. One query selects only `correction_id`, `record_json` (the masked record) and `created_ms`, never `original_json`: rows of the last 60 days (`PACK_LIMITS.window_days`), newest first, at most 50 read (`corrections_read`); the newest per `target.ref`, then at most 5 (`PACK_LIMITS.corrections`); the block goes through `stripPrices` and an item whose text still holds a 3+ digit run is dropped. Each item is a ref `ev:xauusd/owner_corrections/<correction_id>`. `OWNER_CORRECTIONS` is resolved as `OmegaGateway.recordCorrection` resolves it (FS1 `withFeatureSwitches`, then `correctionsEnabled`): off, the section is `unavailable: owner corrections are off (FS1 OWNER_CORRECTIONS)` and the table is not read; a missing table or a failed read is `unavailable` too. The seat prompts are unchanged beyond this evidence block. `test/cfc-pack-corrections-h3g.test.mjs`.
- The AI Worker calls `AETHER` → `cryptophys-aether` `KnowledgeIngress` (Aether, the Knowledge Guardian; RFC_AETHER_KNOWLEDGE_GUARDIAN phase A5, owner go 2026-11-07). It is the second binding to a Worker outside this repository, and it adds no Worker to the topology above.
  - **Identity:** no props, no secret or token. Aether accepts calls only through a Service Binding in this account. No other Worker, and not the preview config, may carry this binding. `test/ssot-topology.test.mjs` guards it.
  - **What is sent** (`src/aether-push.mjs`, once an hour in the first parity sweep after the H1 close): the decision cards of `src/decision-cards.mjs`.
    - Up to 12 cards of the 15m and 1H rooms, and up to 8 of the 4H and D1 rooms. These are the latest cards of each chart, which covers the 4-candle outcome horizon.
    - A card is sent again only when its content changes, for example when its outcomes are scored. D1 `aether_card_pushes` (migration `0012`) keeps the hash last sent.
    - A card that fails the local leak check (any level, price, plan or size) is never sent. Aether checks every card again and may refuse one; a refusal is recorded and that content is not sent again.
  - **Rules:**
    - Advisory only. A push never changes a room, a decision or a card, and the sweep never waits on Aether.
    - A transport failure is non-fatal and leaves the ledger untouched, so the next hour retries it.
    - Aether never writes here.
  - **The pull (phase A7):** once an hour the same binding also reads `learnedPack()`, the items a person approved for shadow testing. Approved parameter candidates run as shadow versions (see "Shadow indicator versions"); nothing from the pack reaches a room, a prompt or a decision.
  - **The Knowledge Council's facts** (owner 2026-11-11: "autonomous doctrine protocol harus dilakukan oleh knowledge council, dilakukan harian dan di propose ke cerebrum untuk eksekusinya"; `src/doctrine-facts.mjs`). In the same hourly sweep the binding sends `ingestDoctrineFacts` (`cryptophys.doctrine-facts.v1`): the Nexus seat (the Decision Engine's switch, cadence, witness weights, guardrails, trading mode and thresholds; the rooms' default quorum share and the 15m confidence cap; the weighting mode and its cadence; the EA blackout; parity and the lane) and the Omega seat (the Omega settings in force and its fixed gates; since ADR-0020 Amendment 10, 2026-11-21, also the limits per profile under those settings, `omega.risk_pct_<profile>`, `omega.max_daily_loss_pct_<profile>` and `omega.max_positions_<profile>` for safe, conservative, aggressive and custom, which the doctrine bounds per profile, and the connected account's `omega.profile` with its `omega.effective_risk_pct`, `omega.effective_max_daily_loss_pct` and `omega.effective_max_positions`; the single `omega.risk_pct`, `omega.max_daily_loss_pct` and `omega.max_positions` stay as information), each tagged with the deployed version (`version_metadata` binding `CF_VERSION_METADATA`). Values only, read from the code, variables and stored settings; no price, level, size or account. The facts are sent hourly while the market is closed too (the idle heartbeat): on 2026-11-21 an old build's facts (21 instead of 91) reached the council of 03:52 UTC and two constitution amendments were executed from them. Aether's council compares them with its canon doctrine (v2) once a day and proposes each difference measured twice from the same version to the Cerebrum consensus, which executes it (an operational fact inside its hard bound after the vote, anything else after one owner click). Nothing here changes a decision.
  - **Status:** `GET /api/aether/status` shows the push ledger per chart, the last 24 hours and the latest refusals, without card content, and the learned pack (`learned_pack`).

## 3. Three AI input lanes (local-primary)
Owner decision, 2026-11-05: the architecture does not depend on webhooks. Approved local records drive the chain, and TradingView webhooks only calibrate the local engines. `INDICATOR_SOURCE` controls this: `"local"` is the default; `"webhook"` restores the legacy webhook-driven chain as a rollback.

1. **Local computation** (decision lane). MizanLib (record id `mizan`), Prophet and GMI are computed by the pull adapter from closed TradingView bars.
   - **One build per bar (CPU, 2026-11-19, owner relayed "setuju semua").** The cron runs every minute, and each publisher (H1, M5, M15, H4/D1, the HUD snapshots) reads its bar's "already published" marker before running any engine. The key is the producer's own candle key of the last candle (`peekCandleKey`), so a bar already sent costs one KV read (in node about 4.5 s of rebuilds a minute became 0.1 ms). The build of a bar not yet sent is unchanged, and the marker is read again after it; `force` still rebuilds.
   - Since 2026-11-05 (owner decision; the daily budget is 500 calls since 2026-11-14, the account's provider quota, see below), the pull adapter pulls XAUUSD M1, H4, D1 and W1, USOIL 5m, and DXY and USDT 4h; the XAUUSD H1 bars are built from the M1 pull since 2026-11-14, like the 5m and 15m bars (on the UTC hour, as TradingView's; checked once a day against a few native bars).
   - **CMC USDT shadow (2026-11-27, TradingView cleanup; owner: "gunakan coinmarketcap sudah disediakan API nya").** `CRYPTOCAP:USDT` is Tether's market capitalisation in US dollars (dominance is `CRYPTOCAP:USDT.D`); since 2026-11-27 (owner: "pilih A, betulkan label USDT market cap") every room, prompt and reading calls it the "USDT market cap" (before: "USDT dominance"). The AI Worker samples CoinMarketCap once per UTC hour from the sweep (`src/cmc-usdt-shadow.mjs`, `runCmcUsdtStep`; the closed-market heartbeat too, since crypto trades on the weekend): `/v2/cryptocurrency/quotes/latest?id=825` (USDT market cap and CMC's dominance) and `/v1/global-metrics/quotes/latest` (the total, for our own dominance), 2 credits an hour. The samples (35 days) sit in KV `cmc:usdt:shadow:v1` with the day's credit count; 4h bars on the UTC grid (close = the first sample after the bar closed) are compared with the stored `CRYPTOCAP:USDT|4h` bar of the same open time (median, p90, max and mean signed error in basis points). Shadow only: no room, decision, engine or page reads it. Switch `CMC_USDT_SHADOW` (production `"on"`, previews `"off"`); dormant without the `CMC_API_KEY` secret. Logs carry status codes, counts and error statistics, never the key or a value. A failed hour is not retried within that hour.
   - **Who spends the TradingView quota** (2026-11-11, owner: "check dulu penyebab utama hari ini reach kuota limit"). The news-ingestion Worker, the system's only TradingView MCP gateway, counts every provider call it makes per caller and UTC hour (`src/provider-usage.mjs`, KV `tv_mcp:calls:<day>:<caller>`, 8 days; callers `pull_adapter`, `gold_history`, `calendar`, `news_cron`, `news_ai_worker`, `news_http`), with the failures and the time and caller of the quota refusal. Shown in its `GET /health` (`provider_calls`, today and yesterday) and in `gatewayStatus().calls_today`; the `market_data_stale` alert names the counts. Calls made outside this system on the same TradingView account (other agents' MCP connectors) are not counted. The counts are a floor (a KV read-modify-write per caller).
   - **Shown in Nexus** (owner 2026-11-14): Status, "TradingView quota": today's calls against `provider_daily_limit`, per caller today and yesterday, per UTC hour, when the quota ran out, the cooldown, and the chart reserve (`chart_reserve` in the news worker's `/health`, relayed by `/api/status`). Since 2026-11-17 (owner, relayed: "setuju perbaikan alert dan kuota") one **account budget** admits every provider call at the gateway (`admitCalls`): cap = quota − `ACCOUNT_SAFETY_MARGIN` (500 − 20 = 480, also the pull adapter's `TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET`); the chart (pull adapter, gold history) first up to the cap; the calendar within `CALENDAR_DAILY_CAP` (30) while the chart's need fits; news last within `NEWS_DAILY_CAP` (60) while the chart's need and the calendar's rest fit. A refused chart job answers `account_budget_exhausted` until 00:00 UTC (the pull adapter keeps the cooldown, spends nothing, and the MT5 fallback takes over). `account_budget` in `/health` and `gatewayStatus()` gives the cap, the chart's calls and need, the calendar's rest, the room for news and the headroom; `TV_ACCOUNT_BUDGET` "off" disables it.
   - **The daily budget is counted atomically (2026-11-14, ADR-0015 phase 4 narrow).** The pull adapter reserves its calls through `LocalComputationIngress.reserveTvCalls` (`src/tv-budget.mjs`, D1 `tv_call_budget`): one statement grants `min(wanted, daily − used)` and adds it, so two runs close together cannot both pass `TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET`; `releaseTvCalls` gives back a refused run's calls. The KV key `market-data:v2:mcp-calls:<day>` keeps a copy for the diagnostics page, and the KV count is used as before when the binding cannot answer (`budget.counter` says which). The pull adapter still owns no D1. Note (2026-11-12/13): the late-evening gaps in the H1 parity comparisons (19:00–22:00 UTC) came from the provider's daily quota running out (about 500 calls, gateway cooldown to UTC midnight), not from KV consistency; so the pull adapter's budget is the provider's quota itself since 2026-11-14 (owner: "sesuaikan batas dengan kuota TV agar sama"): `TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET` 500 = `PROVIDER_DAILY_LIMIT` 500, one number (`test/tv-quota-alignment.test.mjs`); the stale one-day override is gone. The quota is the account's, shared with the news pulls, which yield to the chart near the limit (`chartReserve`); the same number alone does not stop the account reaching it, which is why the chart's own calls were cut (the 1H bars from the minutes) and the Monday check measures a full weekday.
   - **The provider's reset time (QB1 O5, 2026-11-26).** On a quota refusal the gateway reads the provider's reset from its text (`quotaCooldown` in `fundamental-news-ingestion/src/index.mjs`): "until HH:MM[:SS] UTC|GMT|Z" (the 2026-11-11 form "suspended until"), an ISO timestamp, "in N seconds|minutes|hours", then the Retry-After header; a time over 48 hours ahead or in the past is not trusted. When none is read the cooldown stays one hour, the hourly probe as before. Each refusal logs `tv_quota_refusal` with the form read, the end, and the refusal's text with no secret in it (`sanitizeRefusalText`: bearer tokens, token-like runs of 32+ characters, emails and URLs removed, 300 characters), so an unknown form can be read. 2026-11-26: the quota ran out at 00:50Z, the refusal matched no form, and every hourly probe pushed the cooldown on. **Logs kept and redaction fixed (RD, 2026-11-27).** The gateway now keeps its console logs (`observability`, head sampling 1, persisted; invocation logs off, because they record each request's URL and `/oauth/callback?code=...` carries the OAuth code), so `tv_quota_refusal` and the ingest summaries are readable. Before that, `redactDiagnosticText` (the upstream error text in the stored last-pull summary, `tv_mcp:last_scheduled_pull`, served on public `/health` as `last_scheduled_ingest.failure_diagnostic`, and in the ingest logs) had doubled escapes and removed nothing; it now uses `sanitizeText`, the refusal log's rules plus credential key=value pairs (`access_token`, `refresh_token`, `id_token`, `client_secret`, `code_verifier`, `api_key`, `password`, `authorization`), the scheduled catch logs `sanitizeText(error.message, 200)`, and the `/pull` and RPC error bodies carry `sanitizeText(error.message, 300)` as `detail`. Public `/health` serves no free text from upstream any more: `last_scheduled_ingest` keeps status, counts, codes and timestamps, and the diagnostic is reduced to `failure_code` and `upstream_error_code` (`publicLastPull`); its sanitized text stays in the stored summary and the ingest log. A read-only check on 2026-11-27 found no token-like text in what `/health` served.
   - **A cooldown spends no budget.** The pull adapter remembers the gateway's quota cooldown (KV `market-data:v2:gateway-cooldown`) and, while it holds, neither reserves from the daily budget nor calls the gateway (status `mcp_quota_cooldown_active`, so the MT5 fallback still sees the gateway down); a run the gateway refused before calling TradingView gives its reservation back. 2026-11-11: the budget read exhausted by evening from runs that never reached TradingView.
   - **Cadence (2026-11-08, quota, owner decision):** USOIL 5m (macro) is pulled once per 15 minutes, at the 15m close, with every bar (`cadence: 900`). The gold 1m chart stays every 5 minutes (`cadence: 300` since the cron runs every minute, 2026-11-09). The other series are pulled once per new closed bar. A weekday cost about 590 calls until 2026-11-14 (the pull adapter about 455, the news 144), and the account's provider quota refuses the 501st call of the UTC day: it ran out at about 20:00 UTC on 2026-11-12 and 2026-11-13, so the gold chart stood still for the last two hours of New York. Since 2026-11-14 (owner: "kerjakan juga 1-3") a weekday costs about 490:
     - **One news pull an hour, not two.** The AI Worker's hourly news analysis (:06) pulled again for the same headlines, because the scheduled pull's edge cache sits in another data centre. The pull now also keeps a shared copy in KV (`tv_mcp:news:last:<symbols>`, `TV_MCP_SHARED_CACHE_SECONDS`, 900 s), and the news cron runs at :04, so the :06 pull reads it (minus 72 calls).
     - **The 1H bars from the minutes** (above; minus 24).
     - **The chart comes first** (`chartReserve` in `fundamental-news-ingestion/src/provider-usage.mjs`): a news pull is refused (`reserved_for_chart`) while today's calls, the pull and what the chart still needs before UTC midnight (its median calls per completed hour today times the hours left) would pass `TV_PROVIDER_DAILY_LIMIT` (500). `TV_NEWS_RESERVE` "off" leaves the check out. The pull adapter's own budget (`TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET`, 800) is unchanged; it cannot see the news calls.
   - **Weekend (2026-11-08):** no pull for non-crypto series from Friday 17:30 to Sunday 18:00 New York time (`marketClosed`; Friday 22:30 to Sunday 23:00 UTC in winter, an hour earlier in summer), so a closed market spends no TradingView call. An empty series still bootstraps once, and USDT (crypto) is pulled every day until the closure is idle (below). **Closed-market idle (2026-11-14, resource audit):** once a run of the closure left nothing to pull, bootstrap or heal (`pendingWork`), the refresh summary carries `closed_idle` (the closure's Friday, `closureId`), and every later minute of that closure reads only that summary and stops (one KV read; the summary is rewritten every 30 minutes as a heartbeat, `market_closed: true`); a closure with broker bars to heal or an empty series keeps running in full. The reopen runs the full cycle again, USDT's weekend bars included. While gold trades the cron's bundle is built from the records the refresh left (`refreshSeries` `keep`), so each series is read once a minute, not twice. After the reopen, a series whose last pull after the gap brought no new bar waits for its grid's next close (the cached bar's open plus whole bars, plus the settle; `gapRetrySec`, 2026-11-16, task #89): on Monday 2026-11-16 the D1 bar was pulled every hour from the reopen to its first close at 22:00 UTC (about 22 calls) and each 4h series every hour until 02:00, about 35 calls a Monday in all; the first bars now come at their close (4h 02:01, D1 22:01) instead of up to an hour later. When a grid close passed while the market was open and the pull right after it still found nothing (a session moved by a DST change, a holiday), it waits a quarter of its bar (5 minutes to 1 hour, `gapBackoffSeconds`, 2026-11-09) as before.
   - **Context series (2026-11-08, backlog 11.3).** The pull adapter also pulls `TVC:US10Y` 4h, and keeps `OANDA:XAGUSD` 4h (role `context`), for the Nexus correlations panel.
     - **Silver from TradingView again (QB1-R, 2026-11-26).** QB1 O3 built `OANDA:XAGUSD` 4h from the broker's silver at all times; it was reverted the same day, because the bridge sends no macro symbol on the active broker (its corpus holds gold only), so silver froze. Silver is pulled again (6 calls a weekday) and is a fallback macro target as before. The builder stays inert: `BROKER_TARGETS` is empty and no SERIES entry has `broker`; `rebuildFromBroker` (`market-data-ingestion/src/mt5-fallback.mjs`, without the fallback mark) waits until the bridge sends macro symbols.
     - **Cost.** About 6 calls a day (US10Y). Last in priority.
     - **Isolation.** They never enter the engine input bundle (`buildBundle` skips them), so local computation and parity are unchanged.
   - It aggregates XAUUSD M5 and M15 from M1, and USOIL M15 and H1 from USOIL 5m. The daily check against native bars was retired on 2026-11-27 (TradingView cleanup, step 1).
   - See docs/MARKET_DATA_INGESTION.md.
   - **Trust is the frozen identity table (parity removal S1, 2026-11-26).** Owner: "Kita akan remove parity karena efeknya kecil dan sudah terbukti", then "langkah pertama hapus parity dulu saja tapi tetap pertahankan arsitektur sekarang kita akan review lagi nanti"; orchestrator PR1 ruling 1. `isTrusted` (src/parity-registry.mjs) reads `TRUSTED_IDENTITIES` (src/trusted-identities.mjs): the eight identities the registry had approved at their current implementation hashes (prophet, gmi, prophet_m5, gmi_m5, prophet_m15, gmi_m15, prophet_h4, gmi_h4); Mizan is trusted per chart only through a trusted Prophet AND GMI that declare it, as before. `*_d1` and Mizan on D1 stay untrusted, `gmi_hud` stays revoked, group identities (B7) are never trusted. `m5ParityApproved` reads the table too. The registry is still written (comparisons, promotions, revocations, the parity view) but nothing reads it for trust (`registryApproved` is the registry's own view). On the 2026-11-26 registry snapshot the table answers as the registry did for all 16 identities the producers emit (test TI-2 on the snapshot's shape). **A change to a hashed engine file** fails `test/trusted-identities.test.mjs` (TI-1) until the table is updated in the same PR: a behaviour-identical refactor (V14 and the golden outputs equal) by review; a behaviour change or a new engine with a shadow-agreement report against the previous identity on live bars and the owner's explicit OK (doctrine `promotion_by_owner`). There is no automatic promotion. S2 to S6 (stop comparing, webhooks off, parity-only producers, display, data) follow one PR at a time. Before S1: trust was earned only through the registry's 24 consecutive matching candles.
   - **Nothing is compared any more (parity removal S2, 2026-11-27).** `reconcileLocalComputation` (src/parity-sweep.mjs) reads each observed decision record and writes its trusted copy (`localcalc:<candle>:<engine>`, `approved_by_parity`) when its identity is in the frozen table, and removes the copy of a visible record outside it; it reads no webhook and writes nothing to the parity registry (no `recordComparison`, no promotion or revocation; the registry rows stay as they were, read only by the parity view and the status pages until S5/S6). HUD and group identities never get a copy. The queue tasks still call it (the H1 copy is written as soon as the record arrives) and the `10-59/15` sweep still refreshes the recent candles and hosts its chain; the approved_at repair, the input-only reclassification and the intrabar recheck no longer run. **The input-bar check is gone with the comparisons**: a record built on a provisional bar enters the lane, and the republished final bar replaces it at the next reconcile (history 2026-11-03..27: input-mismatch candles were 12 of 3,401 on 5m, 10 of 1,196 on 15m, 14 of 345 on 1H, 2 of 72 on 4H). `barsVerified` (src/hud-store.mjs) is the candle's trusted copy built on native bars (not `feed: mt5_fallback`), no longer a matching comparison row (orchestrator PR1 ruling 2; 10 of 1,196 15m candles read differently, all with an input-bar difference). `/ready` reports `provenance_mode: "frozen_identity_registry"` and `trusted_identities` (8); the deploy smoke test (deploy-worker.yml) and the preview e2e check both. Webhooks were received and stored until S3.
   - **TradingView webhooks off (parity removal S3, 2026-11-27; owner: "bersihkan juga parity yang sudah tidak digunakan", "Lanjutkan pembersihan ketergantungan tradingview").** `POST /webhook` answers **410** `{"error":"gone","reason":"tradingview_webhooks_retired"}` before it reads the body or checks the secret (`src/webhook-retirement.mjs`), and writes nothing: no `webhook_sources` row, no `WEBHOOK_BUFFER candle:*`, no queue task, no rejection row. `WEBHOOK_INGEST` (default `"off"`; `"on"` restores the old handler unchanged, a one-deploy rollback until S5 deletes it). Before: 25 alert streams (Prophet R38, Prophet R37, GMI v12.70 and the two HUD comparators on 5m, 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D), ~1,390 a day. Nothing live read them (`INDICATOR_SOURCE` local: the H1 chain builds its indicator payloads from the local records; the evidence fusion's calibration lane never blocks). Health items 8 (webhook rejected, 401) and 11 (alert silent) run only while `WEBHOOK_INGEST` is on. The owner deletes the 25 alerts on TradingView after the deploy is verified.
   - The chain starts as soon as the parity-approved (`localcalc:`) records of a closed H1 candle exist. It does not wait for any webhook.
   - The indicator values the agents read (`source_payloads`, `origin: "local_computation"`) are the approved records' comparable fields. These are the same field names and codes the TradingView alerts carry.
   - A candle whose approved local records do not arrive within 30 minutes of the close is recorded as a fail-closed WAIT. A revoked or never-approved identity therefore never trades.
2. **TradingView webhook** (calibration only). The sources are `prophet_r38`, `prophet_r37_ssot` and `gmi_v12_70`, on H1 and 5m.
   - **Prophet is read as one indicator** (owner decision 2026-11-06).
     - Prophet R38 (the standalone indicator) and Prophet R37 (the core inside GMI, which sends `prophet_r37_ssot`) share one MizanLib structure core: their `d_enc` and subtype are identical in production.
     - Every reading therefore decodes them as a single "Prophet": R38's signal and trade management, R37's consensus direction and confirmation candle, and the shared structure once. This covers the TradingView reading of the H1 chain, the H1 bus, the timeframe desks and the Prophet HUD agent.
     - The Prophet HUD agent reads, in order:
       1. its signal (R38 confirmations);
       2. its direction, from the R37 consensus (GMI's `prophet_dir`, equal to the R37 alert's direction);
       3. its structure;
       4. its trade management;
       5. its setup (setup gate and cycles);
       6. its regime.
     - The signal, direction, structure and management lines are each verified by parity.
     - The direction is Prophet's bias.
     - The two alerts stay separate in TradingView for calibration, because the R37 alert proves that GMI's structure equals Prophet's.
   - Webhooks were stored until S3 (since S2, 2026-11-27, no longer compared with the local records); since S3 (2026-11-27) `POST /webhook` answers 410 and stores nothing. They never started the chain and were never decision evidence (`input_lanes.tradingview_webhook.role: "calibration_only"`, now with no present source).
   - Approval persists while no webhook arrives. A mismatch during a calibration run revokes the identity, and the chain then fails closed until it re-earns 24/24.
   - **Input mismatch** (owner decision 2026-11-06). When only the input bar differs (open, high, low, close or volume) and every indicator field agrees, the comparison is `input_mismatch`.
     - It neither counts nor revokes.
     - That candle's records stay out of the trusted lane, so the H1 chain reads the TradingView evidence for it.
     - The pull adapter rebuilds an H1 candle whose bar TradingView revised after publication. It cuts the bundle at that close and publishes once more (`republishRevisedH1`). The new comparison replaces the open one and counts.
     - `INPUT_MISMATCH_REVOKE_AFTER` (3) input mismatches in a row on different candles mean the data pipeline is wrong, so they revoke like a mismatch.
     - One-time reconciliation (owner approval 2026-11-06): `reclassifyOnce` reclassifies the earlier input-only mismatches and restores the identities they revoked (the H1 `prophet` and `gmi` revocations of 2026-11-06). It runs once per database, guarded by a KV marker.
       - Single-shot (2026-11-25): the marker `parity:reclassify-input-only:v1` (`SIGNAL_CACHE`, no TTL) is the only gate. It is written before the reclassification, so a run that fails part-way is not repeated; without a `SIGNAL_CACHE` binding it refuses instead of running on every sweep. To run it again, delete the marker deliberately. It never restores a row whose `approved_at` is not an ISO timestamp (an unknown approval time).
   - **Approval times (fix 2026-11-25).** The promotion `UPDATE` in `recordComparison` bound the threshold (24) as the approval time and the time as the `revoked_at` threshold, so every promotion stored `approved_at` "24.0" and never cleared `revoked_at`. The bind is fixed: a promotion stores `approved_at` = the comparison time and clears `revoked_at`. Status and trust were never affected.
     - One-time repair (`repairApprovedAtOnce`, `src/parity-sweep.mjs`, in the parity sweep before the reclassification; KV marker `parity:repair:approved-at:v1` in `SIGNAL_CACHE`, no TTL). For each row whose `approved_at` is set but not ISO, it finds the 24th `match` comparison (by `compared_at`) after the identity's last `mismatch`, or from its first comparison when it has none (`incomplete` and `input_mismatch` neither advance nor reset the streak). If found, `approved_at` becomes its `compared_at`, and an approved row's `revoked_at` becomes NULL. If not (a revoked row, or an approved row with fewer matches after its last mismatch), nothing is written for the row. It never changes status, a counter or `updated_at`. One log line `parity_approved_at_repair` with `{ event, repaired, skipped: [ids], revoked_cleared }`.
   - **Calibration runbook (for example monthly).**
     1. Install the existing alerts on the H1 chart, and on the 5m chart for the 5m lane.
     2. Leave them on for at least a few candles.
     3. Check `GET /api/status` → `ai_worker.local_lane.calibration`. It shows the last calibration per identity and `calibration_due` after `CALIBRATION_INTERVAL_DAYS`, which defaults to 30.
     4. Remove the alerts again.
   - Any change to a hashed engine file creates a new identity. That identity needs a calibration run of 24/24 before it is trusted.
   - **Field groups (B7, 2026-11-25; inert until B11).** Parity can be trusted per group of fields instead of per whole engine, so one bad field revokes only its group (`src/parity-registry.mjs`).
     - `PARITY_GROUPS` declares each group by id: the base engine whose records carry it, the webhook source it is compared with, its `fields` as { local field: webhook field }, and its `kind`.
     - **Identity.** `groupIdentityOf(record, groupId)` is the engine identity (the same source and implementation hashes) under the registry engine id `<group id><timeframe suffix>` (for example `probe_mizan_witness_m15`). Each group therefore has its own 24-candle window, approval and revocation in the same tables (`engine_parity_registry`, `engine_parity_comparisons`), and its own rows in the parity view.
     - **Group ids.** No group id starts with `mizan`, because `isTrusted` reads such an engine id as the Mizan library, which only its dependants vouch for. A group id is never a base engine id and never carries a timeframe suffix (`parityGroupProblems`).
     - **Comparison.** `compareWithWebhooks(record, webhooks, policy, { fieldMap, nullEqual, requireFinite })` compares only the group's fields, plus the input bar as for every comparison. `nullEqual` counts null === null as equal, and `groupCompareOptions` sets it only for a `kind: "hud"` group. Without these options the comparison is exactly the engine's.
     - **Sweep.** `reconcileLocalComputation` compares each group of an observed record on its own, after the engines. A trusted group's copy is `localcalc:<candle>:<group id>`, holding only that group's fields. The input-mismatch and MT5-fallback rules apply as for the HUD identities. The whole-engine comparison and trust are unchanged, and so is the sweep's output for a record without groups (no `groups` key).
     - **HUD store.** `approvedComparable` merges per source and per group, so a trusted group's fields read as verified even while another group of the same source, or the whole engine, is revoked.
     - **The witness group `probe_mizan_witness`** (MZ1, owner 2026-11-23: "Setuju ketiganya", "Sebelum parity"). Engine `probe_mizan` (the Mizan Probe's local port, B26), source `probe_mizan_hud` (the Probe's alert, B10), fields `net_witness` and `prior_edge`, `kind: "witness"`, `nullEqual` off, `requireFinite` on.
       - A candle matches only when both fields are finite numbers on both sides and equal.
       - Null (or any non-finite value) on both sides is `incomplete`, with `reason: "fields_not_finite"` and `null_fields`. It is never a match, and it neither advances nor resets the streak.
       - Finite on one side only is a mismatch.
     - **The adoption HUD groups (B10, `kind: "hud"`, `nullEqual` on).** Engine `gmi_adopt`, source `gmi_v12_70_adopt_hud` (the GMI `*.hud.v2` alert): `gmi_adopt_hud`, `_flow`, `_kin`, `_zone`, `_zone_levels`, `_burst`, `_order`, `_levels`, `_plan`. Engine `prophet_adopt`, source `prophet_r38_adopt_hud` (the Prophet `*.hud.v2` alert): `prophet_adopt_hud`, `_core`, `_ob`, `_scalp`, `_plan`. Engine `probe_mizan`, source `probe_mizan_hud` (the Mizan Probe, 15m): `probe_mizan_core`, `_mtf`, `_decision`, `_state`. The field lists are in `PARITY_GROUPS`; the alerts, their codes and the install are in `pinescript/candidates` and docs/HUD_CANDIDATE_INSTALL.md. None of the three engines is ingested or a base engine yet, so a local record for them is refused (`unsupported_engine`), their groups have no identity, and an alert that arrives first is stored (`hud_comparator_source`, never the AI chain) and compared with nothing. B11 (`gmi_adopt`, `prophet_adopt`) and B26 (`probe_mizan`) wire them.
     - **Why it is inert.** A group is compared only for an observed record of its engine, and only ingested engines (`INGEST_ENGINES`) are observed. `probe_mizan` is neither ingested nor a base engine, and no producer emits `net_witness` or `prior_edge` yet (B26 will). `approvedComparable` never reads an adoption group (below), so it makes the same three reads as before.
     - **The adoption records (B11a, 2026-11-26; owner 2026-11-19 "adopsi semuanya", 2026-11-22 (a)-(c); docs/RFC_INDICATOR_ADOPTION.md "B11's wiring").** `prophet_adopt` (B11a) and `gmi_adopt` (B11b/c) are base engines and ingested (`ADOPT_ENGINES` in `src/evidence-fusion.mjs`), for group parity only:
       - **Producer.** The pull adapter's last step, `publishAdoptRecords` (`market-data-ingestion/src/local-publisher.mjs`), for the latest closed 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D bar while it is fresh (30 min, 2 h, 8 h, 48 h). Marker `market-data:v2:adopt-published:<engine>:<candle_key>`, read first; written only on a post below 300. Switch `PROPHET_ADOPT_PUBLISH` ("true" publishes).
       - **CPU.** It runs no Prophet core of its own: `local-engines/producer/src/adopt/prophet-adopt.mjs` reads the core run that the publishers' setup state made for the same bundle this tick (`chartProphetCore` in `prophet-setup.mjs`, memoised per bundle object and chart; the producers' input, unchanged). A chart without that run this tick is not built (`waiting_for_core`). A reused build costs about 1-3 ms in node; a cold run with the native higher timeframes would cost about 2 s.
       - **Values.** The v2 alert's 62 fields (`PROPHET_ADOPT_FIELDS`), from the core's outputs plus a duplicate of the core's OB zone lifecycle (`obZoneSeries`, held to the core's last-bar zones by AD-1) for the TARGET KEY latch; the manual position inputs at their defaults. Record `cryptophys.local-calculation.v1`, `values.comparable.prophet_r38_adopt_hud`, identity `ENGINE_PROVENANCE.prophet_adopt`.
       - **Ingest.** Group identities only (registered at their first comparison); the engine itself never gets a registry row. `ADOPT_INGEST` "off" refuses the records (a 200 `adopt_ingest_off`, so the publisher marks the bar). An adoption reconcile never starts the analysis path.
       - **No reader.** No agent, room, council, Key Level Agent, zone typing, Position Manager, Omega or public output reads an adoption record. `approvedComparable` skips the adoption groups; `barsVerified` ignores their comparison rows. A group is trusted only after its 24 matching candles against the v2 alert (installed by the owner by 2026-12-08); path-dependent groups compare raw values and may stay unverified (orchestrator Q2).
       - **Retention.** The parity sweep deletes adoption comparison rows older than 30 days, at most 2,000 a run (`pruneAdoptComparisons`, the count as `adopt_pruned` in the sweep result). Other engines' rows are untouched.
       - **Hashes.** No Omega or Position Manager module changed: `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 20e78488… and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged. No hashed local-engine file is edited; `provenance.generated.mjs` gains `prophet_adopt` (existing identities unchanged).
     - **The witness identity resets.** Any later PR that touches the Probe (`pinescript/candidates/Cryptophys-Mizan-Probe.*`), `local-engines/mizan-probe/src/*`, `local-engines/gmi/src/mizan.mjs` or `local-engines/producer/src/macro-aether.mjs` changes the `probe_mizan` hashes. The `probe_mizan_witness` identity then starts its 24-candle parity again.
     - **HUD snapshots.** `validateHudSnapshot` drops an unknown HUD id instead of rejecting the snapshot. The rest of the snapshot is stored; the dropped ids are listed in `dropped_huds` on the stored snapshot, in the 202 answer and in the HUD readings, and logged once (`hud_snapshot_dropped_huds`). A known HUD without a `fields` object is still rejected (`huds_invalid`).
   - The per-event reconcile and the cron (`10-59/15 * * * *`) are idempotent, so a candle is counted at most once.
   - **Closed-market idle (2026-11-14, resource audit; `src/market-idle.mjs`):** from two hours after the Friday close (17:00 New York) to the Sunday reopen (18:00 New York), the parity sweep runs only the macro rooms and, on the first sweep of each hour, a heartbeat (the economic calendar refresh and the health alerts); the war rooms and their fallback, the H1 finalize, the reconcile and backfill, outcome scoring, the hourly tuning and shadow block, pattern calibration, strategy captures, compliance, the Aether and platform pushes and the Omega policy wait for the reopen, and the ledger step (`2-59/5`) does nothing. The first two hours after the close run in full, so the last bars finish; the first sweep after the reopen is the first of its hour, so everything runs there. The hourly news cron is unchanged (weekend headlines are still analysed). `CLOSED_MARKET_IDLE` "off" runs every cron in full.
3. **Fundamental news**: TradingView MCP news. The AI Worker analyses it at cron `:06`.

Coverage per Pine parameter is in `docs/LOCAL_COMPUTATION_OWNERSHIP.md`.

The parity sweep cron also backfills `runAnalysis` for any closed H1 candle within the last 2 hours that has no `analysis_runs` row. It does so when the candle's local records exist (parity-approved or not, `decisionLocalRecords`; owner decision 2026-11-09), or as a fail-closed WAIT 30 minutes after the close. Analysis stays idempotent via `claimAnalysisRun`. A failed run is retried up to 4 attempts; a degraded run (required evidence missing, typically the hourly news landing a minute after the local publish) is retried within 45 minutes of the close, and the claim succeeds only when the evidence fingerprint changed.

### Economic releases, surprise and learned horizon (owner 2026-11-10, `src/econ-releases.mjs`, `src/calendar-agent.mjs`)
- **Source:** ForexFactory's weekly export gives the schedule, forecast and previous (`src/econ-calendar.mjs`); TradingView's economic calendar gives the actual, through the news-ingestion Worker (`NewsIngestionService.economicCalendar`, `TvGateway.economicCalendar`; the one TradingView OAuth grant and quota cooldown). Gold's 1H history for the reactions: the stored KV history (`econ:gold-1h-history:v1`, pulled from TradingView until 2026-11-26), grown from the pull adapter's 1H series and kept without expiry; no TradingView pull since 2026-11-27 (TradingView cleanup, step 1).
- **Stored:** D1 `econ_releases` (high-importance USD): actual, forecast, previous, surprise, surprise in standard deviations, the rule's effect on gold, and the reaction 1/2/4/8/24 hours after in 1H ATR. Live: the day of a High-impact USD event 2 minutes to 3 hours after it while its actual is missing; back-fill about 400 days, one 14-day window an hour.
- **Learned horizon per event kind:** among the horizons with a hit rate of at least 55 % and a positive mean signed move, the peak: the one with the largest mean move (owner 2026-11-12: "ya perbaiki horizon reaksi pakai peak 2 jam"; it was the longest, which let a weak day dilute a strong first hours' reaction: inflation 24 h at 56 % instead of 2 h at 91 %). Its hit rate sets the reaction's strength (`surpriseStrength`). 5 releases at least; the fixed 2 hours before to 4 hours after until then. Production 2026-11-12: inflation 2 h (91 %, strength 1), jobs 1 h (67 %, 0.68), surveys 24 h, growth 8 h, other 8 h, housing 1 h; the pre-release drift keeps the longest qualifying hour.
- **Pre-release drift** (owner 2026-11-12: "ada tendensi market bergerak sesuai proyeksi horizon menjelang release economic calendar", "ya kerjakan 1-3 dan 4a"): for every release whose hour has begun, history included, gold's move from 1, 2, 4 and 8 hours before to the open of the release hour, in 1H ATR, signed by the consensus (forecast above previous: the way a beat moves gold; `consensusOf`, `preDriftOf`, D1 `econ_releases.pre_json`). Per event kind (`preDriftStats`, `calendarIntel().pre_stats`): the hit rate at each hour and the learned pre-release horizon (the longest with 55 % and a positive mean; 5 releases with a consensus at least). It sets the consensus lean's strength (`consensusStrength`: whole while unmeasured; 0.2 at 55 % to 1 at 75 % at the learned horizon; 0 when measured without one) and the imminent window (the learned pre-release horizon, else 2 hours). The Calendar Agent's projection names the evidence; the Macro War Room and the council read `pre_release` and `horizon_hours_before`.
- **Contrarian pre-release drift** (owner 2026-11-12: "ya buatkan opsi kontrarian untuk survei"; `CALENDAR_CONTRARIAN`, `on` in production, `off` by default in code): a kind without a learned pre-release horizon whose gold drifted against the consensus at least 55 % of the time at an hour, with a negative mean and a one-sided binomial p below 0.05 (`CONTRARIAN_P_MAX`), gets a contrarian horizon (the longest such hour; `preDriftStats` `contrarian_hours`, `against_rate`, `against_p`). With the option on, its consensus lean turns the other way at the same scale (`consensusStrength`: −0.2 at 55 % to −1 at 75 % against), its imminent window is the contrarian horizon, and the projection says so (`pre_release.contrarian`). Production 2026-11-12: surveys (ISM, PMI, sentiment) drifted against the consensus 72 % of the time over the 4 hours before (21 of 29, p 0.012), so they lean the other way at 0.9 from 4 hours before; no other kind qualified. Off, such a kind is held at 0.
- **CALENDAR witness** (owner 2026-11-12: "sekaligus horizon news release mempengaruhi bobot", 0.08; `calendarWitness`): the calendar weighs only inside a release's horizon. After a release, inside its learned (else 4-hour) horizon: the surprise's pull (the rule's direction × min(1, |z| / 1.5)) times the strength of the kind's measured reaction; else before a release, inside its pre-release horizon: the consensus lean at its strength. Outside every horizon it has no lean and leaves the quorum (shown as context on the card). The other witnesses gave it 0.08 in proportion, the session's 0.02 kept, so their shares outside a horizon are the B-11 ones (`EXTENDED_WITNESS_WEIGHTS`, `WITNESS_PRIORS`); the auto-tuning tunes it like the others, and a stored set from before is migrated (`withCalendarWeight`).
- **Readers:** the Calendar Agent's projection and bias (after a release with its value the bias follows the surprise by rule), the Macro War Room (release values, the USDT market cap, a follow-up session 2 hours after the release), the Decision Engine (`context.market.calendar`, a caution when a surprise points against the trade inside its horizon) and Nexus (Calendar page, Decision Engine card). Context only: no price level, no order.

### Decision Engine: the single decision (owner decisions 2026-11-09, `src/decision-engine.mjs`)
- **Why:** two decisions were shown (the H1 decision of the 1H room and the deterministic Global decision of the strategy engine), and the Global decision had no model, so it was rigid. Owner: "seharusnya tidak ada dualisme decision regime … hanya ada 1 decision engine sebagai ssot", with every agent taking part, the timeframe rooms and the background and right-panel agents. Owner choices: **AI within guardrails**, **switch now**, **every 15m close**.
- **Tier 1, witnesses** (each from its own data, unchanged; since 2026-11-20 also `POSITIONING`, see "Positioning & Liquidity analyst"), weights `WITNESS_WEIGHTS`: D1 room 0.1667, 4H room 0.1667, 1H room 0.1667 (its quorum is the H1 decision), the strategy agents' consensus 0.1667, 15m War Room 0.125, News Daily 0.0833, Macro War Room 0.0416, strategy engine 0.0833 (multi-timeframe confluence, the Key Level Agent's zones, the 5m trigger, vetoes and cautions). A missing or stale witness drops out and the rest renormalise. A room is stale at twice its period of trading time and News Daily at three hours; since 2026-11-18 (owner, confirmed: "Perbaiki keduanya") the daily break (17:00 to 18:00 New York, Monday to Thursday) is not counted, like the weekend (`goldPausedMsBetween`, `src/market-hours.mjs`), and News Daily ages from its last hourly run that found its reading current (D1 `news_daily_check`, `checked_at`), not from its last new headline.
- **The strategy agents' consensus is a witness** (owner 2026-11-13: "libatkan agen strategi dalam decision council. Atur ulang pembobotannya. agen strategi ini sejajar dengan TF karena merupakan konsensus dari beberapa agen"; `STRATEGY_AGENTS`, `agentsConsensus` in `src/strategy-agents.mjs`). Weighted like the D1, 4H and 1H rooms. A live agent speaks for a side when it sees its own setup there and its plan passes the reward guardrail (the plans the council may choose from); the default agent plans both sides at every bar, so it speaks only where price already holds at its zone; a shadow or suspended agent is not heard. A voice counts 1, moved between 0.5 and 1.5 by the agent's own record (1 + its mean R per setup) once the plan learner holds 15 resolved setups of it. Lean = (buy voices − sell voices) / (all voices + 1): the one silent voice keeps a lone agent at 0.5. While no agent speaks the witness does not count (it drops out like a missing room). The agents' plans read the chart and the key levels, never the quorum, so `assess` makes them before the witness cards. The card keeps `voices` (who speaks for each side); its summary names agents and patterns, never a price. This changes the quorum, so it changes which side may trade. Context, not weighted: the event gate (economic calendar), the 1H market regime, the cross-asset correlations, the 1H decision and the previous decision.
- **Extended witnesses (B-11, `GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED`, on since 2026-11-10), each independent of the strategy engine and the rooms (`independentWitnesses`):** key levels 0.0395 (price against the Key Level Agent's zones), regime 0.0395 (20-bar 1H direction x efficiency, scaled by Hurst; 0 in chaos), cross-timeframe trend 0.0395 (price against its 20-bar mean in 2 ATR on D1/4H/1H), macro drivers 0.0237 (DXY 24-hour change and US 10-year daily change; each counts only while its last 4-hour bar closed within 8 hours, `MACRO_DRIVERS_MAX_AGE_MS`, else it drops out, and with both stale the witness is stale; owner 2026-11-12), session 0.02 (`EXTENDED_WITNESS_WEIGHTS`; reconciled with the code 2026-11-11). While on, every witness takes its B-11 weight (D1/4H/1H and the strategy agents' consensus 0.1263 each, 15m 0.0947, News 0.0632, Macro 0.0315, strategy engine 0.0632, key levels, regime and cross-timeframe trend 0.0395 each, macro drivers 0.0237, calendar 0.08 inside a release's horizon, session 0.02; sum 1; the B-11 shares 0.16/0.12/0.08/0.04/… scaled to make room for the calendar on 2026-11-12 and for the strategy agents on 2026-11-13, the session's 0.02 and the calendar's 0.08 kept). A set the auto-tune stored before a witness was added gets that witness at its prior, the tuned ones scaled in proportion (`withCurrentWitnesses`).
- **Two macro witnesses, kept apart** (reviewed 2026-11-12, owner: "pertimbangkan penggabungan dengan macro war rooms jika ada duplikasi"). MACRO (the Macro War Room, 0.0315 since the strategy agents' witness of 2026-11-13, 0.0367 with the CALENDAR witness, 0.04 before) and MACRO DRIVERS (0.0237; 0.0276 and 0.03 before) read the same dollar and yield series, but they are not the same witness: MACRO is the agents' reasoned bias for the next 4 to 24 hours over the dollar, yields, silver, oil, the calendar and the news, decays 10 % an hour from its conclusion and is stale after 8 hours; MACRO DRIVERS is the mechanical pull of the last 24 hours' dollar and yield moves, and stands when the room is stale or failed. Together they hold 0.064 of the weight (0.07 before the CALENDAR witness). Merging them would end the auto-tuning's separate evidence for each (`witness-tuning`) and the independence the extended witnesses were built for (owner 2026-11-10). The overlap is left to the auto-tuning to weigh. The market card's dollar change (`macroDriversOf`: the lean, the gold-dollar link and the dollar caution) follows the same 8-hour rule (`dollar_stale`).
- **Witness status on the Decision Engine card** (Nexus, owner 2026-11-12: "bedakan status antara stale dan tidak ada keputusan"): `stale` (old data, out of the quorum, dimmed), `no direction` (fresh, |lean| < 0.15, e.g. the regime in chaos; it still counts), `context` (the session clock: no direction, no weight), `no data` (nothing to read, dimmed). The check mark shows a witness that leans with the decision's side, the cross one that leans against it; every other status shows a dash, never a cross.
- **LIQUIDITY, lens B's council-only card** (Track B, B15, 2026-11-23; `liquidityCard`, `withLiquidity`, `liquidityQuorum` in `src/decision-engine.mjs`; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §5.2). The council reads one more card beside the witnesses, `LIQUIDITY` with `counts: false`, and one prompt sentence (`LIQUIDITY_PROMPT`). It is not a witness: `quorumOf` counts every card it gets, so the card never reaches it, nor `decision.witnesses`, the guardrails, the power, `pickStrategy` or the witness weights, and it has no key in `src/witness-weighting.mjs` (`WITNESS_KEYS`, `WITNESS_PRIORS`) or `src/witness-tuning.mjs` (`WITNESS_KEYS`); only the council's own `weighed` list names it. Weight `LIQUIDITY_WITNESS` {share 0.1, cap 0.06, lean 0.6}: w = min(0.06, 0.1 × lens B's council weight in force, `liquidity.council_weight_<mode>` from `lensKnobs`), so the anchors give 0.04/0.03/0.02/0.03 (scalp/intraday/swing/range). No card while lens B is off, floored (`active` false) or its state is stale or unavailable (`liquidityEvidence`, `LIQUIDITY_STATE_FRESH_MS`). Lean ±0.6 only from a reclaimed sweep at most 15 minutes old at the decision (the facts' age plus the state's, `sweepFor`'s rule: a sell-side sweep bullish, a buy-side one bearish) when another counting witness leans the same way (|lean| ≥ 0.15); otherwise 0, with `lean_reason` (`sweep_with_agreeing_witness`, `no_agreeing_witness`, `sweep_stale`, `not_reclaimed`, `no_sweep`). No price: weight, lean, the sweep's side, minutes and words. The record keeps `decision.liquidity` {`card`, `quorum_with_liquidity`}: the quorum with the card added at w and whether each side's quorum check would then hold (`long_met`, `short_met`, `met`), informational, read by no gate. `GET /api/liquidity` shows both. Switch `LIQUIDITY_WITNESS` (`on`; `off`: no card, no `decision.liquidity`, the council's messages byte for byte as before). Decision-engine code is outside `ENGINE_CODE`, so `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` hold.
- **Witness weights auto-tuned** (owner 2026-11-11: "harusnya bobot itu dinamis sesuai dengan auto tune yang ada di aether"; `src/witness-tuning.mjs`). B-11 is the starting set; the weights in force are the set the auto-tuning activated (D1 `witness_weighting_state`), loaded at each decision (`activeWitnessTuning`) and recorded with `witness_weights_source` (`auto_tuned`, `default` for B-11, `base` without `GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED`) and `witness_weights_id`. The same protocol as the rooms' weighting: hourly the sweep replays the stored decisions of the last 21 days (the 15m series holds about four weeks) under the active set and, for each witness except the session (context only), its weight times 1.5 and divided by 1.5 with the other counting witnesses rescaled (every weight 0.02 to 0.28, sum 1). The replay recomputes each side's quorum check with the decision's trading mode; every other guardrail keeps its recorded result; the stance is the side the guardrails allow (the larger share when both do), else WAIT, scored on the closed 15m bars like every stance. The council's own choice is not replayed (`replay_fidelity`: how often the replayed quorum check matches the recorded one). Paired sign test, Holm, 200 discovery decisions, the newest 100 as holdout; activation automatic at most once per 7 days, never a set rolled back before; reverse shadow after 100 decisions on the active set. Each change goes to the owner's Telegram; every report to Aether (`ingestWeightingReport`, `cryptophys.witness-weighting-report.v1`, timeframe `engine`); the doctrine facts carry the weights in force, so the Knowledge Council amends the doctrine through Cerebrum. `WITNESS_TUNING_MODE = "default"` freezes B-11. Read: `GET /api/weighting/engine`. Every auto-tuning step of the sweep (the room weighting of each timeframe and the witness weights) leaves one row in D1 `auto_tune_runs` (`src/tune-runs.mjs`, kept 30 days; owner 2026-11-11: "ya buat catatan hasil auto-tune di D1"): when, how long, the deployed version (`CF_VERSION_METADATA`), what the replay saw (decisions or rooms, scored, accuracy, verdicts, the most-paired candidate, the replay fidelity), what was applied and what Aether answered, and the error if any; read with `GET /api/tuning/runs?step=weighting:15|weighting:60|weighting:240|weighting:1D|witness`. Each decision record also carries `worker_version`. The hand-made regime multipliers of `deriveWitnessWeights` (recorded as `aether_adaptive` from 2026-11-11 06:30 UTC; the regime fields they read were never present, so every weight stayed B-11) no longer weigh a decision.
- **Room guardrail auto-tuned per trading mode** (owner 2026-11-19, relayed and confirmed: "guardrail room biarkan protocol learning dan knowledge yang melakukan adjustment secara dinamis", then "sesuai rekomendasimu"; `src/room-tuning.mjs`). Measured before: on 19 Nov 00:00-11:45 UTC 38 of 48 decisions failed on the room alone, all in scalp mode, whose target is the nearest zone, while the room still asked for one whole 1H range. The room is now a value per mode (scalp, intraday, swing, range) in 1H average ranges, under the witness weights' protocol: hourly the sweep replays the stored decisions of the last 21 days of each mode with the room check recomputed from the stored corridor (`squeeze.corridor_atr`; a decision whose room could not be measured stays failed), every other check as recorded and the quorum replayed with the active witness weights; the arms are the active value plus and minus 0.25, never outside the owner's hard bounds **0.5 to 1.5**, so the guard is never switched off; paired one-sided sign test, Holm across every arm of every mode, the discovery minimum (200 paired decisions of the mode) and the newest 100 as a holdout; automatic activation per mode at most once per 7 days, never a value rolled back before; reverse shadow after 100 decisions of the mode on the active value. The value in force is loaded at each decision (`activeRoom`, D1 `room_tuning_state`, cached a minute; any failure: 1) as `env.ROOM_CORRIDOR_ATR` and recorded as `room_atr` on the decision. Each change goes to the owner's Telegram, every report to Aether (`cryptophys.room-tuning-report.v1`, timeframe `room`), the replay is `GET /api/weighting/room`, and the doctrine facts carry `decision.room_min_atr_scalp`, `_intraday`, `_swing` and `_range` (one per mode, the canon anchors these, so a mode switch alone is no drift), `decision.room_min_atr` (the mode in force), `decision.room_min_atr_by_mode`, the thresholds per mode the same way (`decision.quorum_min_<mode>`, `decision.lean_<mode>`, `decision.min_rrr_<mode>`, the council's live knobs included; owner 2026-11-19: "mau"), `decision.room_bounds_atr` and `decision.room_tuning`: an operational fact the Knowledge Council amends through Cerebrum (owner: no new `promotion_by_owner` entry). `ROOM_TUNING_MODE` "default" holds every mode at 1 and stops every change; `SQUEEZE_GUARD` "off" still leaves the check out. No `tick_value_mismatch`, `volume_below_min` or profile cap is touched.
- **Room breakout waiver (RM1b, owner 2026-11-25, relayed by the orchestrator: "Turn variant (d) on now"; `src/room-waive.mjs`; study RM1a, its variant d6, `scripts/studies/rm1a/`).** RM1a: 238 room-only WAITs from 2026-11-17 to 11-25; in 92 the 15m close was beyond the facing edge of a corridor recorded in the last hour, 90 of them with the trend.
  - **Rule.** Switch `ROOM_BREAKOUT_WAIVE` `on` (production, `wrangler.jsonc`) or `off` (the code default, unset or any other value: the room check byte for byte). Under `on` a side's failing room check passes (`guardrailsFor`, `roomWaiverFor`) only when all hold: room is the side's only failing check (every other guardrail as it is; the room check is unchanged in every other case); the decision's 15m close (`strategy.price`, the close its own corridor is measured around: the last closed 5m close at the 15m decision, which is the 15m close and RM1a's 1m close at `bar_close`) is beyond the facing edge, in the side's direction, of the corridor recorded by a decision whose `bar_close` is exactly 1, 2, 3 or 4 × 15 minutes before (`squeeze.below.high` for a SELL, `squeeze.above.low` for a BUY, as that decision recorded it; a missing or unreadable record does not count); and the side is **with** the trend: the TREND_TF witness's lean × the side's direction ≥ `LEAN` (0.15), whatever the mode's lean. Counter-trend, neutral or a missing TREND_TF is never waived (in RM1a's window "not counter-trend" and "with" select the same set; the stricter "with" is the rule). The nearest broken corridor is the one named.
  - **The read.** `runDecisionEngine` reads the corridors only while the waiver is in force: one D1 query, `bar_close IN` the 4 closes of the last hour (the bar_close index), at most 8 rows, each only `json_extract(decision_json, '$.squeeze')` (a record that is not valid JSON gives none), into `loaded.room_corridors`; `assess` hands it to `guardrailsFor`. Any other caller of `assess` (no corridors) never waives.
  - **Tag.** The room check's detail ends `waived (RM1b): the 15m close is below|above the corridor recorded k bars (… minutes) before, … times the 1H average range wide, with the trend (TREND_TF …)`. A BUY or SELL that trades on a side whose room was waived keeps `room_waived: { corridor: { k, width_atr, words }, broke_at, trend }` on the decision (`decide()`, `buildDecisionRecord`; ATR and words, never a price; `broke_at` the earlier decision's `bar_close`, `trend` the TREND_TF lean). A held stance carries none. Omega's entry intent carries it as `veto_softened` is carried (`bridgeSync`, `roomWaivedTag`, after SV1b's tag), and so does the Signal Ledger row (`signal_ledger.room_waived`, JSON, added by `ensureLedgerSchema`; NULL on every other row). `planIntents` and the EA signal are unchanged.
  - **Value in force** (`resolveRoomBreakoutWaive`, on the decision's env copy beside the veto rule): the owner's FS1 override, else `off` while the automatic revert holds (KV `room-waive:auto:v1`), else the env value; an unreadable KV gives `off`. The value is read from the FS1-resolved env only; whether it is an override comes from the same copy (`FEATURE_SWITCH_OVERRIDDEN`, an enumerable symbol `withFeatureSwitches` sets with the keys it overrode; never in JSON). Owner override > auto-revert > env (test RW-8).
  - **Automatic revert** (`runRoomWaiveRevert`, once a UTC day on the 5-minute ledger step beside SV1b's; no cron of its own): the closed waived trades in Omega R, counted as SV1b counts (the tagged entry intents joined to `signal_ledger.r_omega` where `omega_status` is closed, one per decision); once at least 15 exist, the mean of the latest 15; below 0R the waiver is `off` for 7 days, with one Telegram line, the Omega event `room_waive_reverted` (actor `agent:room`) and the FS1 row's `auto_revert: { since, until }`. After the hold the env value is back (`room_waive_released`) and only the trades opened after the hold count. Under an owner's override nothing reverts. While held, decisions are exactly those of `off`.
  - **Read:** `GET /api/weighting/room` `breakout_waive` (the room guardrail's own route): the value in force and its source (`env`, `auto_revert`, `owner`, `unreadable`), the env value, the hold, the waived ledger rows, the closed waived trades counted since the last hold and their mean R, and the rule's numbers.
  - **Not engine-hashed, not an era switch, no mode-evidence restart:** `guardrailsFor`, `decide()` and `bridgeSync` are outside `ENGINE_CODE`, so `ENGINE_CODE_HASH`, `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (V13, V14) and the parity era are unchanged; v4 replays each recorded signal, so it is not in `ERA_SWITCHES`; `ruleVersionOf` does not read it (plan geometry is unchanged), so `restarts_mode_evidence` is false. The room-tuning replay recomputes the room check from the stored corridor and does not model the waiver: a waived decision counts as a room mismatch in its fidelity.
- **The confirmation candle is the entry trigger, not a guardrail** (owner 2026-11-11: "apa sebaiknya masuk sebagai penentu setup entry saja ?", choice "jadi pemicu entry"). Measured before the change: in 87 decisions from 2026-11-10 14:45 UTC the 5m context arrived verified (84) but its Prophet R38 confirmation never printed at a 15m close, and the 15m Prophet confirmation never either; the confirmation guardrail passed only through the rooms (long 5, short 48) and held back 82 longs. Now each side records `entry_trigger` (a verified 5m or a 15m Prophet confirmation on that side, with the rooms that agree kept for the record); the decision does not wait for it. At execution (`GET /api/ea/signal`, read by the EA and Omega; `entryTriggerFor`) a **market entry** is sent only once the trigger fired: the one the decision recorded, or a verified 5m confirmation printed since (read live from the 5m context, within 16 minutes; the signal's age then counts from that candle). Until then the signal has no side and says what it awaits (`awaiting_signal_id`, `awaiting_side`, `awaiting_reason`, `awaiting_expires_ms`). A **limit at the key level** needs no candle. A record from before the change counts as fired (it passed the old gate). Doctrine facts: `decision.confirmation_required` false, `decision.execution_trigger` `prophet_candle_for_market_orders`. The witness-weight replay no longer applies a recorded confirmation check.
- **The broker's 5m times market entries** (owner 2026-11-11 "ya rancang A+C, timing dari broker langsung", 2026-11-12: a 3-bar window, everything on; `docs/RFC_OMEGA_5M_EXECUTION.md`). At each broker 5m close the pull adapter runs the 5m Prophet producer on the broker's M5 bars (aggregated from the Corpus Oracle's M1, cached in KV `market-data:v2:broker-trigger:m5` and extended; the other series TradingView's) and sends the bar's confirmations, the swing high and low of the last 3 broker 5m bars and the 5m ATR(14) to the AI Worker (`runBrokerTrigger` → `LocalComputationIngress.submitBrokerTrigger`, KV `trigger:broker:m5:latest` with the last 6 bars). Once the lane is ready (400 broker M5 bars) and fresh (its last bar closed at most 6 minutes ago), `entryTriggerFor` fires a market entry only on a broker 5m Prophet confirmation on its side on one of the 3 broker 5m bars after the decision's close (`source: "broker_5m"`); TradingView's live 5m no longer stands in. Not ready, stale or off: TradingView's 5m or 15m confirmation, as above. The signal carries the broker fields flat (`m5_source`, `m5_close_ms`, `m5_ready`, `m5_buy`, `m5_sell`, `m5_swing_high`, `m5_swing_low`, `m5_atr`) for Omega; the EA ignores them. Switches: `OMEGA_EXEC_5M` (AI Worker; `on` by default, `off` restores the TradingView trigger and Omega's plan stop and management) and `BROKER_TRIGGER_MODE` (pull adapter; `on` by default). Doctrine fact `decision.execution_trigger` = `prophet_candle_for_market_orders_broker_5m` while on. The broker trigger is not parity-verified (its bars differ from OANDA's by the basis). **C1 (2026-11-27, cost brief, orchestrator OK):** the trigger reads only Prophet R38's buy/sell confirmation, so it no longer builds every 5m engine: `brokerR38` (`market-data-ingestion/src/broker-trigger.mjs`) calls `calculateProphetR38Core` (protected `prophet-r38/src/core.mjs`, not edited) with exactly the 5m producer's arguments and guards (the last 1000 bars, confirmed, 5 minutes, closed-only HTF, the mintick; `insufficient_m5_history`, `m5_bar_not_closed`, `m5_bar_invalid`), each HTF series trimmed to start `HTF_KEEP_BEFORE` (60) bars before the HTF bar the first chart bar reads (`trimHigherTimeframes`; a series not in order without overlap stays whole). The HTF z-score at bar j reads only bars j-49..j and the bar a chart bar reads only moves forward, so every output is byte-identical (tests BT-C1-1..5, with a weekend gap and the 22:00 UTC session grid). Measured locally: 3.6 s → 0.46 s per trigger, about -0.7M ms of pull-adapter CPU per trading day once `"on"` (in shadow both paths run, about +13% for that day). Switch `BROKER_TRIGGER_R38_ONLY` (pull adapter): shipped as `"shadow"` (orchestrator: the trigger feeds live entries, so prove it on live bars first): the full producer stays live and the R38-only result is computed beside it, a mismatch logged (`broker_trigger_r38_mismatch`) and every comparison tallied per UTC day in KV `market-data:v2:broker-trigger:r38-shadow` (compared, mismatched, failed, last mismatch; 14 days). After 24 trading hours with 0 mismatches a one-line PR sets `"on"` (R38 only live); `"off"` is the full producer alone. The one difference: a failure inside R37, GMI or Mizan no longer blanks the R38 trigger.
- **Tier 2, the AI Decision Council:** one call of the moderator model (`MODERATOR_MODEL`, fallback `MODERATOR_FALLBACK_MODEL`; 90 s deadline; one plain-language correction). It is the only reader across the timeframes. It chooses the stance (BUY, SELL, WAIT), the bias, the confidence, a market entry or a limit at the planned pullback, the target (TP1 or TP2), the summary, reasons and risks.
- **Guardrails** (deterministic; the council cannot break them). A BUY or SELL needs for its side: **market open** (Sunday 22:00 to Friday 22:00 UTC), **fresh** (the 15m and 1H rooms are current), **quorum** (more than the mode's share of the witnesses' weight leans that way, or a lean of at least the mode's `lean` with more weight than the other side), **no veto** (the strategy engine's vetoes for that side), **levels** (a plan from the Key Level Agent's zones, `planForDirection`: never a model price), **reward** (at least the mode's R to the chosen target), **zone ahead** (Z2, owner 2026-11-23: "Langsung prioritaskan"; `zone_ahead`, see "The zone_ahead check (Z2)" below), **room** (owner 2026-11-13: "jika masuk zone kejepit abstain, sehingga akan no trade", then "no trade"; `squeezeOf` in `src/strategy-engine.mjs`: the nearest Key Level Agent candidate below price and the nearest one above it are at least the trading mode's room apart, in 1H average ranges: 1 (`SQUEEZE_RULE.corridor_atr`) until the learning protocol moves it, always inside 0.5 to 1.5 (`roomAtrOf`; see "Room guardrail auto-tuned"); the same check on both sides, so a squeeze is WAIT. Only candidates count: the qualified zones are too dense to measure room with, price sat at one on both sides in 22 of 22 decisions on 2026-11-13. A candidate price is at, within a third of the agent's tolerance, is the place of the trade, not a wall; without a candidate on one side there is room. `SQUEEZE_GUARD` "off" leaves the check out. Every decision record keeps the corridor in `squeeze`, squeezed or not) and **confidence** (at least 0.40). **Trading modes** (`TRADING_MODE`, else the Nexus selector in KV `trading:mode`, default intraday; since 2026-11-13 the selector changes it only by a person's same-origin `POST /omega/api/trading-mode` through the Omega Worker (`OmegaGateway.setTradingMode`, Telegram on each change; refused while `TRADING_MODE` pins it), and `GET /api/trading-mode` is read only; only the configured `TRADING_MODE` (the Worker's var) is a server pin: since MS1 (2026-11-23) `runDecisionEngine` carries the mode in force, the council's knobs and the room on a copy of env for its decision (`{ ...env, TRADING_MODE: mode, COUNCIL_KNOBS, ROOM_CORRIDOR_ATR }`) and never writes env, so a mode the selector or a person chose never reads as the server's pin later in the isolate (the mode selector's `pinned_by_server`, the owner's actions, the range exit, `readStrategy`, `GET /api/trading-mode`'s `pinned`); `MODE_THRESHOLDS`): scalp quorum 45 %, lean 0.10, and since 2026-11-12 the target is the nearest key level ahead with no reward minimum (owner: "untuk mode scalping jangan TP berdasarkan RRR tapi target di Key Level terdekat", "Tanpa batas RRR"; `target: "nearest"`, `min_rrr` 0: the reward check passes when the plan's first target is a key level, not a projection, and the trade always takes `tp1`; the scalp target is the nearest zone 0.25 × the 1H ATR or more beyond the entry, the Key Level Agent's qualified zones (left out by the cap of two candidates per side) included (`SCALP_TARGET`; fix 2026-11-12: at 13:00 UTC a SELL turned WAIT because the nearest support sat 0.46 ATR away, under the 0.5 ATR of the intraday search, and the next one was only qualified)). **TP1 at the nearest key zone, at least 1R** (TP1-KZ, owner 2026-11-23: "ok setuju, TP1 ke key zone minimal 1R, karena di perjalanan position manager juga mengawal jika perlu close lebih cepat untuk mengamankan profit"; it replaces the 2026-11-12 scalp rule above for TP1, "target di Key Level terdekat", "Tanpa batas RRR"), in every mode: a strategy agent's TP1 taken from the key levels (`targetsAhead` in `src/strategy-agents.mjs`, and `tradeLevels`/`planForDirection` for the zone_pullback agent's plan) is the target edge of the nearest Key Level Agent candidate (a key zone) beyond the mode's target gap, and pays at least `TP1_MIN_R` = 1R; a qualified level that is not a candidate no longer counts as a TP1 while a key zone is ahead. A nearest key zone under 1R is an obstacle: that agent has no plan (never a jump to the next key zone). Without a key zone ahead, today's nearest level beyond the gap, else the 2R projection, still at least 1R; scalp still rejects the projection.
