Agents with their own targets (range_fade, the pattern agents, pattern_inside) take TP1 the same way (TP1-KZ2, owner 2026-11-23, "setuju": the 12:00Z pattern_lines BUY had taken its measured target as TP1 at 50.72R; `ownTargetsUnderKeyZone` in `src/strategy-agents.mjs`): TP1 is the key-zone first target above, or no plan; the agent's measured target (a pattern family's) becomes TP2 when it lies beyond that TP1, else the agent's own TP2 when it is not short of TP1, else the key zones' second target, and a measured target left short of TP1 stays named in TP2's note (`trade.tp2` only; no new key). `rrr`, `rrr_all` and the gates read the new targets. The selection gate (`agentPlanPasses`, `liveAgentPlans`, the fallback's default plan) asks `rrr.tp1 >= 1` in every mode on top of the mode's `min_rrr` (scalp's stays 0, so its floor is 1R on TP1); TP2 is unchanged, and the guardrails keep their own plan (`planForDirection` without the rule) for their levels, reward and room checks. The strategy engine's own plan (`buildStrategy`, the fallback decision's plan) follows the rule too, through `modes.tp1KeyZone`; a first target under 1R keeps today's plan for its location and the decision waits (state `LOW_REWARD`). The H1 chain's own-timeframe plan (`ownTimeframePlan`, advisory room text, never executed) is unchanged. One agent's geometry never blocks the side for the others. The Position Manager is unchanged: it can close earlier while the trade runs. Switch `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` (`on` by default; `off` is the targets and gates before it, byte for byte). **The entry zone** (EZ1, owner 2026-11-23: "decision & signal harusnya memberi nilai zone bukan exact, sehingga omega tidak akan info limit through price"; `ENTRY_ZONE` in `src/strategy-engine.mjs`): every plan carries `entry_low`/`entry_high` beside its `entry` (unchanged) where it has a zone: an at-level or pullback plan the band of the key zone its entry came from, clipped to 0.5 × the 1H ATR around the entry; a structural plan (finishPlan: sweep, breakout retest, patterns, range edges) the entry ± 0.15 × the 1H ATR; either way the stop's side never past half the risk; an entry at price with no key zone behind it has none. The decision's trade and `strategy_plans` keep it, the EA signal carries it as the flat keys `entry_zone_low`/`entry_zone_high` (the EA never reads them, its needle is `"<key>":`), and Omega rests a limit inside it where the plan's own level is already through price (docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md §3, `zone_passed`, `zone_tight` and `zone_rr`). Public outputs get nothing new (the keys are in `PUBLIC_FORBIDDEN_KEYS`). Where the plan's level is out of the placement band but the zone's near edge is inside it, Omega rests the limit at that edge (EZ1b, owner 2026-11-23 "setuju": `limit_zone_edge`, `entry_basis: "zone_edge"`, the plan's stop and target kept, the same zone guards). Switch `ENTRY_ZONE_MODE`: `edge` (the default: EZ1 and EZ1b; the decision carries `entry_zone_mode: "edge"` and the signal `entry_zone_edge`), `on` (EZ1 alone), `off` (no zone: today's plans, signals and limits, byte for byte); it reaches `buildStrategy` through `modes.entryZone`. The env switch is the rollback: a deploy flips it. It is not an Omega setting and not in `ERA_SWITCHES`: `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (V14, whose fixtures carry no zone) and the parity era carry over, only `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` moves, and v4 replays each recorded signal's zone with the same code, so parity follows the live account either way. Intraday 50 %, 0.15, 1.5R; swing 60 %, 0.20, 2.0R (swing also vetoes a composite sentiment of -0.6 or worse against the trade). The chosen target honours the mode's R (`tradeFor` `minRrr`, audit fix 2026-11-11: scalp's 1.2R trade had been turned into WAIT by a fixed 1.5R). A market entry only when price is at the level. A council BUY or SELL that breaks one becomes WAIT and the record keeps `overruled`. A high-impact USD event stays a caution (owner decision 2026-11-08); the EA does not enter in its blackout.
- **Market context** (owner 2026-11-09, `marketContextOf`): the right panel's cards go to the council in `context.market` and stay in the record: the market sentiment (weighted lean, news and macro composite, headlines), the regime of the 15m and 1H charts (radar sensors, chaos, stretch, bar regime label), the macro drivers (24-hour gold, dollar, oil, the USDT market cap, stored under its old key `usdt_dominance` for record continuity, the council prompt saying "the USDT market cap" since 2026-11-27; gold-dollar link; correlations) and the session (day, date, market open or closed, active sessions and liquidity, next close or open). They give cautions (thin liquidity, the weekend close within 2 hours, a 2.5 sigma 1H stretch with the trade, a chaotic 15m regime, a 0.3 % dollar move against the trade), never a block; only a closed market blocks.
- **The zone_ahead check (Z2, 2026-11-24; `src/zone-types.mjs` `zoneAheadCheck`, `zonePlanPasses`; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §5).** No trade into a decisive opposing demand or supply zone against the trend:
  - **The check.** `guardrailsFor` adds `zone_ahead` after `reward`. It reads the side's plan's `opposing_ahead` (Z0's `zoneAhead`, run last on the final plan, from the recorded set only). No plan: pass ("no plan to check"). No decisive opposing zone (supply for a long, demand for a short, medium or strong): pass. Room short of need (`ZONE_NEED` = max(1R, 0.75 ATR(1H))): in 'trend' it fails only when dir x trend < 0.15 (`STRATEGY_PARAMS.lean`; that is Z0's `would_block`); a with-trend plan passes with the note in the check's detail and in `opposing_ahead` only, **never in `cautions`**; in 'all' it fails. Details are words, ATR and R, never a price.
  - **The filter, outside the quorum.** `zonePlanPasses(plan, g)` applies the same rule to the strategy agents' plans in `liveAgentPlans`, on the execution non-arm path (a failing speaker is skipped; all of them: `overruled` with check `zone_ahead`) and in `pickStrategy` (ineligible, the candidate marked `zone_ahead: false`). `agentSpeaks`, `agentsConsensus`, the consensus passes, the STRATEGY_AGENTS witness and the quorum are unchanged.
  - **The record.** When `zone_ahead` is a side's only failing check, the decision writes `zone_blocked` = `{ side, agent, plan (slim), opposing_ahead }` (the guardrails' plan, the default agent's). `rule_version` (`ruleVersionOf`, `plan_setups` and `mode_shadow_plans`) adds `+zone_ahead@1` (`+zone_ahead@1:all` in 'all') while the check is in the guardrails, so the mode selector's evidence restarts at Z2's deploy, as at TP1-KZ's. MS2's shadow modes run the same guardrails and filter; a shadow mode the check holds back records the reason `zone_ahead`, and its trade keeps `opposing_ahead`, so a replay reproduces it. Room tuning (`withRoom`) recomputes the room check only and keeps `zone_ahead` as recorded.
  - **The council, only while ZONE_PRIORITY is on.** Each plan in the evidence adds `zone_types` (words), `opposing_zone_ahead_atr` and `with_trend`; the system prompt adds one sentence on the owner's demand and supply priority (`ZONE_PRIORITY_PROMPT`). Nothing goes to the cautions.
  - **Elsewhere.** The doctrine facts' `decision.guardrails` (`guardrailIds`) lists `zone_ahead` after `reward`, only while the check is in the guardrails. `publicRecap`'s `held_back` leaves `zone_ahead` out, so the public recap is unchanged. The EA signal, `managementFields`, Omega, the Position Manager and the hashed local engines are untouched; ENGINE_CODE_HASH, ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH and ERA_SWITCHES are unchanged.
  - **Switches** (AI Worker env, wrangler.jsonc): `ZONE_PRIORITY` = "on" | "off" (wrangler.jsonc "on" since 2026-11-25, after the live-capture typing replay and `ZONE_NEED` re-check) (unset reads "off": the guardrails, record, council and facts byte for byte as before). `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` = "trend" (default) | "all" (owner-set only, from the Worker's env; one `zone_ahead_mode_all` warning line per decision) | "record" (no check; `would_block` stays on the plan; the council words stay) | "off". `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES` "off" (env or the strategy's modes) leaves the zones untyped and so the check out. Z5's breaker can drop the check to 'record' for a decision (`zone_rules_held`).
  - **Live impact** (production D1, the 14 days to 2026-11-23): 419 directional decisions, 402 SELLs; in 'trend' it would have blocked at most 1 (0.24%), in 'all' up to 278 (66%). Nearly inert in 'trend' in this SELL-heavy regime: the priority shows mainly in the record and the council's words until the regime turns.
- **Prices aimed by zone type (Z2b, 2026-11-25; `src/zone-types.mjs` `zoneAim`; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §6). Shipped inert: wrangler.jsonc `ZONE_AIM_OFF` = "tp,stop,factor" (orchestrator ruling 2026-11-25), so plans, signals, the council's messages and `rule_version` are Z2's byte for byte until the owner turns a rule on.** When on, the traded plans' stop and targets read the zone types. Every rule runs on **execution copies**: the guardrails' plan inside `guardrailsFor`, before its levels, reward and zone_ahead checks, and each agent's plan where `assess` attaches `g.plans`, after `agentsConsensus`, the agents' confidence inputs and the quorum. `planForDirection`, the agents' `basePlan`, `buildStrategy` and `ownTimeframePlan` are unchanged, so the STRATEGY_AGENTS witness and the quorum read the plans as before. `zoneAhead` runs last on every aimed copy, so `opposing_ahead` (room_r, need_r, would_block), which Z2 reads, follows the final entry and stop.
  - **tp_typed** (plans whose targets come from the key levels: zone_pullback, sweep_reversal, breakout_retest; `KEY_TARGET_AGENTS`). The target set is TP1-KZ's own (the recorded key zones). `ZONE_AIM_TP` 'label' (default): TP1's price never changes; a decisive opposing TP1 adds its words to `tp1_note` ("(supply, fresh)"). TP2 is the next decisive opposing key zone at least 0.5 ATR beyond TP1 and at least 1.5R, else today's TP2. 'qualified' (owner-set only, once TP1-KZ is amended to tp1_kz@2): a decisive opposing qualified zone at or beyond `ZONE_NEED`, nearer than TP1, becomes TP1, so one zone is never both a TP1 and a Z2 block. Inert with `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` off.
  - **stop_skip** (the zone_pullback geometry only; sweep, breakout, range and pattern stops are unchanged): the stop sits beyond the first zone at least 0.5 ATR beyond the entry that is not a decisive opposing-type zone (supply under a long, demand over a short), padded 0.2 ATR; over 2.5 ATR, the 1.5 ATR fallback.
  - **The reward gate.** A copy that fails the gate its base passed (the agents: `agentPlanPasses` with TP1-KZ's 1R; the guardrails' plan: the reward check) keeps the base with `zone_rules` `[{ rule, held: "rrr" }]`; a base the gate refuses is never aimed.
  - **pickStrategy's score** = r2(confidence x confluence x zone_factor): 1.2 for a decisive own-type entry zone that is fresh or tested, 0.85 for a decisive opposite-type one, else 1 (`zoneFactorOf`), carried on each typed candidate as `zone_factor`. Eligibility and the WAIT do not read it. The 1.41 ratio is below the at-level confluence gap, so an opposite-type at-level plan still outranks an own-type pullback at equal confidence.
  - **The record.** `zone_rules` = `[{ rule: tp_typed | stop_skip, base_tp1_r, base_stop_atr }]` on the trade and on `strategy_plans` when a rule moved a price: distances from the entry, never a price, from which Z5 and MS2's base arm rebuild the base (`zoneAimBase`). Record only: never in the council's evidence, a card, a room, the EA signal or public output (the relabelled `tp1_note` is the one word change the council sees). `rule_version` (`ruleVersionOf`) adds `+zone_aim@1` (`:qualified`, `:no_<token>` per rule off) only while at least one rule is on; inert, it stays `tp1_kz:on+zone_ahead@1`. MS2's shadow rows keep `zone_rules` as recorded.
  - **Switches.** All of Z2b is under `ZONE_PRIORITY` (unset "off": byte for byte as before) and typed zones. `ZONE_AIM_OFF` = comma tokens `tp`, `stop`, `factor`, the rules left off ("" = all on; wrangler.jsonc ships "tp,stop,factor": all off). Each rule is the owner's to switch on, independently; stop_skip and the factor wait for their evidence; `ZONE_AIM_TP` = "label" (default) | "qualified". No EA, cron, Omega or engine-hash change; no era moves (v4 replays the stored plans).
- **Zone readings, counterfactuals and the decision-side zone breaker (Z5, 2026-11-25; `src/zone-readings.mjs`; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §7).** The measurement and the brake behind Z2/Z2b. Recorded in their own D1 tables; plan_setups and its 14 readers are unchanged.
  - **Readings** (`zoneReadings`, the Signal Ledger's 5-minute step `runLedgerStep`, after the plan learner; at most 200 decisions a run behind KV `zone-readings:cursor:v1`). Each `decision.key_levels` zone the decision bar's 15m range touched, read on the next 8 closed 15m bars: `held` (a close at least 0.5 ATR(1H) from the near edge in the zone's direction before a close 0.1 ATR beyond the far edge), `broke` (that close first) or `neither`, with `mfe_atr`, `mae_atr`, `bars` and `approach` (`from_above`, `from_below`, `inside`, from the bar before). A demand or supply zone reads its type's direction; an untyped zone the side it is met from (an untyped zone price opened inside is not read). One reading per (0.1 ATR bucket, approach) per 2 hours. D1 `zone_readings` (CREATE IF NOT EXISTS): words and ATR only (`zone_type`, `zone_strength`, `zone_freshness`, `zone_rank`, `ds_sources` (null until a source records its TF words), `approach`, `outcome`, `mfe_atr`, `mae_atr`, `bars`, `mode`, `regime`, `version`, `rule_version`).
  - **The decision-time rule version.** While `ZONE_READINGS` is on, each decision writes one row to D1 `zone_decision_rules` (`decision_key`, `bar_close`, `rule_version`, `created_at`; INSERT OR IGNORE): `ruleVersionOf` on the run's FS1-resolved env before any hold, so the decision record stays byte for byte as it was. `zone_readings`, `zone_blocked_setups` and `zone_rule_arms` take their `rule_version` from it by `decision_key` when they write, hours later; a decision without a row (before Z5) gives NULL, which the breaker's evidence leaves out.
  - **Counterfactuals, their own tables only.** `zone_blocked_setups` (plan_setups' columns, `source` 'zone_blocked'): the WAIT decisions with `$.zone_blocked`, their would-be plan as setups (`setupsFrom`), scored with plan learning's exported outcome step (`setupOutcome`, the control arm; a limit that never fills is 0R). `zone_rule_arms` (`decision_key`, `agent`, `rule`, `acted_r`, `base_r` and the setup's fields): for the Z2b plans a rule moved, the acted plan against the base rebuilt from the entry, `base_tp1_r` and `base_stop_atr` (`zoneAimBase`), both on the same step. Last 2 days' decisions, at most 200 a run.
  - **The breaker** (`runZoneBreaker`, hourly, beside `syncAgentBreaker`; KV `zone-priority:breaker:v1`). On the evidence of the rule version in force (`ruleVersionOf`): (a) typing, over the last 300 readings with at least 60 per group, the own-type held rate (demand met from above or inside, supply from below or inside) minus the untyped held rate below 0 holds zone_ahead, tp, stop and factor; (b) block, at least 30 resolved zone_blocked setups with a mean above +0.10R hold zone_ahead (to 'record'); (c) per rule, at least 30 resolved arms whose mean acted R is below the mean base R hold that rule. A new hold is stored as `{ by, since, until, rule_version }`, `until` = since + 7 days (`ZONE_BREAKER.hold_ms`, SV1b's revert period): before `until` it stands whatever the evidence says (no hour-to-hour flicker); after it, it is re-evaluated on its stored rule version and released once that evidence no longer trips. When the owner's rule version is no longer the hold's (the owner changed a zone switch), the hold is released at once ("the rules changed": the owner's change wins), and `applyZoneHolds` stops applying it at the next decision. Fresh holds read the owner's current rule version. One `XAUUSD ZONES` Telegram line per change (sent first, then stored).
  - **Read into the decision, for the run only.** `runDecisionEngine` reads the state into its env copy (`applyZoneHolds`): zone_ahead held sets `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` "record" while the check is in the guardrails; a held tp, stop or factor joins `ZONE_AIM_OFF` while Z2b has it on. The decision records `zone_rules_held` = `[{ rule, by }]` (only the rules a hold changed; absent otherwise). The owner's FS1 values, `omega_settings`, the Omega and Position Manager state, the EA signal and `managementFields` are never written or read by it; it never turns a rule on. A hold never changes a stored `rule_version` (plan_setups, mode_shadow_plans, the mode selector, the zone tables all carry the owner's), so it does not restart the mode-selector evidence.
  - **Alarms** (one line a UTC day each, no action): zone_ahead is the only failing check in more than 40% of the last 96 decisions where the council wanted that side; under `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` 'all', that share every day; Omega's zone-attributed trades (below) at 15 or more closes with a mean below 0R: the owner may set zone_mgmt lower.
  - **Omega attribution** (read-only, SELECT only): `omega_intents` by reason and `level_source` 'zone' and `position_actions` by the PM's zone rules (pm_zone_*), joined to their `ea_deals` in R. Z3a's flag: the hourly tick keeps the typing signature (`KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES`, `ZONE_SOURCES_OFF`) and, when it changed while the Omega setting `zone_mgmt` is not off (read SELECT only, never written), sends one `XAUUSD ZONES` line per toggle (`typingFlagLine`; a failed send is told again; a toggle while off is never told). No action.
  - **Read:** `GET /api/zones` (the AI Worker's read API through Nexus' `/api/*` proxy, behind Access; SELECT only, a table not there yet reads as []): `readings` by type x approach x strength (n, held rate shrunk toward the overall rate with a prior of 20), `zone_blocked` per side (n, mean R, fill rate), `rule_arms`, `breaker`, `stances` (7 days) and `omega_attribution`. `breaker.held[]` lists only the holds of the current rule version or carrying none (`zoneHoldCurrent`, the same filter as FS1's `zone_hold`); each entry is `{ rule, by, since, until, effective }` (ZN1): `since`/`until` are the values FS1's `zone_hold` shows (one reader, `zoneHoldTimes`; null on a hold stored without them), and `effective` is whether the hold changes a decision right now under the env in force with the FS1 overrides applied (`zoneHoldEffective`, exactly when `applyZoneHolds` would apply it; e.g. a `tp` hold while `ZONE_AIM_OFF` already has `tp` off is false). Words, ATR, R and booleans only; no account or identity key. POST 405. B8 folds it under INTEL_VIEWS. The Nexus Zones page (`#/zones`, ZN1) reads it; its contract is `test/fixtures/nexus-zones/zones.json` (the seed's real answer, test/support/nexus-zones-seed.mjs).
  - **Switch.** `ZONE_READINGS` = "on" (default; wrangler.jsonc "on") | "off": nothing written, the breaker idle (no hold reaches a decision), the route 404. `ZONE_PRIORITY` "off" still overrides every Z rule. No EA, cron, Omega or engine-hash change; no era moves.
- **Fallback:** when the model fails or its answer is invalid, the strategy engine's decision stands if its side passes every guardrail, otherwise WAIT (`source: "fallback"`).
- **Missed rooms:** when a 15m room never started (no 5m context reached the queue), the sweep runs it at least 4 minutes after the close from the cached closed 5m series (`runMissedWarRoom`, `m5ContextFromSeries`; no Prophet 5m confirmation from bare bars), then the Decision Engine decides.
- **When:** right after the 15m War Room of the bar concludes (queue `live_room`, statuses complete or already_complete), so about 1 to 2 minutes after the close; the sweep (:10, :25, :40, :55) decides a bar the room did not trigger (`runPendingDecision`, from 4 minutes after the close; not while the market is closed). Once per bar (D1 `engine_decisions`, key `OANDA:XAUUSD:15:<open ms>`; KV `decision-engine:latest`, 2 days).
- **It feeds everything:** the MT5 EA (`GET /api/ea/signal`: `signal_id` is the 15m key, `tp1` the chosen target, `issued_ms` the 15m close, `expires_ms` the next 15m close, `source: "decision_engine"`), Telegram (a new stance only; `DECISION_TELEGRAM_MODE = "all"` sends every BUY or SELL bar; after `DECISION_TELEGRAM_HEARTBEAT_MINUTES` (60) without a message, one heartbeat says the stance still holds and since which bar, owner 2026-11-11; since 2026-11-17 the heartbeat goes to the Cryptophys-Cerebrum chat as a kernel report from `xauusd-reasoner`, topic `ea_heartbeat`, not to the Nexus chat, with no fallback: a failure is logged as `decision_heartbeat_relay_failed` and the hour restarts), the Signal Ledger (source `decision`, one open per side; its entry and exit alerts) and Nexus (the "Decision Engine" card, from `GET /api/conclusion/global` → `engine`; also `GET /api/engine/decision` and `/api/engine/decisions`).
- **Swing structure (display only, owner 2026-11-12):** the decision record carries `structure` (`src/market-structure.mjs`): for the 4H, 1H and 15m bars the engine loaded, the last two confirmed swing highs and lows (the Projection Agent's 2-bar fractal turns, `zigzag`) as HH/HL (up), LH/LL (down), HH/LL (expanding) or LH/HL (contracting). Labels only: it is not a witness, not a guardrail and not in the council's evidence; the Decision Engine card shows it beside the decision.
- **While it is on:** no H1 decision message on Telegram, no new `h1_decision` or `strategy` ledger rows and no strategy evaluation at each 5m close (the H1 decision and the strategy are witnesses). The rooms, the H1 decision record and the strategy engine are unchanged.
- **Kill switch:** `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"` restores the H1 decision for the EA, Telegram and the ledger (an owner PR changing the variable).
- No position size; prices only from the key-level plan; the server places no order.

### H1 decision by Consensus Discussion (owner decision 2026-11-07, `src/h1-consensus.mjs`)
- **Why:** the old chain decided at the close, before any discussion: Analyst → Counselor → Reasoner on the evidence alone. The Analyst and the Counselor ran only in the background (no screen showed their output), and in production the Counselor spent its whole token budget on reasoning in 32 of the last 32 runs. Deciding first made the decision ambiguous and almost always WAIT.
- **Now every input comes from the 1H communication bus, and the Reasoner decides last:**
  1. At the close, `runAnalysis` fuses the evidence as before. Degraded evidence (in quorum mode: only a missing or stale closed candle) is still a fail-closed WAIT at once. Complete evidence is parked in KV (`h1:pending:<candle_key>`, 6 hours) and no model runs. The run stays `analyzing`, at the stage `awaiting_consensus` in `analysis_runs.error_code` (the status column has a CHECK constraint).
  2. The 1H War Room of the bar meets: communication bus, Consensus Discussion, the moderator's conclusion with its decision and bias.
  3. The Reasoner (`AI_MODEL_REASONER`) writes the H1 decision when the room concludes (the sweep's `onRoom` hook), or 45 minutes after the close at the latest with whoever spoke (owner choice; `H1_CONSENSUS_FALLBACK_MS`, from the sweep). It reads the room (`CONSENSUS`: bus, Consensus Discussion, conclusion) beside the parked evidence.
- **The H1 decision is the room's quorum (owner decision 2026-11-09: "H1 Decision harus independen … murni dari konsensus agent"; "H1 Decision adalah kuorum"):**
  - Independent of the TradingView webhooks, of parity approval and of any single indicator signal. The webhooks will be removed; parity sampling only labels the evidence.
  - **Quorum** (`quorumOf` in `src/h1-consensus.mjs`, `quorumAllows` in `src/reputation.mjs`, `QUORUM_SHARE` 0.5): BUY or SELL only when the moderator concluded BUY or SELL AND more than half of the weight of the agents who spoke holds the matching bias (BULLISH for a BUY, BEARISH for a SELL). Each agent's weight is its 30-day track record in the room (the P7 crowd line, `crowd.weighted_biases`; an agent without a record weighs 1). Aether tunes the weighting later from what it learns. Anything else is WAIT.
  - **Prices** (`planForDirection` in `src/strategy-engine.mjs`): the entry, stop and targets come from the Key Level Agent's zones through the strategy engine's `tradeLevels`. A quorum without a valid entry, stop and target (stop and target on the right sides of the entry) stays WAIT: the only hard rule besides the quorum. No position size is ever given.
  - **Deterministic** (`applyQuorum`): the direction comes from the quorum and the prices from the plan, whatever the model returns. The Reasoner only writes the reasoning, the confirmation and invalidation text; its own decision is read for the confidence when it agrees. The synthesis records `consensus.quorum` (stance, room stance, share, needed, met, reason), `plan_available` and `guard_overridden` (the model's decision differed).
  - **Indicators are arguments, not gates or vetoes.** The Prophet R38 confirmation and setup state (READY), its trade management `t_enc`, and the GMI verdict, gate and conflict are read by the agents in the discussion (KB `decision_policy.long_arguments` / `short_arguments`). The former rules of the Reasoner (the matching Prophet trigger, the `t_enc` 4 and GMI ±3 vetoes, `consensusGuard`) are retired.
  - **Evidence in quorum mode** (`buildEvidenceFusion` with `decisionMode: "quorum"`): only a missing or stale closed candle blocks the decision. A missing news lane or engine record is noted in the evidence and the room still meets. Local records that parity has not approved are used with `trust_status: "not_parity_approved"`.
  - **No parity cap in the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms:** every agent speaks at the confidence its reading supports; the calibration stays a label next to the agent. The 15m Live War Room keeps its rules for now.
  - Every field describes the closed candle being decided (the bar that just closed), never the running one.
- **The 1H room right after the close (owner request 2026-11-07):**
  - When `runAnalysis` parks the evidence, it enqueues an `h1_room` queue task, so the 1H War Room no longer waits for the next sweep (:10, :25, :40, :55) and its 20-minute HUD wait.
  - The task waits for the bar's HUD snapshot until 2 minutes after the close (`H1_ROOM_HUD_WAIT_MS`, redelivered every 15 seconds), then runs the room. The Reasoner decides as soon as the room concludes.
  - A snapshot that arrives later re-convenes the room (`#hud`); the decision already written stays.
  - A bar not yet in the cached series, or a room still waiting for its quorum, is redelivered.
  - While the task holds the room (`h1room:lock:<candle_key>`, 7 minutes), the sweep skips that bar. The sweep remains the fallback.
  - The synthesis records `consensus.decided_after_close_s`. **Target (owner 2026-11-09): within 1 to 2 minutes of the close.** The chain: the market-data cron runs every minute and pulls the 1H bar 60 seconds after its close (`SETTLE_SECONDS`, 90 until 2026-11-09; a later revision is republished for parity); the same run publishes the HUD snapshot first and then the H1 record, which parks the evidence and enqueues the room (queue batch timeout 1 second); the room takes about 10 to 20 seconds and the Reasoner about 15. Before: about 6 to 7 minutes (cron every 5 minutes, settle 90 seconds, the HUD snapshot published after the record, a 60-second retry).
- **Fail-closed:** a room where no agent spoke by the fallback time gives WAIT without a model call. A Reasoner that keeps failing is retried by every sweep, and after 2 hours gives WAIT.
- **Idempotent:** only the caller that moves the stage from `awaiting_consensus` to `finalizing` decides (a stale `finalizing` is reclaimed after 10 minutes). New evidence of the bar may reclaim a stale `analyzing` run; it then parks the fresher evidence.
- **Stored:** the synthesis carries `analysis_chain: "consensus_reasoner"`, `consensus` (status concluded, partial or empty; who spoke; the room's bias; the conclusion; whether the guard overrode the Reasoner) and the raw Reasoner output. Persistence is unchanged: `ai_syntheses`, `trade_decisions`, `latest:`, Telegram and `analysis:`.
- **Read:** `GET /api/threads/timeframe?chart=H1` adds `reasoner_decision` (decided, awaiting the room, or deciding). The Nexus 1H tab ends with it.
- **Retired:** the Analyst and the Counselor of the H1 chain (they still serve the news analysis), and the old H1 multi-agent bus (the 1H War Room is the bus). The legacy `/v1/analyze` bundle path was removed on 2026-11-15 (owner: "setujui semua"; unused since 2026-11-11).

### 5m execution context
- The evidence key `informational_5m_context` may be surfaced in the fusion bundle. Since B2 it is the
  5m parity record of the bar, reshaped (`local-engines/producer/src/m5-context.mjs`): the pull adapter
  sends it once per closed 5m bar, right after publishing that bar's 5m records. `buildM5ProphetContext`
  (a second, separate 5m computation) is retired.
- The context always declares `informational_unverified`. The AI Worker labels it **VERIFIED 5m**
  (`verification.verified`, `approved_by_parity`) only while `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are approved and that
  bar's observed 5m records come from them (`verifiedM5Reading`); otherwise **UNVERIFIED 5m**.
- Either way it is execution timing only: it is **never** part of `fusion.complete`, and never changes the
  decision, entry/SL/TP, risk/reward, position size or confidence.
- **5m parity records (C0 step 3).** The pull adapter also publishes full mizan, prophet and gmi records for every closed 5m bar (`local-engines/producer/src/m5-local-producer.mjs`, behind `M5_PARITY_PUBLISH`). They have timeframe `"5"` and candle key `OANDA:XAUUSD:5:<open ms>`.
  - The records earn their own approval under the timeframe-scoped ids `prophet_m5`, `gmi_m5` and `mizan_m5`, separate from the approved H1 ids. The parity sweep revisits the last six closed 5m candles.
  - GMI on every chart (5m, 15m, 1H, 4H, D1) runs one engine, `local-engines/gmi/src/gmi-core.mjs` (`computeGmiOnChart`; `computeGmi` is the 1H chart). B4 (owner go for the full re-approval, 2026-11-09) merged the three copies it had grown into: the H1 core, `gmi-core-tf.mjs` (5m, 15m) and `gmi-core-htf.mjs` (4H, D1). The merge was proven output-identical to all three on 80 synthetic bundles across the five charts, and `test/gmi-core-htf.test.mjs` pins golden output fingerprints of the former modules. `gmi-core-tf.mjs` and `gmi-core-htf.mjs` remain as one-line re-exports, so the producers that import them, and the Prophet and MizanLib identities that hash those producers, did not change; every GMI identity (`gmi`, `gmi_m5`, `gmi_m15`, `gmi_h4`, `gmi_d1`, `gmi_hud`) re-earns its 24 parity matches.
  - The provenance has its own `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` entries, so 5m code changes never change the H1 hashes.
- **Verified 15m lane** (owner decision 2026-11-05, same design as 5m).
  - The pull adapter publishes mizan, prophet and gmi records for every closed 15m bar. The producer is `local-engines/producer/src/m15-local-producer.mjs`, behind `M15_PARITY_PUBLISH`, with timeframe `"15"` and candle key `OANDA:XAUUSD:15:<open ms>`, the same key as the 15m HUD snapshot.
  - The records earn their own approval as `prophet_m15` and `gmi_m15`, with their own provenance entries. The H1 and 5m hashes are unchanged. The parity sweep revisits the last two closed 15m candles.
  - Calibration needs the existing Prophet R38 and GMI alerts installed on the 15m chart until both identities are approved (24 matches). After that, the alerts may be removed, as with 5m ("pilihan 1").
  - Once approved, the 15m HUD fields that are alert fields read as verified by parity in the War Room. Engine internals stay uncalibrated until a Pine candidate comparator exists (C1).
- **H4 and D1 lane** (owner decision 2026-11-05: Threads per timeframe D1, H4, H1 and 15m).
  - **Records:** the pull adapter publishes mizan, prophet and gmi records for every closed H4 and D1 bar. The producer is `local-engines/producer/src/htf-local-producer.mjs`, behind `HTF_PARITY_PUBLISH`.
  - **Timeframes and keys:** the timeframes are `"240"` and `"1D"`, as Pine v6 `timeframe.period` reports them. The candle keys are `OANDA:XAUUSD:240:<open ms>` and `OANDA:XAUUSD:1D:<open ms>`.
  - **GMI engine:** the one GMI engine (`gmi-core.mjs`, re-exported as `gmi-core-htf.mjs` for the H4/D1 producer) with Pine's `dyn_ltf` intrabars: M1 below 240, M5 on H4, M15 on D1.
  - **Identities:** the records earn their own approvals as `prophet_h4`, `gmi_h4`, `prophet_d1` and `gmi_d1`, each with its own provenance entry. No other hash changed.
  - **Sweep:** OANDA H4 and D1 bars open at 17:00 New York, not on epoch multiples. The parity sweep therefore takes the last two closed H4 and D1 keys from the cached native bars (`closedHtfCandleKeys`), never from the clock.
  - **Calibration:** the owner installs the Prophet R38 and GMI alerts on the 4-hour and daily charts, set to "Any alert() function call" with the same webhook.
    - 24 matches take about 4 trading days on H4 and about 5 weeks on D1.
    - Until then, H4 and D1 readings are unverified.
  - **Independent rooms (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "masing-masing timeframe independent, masing-masing agent harus independent dilarang untuk refer ke agent atau timeframe lain"; "output masing-masing agen dilarang menyebutkan nama agent tetapi metrics apa yang dia gunakan"; "consensus discussion tetap ada per masing-masing timeframe"):** this supersedes every cross-timeframe and cross-agent rule below.
    - **One timeframe per room.** The 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 rooms each read only their own chart: its closed bars, its HUD snapshot, its local records and the news. The Higher-Timeframe Desk is retired (`htf` stays only as a name for stored rooms). The 15m room no longer reads the H1 decision, the 1H room or the 5m lane, and its moderator no longer names an H1 decision. The News Analyst no longer reads the Macro War Room. The Risk Manager reads its chart's volatility, its own Prophet R38 reading and the economic calendar, no longer another room's news bias or higher timeframe.
    - **The Consensus Discussion stays, inside each timeframe**, over an anonymous bus: every statement appears as `#n` (roster order) with its stance, bias, confidence, evidence label, metrics and claim, never with an agent's name. Rebuttals and the moderator's dissent use `#n` references, mapped back to agent ids for storage and Nexus.
    - **Metrics, not names.** Every opening, rebuttal and conclusion carries `metrics` (one to four plain-language metrics it decided from). A text that names another agent (`names_an_agent`; its own HUD name is its data source and allowed) or lacks metrics (`metrics_missing`) gets one correction call. Since the fix of 2026-11-09 (production lost most room answers from 10:00 to 12:00 UTC and the 1H and 4H rooms stopped concluding): a role name (Analyst, Manager, Desk, Moderator) never appears, but an indicator's name (Supertrend, Prophet, Order Blocks) is a metric and counts as naming the agent only when used as an actor ("Supertrend says", "the Prophet's view", "according to Order Blocks"); a metrics list a model wrote inside its text is recovered and the text cut there, stray model tokens are stripped; and an answer whose only remaining problem after the correction is a missing metrics list is kept with the metrics of its own data source, marked `metrics_inferred` (every other error still rejects it). The moderator and the Reasoner never write a desk or role name. The shared base rules for rooms (`ROOM_RULES_INDEPENDENT`) drop "the H1 decision chain is authoritative" and the 5m lane.
    - **The 15m room is judged by its own quorum** (`entry_without_quorum` on its crowd line), like the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms; its confirmations are the 15m chart's own Prophet R38 reading. The legacy `entryAllowed` stays only for the retired live room.
    - **The H1 decision** reads the anonymous consensus, never the 5m context (`ownTimeframeEvidence`), and takes its prices from the 1-hour chart's own key levels (`ownTimeframePlan`: the Key Level Agent over the 1H HUD snapshot and the 1H bars, the 1H ATR and the last 1H close). The strategy engine and the Global Conclusion stay multi-timeframe readers; they are not room agents.
  - **Timeframe War Rooms (owner decision 2026-11-06; supersedes the five-desk H4/D1 threads of step 4):** every Thread, D1, 4H, 1H and 15m, is the consensus of all agents, as in the Live War Room. 15m is the Live War Room; `src/timeframe-threads.mjs` runs the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms.
    - **Phases:** each closed bar of that chart runs the same three phases: every agent opens on the bus, the minority (by bias and confidence) and the Risk Manager answer named agents in the Consensus Discussion (the term replaces "debate", owner decision 2026-11-07) for at most 3 minutes, and the moderator writes the TLDR.
    - **Agents:** the desks plus one agent per HUD computed on that chart.
      - Desks:
        - The room's own timeframe desk analyses only its chart (owner decision 2026-11-06; `src/timeframe-desk.mjs`):
          - The 1H, 4H and D1 Desks read the local Prophet R38, R37 and GMI records of the bar, decoded into plain sentences.
          - A desk whose records parity has not approved is labelled so; it speaks at its own confidence (owner decision 2026-11-09).
          - A room without local records for its bar has no own desk.
        - The other desks: the News Analyst (News Daily), the Technical Analyst and the Risk Manager. The Higher-Timeframe Desk is retired (independent rooms, 2026-11-09). In the 1H room only (B16), the Risk Manager's data and the moderator's prompt also carry lens B's liquidity conditions, as in the 15m room (see "Lens B's conditions in the 15m and 1H rooms"); the 4H and D1 rooms never read them.
      - HUD agents come from that bar's HUD snapshot. The pull adapter now also publishes H4 and D1 HUD snapshots (`hud-snapshot.mjs`, timeframes `"240"` and `"1D"`, GMI from the one engine in `gmi-core.mjs`).
        - Fresh windows after the close: H1 25 minutes, M15 9, H4 30, D1 60.
        - **Catch-up (owner decision 2026-11-06):** an H4 or D1 bar whose snapshot was never sent in that window is sent once while it is still the chart's latest close (H4 up to 4 hours, D1 up to 72 hours, which covers the weekend). It is built on the bundle cut at the bar's close, so it is the snapshot that bar would have had. The marker `market-data:v2:hud-published:<candle_key>` (4 days) keeps every bar to one send. H1 and M15 have no catch-up: their rooms move on with every bar.
        - The pull adapter's `GET /health` shows `last_hud_publish`: each chart's status and candle key, and `catch_up` when a bar was sent late.
        - **Weekly snapshot (ZF2, owner 2026-11-26: "ZF2 snapshot Weekly"):** the pull adapter also publishes the W1 chart's snapshot (`hud-snapshot.mjs` chart `W1`, timeframe `"1W"`, Pine v6's `timeframe.period`; candle key `OANDA:XAUUSD:1W:<open ms>`; AI Worker KV `hud:1W:<candle_key>` 14 days and `hud:1W:latest` 8 days, `HUD_LATEST_TTL_S`). **Zone typing only:** no room, council, desk, HUD agent, health check, alert, API route or UI reads it. **Without GMI:** the bundle carries 260 closed weekly bars (`SERIES` retain 260; production KV on 2026-11-26: 260), fewer than GMI's 320 (`vp_lookback` 300 + 20), and GMI has no weekly chart (`GMI_CHART_MINUTES`, hashed); `gmi_error` reads `gmi_not_on_weekly` and the GMI HUDs are in `not_computed`. It carries the Prophet R38 and R37 HUDs and the Mizan rows (at least 200 weekly bars), and a weekly chart plane (`buildWeeklyPlane`, version `cryptophys.chart-plane.weekly.v1`: `close`, `atr` and `liquidity.whale_zones` only, the whale map's own function) with `zones.prophet`. Fresh 12 hours after the close (the bar closes at the next week's open; the series is pulled about an hour later), caught up while it is still the latest close (6 days); its marker lives 8 days. `/health` lists it as `last_hud_publish.charts.W1`. **Optional:** a missing weekly snapshot means no weekly boxes, never an error or a degraded state.
    - **No webhook dependency:** the rooms run on local computation only: closed bars, the HUD snapshot and the local Prophet R38 record of the bar.
    - **Calibration:** a HUD statement is verified only where it equals a parity-approved record, and computed from verified bars only where the bar's parity comparison matched (see "Calibration status once"). This is a label, not a cap: since 2026-11-09 the agents of these rooms speak at their own confidence and their weight comes from their track record.
    - **Quorum, not confirmation** (owner decision 2026-11-09): a BUY or SELL conclusion needs more than half of the agents' track-record weight holding the matching bias (`entry_without_quorum`, one correction call, then rejected). The chart's Prophet R38 confirmation or setup still READY (`values.setup.buy_ready` / `sell_ready` of the local record; the desk's reading `chartDeskReading` returns `buy_setup_ready` / `sell_setup_ready`) is an argument the agents weigh, listed for the moderator as "indicator readings (arguments, not gates)". The 1H conclusion names the H1 decision when it differs.
    - **Schedule:** the rooms run from the parity sweep cron in their own `waitUntil`, one after another (D1, 4H, 1H), so the model sees one room's burst at a time; no room reads another (2026-11-09). They run behind `TF_THREADS_ENABLED`, within `TF_THREADS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (production 120, preview 150; about 26 calls per room).
      - A bar waits up to 20 minutes for its HUD snapshot, then runs with the desks only.
      - **Re-convene:** a room that concluded without its HUD snapshot meets once more, for the same bar, when the snapshot arrives late (the catch-up above). The second session is stored as run `<bar open>#hud`, which sorts after the first, so the tab, the Global Conclusion and the room below read it. The API reports the bar open without the suffix and `reconvened: true`.
      - Rooms of one sweep are 65 seconds apart: one room spends about 22 calls within a minute, and the Workers AI per-minute limit is shared.
      - A room concludes only when at least half of its agents spoke. Otherwise the next sweep retries the failed openings. Two hours after the close, it concludes with whoever spoke.
      - Each room runs once per bar, plus at most that one re-convene.
    - **Storage and API:** messages are stored in `agent_thread_messages` (thread `tf:H1` / `tf:H4` / `tf:D1`, run = bar open; steps `brief`, `open:*`, `debate:*` and `conclusion`). Read API: `GET /api/threads/timeframe?chart=H1|H4|D1` (also `1H`, `4H`).
- **News Daily** (owner decision 2026-11-06): `src/news-daily.mjs`, run by the hourly news cron after the hourly analysis, behind `NEWS_DAILY_ENABLED`, within `NEWS_DAILY_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (production 6, preview 12).
  - **Input:** the unique articles of the last 24 hours, taken from the stored hourly analyses (no extra TradingView pull), plus the 24-hour moves of gold, the dollar index, WTI oil and the USDT dominance from the cached closed bars.
  - **Per headline:** each headline gets a gold bias (BULLISH, BEARISH or NEUTRAL) and one plain sentence why. A tag is cached for 3 days, so each run tags only new headlines.
  - **TLDR:** the News Analyst writes the TLDR: one bias for the day, a summary and one to three reasons. A run is skipped while the 24-hour headline set is unchanged.
  - **Consumers:** every News Analyst desk reads it, in the Live War Room and the timeframe rooms. Read API: `GET /api/news/daily`.

- Transport: the pull adapter calls `AI_WORKER.submitContext(record)` on the
  AI Worker's `LocalComputationIngress` RPC entrypoint (no shared secret).
- Storage: KV key `context:m5:latest` (TTL 900 s) on the AI Worker.
- Read API: `GET /api/m5-context` returns the latest stored 5m context.
- **Analysis timeframes.** `POST /webhook` starts the AI chain only for `ANALYSIS_TIMEFRAMES`: `"60"` (H1) in production, and `"60,5"` in preview for the M5 fixture. Webhooks on other timeframes, such as 5m or 15m parity comparators, are stored only and answer `analysis_status: "stored_only"`.
- **Verified 5m lane (C0).** Per-timeframe parity identities and the full 5m producer are in place. Comparison starts once the owner installs the existing alerts on the 5m chart. The label switch is step 4. While `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are both approved, and the observed 5m records for the bar come from those approved implementations, the live room's 5m read is labelled **VERIFIED 5m** (`trust_status: approved_by_parity`). It is then read in plain language with the same reading as the H1 webhooks (`src/m5-verification.mjs`).
  - Otherwise the read stays UNVERIFIED 5m, and the label switches by itself.
  - `GET /api/live` returns `m5_verified`, and the Nexus live badge follows it.
  - **5m alerts after approval (owner decision "pilihan 1", 2026-11-05).** Once `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are both approved, the owner may delete the two 5m TradingView alerts. The approval persists while the 5m source and implementation hashes are unchanged; a 5m engine change needs the 5m alerts again for a calibration run, like the monthly H1 calibration.
  - The H1 chain and the bus read the same verification (B2), so every consumer of the 5m data follows one label.
- Freshness rule for use in `runAnalysis`: accepted only when both
  `now - Date.parse(candle_time) <= 15 min` and
  `now - Date.parse(received_at) <= 10 min`.
- Diagnostics: `GET /api/local-lane` (proxied by the Nexus dashboard to the pull adapter's
  `MarketDataReader.diagnostics()`) exposes the pull adapter's last refresh, latest closed bar
  per series, last publish and quota budget.

### Agent inventory and AI logic (`src/agent-inventory.mjs`, read-only)
- `GET /api/agents/inventory` (`xauusd-agents-inventory.v1`): every room seat from the code rosters, with rooms, models, triggers, switches, tables, kernel id, last message and 7-day count. Dormant after 7 days without a message; `flag_off` when none of its rooms' switches is on.
- `GET /api/ai/logic` (`xauusd-ai-logic.v1`): every AI task of `organism.json` with its logic and its use (`ai_usage_daily`, default 14 days) under all its meter labels (`<task>`, `shadow:<task>`, `shadow:arena:<task>`); verdict `active`, `dormant_flagged` or `declared_only`.
- `organism.json` links the inventory with the top-level `agents_inventory` (`href`, `schema`, `count`); the platform's census ignores it today and reads it later as an annotation.

### The shared model call (`src/model-call.mjs`; the H1 multi-agent bus is retired)
- **The H1 multi-agent bus is retired (owner 2026-11-07 for H1; its code removed 2026-11-15, "setuju semuanya").** Gone: its runner, the `multi_agent_bus` queue handler (a stale task is acknowledged and logged), `GET /api/bus`, its reads in outcome scoring (`bus:*` subjects), in `/api/tldr` (`h1.consensus` is null) and in Threads 1H (`h1:<candle>` now carries the H1 decision and the HUD agents only), and the engines `bus_agent` / `bus_moderator` in `organism.json`. D1 `bus_messages` (335 rows, 2026-11-04 to 2026-11-07) is archived to R2 `xauusd-room-transcripts/archive/d1/bus_messages.ndjson` (binding `ROOM_TRANSCRIPTS`) and dropped by the AI Worker's hourly cron (`src/bus-archive.mjs`; owner 2026-11-15, "setujui semua"): the drop runs only after the archive reads back with the same sha256 and line count. The CI token has no D1 access, so `.github/workflows/archive-bus-messages.yml` only reads the archive back from R2 (workflow_dispatch). Migration `0023` drops the table from fresh databases.
- **What stayed, moved to `src/model-call.mjs`:** `callBusModel`, the model call every room, thread, council and shadow arm uses (role models, the fallback, timeout, rate-limit and cut-off rules below, the usage meter), and the news thread's schemas, validators and technical evidence (`buildBusEvidence`). The names keep their `BUS_` prefix.
- **The flag:** `MULTI_AGENT_BUS_ENABLED` became `NEWS_THREADS_ENABLED` (`"true"` in `wrangler.jsonc` and `wrangler.preview.jsonc`). For one deploy `newsThreadsEnabled` still reads the old name when the new one is unset; a later PR removes that fallback.
- Until 2026-11-07, after the H1 synthesis was persisted (degraded or complete), the AI Worker enqueued a `multi_agent_bus` task on the existing queue. The task reads that synthesis, runs agents `technical` and `news` for 2 rounds, then a `moderator` consensus. That is at most 5 model calls, with a 300 s wall budget and the `MULTI_AGENT_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` cap; the cap fails closed.
- **Models per role (owner decision 2026-11-07, model migration).**
  - **Why:** the Workers AI changelog of 2026-11-05 retires `@cf/zai-org/glm-5.3-flash` and `@cf/nvidia/nemotron-3-120b-a12b` on 2026-11-20 (HTTP 410, error 5028; first announced for 2026-11-15). glm-5.3-flash was also a paid model limited to 20 requests a minute per account, the main source of the rooms' 3021 errors.
  - **Room agents** (openings, Consensus Discussion, headline tags, plain-language rewrites): `ROOM_AGENT_MODEL` = `@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it`, thinking off. Fallback: `ROOM_AGENT_FALLBACK_MODEL` = `@cf/openai/gpt-oss-20b`, low reasoning effort.
  - **Moderators** (every room conclusion and the News Daily TLDR; `stepRole` in `src/agent-threads.mjs`): `MODERATOR_MODEL` = `@cf/openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Fallback: `MODERATOR_FALLBACK_MODEL` = `@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it`.
  - **News chain** (Analyst, Counselor, Reasoner) and the **H1 Reasoner**: `AI_MODEL_ANALYST`, `AI_MODEL_COUNSELOR` and `AI_MODEL_REASONER` = `@cf/openai/gpt-oss-120b`. The analyst and counselor stages reason at low effort; the Reasoners at medium, with 8192 tokens.
  - **Request forms:** `modelRequests` in `src/model-text.mjs` builds them per model family.
    - gpt-oss: Chat Completions with `reasoning_effort` and a strict JSON schema; then without the schema; then the Responses form (`input`, `reasoning.effort`).
    - gemma-4: `chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking` false, with the schema, then without.
    - A form the model rejects (an input-validation error) falls back to the next one and is logged as `model_request_form_rejected`.
    - `extractModelText` reads the legacy, Chat Completions and Responses API shapes.
  - **Precedence:** a role's own variable wins. `MULTI_AGENT_MODEL` / `MULTI_AGENT_FALLBACK_MODEL` override every role when set (tests, rollback). An empty fallback turns the fallback off.
  - **Failures:** on an error (or a 60 s timeout) the call is retried once with the role's fallback. `fallback_used` is recorded, and the primary error is stored as `primary_error` on the message. A pinned primary failure belongs to the failed model only: a room's moderator keeps its own primary when the agents' primary failed.
  - **Moderator robustness:** bus agent ids in a moderator's text and in rebuttals are written as the agents' names before validation (`agentIdsToNames`), and a fourth reason is dropped. A moderator answer still rejected after its correction is asked once from the moderator's fallback model. A failed conclusion shows its errors, model and raw text in the room API.
  - **Proof:** preview-e2e asserts the role models on the news thread and the War Room. A preview-only `/preview/reasoner-probe` makes one call in the H1 Reasoner's request form.
  - **Fallback reliability** (owner decision 2026-11-06, after three preview-e2e failures that day):
    - A primary **timeout** is transient: the step falls back, and the next step tries the primary again. The second timeout in a run (`PRIMARY_TIMEOUTS_TO_PIN`) sends the rest of the run to the fallback, because each timeout costs up to 60 s of the wall budget. Any other primary error does so at once, as before.
    - A fallback answer cut off by its token limit (`finish_reason` length: the reasoning model spent the budget on hidden reasoning) is asked once more, with twice the budget and a note to answer with the JSON only. This happens only when at least 30 s remain before the deadline. The hourly budgets do not count this one retry.
    - The same `callBusModel` serves the H1 bus, the news threads, the War Rooms, the timeframe rooms and News Daily. Beam (`reflection-ai/beam-1.0-501b-a23b`) was evaluated and rejected: it needs AI Gateway Unified Billing credits.
- The bus never wrote into the chain's evidence, prompts or decision. Its output was D1 `bus_messages` (migration `0003_bus_messages.sql`, never edited), read through `GET /api/bus` until 2026-11-15.

### Agent threads and live 15m room (Phase 3, `docs/RFC_NEXUS_AGENT_SOCIAL.md`)
- Code: `src/agent-threads.mjs`. Tables `agent_threads` and `agent_thread_messages` (migration `0004_agent_threads.sql`; new tables only, `0003` is never altered; the schema is also ensured at runtime for the preview D1).
- **News threads** (behind `NEWS_THREADS_ENABLED`; `MULTI_AGENT_BUS_ENABLED` for one deploy when unset):
  - After each news analysis, a `news_thread` queue task makes the analysis a post.
  - `news` and `technical` reply, each then replies to the other's reply (`parent_id`), and a `moderator` posts the consensus.
  - At most 5 calls, using the bus model with the bus fallback.
- **Live 15m room** (behind `LIVE_ROOM_ENABLED`, owner decision 2026-11-04). **Retired in code on 2026-11-15:** the War Room (`WAR_ROOM_ENABLED`) has met at every 15m close since 2026-11-05; the legacy room's runner and its engines are gone, `LIVE_ROOM_ENABLED` still gates enqueueing the 15m task. History of the legacy room:
  - Every 5m context submission (RPC `submitContext`, or the authenticated `POST /evidence/m5-context`) refreshes the 5m execution context in KV.
  - Only a submission whose closed 5m bar also closes a 15m candle (:00, :15, :30, :45 UTC) enqueues a `live_room` task. The task carries the context itself.
  - At every 15m close, `technical` and `risk` (a reply to technical) post, then a `moderator`, using stances BUY/SELL/HOLD/WAIT/CLOSE. There is no "nothing changed" skip.
  - Evidence:
    - `m15`: the closed 15m candles built from the last 12 closed 5m bars; a candle counts only when all three of its bars are present. This is the setup.
    - `m5`: the local-lane 5m read and bars, labelled VERIFIED 5m when parity-approved on 5m (C0), otherwise UNVERIFIED 5m. This is the execution timing.
    - `h1`: the H1 decision and the H1 TradingView reading.
    - `news`: the latest news-thread consensus.
  - The moderator must state the H1 decision whenever its stance differs from it.
  - Each role with its own free-plan fallback since 2026-11-07 (before: primary only, never the paid glm-4.7 fallback). The `LIVE_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` cap fails closed: 4 rooms an hour × 3 calls, well under 36.
  - Thread ids are `live15m:<day>`. `GET /api/live` also returns the older `live5m:` history.
  - The 5m context carries the last 12 closed bars (`recent_bars`). The H1 chain strips them.
- Read APIs: `GET /api/threads?kind=news|h1`, `GET /api/threads/:id` (nested replies; `h1:<candle_key>` serves the stored H1 bus discussions as a thread, history only since 2026-11-07) and `GET /api/live?since=`.
- **Nexus layout (owner decisions 2026-11-05 and 2026-11-06).**
  - **Center, under the chart:** the living War Rooms of every timeframe in one card. Its top is the tab bar `News | 15m | 1H | 4H | D1`, and every page load opens on News (no 5m tab for now).
  - **Each tab** starts with its conclusion directly under the tab bar: the stance in large type, confidence, summary, reasons, dissent, and what binds it.
    - On 15m: the H1 constraint and the entry confirmations.
    - On 1H, 4H and D1: that chart's Prophet R38 confirmation.
    - Under the conclusion come the communication bus and the Consensus Discussion. The 1H tab ends with the Reasoner's H1 decision.
  - **Sources:** 15m reads `GET /api/war-room/latest`; 1H, 4H and D1 read `GET /api/threads/timeframe`. The News tab shows the News Daily TLDR and each headline with its bias (`GET /api/news/daily`).
  - **No separate 15m cards:** they repeated the 15m tab.
  - **The active tab drives the page.**
    - The left panel lists the desks and HUD agents of that tab's room, with their stance and calibration. On the News tab it shows the News Analyst.
    - The Camarilla set and pivot ranges of the Key levels panel, and the regime radar, on the right show that tab's chart. News uses the 1-hour chart.
- **Strategy engine** (owner decision 2026-11-06: "the decision is the consensus of all agents, not three"; `src/strategy-engine.mjs`, `GET /api/strategy`; deterministic, no model call, computed on read).
  - **Why:** the H1 chain's three models (Analyst, Counselor, Reasoner) apply the policy rules, which need a Prophet R38 trigger on the H1 candle itself, so the decision was almost always WAIT. Meanwhile the rooms' agents leaned clearly one way.
  - **Top-down layers** (every room's lean is the confidence-weighted bias of all its agents):
    - **D1 macro trend and 4H intraday trend:** their mean is the trend. A room older than two of its bars is stale and drops out.
    - **1H setup:** the side is the trend's when the 1H room does not lean against it. With a neutral trend, a 1H lean of 0.3 or more counts when the 15m room agrees.
    - **15m location:** the 15m room must not lean against the side. Price must sit at a key level with the trade (within 0.35 × the 1H ATR(14)) or inside a demand or supply zone (order block, mitigation, QM or flip zone of the 15m HUDs). Otherwise the planned entry is a pullback to the nearest level.
      - **Setup type (owner decision 2026-11-08, `setupType` in `src/level-readers.mjs`):** from the 15m chart plane (and the 1H Fibonacci leg when the 15m has none), deterministic:
        - *continuation_pullback:* price in the OTE band (0.618 to 0.786) of a leg with the trade, or back at the KWW mean to −1.618σ against it, with liquidity with the trade within 0.35 × the 1H ATR (an open FVG target, the RBS or SBR level, a whale zone or mitigation block on the trade's side);
        - *reversal_sweep:* price stretched 1.618σ or more against the trade at the QM level or a whale zone with the trade;
        - otherwise *none*.
        A setup type counts as a location with the trade, like a demand or supply zone, and the entry note names its evidence. The strategy reports it as `setup`; the captured strategy inputs keep the chart plane, so the replay reads the same setup.
    - **5m trigger:** a VERIFIED 5m Prophet confirmation in the trade's direction, or the 15m Prophet confirmation. An unverified 5m confirmation never counts.
      - **At every closed 5m bar (owner decision 2026-11-08, "the confirmation is critical"):** the AI Worker re-evaluates the engine when the pull adapter sends a 5m context (`ingestM5Context` → `evaluateStrategyAtM5Close`), with that bar's confirmation (VERIFIED only while `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are approved) and its close as the price. It no longer waits for the 15m room.
      - A TRIGGERED setup goes to the Signal Ledger at once (one per hour, none while one of the same side is open) and its Telegram alert follows.
      - The live 5m inputs count while the bar closed at most 10 minutes ago (`liveM5Inputs`); `/api/strategy` and the Global decision read them too.
      - Deterministic: no model call.
  - **Key levels: the Key Level Agent** (owner decision 2026-11-06: "not only Camarilla"; `src/key-level-agent.mjs`, `GET /api/key-levels`, deterministic):
    - **Sources:** every key level of every chart, weighted by kind and by timeframe (15m 1, 1H 1.5, 4H 2, daily 2.5, weekly 3):
      - Camarilla H3/L3 0.6, H4/L4 1.0, H5/L5 0.8, of the 15m, 1H, 4H, daily and weekly sets (from the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 HUD snapshots). Each Camarilla level, pivot and CPR edge has one value, taken from the freshest snapshot: production on 2026-11-06 showed a stale D1 snapshot's daily set counted beside the current one as a second source;
      - the daily and weekly pivot 1.0, and the CPR top and bottom 0.7;
      - the point of control 0.9 and the value area high and low 0.8, of each chart;
      - the last four swing highs and lows (2-bar fractals of the last 60 closed bars) of the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 charts, 0.9;
      - the previous day high and low 1.0 and close 0.6, and the previous week high and low 1.0;
      - round numbers within four 1H ATRs of price: every 50 dollars 0.8, every 25 dollars 0.4.
      - **the four level readers of the chart plane (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 2 of 3; `src/level-readers.mjs`):**
        - **Band Agent:** KWW ±1.618σ 0.7, KWW mean 0.5, EMA 114 0.6, VWAP 0.6 (15m and 1H only), TVA channel edges 0.5.
        - **Liquidity Agent:** RBS/SBR 1.0, QM 0.9, active whale zone edges 0.9, mitigation block edges 0.7, the three nearest open FVG targets per side 0.7 × their mass (clamped 0.3 to 1.5), HVN 0.8, LVN 0.5.
        - **Projection Agent:** Fibonacci 0.618 and 0.786 (OTE) 0.8, 0.5 0.6, the 1.272 and 1.618 extensions 0.7, the probability cone p90/p10 0.4, the tactical trendlines 0.5, PA support and resistance 0.6. **Chart patterns** (owner 2026-11-12: "adopsi semua yang ada di buku untuk main muscle knowledge Projection Agent"; `src/chart-patterns.mjs`, `docs/books/bulkowski-chart-patterns/NOTES.md`): the 53 chart patterns and 10 event patterns of Bulkowski's *Encyclopedia of Chart Patterns* are catalogued by chapter (`PATTERN_CATALOG`); deterministic detectors written from scratch read each chart's closed bars (15m, 1H, 4H, daily; sizes in multiples of the chart's average true range, never the book's percentages or statistics) and give at most five patterns per chart, the broken-out first. Each pattern gives levels under the Projection Agent, in the projection family (a zone counts it once per chart): a forming pattern's breakout lines 0.6; after a close beyond a line, the broken line 0.8 (where a throwback or pullback returns), the measured target 0.7 (the height projected from the breakout; 0.6 for a busted pattern) and the other line 0.5. A one-sided pattern that closes beyond its other side is busted and reads the other way; a pattern whose target was reached or whose last turn is more than 15 bars old is dropped. **Event patterns:** gold's event is a released USD figure with a direction for gold (`recentReleases`, the last 5 days, kept in the strategy inputs for the replay): the release bar's high and low are the breakout lines (bullish and bearish release surprise), a flag after it is the release flag, and a bar that moved price by 4 average ranges followed by a 20 to 60 % retrace is a dead-cat bounce (or its inverse); the five stock-only events (FDA approvals, same-store sales, analyst ratings) are catalogued as not applicable. The Key Level Agent's reading lists the patterns (`key_levels.patterns`) and the decision record keeps them (`chart_patterns`: chart, pattern, status, lines, target) so each pattern's outcome on XAUUSD can be measured. **Calibration on gold** (owner 2026-11-12: "ya kerjakan kalibrasi bobot pola chart"; `src/pattern-calibration.mjs`): every hour the parity sweep replays the same detector over the last 60 closed bars at every bar of the cached series (15m, 1H, 4H, daily) and follows each breakout of a pattern (one at a time per pattern and direction on a chart) for twice its width in bars (30 to 120): `target` reached first, `failed` (a close beyond its other line first, or both in one bar), or `expired`. A pattern's success rate is shrunk toward the rate of all patterns (a prior worth 10 breakouts); its factor is that over the base rate, within 0.5 to 1.5, and applies from 20 resolved breakouts of that pattern (else 1). The factor scales a confirmed or busted pattern's broken-line, target and other-line weights (at most 1); forming patterns and event patterns keep the defaults. Stored in KV `chart-patterns:calibration:v1` (read by the strategy inputs, `buildKeyLevels({ calibration })`), shown at `GET /api/patterns/calibration`; switch `PATTERN_CALIBRATION_MODE` (`auto` by default, `off` = the default weights). Since v2 (2026-11-22, Track A PR 4) the same KV value (key and TTL unchanged) also carries `by_tf` (per chart: its rate, `bars_to_target` {n, median, p90} from target outcomes only, and per pattern the target/failed/expired split, `rate` (raw, the same meaning as in the pooled block) and `shrunk_rate` (shrunk toward the chart's rate with a prior of 10), active from 20 breakouts) and `open` (each chart's pending breakouts, with the pattern as it was on the bar the replay first detected it broken out, v1's `at`, and `bars_at_calibration`, the bars from that bar to the run, frozen at `computed_at`; a live count is recomputed from `breakout_close`, and the name `bars_since_breakout` is reserved for that live count, A10); `horizonFor` gives a pattern's follow-up horizon in bars (its own p90 on the chart when active there and drawn from at least 20 targets, else the chart's p90 from 20 targets, else 30, never above the replay's horizon). The pooled block and the factors are as in v1. Switch `PATTERN_HORIZON_MODE` (`on` by default; `off` strips `by_tf` and `open` on read). **Per-trade record** (Track A PR 5, 2026-11-26; recorded only): a pattern trade carries its pattern and chart on the decision's trade (`tradeFor`; `valid_until_ms` too once a plan carries it), the signal adds `trade_pattern`, `trade_chart` and `trade_tp2_plan` (the plan's TP2, feed scale; never a key named `tp2`), Omega stamps `pattern`, `chart` and `plan_tp2_feed` on its open and place_limit intents, v4's `ledger_omega4` row gets `pattern` and `chart` columns (an idempotent ALTER, filled from the decision's trade), and `GET /api/ledger` adds `by_pattern` (the decision signals' plan R for TP1 and TP2 per `pattern@chart`, through the engine_decisions join). A trade without a pattern is byte for byte as before; the EA and the bridge's intents file are unchanged; `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 2e40fac9… → 12cdf5a5… (`managementFields`, `planIntents`), V14 unchanged. Switch: `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` (`on` by default; `off` leaves them out of the key levels, the strategy agents' pattern plans, the council's price action and the four rooms' projection data, and keeps the projection levels; it travels in the strategy inputs as `modes.chartPatterns`, so every reader of the strategy and the hourly capture honour it).
        - **Session Agent:** true month and year open 1.0, week and day open 0.8, Asia, London, NY AM and NY PM opens 0.5, the trading day open 0.6 and week open 0.8, the session POC 0.9 and VAH/VAL 0.8. Session levels are one value for every chart: they carry the daily timeframe and the freshest snapshot's value counts once.
        - **Opening-range edges** (Track A PR 8, orchestrator 2026-11-26; `rawLevels` in `src/key-level-agent.mjs`, `openingRanges` in `src/opening-range.mjs`): while a London or New York range is formed and its window is live (status `ok`), its high and low are levels: kind `open` (the session family), the 15m timeframe, reader `session`, weight 0.5 (`ORB_LEVEL_WEIGHT`), not unique, labelled in words only ("London opening range high", "New York opening range low"). They count under session on the 15m chart: they can add the session family to a zone (and widen it), never make a candidate on their own. Switch `ORB_LEVELS` (`on` by default; `off`, or `orb_breakout` off through `orbMode`, removes them); it travels in the strategy inputs as `modes.orbLevels`, present only when off, and the capture keeps it, so a replay rebuilds the same levels. 15m levels are on no per-chart map (KM1). No engine hash moves: the Key Level Agent is not in `ENGINE_CODE`.
        - The TDM POC/VAH/VAL and the previous day and week high/low are read elsewhere already, so the readers skip them. `GET /api/key-levels` reports how many levels each reader added (`readers`), and each zone member names its reader.
    - **Per-chart key-level maps (KM1, owner 2026-11-25, for the public analysis's `zones_by_chart`; `keyLevelsByChart` and `chartOfLevel` in `src/key-level-agent.mjs`, `keyLevelChartsOf` and `readStrategyWithCharts` in `src/strategy-engine.mjs`):** a second, separate reading of the same raw levels as the agent's (the same inputs and arguments as `buildStrategy`'s, through `keyLevelArgs`), clustered per chart over only that chart's levels with the agent's own tolerance, score, strength, family independence, candidate rule, cap per side and four-ATR window. **Which levels go where** (`chartOfLevel`): H1 every level whose timeframe is 1H (its Camarilla set, value area, order blocks, Supertrend line, swings, chart-plane band, liquidity and projection levels, chart patterns); H4 the same on 4H; D1 the same on the daily chart plus the shared levels that belong to no intraday chart: the daily and weekly pivot and CPR, the daily and weekly Camarilla sets, the previous day high, low and close, the previous week high and low, the session levels (true opens, trading day and week opens, session profile; daily timeframe in the Session Agent) and the round numbers. 15m levels are on no chart. `buildKeyLevels`' output, its candidates, the strategy and everything the decision engine reads are unchanged, and no stored record gains a field: the maps are computed on read from the inputs `readStrategy` already loads. Public output: `src/public-analysis.mjs` `chartMaps` (`CHART_ZONE_MIN_SOURCES` 2). **Public numbers (BP1, 2026-11-27):** a null level is never read as 0 (`numOrNull`); a zone or pattern with a missing or non-positive level is left out; a confirmed pattern's `projection` is shown only when `publicProjectionOf` finds a positive target beyond the broken line and within `PROJECTION_MAX_HEIGHTS` (10) of the pattern's height, else null (a gap or island has no measure). `src/public-projection.mjs` `round2` keeps null as null.
    - **Chart plane (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 1 of 3; `local-engines/producer/src/chart-plane.mjs`):** every HUD snapshot (15m, 1H, 4H, D1) carries `chart_plane`, every price object GMI v12.70 draws on the chart, as numbers at the closed bar. It is not a HUD section, so it adds no room agent; the Key Level Agent reads it from PR 2, the Nexus chart from PR 3.
      - **bands:** Z-KWW MA, ±1σ and ±1.618σ with the z-score; Dynamic EMA 114; VWAP (hlc3, reset at the trading day); the adaptive TVA regression channel (2 deviations).
      - **liquidity:** Aether FVG open targets with their mass; the Spacetime Whale Map (active zones and mitigation blocks, with HP); GABFX QM BUY/SELL and RBS/SBR; the TDM profile POC, VAH, VAL, LVN and HVN.
      - **projection:** the SMC Fibonacci grid (15 ratios, warped); the probability cone (p90/p10, 50 bars); the tactical trendlines; PA support and resistance.
      - **session:** True Openings (year, month, week, day, Asia, London, NY AM, NY PM); day and week open, previous day and week high and low; the Daily session volume profile (30 rows, 70 % value area, from 1m bars on 15m and 1H, 5m on 4H, 15m on D1), current and previous session.
      - **zones (Z1a, `local-engines/producer/src/adopt/prophet-ob.mjs`):** `zones.prophet`, every active Prophet R38 order block the core returns at the last bar (`active_order_block_zones`), the 4 nearest to the close per side: `{ side, top, bottom, age_bars, high_volume, touches }` (touches by the core's own rule: 0.15 x the chart's ATR(14), 40 bars, 3 bars apart). An adapter outside the hashed core; a failure leaves the plane without it. Read only by the demand/supply typing, never as a key level or by a room.
      - **zones.aegis (Z1b, `local-engines/producer/src/adopt/gmi-zone.mjs`; built, not live until merge):** GMI v12.70's active Aegis validated order blocks, from a duplicate of gmi-core.mjs's loop (the core and mizan.mjs unedited; an equality test holds it to the core on every bar), on the bars and mintick the GMI run used, on every chart GMI ran on; the 4 nearest per side: `{ side, top, bottom, age_bars, touches }` (touches = obProgressiveUpdate's own touched events). A failure leaves the plane without it. docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §4b.
      - **Limits:** path-dependent state is replayed over the engines' 1000-bar window; True Openings come from the 15m bars, so they are the true open at the stated New York time on every chart. Nothing in it is a TradingView alert field: it is unverified context, never a decision. The pull adapter's `/health` names its version per chart (`last_hud_publish.charts.<chart>.chart_plane`).
    - **Zones:** the levels, sorted by price, are grouped while a group's span stays within 0.3 × the 1H ATR (at least 1 dollar).
      - **Independence (owner decision 2026-11-08, after the chart plane gave 11 candidates):** a zone's sources are its distinct level FAMILIES (Camarilla, pivot/CPR, value area, swing, previous day/week, round number, order block, Supertrend, and the readers' Band, Liquidity, Projection and Session), not (kind, timeframe) pairs, so one chart's KWW mean, Fibonacci and PA line no longer count as three confirmations. A zone's score adds, per family and timeframe, only the heaviest level.
      - Strength: strong (score ≥ 6), medium (≥ 3) or weak.
      - **Candidate key level:** a zone with three or more families and at least medium strength, at most two per side (the strongest, then the nearest; a zone at price always stays; the others read `qualified`). `cryptophys.key-level-agent.v2`. The owner tightened this on 2026-11-06: at two sources, production marked 14 of 18 zones; at three sources and medium, it marked 9 of 18.
      - Role: support, resistance, or "at price".
      - **Demand and supply type, strength and freshness (Z0, owner 2026-11-23: "Langsung prioritaskan"; `src/zone-types.mjs`, docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md). Recorded only: no zone, candidate, price, guardrail, council, EA signal, Omega or public output changes.** (Since Z3a the EA signal's management fields carry the words as `key_level_types`; Omega reads them only while the owner's setting `zone_mgmt` is not off, see the Omega broker engine.)
        - **Sources.** After `limitCandidates`, each zone near price is typed from the charts' boxes (15m, 1H, 4H, D1 and, since ZF2, the weekly snapshot `"1W"` when there is one). Each box is its base weight times `TF_WEIGHT` (weekly 3, labelled `weekly`). The weekly snapshot types zones only: it adds no raw level, so zones, members and candidates are those of the four charts (`levelSnapshots` in `src/key-level-agent.mjs`); KM1's per-chart maps do not read it. It carries no GMI, so it gives `prophet_zone`, `whale` and (without Prophet zones) `prophet_best` boxes, never `gmi_ob`, `gabfx` or `gmi_flag`. `loadStrategyInputs` reads `hud:1W:latest` and adds `w1Snapshot` only when present; the capture (`slimInputs`) keeps it only when present, so stored inputs and replays without it are unchanged:
          - whale zones: support = demand, resistance = supply (a mitigated block already flipped); 0.9 active, 0.7 mitigation;
          - the best Prophet order blocks (`prophet_best`): 0.7;
          - the GABFX points: RBS 1.0 and QM buy 0.9 for demand; SBR 1.0 and QM sell 0.9 for supply;
          - the 15m GMI order-block and mitigation flags (`gmi_flag`): 0.7, on the zone at price only;
          - every active Prophet order block (`prophet_zone`, Z1a): the chart plane's `zones.prophet` (read by `prophetZoneBoxes` in `src/level-readers.mjs`, never as a level); bull = demand, bear = supply; 0.7, or 0.9 when the core flags the block `high_volume`. On a chart that has them, `prophet_best` is dropped (it is one of them).
          - GMI's Aegis validated order blocks (`gmi_ob`, Z1b; built, not live until merge): the plane's `zones.aegis` (`aegisZoneBoxes`); 0.8; one block counts once: a box with both edges within 0.001 (price) of a `prophet_zone` box on the same chart and side adds no weight, only provenance (`also`, `ds.also`); with `prophet_zone` off they all count; on a chart that has them, `gmi_flag` is dropped; their touches age a zone as `prophet_zone`'s. RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES §4b. **Recorded source labels (`ds_sources`, Z1b).** Each typed level of a decision's recorded `key_levels` (aroundSet) carries `ds_sources`, an array of words: first its weighted sources `<source>@<tf>` sorted (`prophet_zone@1H`, `whale@15m`; tf one of ZONE_TF_NAME's words: 15m, 1H, 4H, daily, weekly), then the same-block duplicates that added no weight as `<source>@<tf>(dup)` sorted (`gmi_ob@1H(dup)`); at most 12 labels, each at most 32 characters, matching `^[a-z_]+@(15m|1H|4H|daily|weekly)(\(dup\))?$`; absent when the level has none. Z5's `zone_readings.ds_sources` stores them comma-joined from Z1b's merge on (older rows stay NULL, no backfill). Not in the EA signal, `managementFields` or any public output (public analysis copies a fixed field list).
        - **Typing.**
          - A box counts on a strict overlap; a point must lie inside the zone.
          - ZF1 (owner 2026-11-25, "ZF1 tepi kotak"): a box (or a point) also counts when one of its edges is within `BOX_EDGE_TOL` (0.001) of one of the zone's member prices, i.e. the box's own edge made the cluster. Cluster edges are rounded to two decimals, so such a box could otherwise miss its own cluster (3433.454 against 3433.45). A box that only touches a cluster it did not make still does not count.
          - Per side, each (source, timeframe) adds its heaviest box.
          - Type: demand when demand_w > 0 and at least 2 x supply_w; supply in the mirror case; mixed when both are > 0; else none.
          - Strength on the type's score: strong >= 2.0, medium >= 1.0, else weak.
          - A lone 1H-or-higher box, or a lone 15m RBS/SBR point, is medium. A lone 15m whale is weak.
          - Decisive: demand or supply, and medium or strong.
        - **Freshness.**
          - Prophet's touch rule: touches on the 40 closed 15m bars before the decision bar, within 0.15 x ATR(1H), at least 3 bars apart, counted up to 3.
          - 0 touches = fresh, 1-2 = tested, 3 = worn.
          - The window is 10 hours for every zone timeframe, so a 4H zone touched two days ago reads fresh.
          - Z1a: a zone a `prophet_zone` box types reads the most touches of its own count and of those boxes (each counted by the core's rule on its own chart's bars), capped at 3: worn dominates.
        - **Output.** `z.ds = { type, strength, score, freshness, touches, sources }`. Source labels use timeframe words (`whale@4H`), never period numbers.
        - **Recorded.**
          - `decision.key_levels` (`aroundSet`, `keyLevelsAround`'s exact rule) adds `zone_type`, `zone_strength`, `zone_freshness` and `zone_rank` beside low and high.
          - Each plan carries `zone_types { entry, stop, tp1, tp2 }`. Producers: tradeLevels, targetsAhead and finishPlan; the trade, `strategy_plans` and MS2's shadow rows copy it.
          - Each plan also carries `opposing_ahead`, from `zoneAhead`, which runs last on the final entry and stop and reads only the recorded set:
            - the nearest decisive opposing zone ahead (supply for a long, demand for a short);
            - `room_atr`, `room_r` and `need_r`, where need is `ZONE_NEED` = max(1R, 0.75 ATR(1H));
            - `trend_lean`, `with_trend` (dir x trend >= 0.15) and `would_block`;
            - a stored decision replays it from its key_levels and trade.
          - `plan_setups` adds `entry_zone_type`, `stop_zone_type`, `tp1_zone_type` and `opposing_room_atr` (run-time ALTERs).
        - **Switch.** `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES` = "on" (default) | "off".
