It travels through the strategy modes (`modes.zoneTypes`, as CHART_PATTERNS_MODE), and "off" removes every typed field: main's output byte for byte. With `zone_mgmt` not off it also takes the words out of Omega's live management with no era move (emergency only; Z5 flags it); the documented rollback for Omega is `zone_mgmt` off.
        - **Source switch (Z1a).** `ZONE_SOURCES_OFF` (AI Worker; unset = every source on): tokens `prophet_zone`, `prophet_best`, `whale`, `gabfx`, `gmi_flag`, `gmi_ob` (Z1b), comma- or space-separated, through the strategy modes as `modes.zoneSourcesOff` (present only when a source is off; the capture keeps it). `prophet_zone` restores Z0's typing byte for byte; `gmi_ob` restores Z1a's.
        - **Read by Z2.** Z2's `zone_ahead` guardrail and plan filter read `opposing_ahead` (see the Decision Engine's "The zone_ahead check (Z2)").
        - **Box levels (Z6, owner 2026-11-26: "Langsung signal + zone_sd"; `sdLevels` in `src/zone-types.mjs`, docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §10).** `buildKeyLevels` reads the same evidence once more after the typing and returns `reading.sd_levels` beside the zones (the decision records `sd_levels`, `sd_levels_mode` and `opposing_ahead_sd`); clusters, `zoneLevels`, `squeezeOf`, `aroundSet`, the plans, the guardrails and `zoneAhead` do not change.
          - Each box (prophet_zone, prophet_best, whale, gmi_ob; never a GABFX point or a GMI flag) stays one zone `[bottom, top]`. Same-side boxes overlapping by half the narrower merge: the higher timeframe's edges and weight win, the others are provenance (`<source>@<tf>(dup)` in `ds_sources`).
          - Left out: a box the kept clusters (the recorded 12) cover for half its width, or whose near edge (both edges with price inside it) lies within 0.3 ATR(1H) of a kept edge.
          - Window from price (ATR(1H)) and cap per side (demand, supply) per timeframe: 15m 3 / 1, 1H 4 / 2, 4H 6 / 2, daily 8 / 2, weekly (`1W`) 10 / 1; at most 8 overall (decisive first, then the nearest), on top of the 12 clusters.
          - Words from the box's own weight (`ZONE_RULES`), freshness as the typing's, `zone_rank: "sd"`, `tf` (words). Only medium-or-stronger levels reach the signal; weak ones (every 15m box) are recorded only.
          - `opposing_ahead_sd`: each side's guardrail plan measured by `zoneAhead` over the recorded set plus the decisive box levels. A counterfactual only: the guardrails' `zone_ahead` keeps reading the recorded set.
          - **Switch.** `SD_LEVELS` (AI Worker, FS1 group zones) = "off" (code default: nothing computed or recorded, main byte for byte) | "record" | "signal" (also the signal, below). Production: "signal" in `wrangler.jsonc` (the owner's word). It travels through the strategy modes (`modes.sdLevels`, kept by the capture's slim inputs when set). Not an era switch, no mode-evidence restart.
          - **Signal.** With "signal" and every kept level typed, `managementFields` appends the decisive box levels to `key_levels`, each with a fourth token part `/sd` in `key_level_types`. No new key; the EA reads neither key.
        - **Replay.** 245 hourly captures, 2026-11-09 to 2026-11-23, without the pattern levels:
          - 34% of recorded zones are decisive;
          - decisive zones held 63% fresh, 51% tested and 39% worn;
          - `would_block` fired on 3.3% of the live BUY/SELL decisions' sides and never with the trend.
    - **The strategy plans on the zones:**
      - entry: at a zone with the trade (inside it, or within 0.35 ATR of it), or a pullback to its near edge;
      - stop: beyond the far edge of the first zone against the trade at least 0.5 ATR from the entry;
      - targets: the near edges of the next two candidate zones.
      - Non-candidate zones anchor entries and stops but are never targets.
  - **Plan and reward-to-risk:**
    - entry: price at the level, or the planned pullback level;
    - stop: beyond the next level against the trade at least 0.5 ATR away, padded by 0.2 ATR. Without one, or beyond 2.5 ATR, the stop is 1.5 ATR;
    - TP1 and TP2: the next two major levels with the trade, each at least 0.5 ATR beyond the previous one, or 2R and 3R projections;
    - RRR to each target.
  - **Vetoes and cautions:**
    - Vetoes (state `BLOCKED`): a Prophet cut loss (t_enc 4) against a **long** only, or a strong GMI verdict against the side (∓3), on the 1H chart. Prophet R38 sets t_enc 4 "RUTHLESS CUT LOSS" only from d_enc 3, a bearish structure break (a bull trap or a true breakdown without a strong trend); its bullish mirror d_enc −3 is t_enc 1 "PYRAMID BULL". So for a short it is a **support** (`supports`, +0.1 confidence; the Decision Engine records `supports` per side), never a veto (owner 2026-11-12: "setuju … justru jadikan ini sebagai opportunity"; production 07:01–08:46 UTC held eight bearish decisions, quorum −0.65 to −0.76 with every other short guardrail passing, at WAIT because it vetoed both sides).
    - Cautions (−0.1 confidence each):
      - a GMI and Prophet conflict, or a closed GMI gate (−1), on the 1H chart. These were vetoes until the owner loosened them on 2026-11-06: at 14:15 that day they blocked a clear bearish consensus;
      - both the 1H and the 15m regime chaotic;
      - News Daily leaning against the side at 0.6 or more;
      - **event risk** (backlog 11.1, owner decision 2026-11-08): a High-impact USD event of the economic calendar, from 2 hours before to 30 minutes after it. Never a forced WAIT.
        - **Source:** `src/econ-calendar.mjs`, the ForexFactory weekly export, fetched by the sweep at most once an hour and cached in `SIGNAL_CACHE` (`calendar:ff:thisweek:v1`). A failed fetch keeps the cache.
        - **Rooms:** the Risk Desk of the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms sees the events due now and in the next 24 hours.
        - **Calendar Agent (owner decision 2026-11-08, `src/calendar-agent.mjs`):** the projected effect of each USD event on gold, by fixed rules per event kind (stronger US data or a hawkish Fed: gold down; unemployment and claims the other way; a speech by its tone). It gives the effect of an actual above or below the forecast, the consensus lean (forecast against previous, at the strength the measured pre-release drift gives it), the status and the horizon (the learned pre-release horizon, else 2 hours, before; the learned reaction horizon, else 4 hours, after). The export has no actual figure; after the release the outcome is read from the headlines. `GET /api/calendar` carries `projection` and `outlook`; the News Daily TLDR and the Risk Desk read it and may not reverse a direction. Context only.
        - **Replay:** the strategy inputs record carries the gated events, so the replay reproduces the caution.
        - **Read model:** `GET /api/calendar` and the Nexus page `#/calendar`.
  - **States:** `NO_SETUP`, `BLOCKED`, `NO_LEVELS`, `SETUP_FORMING` (wait for the pullback), `SETUP_READY` (at the level, waiting for the trigger), `LOW_REWARD`, `LOW_CONFIDENCE` and `TRIGGERED`.
    - The decision is BUY or SELL only when `TRIGGERED`: the trigger fired, no veto, at least 1.5R (`MIN_RRR`) to TP2, and a confidence of at least 0.4. Every other state is WAIT, and it still shows the side, the levels and the RRR.
    - Confidence: 0.6 × the weighted alignment (D1 30, 4H 30, 1H 25, 15m 15), +0.15 at a level, +0.2 when triggered, minus cautions, and −0.1 when any room is stale.
  - **Authority:** the Global Conclusion's action is the strategy decision. The H1 chain keeps running and is shown beside it as a reference. Kernel reports are unchanged, and never carry levels.
  - **Advisory only:** no orders, no broker and no position size. The rooms still never give levels; the engine's levels come from closed bars only.
- **Trading assistant panel (right, owner decision 2026-11-06; charts only).** It is display-only: every number comes from the rooms, News Daily, the 5m context and the strategy engine. Nothing is computed outside the parity lane.
  - *Global decision* (first; owner decision 2026-11-06: the Global Conclusion and the strategy engine were redundant, so they are one card). It reads `GET /api/conclusion/global`, which carries the full strategy, and shows:
    - the decision, direction, global bias with its score, confidence, price and the 1H ATR;
    - the strategy headline;
    - one row per timeframe (D1, 4H, 1H, 15m, 5m) plus News: its lean as a diverging bar with a −100 to +100 value, whether it passes the strategy, and its note on hover;
    - the plan ladder (TP2, TP1, entry, stop with prices, the level behind each, and the RRR against the 1.5R minimum);
    - vetoes and cautions;
    - the H1 chain decision and the R38 confirmations as a reference.
    - **Lean vs bias:** a bias is the BULLISH, BEARISH or NEUTRAL label each agent gives. A room's lean is the confidence-weighted mean of its agents' biases, from −1 to +1; inside ±0.15 the strategy reads it as neutral.
  - **Chart zones** (owner request 2026-11-06): the price chart draws the candidate zones as bands behind the candles, using a lightweight-charts series primitive (`ui/src/lib/zones-primitive.ts`).
    - Bands are coloured by role (support green, resistance red, at price cyan) and shaded by strength. Labels show only where there is room.
    - The strategy plan's entry, stop, TP1 and TP2 are drawn as price lines, with the RRR on the axis label.
    - The "Zones" button toggles both, and is remembered per viewer.
  - *Key levels* (second; one panel since backlog item 18, owner approval 2026-11-09, `ui/src/components/KeyLevels.tsx`, which replaced the separate Levels card and Key Level Agent panel): one price axis for both. Left lane: the Camarilla set of the active tab's chart (H6 to L6, the H3–L3 range shaded; a set picker when the snapshot carries several) with the daily and weekly pivot ranges. Right lane: the Key Level Agent's zones of every chart, candidates coloured by role and shaded by strength. The price is one dashed line across both. The axis is fitted to price, the candidate zones and H3/L3/H4/L4, plus anything within half again of that reach; a level beyond it is listed at its edge with its price. Below: the close's place in the Camarilla, the six nearest candidate zones with their range, strength and leading levels (hover lists every level), and the agent's summary. Without the Global Conclusion the panel shows the Camarilla side alone.
  - *Global Conclusion* (data only, no card of its own; `GET /api/conclusion/global`, `src/global-conclusion.mjs`, deterministic, no model call). One multi-timeframe summary of the D1, 4H, 1H and 15m War Rooms and News Daily:
    - the global bias from the weighted room leans (below);
    - a heat strip of the five leans, with stale rooms faded;
    - how many timeframes agree;
    - a summary;
    - the action.
    - The action is the strategy engine's decision. Without a strategy it falls back to the H1 decision, which becomes WAIT when the global bias leans against it. The H1 chain decision shows as a reference.
  - *Market sentiment:* a semicircle gauge from −100 (bearish) to +100 (bullish): the Global Conclusion's score.
    - It shows the weighted mean of each room's lean: D1 30, 4H 25, 1H 20, 15m 15, News 10. A missing input drops out and the rest renormalise.
    - A room's lean is the confidence-weighted mean direction of its agents (BUY +1, SELL −1, WAIT, HOLD and CLOSE 0). News lean (`newsLean`, owner 2026-11-12) is 0.7 × the TLDR's stance × confidence + 0.3 × (bullish − bearish) over all tagged headlines while the TLDR concluded, else the headlines' balance alone. Before, the headlines alone: on 2026-11-12 00:32 a BEARISH TLDR at 0.72 over mostly neutral headlines read −0.10.
    - Under the gauge, a donut shows the headline biases of the last 24 hours.
  - *Trend by timeframe:* one diverging bar per timeframe: D1 macro trend, 4H intraday trend, 1H swing trend, 15m structure, 5m confirmation.
    - Each bar carries the room's lean, the moderator's action and one line of interpretation.
    - The 5m row uses the 5m context bias, at full weight when a confirmation printed.
  - *Levels* (`GET /api/hud/levels?tf=15|60|240|1D`, `src/hud-levels.mjs`): a vertical Camarilla ladder from H6 to L6 for the active tab's chart.
    - Shaded zones: the H3–L3 range, the H4–H5 breakout zone and the L4–L5 breakdown zone.
    - Chips switch between the 15-minute, 1-hour, 4-hour, daily and weekly sets.
    - The daily and weekly pivot ranges show as bands, or as edge markers with their price when far away. The close is a marker.
    - It reads "from verified bars" when the candle's bar is verified (see the three calibration tiers).
  - *Regime:* a radar of six engine sensors of that chart, each scaled 0–100: trend (Hurst), efficiency, stretch (|z| of 3), volume (of 2× average), cycle agreement and order (1 − entropy). A chaos flag is shown. It is not yet calibrated.
  - *Agent consensus:* one stacked bar per room of its agents' stances.
  - *Macro drivers 24h:* diverging bars for gold, DXY, WTI oil and the USDT market cap, with whether the gold–dollar inverse link holds.
  - *Session clock:* a 24-hour ring of the Tokyo, London and New York sessions in New York time, as the Chronos Matrix defines them, with a needle at now.
- **Agent compliance and decision cards (Aether phase A1, owner go 2026-11-07; `docs/RFC_AETHER_KNOWLEDGE_GUARDIAN.md`).** Both are read-only over the rooms: they never change a message, a stance, a conclusion or a decision.
  - **Compliance** (`src/compliance.mjs`, D1 `compliance_checks`, migration 0011): the sweep checks every stored room message once (openings, Consensus Discussion answers, conclusions of the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 rooms). The checks:
    - `order_wording`: order, size or lot wording.
    - `stance_bias_conflict`: BUY with a bearish bias, or SELL with a bullish bias.
    - `calibration_talk`: parity, calibration or verification named in the text, against the rule "Agents never mention calibration in their text".
    - `natural_language`: field names, evidence paths or key=value in the text.
    - `data_scope`: a HUD or indicator agent names another agent's indicator.
    - `GET /api/compliance?days=7` returns rule breaks per 100 messages per agent and room. Nexus shows them on the Scorecards page.
  - **Decision cards** (`src/decision-cards.mjs`, `GET /api/decision-cards?chart=60&limit=6`): one card per room conclusion, for Aether. Each card holds:
    - the conclusion, the crowd line and each agent's final stance, bias, confidence and claim;
    - the H1 decision and the confirmation booleans;
    - the open stuck states, the compliance findings, the scored outcomes, and the provenance (scoring version, KB hash, parity).
    - **No levels:** never an entry, stop, target, plan, zone, key level, close or ATR. Price-like numbers in agent text become "[level]", and a test walks every key and string.
- **Macro War Room (owner decisions 2026-11-08, `src/macro-room.mjs`, thread `macro:XAUUSD`).** The correlations become a room that discusses the news and the scheduled events that move gold.
  - **Agents:**
    - the Calendar Agent (fixed-rule projections; before a release its bias must follow the consensus lean, after it the headlines decide);
    - the News Analyst;
    - the Dollar Analyst (DXY), the Rates Analyst (US 10-year) and the Metals Analyst (silver): changes and correlation with gold, never a gold price level;
    - the Oil Analyst (owner request 2026-11-08, "geopolitics causes inflation"): WTI crude oil (`TVC:USOIL`, its 1h series derived from the 5m pull GMI already makes, sampled at gold's 4h closes, so no new TradingView call), its changes and correlation with gold, and the News Daily headlines on oil or geopolitics (supply, OPEC, sanctions, conflict, shipping routes). The rule it reads: a supply shock or conflict that lifts oil raises inflation expectations and safe-haven demand, which usually supports gold, unless the same rise pushes yields and Fed expectations higher;
    - the Correlation Analyst (gold against the dollar, yields, silver and oil).
  - **Phases:**
    - bus;
    - Consensus Discussion: the agents whose bias differs from the room's, at most 4;
    - the moderator's macro bias for gold over the next 4 to 24 hours, with reasons and drivers. A BULLISH or BEARISH conclusion needs an agent holding it.
  - **Schedule:**
    - 20 minutes after each 1-hour gold close (owner 2026-11-11, "session update per 1H close"; after each 4-hour close before), from the cached `OANDA:XAUUSD|1h` series, until the next 1-hour close (a missed hour is not replayed); and 30 minutes after a High-impact USD event (same-minute events share a session), within 3 hours;
    - each session once, at most one per sweep (the sweeps at :25 run the hourly session);
    - behind `MACRO_ROOM_ENABLED`, within `MACRO_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (30); about 12 calls a session with the Oil Analyst, so an hour with an event session stays within the limit.
  - **Context only:**
    - every room's News Analyst reads the latest conclusion (8 hours fresh);
    - the Global decision shows it as a MACRO row outside the weighted score;
    - it never changes the strategy engine's decision.
  - **Read:** `GET /api/macro`; Nexus `#/correlations` ("Macro War Room").
- **Signal Ledger (owner request 2026-11-07, `src/signal-ledger.mjs`, D1 `signal_ledger`, migration 0010).** Every directional signal, followed to its outcome in R. Advisory only: R multiples of the advice, never money, lots or positions. It never feeds a room, a prompt or a decision.
  - **Sources:**
    - `h1_decision`: the Reasoner's BUY/SELL from `trade_decisions` (entry, stop, one take profit).
    - `strategy`: the strategy engine's TRIGGERED setup (entry, stop, TP1, TP2), recorded by the sweep. It is recorded at most once per hour, and only when no signal of the same side is pending or open.
    - `decision`: the Decision Engine's BUY/SELL (`ingestDecision`), once per 15m key. **No stacking, as Omega** (owner 2026-11-13; LH1 2026-11-23, `blocksNewDecision`): an earlier `decision` row of the same side holds a new one back (a `signal_ledger_held` row with the blocker and the reason) only while Omega itself would, read from the row's times at the new decision: Omega's side skipped or closed holds nothing; Omega's position filled and open holds while unprotected (no `protected_at` in `omega_json`); a row Omega has not filled holds only inside Omega's entry window (`omegaEntryWindowEnd`: an `ENTER_LIMIT` plan the mode's `max_pending_life` from the decision, scalp 30, intraday and range 60, swing 240 minutes; a market entry until the next close or `max_signal_age`); a TP-policy row already open holds while it has neither TP1 nor SL+; a row the 5-minute run has not simulated yet, or one from before the Omega policy, keeps that same TP-policy rule. The TP-policy row keeps its own 8-hour fill window for its own result. (On 2026-11-23 the 04:15 BUY row waited 8 hours for its TP-policy fill after Omega's side had closed at 05:55 and held back six BUYs, the 08:45 one that Omega traded live included.) **The one-shot re-ingest** (owner: "Ya, masukkan 6"; `reingestHeldOnce`, `LH1_REINGEST`, KV marker `ledger:lh1:reingest:2026-11-23:v1` with the report, 90 days): in the first ledger run after LH1's deploy, every decision the old rule held on 2026-11-23 (00:00 to 23:59:59 UTC; the old rule held until the deploy, and the one-shot run comes right after it), oldest first, each from its own `engine_decisions` record through `ingestDecision` (decided_at kept, INSERT OR IGNORE by id), the open rows simulated after each insert so the next one reads the ledger as live would have; a re-ingested decision leaves `signal_ledger_held`, one the new rule still holds keeps a new trace with its blocker.
  - **Rules:** the simulation runs on the finest closed bars available at each time (owner 2026-11-12, "ya kerjakan keduanya"): the feed's 1m bars where the 1m series reaches (about two days), 15m bars before them, 1H bars before those (`stitchBars`). Entries, SL+, targets and stops are therefore recorded at the minute they happen; older history keeps its 15m resolution.
    - The entry is a limit order. It fills when price reaches it within 8 hours.
    - Before the fill, a stop touch cancels the signal as `invalidated`, a TP1 touch cancels it as `target_before_entry`, and no touch at all cancels it as `unfilled`.
    - Once filled, two policies run side by side: exit at TP1, and exit at TP2. Each ends at its target, at the stop (−1R), or after 72 hours at the close.
    - A bar that reaches both the stop and the target counts as the stop and is flagged as ambiguous.
    - MFE and MAE are kept in R.
  - **The Omega policy (owner 2026-11-12: "kebijakan Omega di Signal Ledger sesuai dengan mode intraday, swing, dan scalping … inputan ke aether dan selanjutnya akan menjadi knowledge").** A third policy on the `decision` signals, each kept with its `trading_mode` (scalp, intraday, swing) and `entry_action`: what the Omega engine would have done (`simulateOmega`, version `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v1`).
    - **Entry (version `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v2`):** the window runs from the decision (`opened_at`, never before the decided bar's close) to the next 15m close or `max_signal_age` of the Omega settings after the decision, whichever comes first. At market on the first 1m bar opening after the decision (on older 15m bars, the bar opening at the decided bar's close); an `ENTER_LIMIT` plan waits within the window for price within 0.35 × the stop distance of the entry, on either side (beyond it toward the target or, since 2026-11-13, toward the stop; `waiting_for_pullback`), and fills at the band's edge when a bar opens outside it; no bar in the window is `expired`; a fill past the stop is `stop_breached`. The stop and the target keep the plan's distances from the fill.
    - **Gates, decided once at the fill (`omegaGate`, kept in `omega_json`):** in R, with the day's limit `max_daily_loss_pct / risk_pct` of the Omega settings (2 % / 2 % = 1R) on the broker day from 22:00 UTC: `daily_loss_limit` (the day's realized Omega R at the limit), `max_positions`, `same_side_unprotected`, `daily_risk_budget` (the limit plus the day's R less 1R per open unprotected trade); the budget left below 1R caps the size (`omega_size`).
    - **Management at every bar close (every minute on 1m bars):** the latest decision already made then (from its `decided_at`, not its bar's close), its quorum score (power) and key levels through Omega's `keyLevelPlan` (SL+ behind passed levels, trailing TP, the close of a winner on a reversed power: `power_reversal`), an opposite BUY/SELL decision (`opposite`), 72 hours (`time`); within a bar the stop first, a gap exits at the open. **Version `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v3` (owner 2026-11-16: "lanjutkan Signal Ledger untuk lean_flip dan confidence_drop"):** a decision made with the strategy execution rules (`strategy_execution`, `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_EXECUTION.md`) also runs Omega's own `strategyExits` on the trade, with the agent of its decision (`trade.agent`; without one, the default agent's): the lean flip closes it, in profit or not (`lean_flip`); a drop of its agent's confidence closes it in profit (`confidence_drop`); the trailing TP moves only while that confidence is stable or rising. Decisions made before the rules are replayed as before. Exits: `target`, `trailed_target`, `stop`, `sl_plus`, `power_reversal`, `lean_flip`, `confidence_drop`, `opposite`, `time`.
    - **Approximations:** the feed's bars (the key levels are on its scale, no broker basis), a fixed 0.2 spread, the 5m ATR as the 15m ATR(14) / √3 and the 5m swing as the last 15 minutes of bars; no 5m or adaptive stop, no wait for the 5m entry trigger, no scale-out (Omega trades 0.01 lot). The ledger's one open signal per side still applies, so Omega and the plan are compared on the same signals.
    - **Columns:** `omega_status` (pending, open, closed, skipped), `omega_gate`, `omega_filled_at`, `omega_closed_at`, `omega_exit`, `r_omega` (per trade), `omega_size`, `omega_json`. Decision rows from before still in the 15m series are followed once.
    - **Entry as live Omega enters (V3L, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Make v3 follow the limit entry (Rekomendasi)"; `src/ledger-v3-limit.mjs`, rows tagged `omega_json.version` `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v3.1-limit`).** v3 reads the Omega setting `entry_order` in force at each decision from the settings' audited history (`omega_events` action `settings`, each save's whole settings, never its actor; after the last save the stored `omega_settings`; before any save off market, market). `"market"`: everything above, byte for byte (no version key; test V3L-3 against main's code and production's stored v3). `"limit"` and `"council"`: a resting limit through the live and counterfactual functions, none copied:
      - **Council.** The live entry council's recorded answer for the signal (`limit_council` kind `entry`, by `open:<close ms>:<B|S>`; as v4 replays the Position Council's recorded verdicts). Under `limit` anything but `place_limit` skips (`council_skip`), no answer is the mechanical fallback (placed); under `council` a skip skips, `place_limit` rests a limit, `enter_market` or no answer (live's silent council) is v3's market entry above.
      - **Placement.** `limitPlacement` (the level on the feed's scale, basis 0, the placement band, EZ1's `zoneLimit`, EZ1b's `zoneEdgeLimit`, `zone_passed`/`zone_tight`/`zone_rr`, `limit_through_price`, `limit_out_of_band`) at each 1m bar's open from the first after the decision and not before the council's answer, while the signal is live (the next 15m close and `max_signal_age`), as planIntents tries at every sync; the last refusal is the row's gate (no market fallback).
      - **Resting.** From the next bar: `limitCancelReason` (`pending_expired` after the mode's `max_pending_life`, `decision_against`, `quorum_share_drop` with CH1's thresholds), then SS1 (`limit_supersede` on): a newer same-side decision ends it (`superseded_by_signal`).
      - **Fill.** `limitFillPrice` (shared with the counterfactual): a BUY when the ask's low reaches the level, a SELL when the bid's high does; a gap through it fills at the open; a fill past the stop is `stop_breached`. The stop and target stay where the placement put them (the plan's, also for a level inside the zone); the management above runs from the fill; R is on the risk from the fill to that stop. The account gate (`omegaGate`) is decided at the fill, as for a market entry.
      - **Only the rules live had (`V3L_RULES_FROM`, orchestrator ruling).** EZ1 from its AI Worker deploy 2026-11-23 04:05:15Z (#533), EZ1b from 07:57:38Z (#534), each read at the placement attempt's minute; SS1 from its live deploy 15:43:27Z, read at the bar a newer signal would end the order and at the new decision's hold-back; `entry_order` from the settings' history. Before a rule's time v3 enters without it.
      - **Unfilled** (refused, cancelled, superseded, expired): `omega_status` skipped with that code as `omega_gate`. `omega_json.limit` keeps the placement time, the wait, the distance in R and the basis (`level`, `zone`, `zone_edge`), no price.
      - **Hold-back.** Under a limit entry every entry window is the mode's `max_pending_life`; with SS1 on, a row Omega has not filled holds a newer same-side decision back no longer (it supersedes the resting order, as live); `ingestDecision` reads the `entry_order` in force and the switch.
      - **Recompute, once (`recomputeV3LimitOnce`, from `runSignalLedger`, KV `ledger:v3l:recompute:v1`).** The rows since the first save off market (2026-11-20 11:46:03 UTC, `council`; `limit` from 13:40:51, `council` 13:43:53, `limit` from 16:29:22) are simulated again, oldest first, at most 400 a run, their gates decided again; each row's earlier v3 columns and `omega_json` are kept in `omega_json.prev` (never overwritten; a row with `prev` is never taken again). The bars add the D1 1m archive before the KV 1m series. The marker keeps `{ at, from, rows, before, after }` (closed rows and their sized R).
      - **Parity with live (`parity_v3` in `GET /api/ledger`, `v3LiveParity`).** Per UTC day of the decision: v3 traded (open or closed) against an Omega entry filled live for it (`omega_events` `limit_filled` by `signal_issued_ms`, or a done `open:` intent with a ticket): decisions, agree, both, v3 only, live only, the share. On 2026-11-23 v3 agreed with live on 4 of 15 decisions before V3L and on 14 of 15 after (the one left: the 14:15 BUY live skipped behind the 14:00 BUY's resting limit, the SS1 case before SS1).
  - **Omega policy v4 (owner 2026-11-20: "pastikan omega performance sama dengan omega yang live sekarang"; `src/ledger-omega4.mjs`, version `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v4`).** v3 above stays as it is beside it. v4 replays the live engine itself, so it cannot drift from live trading:
    - **One account, minute by minute, on the feed's 1m bars**, over every Decision Engine BUY/SELL: also the ones the ledger's own no-stacking rule held back, because Omega's own gates decide there (on 2026-11-19, 5 of 11 live trades had no ledger row).
    - **The live functions.** The signal is `eaSignalFromDecision` and `managementFields` on the decision current then (basis 0, the key levels on the feed's scale), with `applyGridChoice`, `applyRangeMode` and `applyArmGate`. `planIntents` runs at each minute's open with a synthetic executor that passes the live gates honestly: connected and fresh, gold in USD (contract 100, tick 0.01 worth USD 1), OmegaExecutor 1.20, the fixed 0.2 spread, the M15 ATR(14) with its forming bar, the positions with their open intent ids and opening times.
    - **Applied intents.** The plan's `close`, `close_all`, `modify_sl`, `modify_tp`, `modify_sltp`, `partial_close` and `open` are applied to the simulated positions, so the order is the live one: kill, the opposite close, the strategy exits, the Position Manager, the key levels, the TP ladder and its lock, the partials, the entry gates, `reentryGate`, then the sizing within the profile.
    - **Replay state.** `recentIntentIds` are the replay's own intent ids (partial counts, `pl:`/`ps:`, dedupe), `stops` come from its own modifications (`commandedStops`), `reentry` from its own `pm_take_profit_weakening` and `pm_cut_loss` exits, and `candles` from `lastClosedCandle` over the last 125 1m bars. The 5m fields come from the pull adapter's `structureOf` on 5m bars of the feed.
    - **Position Manager.** `positionFacts` and `positionAction` run 3 minutes past each 5m close (live: about 2.5), with `quorum_prev` as `latestDecisions` builds it. The council is the recorded verdict for that position and 5m bar where the live account had one (`position_actions.council`, mapped through the open intent id). Otherwise there is none: the mechanical fallback (the locks apply; nothing is cut or closed on weakening). The 1m fast-protect layer (PM-1M) runs at every minute with a position, after it, on v4's 1m bars (`fastProtect`, as the sync does on the broker's; no reconnect quarantine or spread-spike hold in v4). The 1m re-entry (RE1) is checked at every minute after a profit-lock stop's exit (`reentryCheck`), its re-entry a trade of its own (`re:<decision key>`).
    - **Broker.** The stop and the target are hit at the broker inside each minute: a gap at the open exits at the open, within the minute the stop comes first, and a SELL closes at the ask.
    - **Account.** The live USD account's profile in force then (`omega_profile_changes`), and its balance rebuilt backward from `omega_accounts.last_balance` and its deals. Without a live account: the current profile and a USD 1000 start. The settings are those stored now, with the engine switch and the live arm taken as on. The EA control comes from `ea_control_events`; the news blackout from the cached week's calendar.
    - **Unit.** R per trade: the realised P/L over the risk at the open (|entry − stop| × the volume at the open); partials count their share. `omega4_size` is 1. The lot's risk as a share of the equity is kept in `omega4_json` (`risk_pct`).
    - **Not reconstructable (said in `rules.omega4`).**
      - The strategy arm and the grid's choices are applied as they are now.
      - The range strategies' cell gate is not stored, so their decisions are not traded (`cell_gate_unreconstructable`; no probation trade).
      - The broker's 5m Prophet confirmations are not stored: `m5_ready` is false, so there is no `opposite_5m`, and a market entry still waiting for its confirmation is not traded (`entry_trigger_unreconstructable`).
      - The owner's daily-loss resets and deposits are not told apart from P/L.
      - `EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED` is read as never off.
    - **Run.** In the 5-minute ledger step after v3 (`runOmega4Step`, loaded on demand; `LEDGER_OMEGA4` "off" stops it and the archive below). Each run goes on from the checkpoint (KV `ledger:omega4:checkpoint:v1`): the whole replay state at its next minute (the open positions and their trades, the recent intent ids, the re-entry exit, the gate notes, the synthetic balance, the reach). So runs resumed from it give exactly the records of one long run (test).
      - **Bound:** at most `MAX_REPLAY_MINUTES` (720) replay minutes a run. A longer catch-up, such as the first run over about three days, goes on at the next runs.
      - Only the decisions whose entry window falls in the run's span are written.
      - Each run returns its work for the `ledger_omega4` log line: the decisions seen, the minutes replayed, the `planIntents` and `positionAction` calls, and the wall time.
    - **The 1m archive (D1 `ledger_bars_1m`: `minute_ms`, o, h, l, c).** Each run first appends the closed feed 1m minutes newer than the last archived one, in batches (about 1,440 rows a day, kept 120 days). The replay reads the KV series and, where it does not reach back to the checkpoint less the 4-hour warm-up, the archive, merged one bar a minute. Only when no bar at all lies before the checkpoint is a trade still open there closed as `data_gap` at its last R, and the replay restarted.
    - **Cost.** Measured in node on two days of 1m bars (3,180 bars, 128 signals): about 1.1 to 1.4 ms a signal, 140 to 180 ms in all. A 5-minute run replays about five minutes.
    - **Storage.** D1 `ledger_omega4` holds one row per decision: `omega4_status` (pending, open, closed, skipped), `omega4_gate`, `omega4_filled_at`, `omega4_closed_at`, `omega4_exit`, `r_omega4`, `omega4_size`, `omega4_json` (no price). The same columns sit on the ledger's own rows, so v3 and v4 pair on the same signals.
    - **Exits:** `target`, `trailed_target`, `stop`, `sl_plus`, `pm_take_profit_weakening`, `pm_cut_loss`, `opposite`, `mean_reversal`, `lean_flip`, `confidence_drop`, `power_reversal`, `opposite_5m`, `kill`, `data_gap`.
    - **Parity with live (`omegaParity`, `runOmegaParity`; at most once an hour from the ledger step; KV `ledger:omega4:parity:v1`).**
      - Omega's real trades on the live USD account since 2026-11-19 12:00 UTC: `ea_deals` with source `omega`, currency USD and mode not `demo`, grouped per position with partial exits summed. Each is joined to its open intent (entry, stop, volume, `r_dist`, `signal_issued_ms`, through `json_extract(result, '$.ticket')`) and to its close intent's reason.
      - Live R = the P/L over `r_dist` / 0.01 × USD 1 × the open volume (gold in USD).
      - Each live trade is matched to its v4 row by `decisionCloseMs(key)` = `signal_issued_ms` and side. Each row gives: matched or not, the entry time apart, the exit live and in v4, R live and in v4, ΔR and the cause.
      - A v4 trade with no live trade counts too.
      - Exit table: a deal `tp` is `target` (v4's `trailed_target` counts as `target`); `sl` is `sl_plus` when the deal's stop is at or past the entry, else `stop`; `so` is `stop_out`; `expert` is Omega's close intent's reason (`pm_take_profit_weakening`, `pm_cut_loss`, `opposite`, …); a hand close is `manual`.
      - **Counted:** only the live trades opened in the current era of the engine's behaviour (below), and v4 trades of that era that live did not take. Earlier trades are listed, marked `live_code: "earlier"` and summarised apart (`earlier`), but not counted.
      - **Status:** `insufficient` below `PARITY_MIN_TRADES` (20) live trades in the era, never `pass`. Otherwise `pass` with the same exit on at least 90 % of the trades compared and a mean |ΔR| of at most 0.1 over the closed pairs, else `fail`.
      - **Era (KV `ledger:omega4:era:v1`: `{ behaviour_hash, code_hash, switches, since_ms, commit, reason, carried, previous }`, exposed as `parity.era`: since, commit, reason, the 12-character hashes, the switches, the carries and the last 3 eras before).**
        - **Identity:** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` and the switch signature. A new era starts only when one of them changes.
        - `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` is the SHA-256 of the engine's outputs over the committed fixtures, every switch at its default (`OMEGA_DEFAULTS`): `planIntents` over the market-path fixtures (`test/fixtures/omega-market-path.mjs`: hand inputs, v4 replay traces and records); `positionFacts`, `positionAction`, the council's messages, schema and cleaning, `positionManagerIntents` and `planIntents` with PM actions over the A8 switch-off fixtures (`test/fixtures/pm-a8-off.mjs`); `replayOmega4` traces and records over the v4 replay fixtures (`test/fixtures/omega-v4-replay.mjs`). Outputs only, never inputs. The text is built by `test/fixtures/engine-behaviour.mjs` `engineBehaviourSource()`, which only tests import. `test/ledger-omega-v4.test.mjs` V14 recomputes it and fails, printing the new value.
        - **Switch signature:** the `ERA_SWITCHES` (`entry_order`, `pm_loosen_enabled`, `positioning_enabled`, `liquidity_enabled`, `profit_lock_gate`, `change_thresholds`, `pm_fast_1m`, `reentry_1m`, `limit_supersede`, `cd_gate`; next `wait_bias_entry`) off their default in the stored settings (`omega_settings` id 1), sorted (`eraSwitches`: `entry_order=council`, `pm_loosen_enabled`). Read from `env.DB` at each check; unreadable keeps the stored signature.
        - **Behaviour change:** a new era at the deploy (`DEPLOYED_AT`, else the version metadata's timestamp, else now), reason `behaviour change`.
        - **Switch change** (on, another value, or back to default): a new era at the settings' `updated_ms` (any later settings write moves it later, never earlier), else now; reason `switch on: entry_order=council` or `switch off: …`. Both at once: the later start.
        - **Source-only change** (another `ENGINE_CODE_HASH`, the same behaviour and switches): the era carries over with the same `since_ms`; `code_hash` is recorded and the carry kept in `carried` (last 10). The first check logs `era carried (behaviour identical)` in `era_events` of the `ledger_omega4` log line.
        - **Migration:** a stored era of the old shape (`{ hash, since_ms }`) is adopted with its `since_ms` when its hash is in `ERA_ADOPTABLE_CODE_HASHES` (`7497aa51…` stage 3, `bbff5fad…` A8; S12 and A8-2 prove their switch-off behaviour identical). `code_hash` comes from `hash`, the behaviour hash and switches are recorded; then the rules above apply. Production's stage 3 era (since 2026-11-20 08:05 UTC, switches at default) is kept. Any other old hash starts a new era at the deploy.
        - `ENGINE_CODE` lists the live functions and constants that decide Omega's entries and management: `planIntents`, `manageIntents`, `keyLevelPlan`, `ladderPlan`, `ladderTp1Locked`, `strategyExits`, `positionManagerIntents`, `reentryGate`, `positionFacts`, `positionAction`, `eaSignal`, `eaSignalFromDecision`, `managementFields`, `runPositionManager`, the sync's inputs, `effectiveLimits`, the gold checks, and the grid, range, arm and confidence helpers.
        - `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` is the SHA-256 of their source text and values, as the repository has them. Informational: recorded in the era as `code_hash`.
        - It is a committed constant, recomputed in node by `test/ledger-omega-v4.test.mjs`. That test fails, and prints the new value, until a change to any of them updates it. It is not taken inside the Worker, because the bundle strips comments and renames identifiers when other modules change (`finite` becomes `finite8`, checked with `wrangler deploy --dry-run --outdir`), so a runtime hash would move with unrelated code.
        - With no stored era, the first is seeded from 2026-11-19 17:48 UTC (after #491).
        - The deploy time is the Worker's `DEPLOYED_AT` (as `/health` reports it, with `DEPLOY_COMMIT`). The last 10 eras are kept.
        - A change confined to a private helper of those modules (`pickSettings`, `roundTo`) is not in the hash; it needs the hash bumped by hand.
        - Stage 3 of limit entries (2026-11-20) adds `limitPlacement`, `limitActionIntents`, `fillGuard`, the knobs (`src/omega-limit-knobs.mjs`), the limit council's rules (`src/limit-council.mjs`) and the sync's `limitInputs`; its hash started the era production holds.
        - ADR-0020 Amendment 8 (2026-11-20) adds `a8Intent`, `loosenRecords`, the Position Manager's `ruleAction`, `a8Action`, `loosenHold`, `loosenPlan`, `targetPlan`, `loosenFacts`, `loosenInputs` and `cleanCouncilA8`, the knobs (`src/pm-loosen-knobs.mjs`) and the sync's `pmLoosenInputs`; its code hash carries the stage 3 era over (behaviour identical with the switch off).
      - No price, ticket or account in the result.
  - **"Omega with limit entry" (docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md §8, stage 2, measurement only; owner 2026-11-20; `src/ledger-omega-limit.mjs`, version `cryptophys.ledger-omega-limit.v1`).** The maker counterfactual beside v4 (the taker) on the same Decision Engine signals. No live behaviour changes: `LIMIT_ENTRY_AVAILABLE` stays false (stage 3 flips it).
    - **v4's machinery, not a copy.** `replayOmegaLimit` is `replayOmega4` with an entry policy (`entry`): the same simulated account, bars, decisions, gates and management. Without one, `replayOmega4` is v4 exactly (checked byte for byte against the code before the hooks; `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` unchanged, no new parity era). With one, planIntents' market opens are never applied.
    - **Entry.** A resting limit at the decision's level (`signal.entry`, feed scale, basis 0), placed at the first minute planIntents' own gates pass for the signal: a probe call of `planIntents` on the same inputs with the symbol priced at the level, so the lot is sized as if filled at the limit (risk_pct, the profile caps, the open-risk cap, the daily budget, the adaptive stop). It fills when a 1m bar's range reaches it: a BUY when the ask's low (bid low + the 0.2 spread) does, a SELL when the bid's high does; a gap through it at a minute's open fills at the open with the stop and target where they were placed. In the fill's own minute only the stop counts (the order of the prices is unknown); from the next minute it is a v4 trade (planIntents, the TP ladder, the Position Manager).
    - **Missed (`limit_status` missed, `limit_gate`):** at placement `limit_through_price` (price already through the level: a missed entry, never a market fallback) and `limit_out_of_band` (further than `placement_band` × the plan's stop distance from price); while it rests, checked at each minute's open in this order: `kill`, `paused`, `blackout`, `daily_loss_limit` (the profile's daily loss floor), `profile_downgrade`, `pending_expired` (`max_pending_life` minutes from the placement), `decision_against` (a later decision on the other side), `quorum_share_drop` (its side's `quorum.long_share` or `short_share` fell by more than `cancel_share_drop` from the placing decision; with CH1's token `limit`, by at least it, outside scalp on the decision before too, as live). One resting order at a time: another signal meanwhile is `pending_order_exists`.
    - **Knobs per mode** (the RFC's start values, clamped to ADR-0020 A7's bounds, `limitKnobs`): `max_pending_life` 30/60/240/60 minutes (scalp/intraday/swing/range), `move_max_atr` 0.25/0.5/0.5/0.25 (recorded; no council moves in a replay), `cancel_share_drop` 0.05 (with CH1's token `limit`, the default, 0.10/0.10/0.15/0.10; bound 0.03–0.20), `placement_band` 0.35. No reader of the platform's `omega.limit_*` facts exists yet (the doctrine consumer reads rules, not bounds), so the start values hold (`knobs_source: "start_values"`).
    - **Not modelled:** the council's place, keep and move; the Decision Engine veto (`de_veto`) and disarm (the replay keeps the arm on, as v4); the fill-time re-check of the hard guards (the daily loss floor is checked every minute while it rests).
    - **EZ1b's switch (2026-11-23, found by V3L).** v4's `DECISION_SQL` has no `entry_zone_mode`, so the counterfactual never rested a limit at the zone's near edge. It now reads the decisions with `LIMIT_DECISION_SQL` (v4's query plus that column) and, when it reuses v4's decisions in the steady state, joins the column from `LIMIT_ZONE_MODE_SQL` (`withZoneModes`). v4's own `DECISION_SQL` and records are unchanged. Records already written are not replayed again; the change applies from the next placements.
    - **Storage.** The columns `limit_status` (pending, skipped, resting, missed, open, closed), `limit_gate`, `limit_filled_at`, `limit_closed_at`, `limit_exit`, `r_limit`, `limit_json` (knobs, placement time, wait in minutes, distance from price in R; no price) and `regime` (the decision's 1H regime) on v4's own `ledger_omega4` row of each decision (added in place by `ensureLedgerSchema`; v4's upsert never touches them), so maker and taker pair on one row. KV `ledger:omega-limit:checkpoint:v1` holds the replay state with the resting order.
    - **Pairing (`makerTaker`).** Per decision once both sides are final and at least one traded: maker R (`r_limit`, 0 when missed or skipped) against taker R (`r_omega4`, 0 when skipped), ΔR and which did better. `limitStats`: fill rate = filled ÷ (filled + missed), missed by reason, the pairs (n, both traded, maker better, taker better, even, mean ΔR).
    - **Run.** In the same 5-minute ledger step after v4 (`runOmega4Step`; `LEDGER_OMEGA_LIMIT` "off" stops it, `LEDGER_OMEGA4` "off" stops both). It never replays past the minute v4 has replayed, and only the minutes v4 left of `MAX_REPLAY_MINUTES` (720) that run, so the run's bound is unchanged. In the steady state it reuses the bars, decisions and context v4 loaded (no second read). Measured in node on two days of 1m bars (3,180 bars, 128 signals): about 0.8 to 1.5 ms a signal beside v4's 1.0 to 1.8 ms (it makes fewer main planIntents calls and one probe per entry minute until it places).
    - **Read:** `GET /api/ledger` carries `limit` beside `omega4` in each block and `by_mode` (its performance, fill rate, missed by reason, skips, exits, the pairs) and `rules.limit`. Nexus shows the "Maker vs taker" panel on the Performance page (per mode: fill rate, maker R per trade, the paired R maker against taker, mean ΔR, maker better, taker better, missed by reason).
    - **To Aether:** the sweep builds `cryptophys.execution-report.v1` (`executionReport`, 30 days: per mode the plan's and Omega's win rate, expectancy, total R, profit factor and drawdown, the paired comparison and the gates that skipped; per trade the bar open, session, mode, side, R and exit, never a price, level or size; since 2026-11-20 an added top-level `limit` block (schema kept at v1; Aether's validator allows extra top-level fields) per mode and regime: fill rate, missed by reason, maker and taker R per trade, profit factor and total R, the pairs with n, maker better, taker better and mean ΔR, R and counts only, no price key at any depth) and sends it to `KnowledgeIngress.ingestExecutionReport` when its content changed (D1 `aether_execution_report_pushes`, one row; `rejected_streak` and `rejected_since` count the refusals in a row, and two in a row, or one older than 24 hours, are the health alert `aether_execution_rejected`). Aether's curator turns a measured gap into a knowledge item. Aether's validator (cryptophys-aether `src/execution.mjs`) accepts any well-formed mode name and exit codes with digits since aether #38 (2026-11-20); before it, it refused every report since range mode was added (18 to 20 Nov).
  - **Live setups (owner 2026-11-12):** `activeSetups` returns the `decision` rows still pending or open, and those ended within 2 hours, as setups: the levels locked at the decision that opened them (later decisions never move them: a same-side decision confirms the setup, an opposite one is counted against it), the lifecycle with times (signal, entry, SL+, TP1 from `tp1_closed_at`, TP2 from `tp2_closed_at` and `tp2_exit_reason`, invalidation, or cancellation and its reason), the R so far and Omega's trade of it. `GET /api/conclusion/global` carries them as `setups`; the Decision Engine card in Nexus shows them, and shows the decision's own plan ladder only when no live setup of its side holds locked levels.
  - **Run:** on its own cron every 5 minutes, 2 minutes past each 5m close (`LEDGER_CRON` `2-59/5 * * * *`, `runLedgerStep` in `src/index.ts`; owner 2026-11-12): new signals, the simulation, the Telegram alerts and the execution report to Aether. It left the 15-minute parity sweep, so an event reaches Telegram within about 5 to 10 minutes (the 1m series is refreshed every 5 minutes).
  - **Read:** `GET /api/ledger?days=30` returns per source and in total: win rate, expectancy, total R, profit factor, maximum drawdown, fill rate, the equity curve and the signals, with the Omega policy (`omega`, v3) and `omega4` (v4: its performance, exits, skips, the decisions the ledger held back that it traded, and `paired` with v3) beside TP1 and TP2, `by_mode` (scalp, intraday, swing, range), `rules.omega4` (the approximations), `parity` (the latest v4 parity check, per trade in R) and `parity_v3` (V3L: v3's trade/no-trade against the live account per UTC day). Nexus shows it on the Performance page (the "Omega policy (v3)" and "Omega v4 (live engine)" targets, the per-mode table with v4 and v4 against v3, a v4 column, and the "Omega v4 Parity with Live" panel).
  - **Alerts (backlog 11.2, owner decision 2026-11-08: Telegram; `src/ledger-alerts.mjs`, D1 `ledger_alerts`, migration 0017).** After each ledger run the sweep sends each new ledger event once over the existing Telegram bot (`TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`, `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`):
    - `setup`: a strategy-engine setup triggered (the H1 decision already has its own Telegram message);
    - `entry`: the entry was reached;
    - `exit`: TP1, the stop or the time limit was reached (with the R result);
    - `closed`: the TP2 part of a two-target setup ended;
    - `cancelled`: a setup that will no longer fill (invalidated, target before entry, or unfilled).
    - Each message ends with "Advisory only: no order is placed and no position size is given." It never gives a lot size or a position.
    - The first run records the events that already exist without sending them, so a deploy never floods the chat. At most 5 messages a run; a failed send is retried by the next run. Only signals opened in the last 7 days are looked at.
    - Off with `LEDGER_ALERTS_ENABLED = "false"`; nothing is sent without the bot secrets.
  - **The H1 decision message (owner decision 2026-11-08, `src/h1-telegram.mjs`):** sent only for a BUY or SELL, or when the decision changes from the previous bar's; a WAIT after a WAIT is not sent. The previous decision is kept in `SIGNAL_CACHE` (`telegram:h1:last-decision:v1`), so the same bar is never sent twice. `H1_TELEGRAM_MODE = "all"` restores one message per bar.
    - **Read:** `GET /api/alerts?days=7` (read-only; never sends and never creates the table): `enabled`, `switch_on`, `bot_configured` (a boolean, never a secret value), `started`, `pending` (the events the next sweep would send, with their text) and `recorded`. Nexus shows it on the Alerts page.
- **Dismantled agents (owner 2026-11-11: "hardening juga untuk dismantle dan delete agent yang sudah tidak berkontribusi"; "dismantle sekarang").** R37 Funnel, GABFX Price Action, Playbook Execution and Grid Bot Protocol no longer speak in any room (15m War Room, 1H/4H/D1 rooms, HUD threads): over 2026-11-08 to 11 their 15m openings were NEUTRAL in 87-97 % of about 190 openings, and none ever took a BUY or SELL. Their manifests carry `dismantled` and `rooms: []`; `hudAgentsIn(room)` (src/hud-reading.mjs) builds each room's HUD roster from the manifests. Their HUD sections are still computed in the snapshot (the R37 funnel stays a confirmation source of the 1H/4H/D1 rooms). The quorum is counted over the agents who speak, so these four no longer dilute it. Their platform identities were deleted by cryptophys-cloud-platform migration 0047.
- **Workers AI usage and the room memo (owner 2026-11-11: "kurangi neuron/hari tanpa mengurangi kapabilitas agent"; `src/ai-usage.mjs`, `src/room-memo.mjs`, D1 `ai_usage_daily` and `room_memo`, migration 0018).**
  - Every Workers AI call (rooms, News, Decision Engine council, H1 chain) adds its input and output tokens to `ai_usage_daily` per day, source (the request's schema name; the hourly news chain's stages `news_analyst` / `news_counselor` / `news_reasoner` (named `h1_*` until 2026-11-15); the H1 Reasoner `h1_reasoner`; the `/v1/analyze` bundle path was removed on 2026-11-15) and model, with failed calls and calls abandoned at their timeout (Workers AI still bills a call that finishes; its tokens land when it does). Neurons are estimated from the published per-model rates.
  - Room memo: a War Room, 1H/4H/D1 room or HUD thread step whose whole request (primary model, schema, messages) equals a stored answer of the primary model has that answer. With `ROOM_MEMO_ENABLED = "true"` it is reused after this room's validation passes again, and no model call is made (the step is marked `reused`); `"false"` only counts the repeats (`memo_hits`) and whether the model then gave the same stance and bias (`memo_same`). Production runs `"true"` since 2026-11-12 (owner's Tahap 1: reuse once the shadow shows `memo_same / memo_hits` of at least 0.9 on `war_room_opening` and `tf_room_opening`; the 24 h before the switch measured 436/444 = 0.98 and 103/103 = 1.00). Answers expire after 24 hours; fallback answers and answers with inferred metrics are never stored.
  - **Read:** `GET /api/ai-usage?days=2`: per day calls, tokens, estimated neurons, the estimate above the 10,000 free neurons a day, and the memo counters, per source and model.
- **Nexus UI (owner decision 2026-11-06).** Nexus uses the obsidian design of the Cerebrum Console (`cryptophys-cloud-platform` `workers/console/ui`), because it will merge into the Console control panel. The `nexus` subdomain will later replace `home`.
  - **Stack:** the same as the Console: React 18, Vite, Tailwind with the obsidian palette, lucide-react, framer-motion, and three.js for the neural-mesh background. The chart uses lightweight-charts. Its licence's attribution: every chart (War Room, Liquidity, Chart patterns, Opening range, Omega) sets `attributionLogo: false` (the library's logo injects a `<style>` the CSP refuses) and shows a quiet "Charts by TradingView" link to tradingview.com in its caption (`ChartCredit` in `components/ui.tsx`; test/nexus-chart-attribution.test.mjs).
  - **Aether page** (owner 2026-11-09: `nexus.cryptophys.work/aether`, behind the same Cloudflare Access sign-in): the canon and its doctrine, the decision cards and their ingest, the AI budget and vector indexes, the room quorum weighting of each timeframe (active point, regimes, agent weights, candidates, reverse shadow, history), the guardian's compliance findings of the last 24 hours and the knowledge proposals. Read-only, from `GET /api/aether/overview` (Service Binding `AETHER` to `cryptophys-aether`, entrypoint `AetherReader`). The app reads its page from the path when there is no hash, so `/aether` and `/#/aether` both open it.
  - **End-to-end sync** (hardening 2026-11-09, `src/aether-sync.mjs`): the AI Worker reads Aether's `AetherReader.sync()` every 15 minutes through a second, read-only binding `AETHER_READER` and adds this side's checks: Aether reachable, the KB v4 hash of Aether's canon equal to this repository's (`kb_match`; a KB change here needs a re-import there) and its own card push within 2 hours. Aether's checks are card ingest, the canon index, the embedding backlog, each timeframe's weighting report and the AI budget; each is ok, warn, or idle while the market was closed. Every warning is a health alert (`aether_sync:<check>`, the Priority Queue and a kernel report); `GET /api/aether/status` carries the combined status, and the Aether page shows both. The Nexus deploy smoke test reads `/api/aether/overview` (canon, sync checks, no price field).
  - **Build and serving:** the source is in `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui`. It is built into `ui/dist` and served as Workers Static Assets (`run_worker_first`). The deploy workflow builds it; `validate-nexus-dashboard.yml` builds it on pull requests, then runs the page render check (`ui/scripts/render-check.mjs`, `npm run render-check`, Track C1): each pure page view of `ui/scripts/render-entry.tsx` rendered (react-dom/server, an esbuild CommonJS bundle with lightweight-charts stubbed) over its fixture in `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/`, the not_found bodies, each path nulled and each array emptied (at most 600 variants a view) fails on a throw, "NaN", "undefined" or "[object Object]"; `--self-test` must fail.
  - **Layout:**
    - Same header, glass panels and status footer as the Console.
    - **Left sidebar (owner request 2026-11-07):** navigation grouped by analytic layer. Live: War Room, System status (`#/status`: the Workers and lanes (with the TradingView alerts lane: last accepted alert and the last hour's 401s, `ai_worker.webhook` of `/api/status`), the Priority Queue, the pull adapter series and budget, and the parity registry and recent comparisons from `GET /api/parity`; the War Room shows a System issues panel only when a lane is amber or red), Data feeds (`#/feeds`, F4, owner 2026-11-27 "tempatkan di nexus > live": the Omega overview's `broker.feeds` per data-only feed bridge, fresh or stale, terminal, server, symbol, login status and version, the last bar's age (`last_bar_age_ms`, "—" when none), a warning for a login sealed to an old key and, OS1, a bars-stopped warning when the server flags `content_stale` (remedy: re-enter the feed login); for the owner (`viewer.can_control`) a login form sealed in the browser with the Omega page's own `buildEnvelope` to that feed's key, then `POST /omega/api/feed-connection`, and a disconnect with a required reason, `DELETE /omega/api/feed-connection`; the password is never state, stored or logged and every field is cleared after a submit). Analytics: Performance (`#/performance`), Scorecards, Shadow versions. Market: Economic calendar, Correlations. Intelligence: Aether, Model & Learning (`#/model`, owner 2026-11-13: "pastikan semua ada visualisasi": the learning loop in four steps with their live state, the dataset (rows by label status, outcomes per side in R), the baseline with its council gate (connected or not, why, the rules) and its walk-forward and prospective scores per model with the progress toward 300 decisions, the decision model (LightGBM, shadow) the same way, and the knowledge notes that went live or retired by themselves), Opening range (`#/orb`, Track C4, directly after Strategy agents: the London and New York opening ranges and the orb_breakout agent's plans, `pages/Orb.tsx`, read-only, no arm control (arming stays on Strategy agents); GET only, from its allow-list `lib/intel.ts` `ORB_READS`, one poll each: `/api/orb` 60 s (B14: everything it carries is read from it: the sessions, the plans, the records, the recent setups, the edges in key zones and the decision's `session_range`), `/api/strategy-agents` 120 s (orb_breakout's status only), `/api/engine/decision` 60 s (`armed.ids` and `selection.candidates` only), `/api/ohlcv?timeframe=M15&limit=200` as a chart read (60 s). The heading's chips: the agent's status, "armed" (ok) or "not armed: plans are recorded, not traded" (another agent armed) or "no strategy armed: the best plan of all is picked" (none armed), ORB levels on or off. The Sessions panel: a card per session (the range and the window's end in New York time, then UTC and GMT+7; the phase in words, "building 2 of 3 bars", "window · 9 bars left", "ended"; the status as a chip in words, "clock mismatch (New York on summer time, London not)", "range event (high-impact USD 08:30 NY)", its `status_detail` in the ⓘ; high, low, mid on the feed scale and the width in ATR (1H) on a 0 to 4 gauge with the 0.25 to 3.5 band; the breakout with its bars since and its state; a plan per side, Entry "at price" or "limit at the edge", Stop, TP1, TP2, R:R at TP1 and the lapse in New York time, or why there is none: invalidated (grey), stands aside, no breakout on that side, not traded with its reason), the overlap line "New York holds the long side" while both windows are live, "No ORB setup yet" while no session has a plan, and "Market closed" with the next range's set time (`market.next_range`). The chart (`components/IntelChart.tsx` with `lib/boxes-primitive.ts`): each formed range a box from its first to its last bar (fg-2 outline, track fill), its edges dashed to the window's last bar or the last candle (the label carries the window's end time), the broken edge in its breakout colour (grey once invalidated), the midpoint dotted, the breakout bar a circle, and one plan's lines (the picked one, else the overlap holder's) through IntelChart's opt-in `plan` with `planMark`; none without a plan. Records per session (setups, fill and target rates, mean R, held back by the breaker), Recent sessions (the agent's last 10 setups: date in New York, session, side, entry, the control arm's exit in words and R) and Where it counted (the selection's candidates: confidence, confluence, score, R:R, eligible with why not, chosen; the edges in key zones; the decision's `session_range`). Rates, outcomes and R neutral; every price on the feed scale; explanations in ⓘ only. `ORB` mode off reads "Opening range off"; `INTEL_VIEWS` off reads intel_views_off; "/api/orb 500 orb_unavailable" through the shared error box; the deploy smoke checks the route, its 404 off state a pass), Chart patterns (`#/patterns`, Track C3, directly after Opening range: the Projection Agent's chart patterns, their measured record, its seat and the pattern agents, `pages/Patterns.tsx`, read-only; GET only, from its allow-list `lib/intel.ts` `CHART_PATTERNS_READS`, one poll each: `/api/chart-patterns` 120 s (its own 60 closed bars per chart draw the candles and its `decision.trade_agent` marks the armed plan: no `/api/ohlcv` or `/api/engine/decision` read), `/api/strategy-agents` 120 s, `/api/neuron-allocation` 300 s. Tabs 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D (B9's "daily"); the lines on the chart (`components/IntelChart.tsx` with `lib/lines-primitive.ts`: each pattern's two lines from `line_from` to `line_to`, each close time snapped to its candle, forming dashed amber, busted grey; its measured target dotted, "measured target · <name>"); a plan's lines (its EZ1 entry zone, Entry, Stop, TP1, TP2, each with its price tag) only on the chart of the plan's own pattern, through IntelChart's opt-in `plan` (by default no order line: the Liquidity page passes none), the other charts pointing to it; "TP2" only on `plan.tp2`; a plan's notes worded from its family's rule (`lib/patternRules.ts`, equal to `PATTERN_FAMILIES`); the measured targets of every chart; the patterns of the chart (bars since the last turn, the measured target with its ATR(1H) distance, the record here and pooled, the key zone holding the pattern, the plan); the measured record per chart; the Projection Agent's seat per room (hit rate against 45% and 50%, 60 scored); the pattern agents (target and stop rates over filled setups, armed, held-back patterns). Every price on the feed scale. A field a later backend PR fills (`held`, `breakout_bars_ago`, `bars_since_breakout`, `horizon_bars`, `bars_left`, `seat.horizon_bars`, `plan.reach`) shows an Empty "Filled by a later backend PR."; `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` off reads "Chart patterns off"; `INTEL_VIEWS` off reads intel_views_off; the deploy smoke checks the route), Liquidity (`#/liquidity`, Track C2, after Chart patterns: lens B of the Positioning & Liquidity analyst on its own page, `pages/Liquidity.tsx`, read-only; GET only, from its allow-list `lib/intel.ts` `LIQUIDITY_READS`, one poll each: `/api/liquidity` 120 s, `/api/lens` 120 s, `/api/ohlcv?timeframe=M15&limit=200` and `/api/hud/levels?tf=15` as chart reads (60 s, 300 s), `/api/engine/decision` 120 s. Eight panels: the analyst (`LensView`), the score against the floor by kind, the levels on the feed's 15m chart (`components/IntelChart.tsx`, the bands through `lib/zones-primitive.ts` from `*_feed` only and none while the broker-to-feed basis is unknown, labelled on the broker scale; the chart plane's whale zones and FVG on a toggle; today's management levels "management only, never an entry"), the facts of the last 24 hours, the sweeps and their outcomes, the baseline per New York hour of the week, what lens B changed in Omega, and the LIQUIDITY card with the quorum with liquidity (B15: the card's own weight with its rule in words, its lean toned by `side`, the sweep, the `lean_reason` in short words with the code beside it, "never counts toward the quorum" in words, its times in UTC and local, `stale` in the late tone; the quorum with liquidity marked informational, read by no gate; both null when the latest decision has no `decision.liquidity`, one Empty saying why). Every price carries its scale; no order line or order price. Lens B's conditions (B16, `conditions`) sit in the facts panel: spread, depth and activity in words with their ratio or percentile (spread widened in the fault tone, as Omega's entry gate refuses at that ratio; thin and a spike late), the session and the levels in one line each, the mode, baseline and time once (`stale` late with its age), the mode's thresholds in the chart's legend and the spread's widen line on its axis (only from `spread.widen`, `activity.spike_pctl`, `depth.thin_pctl`), and the words the 15m and 1H rooms read (`line`), with one note when they are not reading them (`in_rooms` false, `room_line` brief); null: an Empty. The level moves (B19, `effects.level_moves`, LM1) are a table grouped by action (ladder lock, key level lock) with each move's time in New York, UTC and GMT+7, its side (▲ long, ▼ short in the direction colours), its mode and its outcome in a neutral chip, no price, and one ⓘ with the scoring's numbers (8 closed 15m bars, 0.1 × ATR15); null (`LENS_LEVEL_READINGS` off, or before B19) shows an Empty; `INTEL_VIEWS` off reads "The Intelligence views are off on the AI Worker."; the deploy smoke checks the route), Zones (`#/zones`, ZN1, after Liquidity: Z5's demand and supply zones, `pages/Zones.tsx` and `lib/zones.ts`, read-only; GET only, from its allow-list `lib/intel.ts` `ZONES_READS`, one poll each at 120 s: `/api/zones` and the read-only `/api/feature-switches`, whose `zone_hold` gives a hold's since and until while `breaker.held` has none (none shown without either). The zone breaker (one cell per holdable rule, clear ok, held late with its `by`; the typing, block and rule checks), the readings by type x approach x strength, `zone_blocked` per side, the rule arms, the stances of 7 days and Omega's attribution; rates, outcomes and R neutral, no price; `ZONE_READINGS` off reads its 404 as the off state; the deploy smoke checks the route), Decision audit (`#/audit`), Alerts (`#/alerts`). The Omega page's Signal panel shows "Omega right now": the planner's current notes (`planner` in the broker overview) as plain sentences; the Performance page lists the decisions the ledger's no-stacking rule held back (D1 `signal_ledger_held`, `/api/ledger` `held`). The Decision Engine card shows the model's advice to the council when it is connected (long and short chance against the base rates, its record) and says "not connected yet" otherwise, and the calendar line shows the CALENDAR witness's phase and the learned pre-release drift. The Decision audit shows the decision card of each room conclusion (15m, 1H, 4H, D1: final stances, opening claims, crowd line, rule breaks, outcomes; no levels), the rule breaks of the last 7 days (`/api/compliance`) and the push status to Aether (`/api/aether/status`). The Alerts page shows the Telegram alert state (`/api/alerts`): whether the bot is configured, the events the next sweep would send, and the recorded ones. The Economic calendar (`#/calendar`) shows the week's events: by default only the High-impact USD ones, with a switch for all. Events inside the gate window are highlighted.
    - **Correlations (`#/correlations`, 2026-11-08).** `GET /api/correlations` (`src/correlations.mjs`) shows gold beside DXY, the US 10-year yield and silver on closed 4h bars:
      - the last close and the change over 4h, 1 day and 5 days (basis points for the yield);
      - the Pearson correlation of 4h returns with gold over 30 bars (about a week) and 120 bars (about a month);
      - each series is as-of aligned to gold's 4h closes.

      It is context only and reaches no room or decision. On phones the sidebar opens from the menu button.
    - Left: Priority Queue, War Room agents and Worker status.
    - Center: the chart, then the War Rooms card with its tab bar `News | 15m | 1H | 4H | D1`.
      - **Chart layers (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 3 of the key-level plan):** a row of toggles above the chart, kept per viewer.
        - **On by default:**
          - *Zones* (the Key Level Agent's candidate zones and the strategy plan);
          - *Bands* (Z-KWW mean, ±1σ and ±1.618σ, and EMA 114, computed in the browser from the chart's closed bars with the Mizan formulas);
          - *Volume* (the session POC, VAH and VAL of the chart plane).
        - *Fib*: Auto by default. The OTE band (0.618 to 0.786), 0.5 and the 1.272 and 1.618 extensions of the chart plane's leg show by themselves while a setup forms (SETUP_FORMING to TRIGGERED). The toggle cycles Auto, On and Off.
        - **Toggles:**
          - *VWAP* (5m to 1H only);
          - *Liquidity* (whale zones and mitigation blocks, the nearest FVG targets, QM and RBS/SBR);
          - *Session* (the true day, week and month opens).
        - The levels come from `GET /api/hud/levels`, which now serves the snapshot's `chart_plane`. The 5m chart reads the 15m plane.
      - **Key levels panel (right):** the level count of each reader (Band, Liquidity, Projection, Session), the setup type at the 15m location with its evidence, the Camarilla set and the zones on one price axis, and the nearest candidates; hovering a zone lists its levels and their reader.
    - Right: the trading assistant panel (charts).
    - On narrower screens, the side panels move under the center instead of hiding.
    - Shadow versions are at `#/shadow`; `/shadow` redirects there.
  - **Read-only:** the Worker forwards only GET/HEAD `/api/*` calls to the AI Worker.
- The AI Worker's old `/dashboard` page (Analyst/Counselor/Reasoner chat timeline and raw output) is retired. `GET /dashboard` redirects (302) to the Nexus dashboard (`NEXUS_DASHBOARD_URL`, default `https://nexus.cryptophys.work/`).
- The dashboard shows agents by name only: Technical Analyst, News Analyst, Risk Manager and Moderator. It never shows model names.
- Agent text must be natural language: validators reject snake_case field names, dotted evidence paths and key=value pairs in insights, summaries and dissent (`src/natural-language.mjs`). Evidence paths stay in `refs`.
- The fail-closed WAIT reasoning is plain language too (`describeDegradedEvidence`).
- **H1 `trade_plan`** (`src/trade-plan.mjs`, RFC_HUD_AGENTS §7):
  - The Reasoner prompt (`TRADE_PLAN_RULE`) asks for `trade_plan` on BUY or SELL. It covers action, entry, invalidation, take profit, exit reason, progressive stop and zone.
  - `parseDecision` validates the plan with `validateTradePlan`:
    - the prices must be positive, correctly ordered for the side, and equal to the decision's validated `entry_price`, `stop_loss` and `take_profit`;
    - the progressive stop must lie between invalidation and take profit;
    - the zone needs `low <= high` and a side of DEMAND or SUPPLY;
    - unknown keys, such as grid levels, are dropped.
  - An invalid plan becomes null, with `trade_plan_errors` recorded. The decision itself is never changed. WAIT and NO_TRADE never carry a plan.
- **H1 decision text is plain language too** (`src/plain-decision.mjs`):
  - The Reasoner prompt carries `DECISION_TEXT_RULE`.
  - When the reasoning, confirmation or invalidation text still reads like code (field names, key paths, `key = value`, `==`), one call to the bus model rewrites only those fields.
  - The decision, confidence, prices and conditions never change.
  - A rewrite that still reads like code, or that adds a new standalone number, is rejected, and the original text stays.
  - `decision.text_rewritten` lists the rewritten fields. The raw reasoner output stays in the synthesis.
- In news threads and the live room, an answer that fails only the natural-language check gets one correction call (`corrections` in the message body). That call is counted by the hourly budget and never runs past the budget or the wall deadline. A second failure stays `invalid_output`.
- TradingView webhook reading (`src/webhook-interpretation.mjs`):
  - It decodes the three alerts of each H1 candle into plain sentences: Prophet R38, Prophet R37 and GMI v12.70.
    - It covers signal, trade-management state, divergence or structure break, confirmation candle, GMI verdict and confluence gate.
    - It also gives an overall lean.
  - The codes follow the Pine sources exactly.
  - It is deterministic, never a model output, and never changes the H1 decision.
  - It is served by `GET /api/decision/:key` (`tradingview_reading`) and in the Threads 1H post (`post.tradingview`). Nexus shows it as "TradingView signals (H1)".
  - It is fed to the bus technical agent (evidence key `tradingview`, no underscore so the model does not echo a field name), which must interpret each indicator by name, even on WAIT.
  - It is also fed to the bus moderator, to news threads (technical side) and to the live room (as the H1 backdrop).
- Neither feature writes into the H1 chain's evidence, prompts or decision. Stances are discussion only: no orders.
- `POST /preview/news-analysis-fixture` exists only when `PREVIEW_FIXTURES_ENABLED` is `"true"` (set only in `wrangler.preview.jsonc`), so preview-e2e can prove a news thread.

### HUD agents (Phase C; the per-H1 runner retired 2026-11-15)
- **Retired in code on 2026-11-15** (owner: "setuju semuanya"): the per-H1 HUD agents (`runHudAgents`, `hud_agent` / `hud_moderator`, `HUD_AGENTS_ENABLED`) had not run since 2026-11-05, when the War Room took their place; each HUD now speaks as a seat of the War Room and the 1H/4H/D1 rooms. Their stored Threads 1H messages stay readable. History below.
- Owner request, 2026-11-04: every HUD in the installed Pine indicators becomes its own agent.
  - Each agent is named after its HUD.
  - Each agent analyses only the data its HUD produces.
- The brief for the VPS agent is `docs/BRIEF_HUD_AGENTS.md`. It covers the HUD inventory, the rules, and an assessment → RFC → flagged implementation sequence.
- Owner decisions, 2026-11-05:
  - the untitled Prophet R38 top-right HUD is named "Prophet";
  - Matrix & Omega Sensors gets one agent per titled section.
  - That makes 14 agents in total.
- Phase A assessment: `docs/HUD_AGENTS_ASSESSMENT.md` (PR #66). The owner's answers (2026-11-05) are in `docs/BRIEF_HUD_AGENTS.md`:
  - HUD agents also speak in the live 15m room, on 15m HUD data that must be parity-verified like H1.
  - Verification uses a Pine candidate alert that carries the HUD fields, installed by the owner for H1 and 15m.
  - Rows that cannot be recomputed locally become output of the H1 decision reasoning.
  - The R37 Funnel is read per current candle.
  - `HUD_AGENTS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR = 30`.
- RFC: `docs/RFC_HUD_AGENTS.md` (PR #69).
- Owner decisions, 2026-11-05:
  - Aegis joins the live 15m room;
  - HUD data must be verified through a Pine candidate comparator;
  - the candidate ships first (C1).
- The Phase C order (C1–C7) is in `docs/BRIEF_HUD_AGENTS.md`.
- **Indicator adoption** (stages 1-6 and the Mizan Oracle; owner 2026-11-19, then 2026-11-22 "tidak perlu shadow, langsung dipasang sesuai desainmu juga": live without shadow, the owner's word is the `promotion_by_owner` act): `docs/RFC_INDICATOR_ADOPTION.md` holds the owner's words, the binding rules and the design. Only the B7 field groups are merged (inert until B11); the rest is planned. The Pine fields go only into `pinescript/candidates` (`*.hud.v2` and `Cryptophys-Mizan-Probe.v1`); `pinescript/ssot` stays untouched. The Mizan Oracle will be a HUD agent of the 15m room only, never a Decision Engine witness.
- **Live War Room 15m** (`src/war-room.mjs`, behind `WAR_ROOM_ENABLED`, on in production; owner decision 2026-11-05). At every 15m close, every agent discusses in one room, in three phases:
  1. **Communication bus.** Each agent posts one opening claim from its own data only. The agents (owner decision 2026-11-06: the 15m room has no H1 Desk):
     - **15m Desk:** the parity-approved 15m Prophet R38, R37 and GMI records of the candle, in plain sentences. It speaks at 0.4 or lower when they are not approved.
     - **Higher-Timeframe Desk:** retired 2026-11-09 (independent rooms); the 15m room no longer reads the H1 chain.
     - **News Analyst.**
     - **Technical Analyst:** the 15m candles (the 5m execution read is retired, 2026-11-09). While a London or New York session window is open and `orb_breakout` is on (Track A PR 9), its data also carries `session_opening_range` (`sessionRangeReading` in `src/opening-range.mjs`, the 1H ATR from the same 15m bars, `h1AtrOf15`, and the calendar's releases) with one rule line (`TECHNICAL_RANGE_RULE`); outside a window, or with the agent off, its data and messages are as before.
     - **Risk Manager.** Since B16 (2026-11-23) its data also carries lens B's liquidity conditions (`liquidity_conditions`), and the moderator reads them too, while lens B is on, above its floor and fresh; the brief stores them (`context.conditions`). No sweep, no price; `LENS_ROOM_LINE` `brief` turns it off (see "Lens B's conditions in the 15m and 1H rooms").
     - **Projection Agent** (owner 2026-11-12: "Projection Agent sekarang harus masuk ke room"; `src/projection-agent.mjs`; in the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms too): the chart patterns of the room's last 60 closed bars (the Key Level Agent's detector), each with its record on gold from the pattern calibration, and the projection levels of the chart's HUD plane (Fibonacci, the probability cone, trendlines, PA levels). Rooms never state prices, so every level is a distance from the last close in average bar ranges, above or below. It speaks when it has a pattern or a level to read; its evidence label is the closed bars of the chart. The War Room cap is 185 calls an hour for it.
     - **One agent per computed 15m HUD** (13).
  2. **Consensus Discussion** (until 2026-11-07 called the debate; stored steps keep the `debate:` prefix). At most 8 speakers answer named agents. It is open for at most 3 minutes, then closed.
