- **The plan** on each sync, from the EA signal (`eaSignal`: the Decision Engine's 15m decision, or the H1 decision with `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"`), the control, the settings and the executor state: nothing when the executor is missing, disconnected or older than 60 s; close a position against the signal; open at most once per signal (`open:<issued_ms>:<B|S>`) only before `expires_ms`, within `max_signal_age_ms` (10 min, like the EA), outside the event blackout, at or below `max_spread_points` (60), below `max_positions` (1) and above the daily loss limit (`max_daily_loss_pct` 2 % of the day's starting balance; deposits and withdrawals are never the day's loss or profit, owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju keduanya": the broker's day start, as the EA reports it, is the balance less today's closed deals, so it already holds them and is used as it is; an onboarding reference already moves with them; only the owner's reset over the day's stop is moved by the operations after it (`dailyLossExecutor`, D1 `omega_accounts.day_ops`). Fixed 2026-11-19: the broker's start was moved too, so a $300 deposit counted twice and read as a daily loss). **The connected account** is the one whose opaque reference (`account_ref`, from the EA's login hash) the last bridge sync stored with the state (`connectedAccount`), never the first account on the same server: the 5-minute profile agent, the mode selector, the owner's controls and the overview's `connected` and `day_pl_pct` read it (fixed 2026-11-19, when a USD and a cent account on one server were mixed up). Every owner control (profile, ceiling, agent mode, accept, decline, reset) acts on the connected account only and refuses any other, naming it (each refusal is recorded as the event `profile_control_refused`); each account carries `identity` (USD or cent, currency, gold symbol) and the Console shows it (owner 2026-11-19: "harus bisa membedakan mana akun sen dan usd"). **Daily risk budget and no stacking** (owner 2026-11-12, after four SELL stops lost 4.06 % of the day on 2026-11-12 against the 2 % limit): the day can still lose the equity above the floor (the day's starting balance less `max_daily_loss_pct`) minus what the open positions would lose from the broker's price to their stops (`openRiskAtStops`; the equity carries their floating profit and loss, so a protected position adds only the floating profit it could give back; a position without a stop counts as the whole account; manual positions count, other symbols are not valued); a new entry's lot is capped to that budget at its stop (note `lot_capped_daily_budget`), and below the minimum lot there is no entry (`daily_risk_budget`). A second position on a side opens only once every position already on that side is protected, its stop at or beyond its open price (`isProtected`; note `same_side_unprotected`). A resting Omega limit counts as such a position, except one of an older signal on the current signal's side under `entry_order` limit or council (SS1, `limit_supersede` on: deleted as `superseded_by_signal`); an open position, a filled limit included, always counts. SL and TP keep the plan's distances from the broker's own price (BUY from the ask, SELL from the bid). Volume = `risk_pct` (0.5 %, at most 2 %) of equity / the loss per lot at the stop, floored to the volume step; below the minimum there is no trade. Every skip is a note code; a change of the notes is one `plan` event. Further gates and management (reconciled with the code 2026-11-11, `src/omega-engine.mjs`): no new entry in the daily rollover quarantine (21:55 to 22:15 UTC, `ROLLOVER_QUARANTINE_UTC`); with `adaptive_volatility` (on by default) a stop closer than 0.75 x the symbol's ATR is widened to it, the volume is sized on the widened stop and the target moves in proportion, so the trade keeps the plan's reward-to-risk (audit fix 2026-11-11; note `adaptive_stop`); a limit entry waits while price is more than 0.35 stops away toward the target (`waiting_for_pullback`), and since 2026-11-13 (owner: "setuju") also while it is more than 0.35 × the plan's stop past the entry toward the stop (`price_past_entry`): before, such an entry kept the plan's stop distance from its fill and so put the stop well beyond the plan's key level (a SELL at 3903.98 against a 3888.48 entry); positions are closed on an `EXIT`/`REVERSAL`/mean-reversal signal. **Position management** (`manageIntents`, audit fix 2026-11-11) runs on every sync with the engine on and a fresh executor, whatever the entry gates say (blackout, pause, spread, rollover, full book, no side): with `trailing_stop_r` a position at that many R from its open price moves its stop to the open price (`break_even`); a farther TP2 or extended target of the current signal moves the take-profit of the signal's side; a position of 0.02 lots or more banks half at 1.5R or its target, once per position (`tp1_scale_out`). Before, management ran only when a new entry passed every gate, break-even read a field the executor never sends (`entry` instead of `open_price`) and fired on any profit, and a scaled position could be halved again.
- **Range mode step 1: the H4/D1 trend filter and the evidence gate per cell** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `src/htf-cells.mjs`). The HTF trend is H4 and D1 each "up", "down" or "flat" (EMA(50) and its slope over 5 closed bars), combined ("mixed" when they disagree); a side is "with", "against" or "neutral" to it. The range strategies (`RANGE_GATED_AGENTS`: `range_fade`, `sweep_reversal`) trade only with the trend and only in a cell (agent × side × HTF context) whose replayed record (the plan learner's control arm, `plan_setups.htf`, state `cells`) has 30+ setups at +0.10R or better (`CELL_GATE`); otherwise the signal has no side and the Omega page says why (`htf_against`, `htf_unknown`, `cell_collecting`, `cell_below_bar`). Applied in `eaSignal` before the strategy arm, so every sizing and risk check stays in force. `RANGE_CELL_GATE` "off" stops it (emergency only).
- **Range, the 4th trading mode** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `TRADING_MODES_LIST`). Only the range strategies trade in it, through the HTF filter and the cell gate, and only while the decision's 1H regime ranges (`applyRangeMode` in `eaSignal`: `range_mode_agent`, `range_breakout`); intraday thresholds, intraday ladder. The selector proposes it while ranging when a range cell (with the trend or neutral) passes (`rangeEvidence`), through the mode council; the owner may pin it. `rangeExitTick` (5-minute ledger step) returns to the mode it came from (`range_return`) once the 1H regime trends, without the council.
- **The selector's daily outcome** (MS1, 2026-11-23; `runModeSelector`, once a UTC day from the ledger cron's `modeSelectorTick`). Every outcome is one Omega event (`omega_events`, actor `agent:mode`, action `mode_selector_run`, classified `NOT_A_CHANGE` in the change audit; a switch keeps its own `mode_changed` row and event): `{ day, status, reason, action, mode, to, proposed, regime, evidence }` with `status` `pinned_by_server` (reason `server_trading_mode`), `pinned` (`owner_pin` or `auto_off`), `unchanged` (the step's `none` with its reason, e.g. `current_mode_best`, `candidate_holding`, `evidence_collecting`; or `council_rejected`), `changed` or `failed` (the error); `evidence` per mode `{ n, mean_r, status, scope }` (no stop, no price), read on a pinned day too; `fresh` (already run today) and `unavailable` record nothing. Best effort: a failed write never changes the outcome. The setups load by `opened_at`, then `setup_key`, so the walk-forward's holdout (its last 30 %) is the same on every run.
- **Counter-trend reversal probation** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `src/htf-cells.mjs` `PROBATION`). The reversal strategy (`sweep_reversal`) against the H4/D1 trend, before its cell passes: only after a change of structure on a closed H1 or H4 candle (`structureShift`), at half the risk and half the hard cap (`signal.probation`, `risk_scale` in `planIntents`), one at a time (`probation_busy`), never hedged (`probation_hedge`). It graduates to full risk when its cell passes and reverts when its last 5 probation trades average under 0R (`probationTick` in the 5-minute ledger step, KV `omega:probation:v1`). The owner's switch: `POST /omega/api/probation` (on clears reverted cells).
- **Modify and Delegate on the Trade tab** (owner 2026-11-19: "di tab trade perlu ditambah modify untuk manual interuption Entry/TP/SL dan juga delegate supaya entry manual selanjutnya dihandle oleh omega"; confirmed: "Posisi + pending order", "Omega lanjut biasa", "Tombol + toggle", "Kelola penuh"; `src/omega-hitl.mjs`, OmegaExecutor 1.18). **Modify:** the owner sets an open position's S/L and T/P (Omega's or manual) and a pending limit or stop order's price, S/L and T/P from the Trade tab; `POST /omega/api/position/modify` (owner only, same origin) reaches `ownerModify`, which checks them against the live price and the broker's stop distance (a BUY's stop below the bid, its target above; a SELL the other way; a BUY LIMIT below the ask, and so on; empty removes a level), queues one `modify_sltp` or `modify_order` intent with a `manual:` id and audits `owner_modify_position` / `owner_modify_order` with the values before and after. Omega keeps managing an Omega position afterwards: a later ladder lock or trail may move the stop again. **Delegate:** "DELEGATE" on a manual position (`POST /omega/api/position/delegate`), or the toggle "Delegate my next manual entries to Omega" (`POST /omega/api/delegate-mode`): every manual position opened while it is on is delegated at the next sync (event `position_delegated` with `auto`); "TAKE BACK" releases one, and a released position is never adopted again by the toggle. D1 `omega_delegations` and `omega_delegate_mode`. A delegated position is managed like Omega's own (`isOmegaOwned`): a stop from the plan when it has none (the signal's stop distance on its side, else 1.5 × ATR(M15), from its open price; `delegatedStopIntents`), break-even, the TP ladder and lock (its mode is the current one, R from its stop), the Position Manager (its own stop and target as its plan), the closes on an opposite signal; never an add, a larger size or a reversal. Its intents carry a `d:` id (`markDelegatedIntents`). The overview carries `delegation` (tickets, toggle, `supported`); the page marks a delegated position "MANUAL → OMEGA". Both need OmegaExecutor 1.18: before it Nexus refuses an edit (`ea_1_18_required`) and delegated positions wait (note `delegate_needs_ea_1_18`).
- **Universal broker, server and symbol** (owner 2026-11-19: "desain tolol kalau hardcoded ke broker/server tertentu seharusnya berlaku universal"; OmegaExecutor 1.19, omega-bridge 1.5.0). Nothing in Omega depends on a broker, server or symbol name. **Gold** is what the terminal reports: profit currency USD, a contract of 1 to 100 oz, no `symbol_error`, and a bid within 2% of the reference (`goldSymbolCheck`, `goldPriceProblem` against the signal's entry, note `symbol_price_mismatch`; the EA checks the same against `omega/reference.txt` from the sync's `reference_price`). The owner's symbol is a hint tried first under the same checks. Base currency is not used (FxPro `GOLD` reports base `USD`). **Account money** (`accountMoney` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`, `toUsd` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`): units of the account currency per US dollar are 1 for `USD`; otherwise measured from the account's own positions and deals (profit ÷ (move × volume × contract), moves of 50+ ticks, median of the latest 8); otherwise the owner's value given with the connection (`units_per_usd`, D1 `omega_connection.units_per_usd`, Setup → Connection & EA "Units per US dollar"); otherwise nothing opens (`money_unverified`), and a measurement more than 5% from the owner's value opens nothing (`money_mismatch`). Every amount is divided once by that number; `cent` means about 100, never a currency code. The tick-value check (`tick_value_mismatch`) and `volume_below_min` stay. Deals stored before #461 keep the USC/USX rule (`isLegacyCentDeal`). **Labels:** the broker's name is the EA's `account.company`, otherwise the full server name. The Console has no broker presets: the server is typed as MT5 shows it. Arming, resets and profile changes stay the owner's.
- **The reference across brokers (MX1, owner 2026-11-27: "monex ubah aturan dulu agar bisa eksekusi").** The reference gold price the EA finds and checks its symbol by (`referenceFrom`, `src/gold-symbol.mjs`) is withheld while the feed runs on the MT5 fallback only when the fallback's broker is the account's own: the fallback's corpus source (`fallback.source` on the 1m series, `fallbackSourceOf` in `src/feed.mjs`) against the account's server, compared as brokers (`brokerIdentity`/`sameBroker`: platform, account-type and server-number words dropped, "FxPro-MT5" = "FxPro-MT5 Demo", unsure counts as the same). Another broker's fallback is sent, with its 4h closes after the fallback's start; an unknown source or account server withholds, as before. A past fallback interval (its broker not recorded) still leaves out its 4h bars. `GOLD_PRICE_TOLERANCE`, the contract checks, `accountMoney` and the entry gates are unchanged. The Omega overview's `bridge.symbol_check.reference` says where the reference comes from (`referenceOrigin`: `feed`, `fallback_other_broker` with its server, or `withheld` with why) and its line, e.g. "reference: fxpro-mt5 (fallback, another broker)". A reference still needs a fresh price: the EA drops one older than 20 minutes.
- **Feed bridges (F1, OF1; owner 2026-11-27 "gunakan vps existing dikerjakan sekarang sebagai prioritas utama").** A second MT5 terminal on the owner's VPS can be a DATA-ONLY feed (`src/feed-bridge.mjs`). A feed bridge syncs at `POST /api/bridge/feed-sync` on the Omega Worker with its own Access service token (`OMEGA_FEED_CLIENT_ID`, set 2026-11-27 to the `cryptophys-agent-omega-feed` token's client id; unset refuses every feed; the executor's token never reaches it and the feed's never reaches `/api/bridge/sync`). `feedSync` stores its key and a stripped state (connected, server, quote: never an account amount, a position or a deal) in D1 `omega_feed_bridges` (keyed by bridge id, `role = 'feed'`), returns its own sealed login from `omega_feed_connections` (set and cleared by a person at `POST`/`DELETE /api/feed-connection`, the same RSA-3072 envelope sealed to the feed's own kid, `kid_mismatch` per bridge), and never returns an `intents` key. The executor's `omega_bridge`, `omega_connection` and intents are untouched. Its gold M1 bars (`ingestFeedCorpus`) are stored as their own corpus source `mt5:<feed server>:<symbol>`, marked in `corpus_feeds`, validated against the feed's own connection and state; refused on the executor's server (`feed_on_executor_server`), into an executor's source or another feed's. The MT5 fallback (the pull adapter's `corpusBars` with `prefer_feed`) reads a feed whose newest bar is at most 3 minutes old (`FEED_FRESH_MS`) first, else the executor's own corpus as before; the ladder, the gates, the basis and the broker shadow lane read the executor's source only. With a fresh feed on another broker, `fallback.source` names it and MX1 sends that reference. The Omega overview lists `broker.feeds` (id, key, freshness, server, connection status; never the envelope). Bridge 1.8.0's feed role (`OMEGA_ROLE=feed`, `bridge/omega-bridge/src/feed-daemon.mjs`: its own terminal, ini and service; algo trading forced off; the password emptied after login; never an intent) and its exporter `ea/mt5/OmegaFeed.mq5` (no trade code, test-pinned) are F2 and F3; the VPS install is docs/RUNBOOK_OF1_FEED.md (F6). A feed on the executor's own broker (`sameBroker`) is refused. **Macro export, step 1 (OmegaFeed 1.01, 2026-11-27, owner 08:15 "yang bisa ambil data raw dari mt5 ambil saja"; Variant A).** The feed EA also writes `omega/corpus_macro.json` once per closed gold M1 bar, in OmegaExecutor's corpus_macro format: the closed M1 bars of an explicit `MacroSymbols` map (`BASE=SYMBOL`; OANDA default `XAGUSD=XAGUSD,USOIL=USOIL.sml,EURUSD=EURUSD.sml,GBPUSD=GBPUSD.sml,USDJPY=USDJPY.sml,USDCAD=USDCAD,USDCHF=USDCHF`; no guessing, a missing symbol is left out; USDSEK is not on OANDA's feed, Brent has no reader; `MacroEnabled=false` stops it). Data only, still no trade code (pins OF-1..4). **Feed restarts keep the EA (bridge 1.8.2, feed incident 2026-11-27).** After the feed daemon empties `Password=`, the old `run.sh` started the terminal without `/config`, so a restart dropped OmegaFeed. 1.8.2 keeps a credential-free `startup.ini` beside the login ini (`feedStartupIni`: no `[Common]`, no login or password; `[StartUp]` the EA on the feed's symbol, M1; algo trading off; owned like the login ini, mode 600; path `FEED_STARTUP_INI`, never the login ini or the executor's), and `bridge/omega-bridge/feed-run.sh` always passes a `/config` (the login ini while it holds a password, else `startup.ini`). The executor is unaffected: its daemon empties its login only when the owner clears the connection, so its terminal always starts with `/config` and its StartUp chart (the Omega profile is emptied at each start). **Feed content staleness (`src/feed-stale.mjs`).** A feed bridge that syncs while its corpus source's newest bar is older than 5 minutes, outside gold's weekend and daily break (`goldPausedAt`, also 5 minutes after either), is `content_stale` on the Data feeds card (`feedOverview`: `content_stale`, `last_bar_age_ms`) and gets one `feed_stale` event per episode (`agent:cerebrum`; the bridge id and the bar age only), which the relay sends to the Cerebrum Telegram; a new episode starts only after a fresh bar. Checked in `feedSync` at most once a minute per bridge and isolate. A feed that never sent a bar is not stale. The MT5 fallback already skipped a stale feed by its bar times (`FEED_FRESH_MS`). **Step 2 (bridge 1.8.1).** The feed daemon adds `corpus_macro` to its feed-sync: per base only the bars after the server's `corpus_macro_cursors`, only once the server answers them (until then nothing is sent), only when the file changed since the last complete send; the gold bars keep priority in the 64 KB body (macro halved first, the rest on the next syncs). The executor's daemon is unchanged. The server ingest under `mt5macro:` (step 3), the pull adapter's shadow comparison (step 4) and the switch (step 5) follow as their own PRs.
- **Alert wording (2026-11-27).** The market-data-stopped alert adds "The MT5 fallback has no new broker bars either" while the fallback runs and its last broker bar is older than 30 minutes (`fallbackLastBar`).
- **Symbol identity, money and the account card (follow-up, owner 2026-11-19 relayed: "harus diperbaiki semua sampai ultimate, dan ini yang kubilang tolol, kosmetic hardcoded ke broker/server/account tertentu"; OmegaExecutor 1.20, omega-bridge 1.6.0).** **Identity:** the sync's `reference` (`referenceFrom` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`) carries the feed's last 5-minute close with its time and the last 12 closed 4h closes of `OANDA:XAUUSD`; none while the feed runs on the broker's bars (`fallbackSinceMs`). EA 1.20 chooses a symbol only when its own M5 closes follow those closes (2% level, 0.5% path), refuses contracts with an expiry, refuses to pick between passing symbols with different contracts unless the owner names one, re-checks the chosen symbol against every fresh reference and ages the reference on the price's own time. **Money:** a measurement that agrees with the owner's value converts with the owner's value (`source: "owner_verified"`); without one, a measurement within 1% of a power of ten is that power (`snapPowerOfTen`). The state D1 keeps (`omega_bridge.state`) is the converted executor: read back with `executorFrom(state, { stored: true })` it is never converted again, and the live push converts the raw state with the connection's value. **Blocks:** a daily-loss block records the raw inputs it stands on (`upgrade_block_evidence`, `blockEvidence`: the EA's day start, balance, equity, the limit, and the row's onboarding, day operations and override); each agent tick runs the current day-reference code (`dailyLossExecutor`) on those recorded inputs and lifts the block only when it finds no loss there (event `upgrade_block_lifted`). Only a code change can lift a block; the market, the broker's day rolling before the UTC day, a withdrawal or the clock never do. A block without evidence runs to its end. The EA keeps its verified symbol across restarts (`omega/verified_symbol.txt`, this login only) and skips gaps in the price path (6 matched closes over 12 hours, 75-minute windows); the server leaves out the 4h bars inside the last recorded MT5 fallback, and the Console names ambiguous symbols with their contracts (`eaRefusalText`). **Account card and texts:** the Risk profile card shows the connected account only, described by what the terminal reports (currency, gold symbol, the broker's own name `account.company`); no list of other accounts, no internal id, no server name, in the card, the confirm, the history line, Telegram (`Omega · USD account · GOLD: ...`), refusals and the pages' broker label (`broker`: the company, else "MT5 broker"). The internal id travels in the requests only, so the server refuses anything but the connected account.
- **Shadow per-mode evidence and the probation** (MS2, 2026-11-23; `src/mode-shadow.mjs`, docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md §7). The selector used to have evidence only for the mode that traded. **Shadow pass:** after each 15m decision is stored, `runDecisionEngine` runs `shadowModeDecisions` for every mode not in force: the deterministic part of the decision only (no model call, no room, no price in a room), with the live decision's witnesses, quorum and market and the live council's answer, and the mode's own geometry (the scalp flag and `SCALP_TARGET`, under TP1-KZ), thresholds (`modeThresholds(m, knobs)`), room, vetoes, reward gate on each agent's plan and so its speakers and agent selection, through `decide()` as live. The side is the council's BUY or SELL (the one it wanted); on its WAIT, a side the mode's guardrails open where the live guardrails were closed (a side the live gates had open and the council still declined stays WAIT; both open: the quorum's lean); without a valid answer the live fallback (the strategy engine's decision and confidence); the council's confidence, entry type, target and strategy carry over. Range only while the 1H regime ranges and only from `RANGE_GATED_AGENTS` (the other agents' plans are not offered; a trade by another is WAIT). A mode that would trade writes one row to D1 `mode_shadow_plans` (decision key, bar close, decided at, mode, side, agent, the decided side's agent plans and the trade, ATR, regime, `rule_version`); the decision record is unchanged. **Retention:** rows older than 90 days by their bar close are dropped in the AI Worker cron's retention line, beside `pruneShadowKnowledge` and `pruneModelArena` (`pruneModeShadowPlans`, at most 5000 a run, oldest first; MSH-19); the plan learner reads only its own window of them (2 days, 30 at its first run). The shadow setups in `plan_setups` are not pruned: no sweep prunes `plan_setups` by age (a follow-up, not a policy of MS2). **Evidence:** the plan learner derives shadow setups from those rows (`plan_setups.source = 'shadow'`, see "Plan learner"); the selector's evidence per mode is its live and shadow setups of the target rule in force (`rule_version` = `tp1_kz:<TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE>`; TP1-KZ changes the geometry in every mode, so the evidence restarts at its deploy and the shadow refills it), one per mode, opening bar and agent with the live one first (`unionSetups`); range's cells (`cellRecords` on the control arm) over the range strategies' live setups in any mode and the shadow range setups, the same way (`rangeShadowEvidence`). The walk-forward gate, the +0.10R margin, the streak of 2 and the 4-seat council are unchanged. The outcome's and the change rows' evidence add `n_live` and `n_shadow` per mode. The selector is the only reader of shadow setups. **Probation:** a selector switch (the council's approval or a feasibility drop) to a mode other than the baseline with fewer than 31 live resolved setups (`MODE_PROBATION_RULES`) is a probation, stored additively in KV `trading:mode:control:v1` (`probation`: mode, from, baseline, started, live setups, risk scale) and named in the change row's evidence and its Telegram line. While it lasts, Omega sizes the new entries of a signal decided in that mode at 0.5 of the risk per trade (`modeProbationRisk` in the bridge sync, `planIntents` `modeRisk`; the profile's hard cap is not scaled, so the minimum-lot rule stays within it; open positions are never touched; with the reversal probation as well, the smaller scale; the open intent carries `mode_probation`). The EA sizes from the owner's input and is unchanged. Daily, before the step: its live setups since it started reach 15 with a mean under 0R, or its walk-forward fails: back to the baseline with reason `probation_failed` through the existing change path (a `omega_mode_changes` row, the Omega event, one Telegram line, a `mode.v1` item); at 31 live setups and a walk-forward pass it is cleared (event `mode_probation_passed`, one Telegram line, full risk). Any other mode change and the owner's pin or veto clear it; Auto keeps it; the baseline stays the owner's. Not an Omega setting and not in `ERA_SWITCHES`: `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` is unchanged (no fixture carries `modeRisk`), only `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` moves; v4 counts each trade in its own R (`omega4_size` 1), so parity is unaffected (its `omega4_json.risk_pct` is v4's own, full risk). **Status:** `GET /api/trading-mode` adds `selector` (`modeEvidenceStatus`: each mode's n and mean R per 1H regime, live against shadow, the rule in force and the probation with its live record); every field before it is unchanged. **Switches:** `MODE_SHADOW_EVIDENCE` (`on` | `off`: no shadow row written or derived, the selector reads live setups with no rule cut, as before) and `MODE_PROBATION` (`on` | `off`: no probation, no scale); with both off the decision record, the plan setups and the selector's outcome are byte-identical to before (test/mode-shadow.test.mjs MSH-15, hashes taken on a git-archive of main fdc6c38). Cost: the shadow pass measured about 1 ms per mode (3 to 4 ms per decision in node, against about 1.7 ms for the live `assess`), three KV room reads and one D1 batch.
- **Range shadow: the range key, both-side setups and intraday episodes** (owner via the orchestrator 2026-11-27, "setuju c1 + c2 sekalian b"; `src/range-shadow.mjs`, switch `RANGE_SHADOW_MODE`, default `off`, production `on`, previews `off`). Range's evidence was thin: its cells fill from the range strategies' live setups (the decided side only) and the range-mode shadow, and each change of the full rule version restarted them. **c1, the range key:** `rangeKeyOf` keeps only the rule-version tokens that change a range plan (`tp1_kz:*`, `zone_ahead@*`, `zone_aim@*`; a version with none is its own key); `rangeShadowEvidence` compares setups by it, so a later token that does not touch range plans does not restart the range cells. **c2, both sides:** in the hourly plan-learner step, every engine decision of any stance whose 1H regime ranges gives both sides' `RANGE_GATED_AGENTS` plans to the live setup rules (`rangeBothSetupsFrom` → `setupsFrom`): `plan_setups` rows with `source = 'range_both'`, key `rangeboth:<live rule's key>`, `rule_version` the range key in force. At most `RANGE_BOTH_DAILY_CAP` = 150 new rows a UTC day by `opened_at`; at the cap one `range_both_capped` log line per day per isolate (`{ day, count, cap }`) and every run's result carries `range_both.capped` (never silent). The first run backfills 30 days but from no earlier than the first live range setup of the range key in force (an older decision would be stamped with a rule it was not planned under); then the 2-day window. They are replayed in their own queue on the control arm only, by the live replay (`replaySetup`): a limit entry counts as filled only when a bar after the decision trades through it within the plan's fill window (`fillWindowOf`: last seen + 15 min from its opening, at least 15 min, at most `FILL_WINDOW_MS`); an at-level plan enters at market only when the first bar opens within `market_band` (0.35) of its stop distance; an unfilled setup is a non-entry at 0R (`f` 0), as for live and shadow setups; the H4/D1 tagger tags them as any range setup. **Where they count:** only in the mode selector's range evidence (with `MODE_SHADOW_EVIDENCE` on): one setup per opening bar, agent and side, live before shadow before range_both (`rangeSetupsForEvidence`), with `n_both` beside `n_live`/`n_shadow`, `without_both` (the same evidence without them) and `cells` (each cell's status, setups and mean R with range_both and without it, its source counts and fill rate); the `mode_selector_run` evidence carries range's `n_both`, `without_both` and each cell's status both ways. **Never** in what arms or gates live trading: `unionSetups` drops them (the modes' walk-forward and the probation's live count), and every other `plan_setups` reader reads `source = 'live'` (the plan learner's state and so the cell gate on a range trade, the agents' records and breaker, the strategy council's arm/disarm, grid-adapt, the CFC pack and triggers, the knob tuning, the recommendation cards; test/range-shadow.test.mjs RS-10). **b, intraday episodes:** a run of adjacent ranging 1H closes (the decisions whose bar closes at minute :00) is an episode from its first ranging close once it reaches `RANGE_EPISODE_MIN_HOURS` = 4 (opened at the 4th), ended at the first non-ranging close (`ended`) or at a missing hour (`gap`, at its last ranging close), else `open`; D1 `range_episodes` (start_ms key, opened_ms, end_ms, hours, status, updated_at; run-time schema, `migrations/0027_range_episodes.sql` records it), upserted each hourly run from the open episode's start (else the 2-day window; the first run 30 days; an episode starting at the first loaded close is not written, it may have started earlier). **Status:** `GET /api/range-shadow` (`rangeShadowStatus`): the switch, the rule version and range key, today's range_both rows against the cap, range's evidence and its cells both ways, and the last 30 episodes with the range setups opened in each (open ones included) per agent and side (n, resolved, mean R, source counts). No price, no lot. Off: no range_both row, no episode, the run's result as before; the decision record, the cell gate and every live reader are unchanged either way. Cost: one D1 read of the ranging decisions' plans and one of the hourly regimes per hourly run, the range_both inserts and their control-arm replays (about 12 to 30 ranging decisions with range plans a day in the last four days, so well under the cap).
- **Cent accounts and the owner's gold symbol** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; OmegaExecutor 1.17, omega-bridge 1.4.0). **Symbol:** the owner may set the account's gold symbol with the connection (Setup → Connection & EA, the "Symbol" selector, "Other" for a typed name such as `GoldOne`; `trade_symbol`, D1 `omega_connection.trade_symbol`, cleared with the connection). It is the one extra name the server (`goldCheckOf` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`), the bridge (`stateSymbolProblem`) and the EA (`omega/trade_symbol.txt`) accept, under the same contract checks; the bridge opens its chart first. A name off the list also opens nothing until its bid is within 2% of the signal's reference entry (`mappedPriceProblem`, note `symbol_price_mismatch`). **USC and USX (US cents):** `executorFrom` divides every amount once by 100 (`centToUsd`: balance, equity, the day's start and result, free margin, a lot's margin, each position's and deal's profit, commission and swap). **Tick value** (incident 2026-11-18: GoldOne on a USX account reported 0.01, its value in USD, and a 3-lot SELL risked ~82% of $54.50): for a USD-profit symbol on a USD or cent account Omega sizes on contract size × tick size dollars (`usdTickValue`), keeps the broker's figure as `tick_value_reported`, and opens nothing when that figure is neither the computed value nor 100 times it (`tick_value_mismatch`); other accounts keep the broker's figure, so limits, sizing, baselines and pages are in USD; the account keeps `currency: "USC"` with `cent: true`, the pages label it CENT, and its deals are stored in USD. A USC profit currency passes only on a USC account. **Terminal errors:** while no EA state exists, the bridge sends the terminal journal's last error (`bridge_symbol.terminal_error`; a run of 5+ digits outside a price becomes `[account]`, on the host and again on the server, `redactAccount`); the server records a `terminal_error` event once per change and the overview shows it for 30 minutes after the connection was stored.
- **5m execution** (owner 2026-11-12, `docs/RFC_OMEGA_5M_EXECUTION.md`; with the broker fields on the signal, `OMEGA_EXEC_5M` on). **The stop** of a market entry is the swing low (BUY) or high (SELL) of the last 3 broker 5m bars beyond a buffer of max(2 spreads, 0.1 × the 5m ATR), used only when it is tighter than the plan's stop and no closer than max(0.5 × the 5m ATR, the stops level + 3 spreads); the target stays the plan's (the RRR rises); the adaptive volatility stop is skipped for it (note `stop_5m`). The lot is sized on it but capped at `tight_stop_max_lot_factor` (1 to 2, default 1.5) × the lot at the plan's stop (note `lot_capped_5m`). A limit keeps the plan's stop. The open intent records `stop_source` (`stop_5m`/`plan`) and `trigger_source`. **Management** of Omega's own positions: from 1R (or once at break-even and in profit) the stop trails the 3-bar 5m swing minus the buffer, only forwards and at most once per broker 5m bar (`ms:<ticket>:t5:<minute>`, reason `trail_5m`); from 0.5R in profit (or protected) a broker 5m Prophet confirmation against the position closes it (`cl:<ticket>:o5:<minute>`, reason `opposite_5m`). Break-even and the TP1 scale-out are unchanged. **Key-level management** (owner 2026-11-12: "semua dikontrol dari omega bukan EA", "atur SL+ dan Trailing TP juga harus berdasarkan keylevel menyesuaikan Bull/Bear Power"; Power = the Decision Engine's quorum score): the decision record keeps the Key Level Agent's 12 candidate zones nearest to price (`key_levels`), and the signal carries, whatever the stance, `power` (the latest quorum score, -1 to +1) and `power_ms`, `key_levels` ("low:high" on the feed's scale), `trading_mode` and `feed_basis` (the broker's M1 close minus the feed's 5m close, the median over the last two hours, at least 3 matches, kept 5 minutes per isolate; `feed_basis_known`). With a known basis Omega moves the levels onto the broker's scale and, per own position (`keyLevelPlan`, power signed for the position, fresh for 35 minutes): **SL+** once price passed a key level wholly beyond the entry, the stop locks break-even plus max(2 spreads, the buffer) and steps behind the passed levels: strong power (≥ 0.5) behind the one before the last (room to run), normal behind the last, weak (< 0.2) at the tighter of that and the broker's 5m swing (reason `sl_key_level_<band>`, id `ms:<ticket>:k:<cents>`); **trailing TP** near the target (within max(0.5 × 5m ATR, 3 spreads, 0.2 × the open-to-target distance)) with a strong power the target moves to the next key level (`trail_tp_key_level`) and the stop to the old target less the buffer when it leaves the stop distance; with a weaker power the target is taken; **close** a position in profit when the power is -0.5 or worse against it (`power_reversal`, `cl:<ticket>:pw:<minute>`). The stop only moves forwards and keeps the stops level plus a spread from price. These replace the 5m trail and the extended target, which remain the fallback without a known basis; the 5m opposite exit and the scale-out stay. In scalp an adaptive stop no longer moves the target: it stays at the key level. OmegaExecutor 1.11 turns its local break-even and micro trailing off by default (`AutoBreakEvenEnabled`, `TrailingStopEnabled`): before, they moved stops on the terminal beside Omega (2026-11-11: two shorts' stops moved to about 3990 by the EA, gapped out at the 23:00 reopen). **OmegaExecutor 1.12** (owner 2026-11-12: "ya tambahkan versi EA dan status input ke server") writes `executor` into `state.json`: its name, version and the inputs `AutoBreakEvenEnabled`, `TrailingStopEnabled`, `DynamicAtrStopEnabled`. The bridge passes it through; the AI Worker keeps it checked (`executorInfoFrom`: a malformed value is null) and the overview's `bridge.executor` reads `ok` (1.12 or newer, local break-even and trailing off), `local_management` (one of them still on: the EA moves stops beside Omega), `outdated` (older than `EXECUTOR_MIN_VERSION`) or `unknown` (1.11 and older report nothing), with a line both Omega pages show. While the EA still trails by itself the plan carries the note `ea_local_management`; it never blocks a trade.
- **Typed zones in SL+ and the trailing TP (Z3a, 2026-11-25; the Omega setting `zone_mgmt`; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §8).** The key-level management above was type-blind; with `zone_mgmt` "omega" the zones' demand and supply words steer its SL+ and trailing TP. `zone_mgmt` = "off" (default: today's engine byte for byte, the documented rollback) | "omega" (Z3b adds omega_pm, Z4 full).
  - **Owner-only.** `zone_mgmt` is the owner's switch only: written by the Omega settings page's path or the FS1 panel's Omega row (which forwards to it; the change audit's Telegram line "zone-typed management: off → omega"), validated in `settingsFrom`. Aether's policy and its rollback carry the stored value and never write it; Z5's zone breaker reads it (SELECT only) and never writes it; no automatic rule ever writes it. In `ERA_SWITCHES`: at merge the era is carried (off is the default), and the owner's flip writes `zone_mgmt=omega` and starts a new era at the settings' `updated_ms`.
  - **The signal.** `managementFields` adds `key_level_types` only when every kept level carries Z0's words: comma tokens aligned 1:1 with `key_levels`, each `type/strength/freshness`, `-` for none. `key_levels` is unchanged and an untyped decision's signal is byte for byte as before; the EA reads neither key.
  - **The levels.** `brokerKeyLevels(signal, { typed })` attaches `{ type, strength, freshness }` when the token count matches the key levels (lens B's levels type none); otherwise, and without `typed`, today's objects. Only `keyLevelPlan` reads the typed levels.
  - **SL+.** The own passed zones (demand for a long, supply for a short; medium or stronger): strong power behind the own zone before the last (two needed), normal behind the last own one, weak the tighter of that and the 5m swing; always at or past the lock (break-even plus the buffer, or SLG1's first lock). Taken when at or tighter than today's SL+, or looser by at most the giveback, `ZONE_GIVEBACK_R[mode]` × the position's R at entry (scalp 0.15R, intraday and range 0.25R, swing 0.30R; an unknown mode intraday); with an unknown R, tighter only. Then today's room and forward-only checks.
  - **Trailing TP.** The first decisive opposing zone among the first two beyond the target, else today's next level: at most one zone further.
  - **The record.** `level_source: "zone"` on the intent only when the typing changed its price; ids and reasons are today's.
  - **With PM-1M: one modify per position per 3 minutes** (`ZONE_MGMT_RATE_MS`). A fast action of the position in the last 3 minutes leaves its management untyped (today's); a zone-driven move of the last 3 minutes holds a fast action (noted, with the reason; the rate only, never the hysteresis). A fast intent in the same plan already skips the routine management of that position. v4 does the same.
  - **Unchanged.** The ladder, the outward-TP hold, `power_reversal`, the entries, the limit council and the Position Manager. Z5's hourly tick flags any `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES` or `ZONE_SOURCES_OFF` change while `zone_mgmt` is not off (emergency switches that move live management with no era move).
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 6c0f8142… → 38daf894… (`ENGINE_CODE` gains `typedLevels` and the Z3a helpers); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (V14's fixtures are untyped and at defaults).
- **Box levels in management (Z6, 2026-11-26; the Omega setting `zone_sd`; owner: "Langsung signal + zone_sd"; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §10).** `brokerKeyLevels` and the Position Manager's `brokerLevels` drop a signal level whose token carries `/sd` unless `zone_sd` is "on"; with it on they carry `sd: true`.
  - **Era note (Z6 deploy).** Z6 (#602, 444fb8a60bb91ac0603eaf08d1934890ab71474b) went live in the AI Worker at **2026-11-26T03:51:01Z** (Cloudflare deployment). `zone_sd` defaults to on, so it adds nothing to the switch signature and the era carries (orchestrator ruling 2026-11-26: the behaviour fingerprint V14 is unchanged and the live-settings replay was identical). A v4 parity reader that needs to split before and after Z6 uses that timestamp: decisions with `bar_close` at or after 2026-11-26T04:00Z are the first that can carry `/sd` levels.
  - **Setting.** `zone_sd` off/on, default "on" in `OMEGA_DEFAULTS` (the owner's decision); `settingsFrom` validates it; owner-only (Aether's policy write and its rollback strip it; the FS1 panel's Omega row forwards to `writeSettings`). Off: Omega and the Position Manager are main's byte for byte, whatever the signal carries.
  - **Safety (binding).** SL+ (`sdKeyPlan`): a box counts as passed only once wholly passed (keyLevelPlan's own rule), and the plan over the box levels is taken only where its stop is tighter than today's (or today's makes none), so a box level never loosens a stop and no stop sits looser than the giveback cap. Trailing TP: the box plan's target only where it is nearer. Ladder (`sdLadderPlan`): a box rung at its far edge, its lock only where tighter than today's; the partials, and the TP1 lock of Amendment 4, keep today's levels. Position Manager: TP2 may come nearer (a box level beyond TP1); the ladder hold and the bounded loosening (A8) read today's levels; the Position Council keeps 12 levels (`councilLevels`: more than 12, the 12 nearest to price, box levels counted inside them). `zoneAhead` is unchanged.
  - **Marker** (orchestrator 2026-11-26). A stop or target move whose price the box plan set (SL+, the trailing TP, the ladder's lock) carries `sd: true` in its `omega_intents` payload; `level_source` keeps its meaning (`"zone"`: the typing set the price). Prices, reasons and ids are unchanged, and the EA never sees it (the bridge writes fixed fields). `zone_sd` off or no winning box level: the payload byte for byte. `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 63e1f4dd… → 2e40fac9…; V14 unchanged; the era carries.
  - **Era and hashes.** `zone_sd` is in `ERA_SWITCHES`; at its default it adds nothing to the switch signature, so at the merge the era is carried (a new code hash, the same behaviour hash); the owner's "off" starts a new era at the settings' `updated_ms`. `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 70930517… → 63e1f4dd… (`managementFields`, `brokerKeyLevels`, `manageIntents`, `settingsFrom`, `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`, `brokerLevels`, `positionFacts`, `positionCouncilMessages`, `runPositionManager`, and the new `zoneSdOn`, `sdMarks`, `sdKeyPlan`, `sdLadderLevels`, `sdLadderPlan`, `ZONE_SD_MODES`, `councilLevels`, `COUNCIL_LEVELS_MAX`); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (V14: its fixtures carry no `/sd` token). v4 replays each decision's recorded `sd_levels` (DECISION_SQL extracts them).
- **Lens B in one management level set (Track B, B17, 2026-11-26; owner 2026-11-22: "tidak perlu shadow, langsung integrasikan ... position dan lainnya"; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §4.4).** `brokerKeyLevels(signal, { spread })` is the management mode (`managementSpread`: the hour's spread median `liquidity_spread_median` × the symbol's point, null when unknown).
  - **The set.** Lens B's levels join only while `liquidity_on` and `feed_basis_known` both hold; one within two spreads of a Key Level zone (a box level too) merges into it (the zone keeps its edges; the duplicate goes); an unknown spread merges nothing. Without `spread` (the limit council's moves, errata E0) the function is as before.
  - **Readers.** `manageIntents` (SL+, the trailing TP, the ladder), the ladder's TP2 wait in `planIntents`, the Position Manager (`runPositionManager`) and v4's Position Manager step. A stop or target move that the plan without lens B's levels would not make carries `level_source: "liquidity"` (a zone-typed one keeps `"zone"`).
  - **The Position Manager.** Its council-visible fields stay on `brokerLevels` (the Key Level Agent's zones). The management set goes in beside them (`positionFacts({ ..., managementLevels })`); only with a lens B level in it do the facts carry `management` { tp2, tp2_source, ladder_hold, loosen_levels, liquidity }, read only by `positionAction`'s TP2 move, the ladder's hold and the bounded loosening. The council sees lens B only as `liquidity_levels` [{ kind, side, distance_atr }] (5m ATR, at most 12, nearest first) and one prompt sentence, never a price; without a lens B level its messages are byte for byte as before.
  - **Switches.** `PM_LINES_OFF=liquidity` (or the B5 breaker) reads the management set as absent in the Position Manager (`lines_json.cause` `liquidity@1` when lens B changed the action); `liquidity_enabled` (owner) turns lens B off everywhere. No new reason.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 12cdf5a5… → 9766e732… (`brokerKeyLevels`, the new `managementSpread` in `ENGINE_CODE`, `manageIntents`, `planIntents`, `positionFacts`, `positionAction`, `pmLinesJson`, `positionCouncilMessages`, `runPositionManager`, `replayOmega4`); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (V14's fixtures carry no lens B).
- **Lens B members as management candidates (Track B, B18, 2026-11-26; owner 2026-11-22; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §5 item 5).** The Key Level Agent's family `broker_liquidity` (`withBrokerLiquidity`, weight 1) names the zones a lens B member would promote to candidate (`key_levels.management`, at most one per side; only uncapped non-candidate zones; families + 1 ≥ 3 and score + 1 ≥ 3). The zones, candidates, plans and entries never change.
  - **Gates.** `loadStrategyInputs` passes `brokerLiquidity` (the ledger step's `levels_feed`) only with lens B on and above its floor, a fresh state and a known feed basis (`brokerLiquidityInputs`); `KEY_LEVEL_FAMILIES_OFF=broker_liquidity` turns the family off through `modes.keyLevelFamiliesOff` only. The ledger step writes `feed_basis`, `feed_basis_known` and `levels_feed` into `liquidity:state:v1`.
  - **room_liquidity.** A guardrail check after `room` (inside SQUEEZE_GUARD), only with management candidates: it fails only when they make the squeeze (never when `room` already fails); the RM1b waiver never lifts it; it only adds a WAIT; its detail is in ATR. `decision.squeeze`, the `room` check and room-tuning's fidelity are as before. The public recap leaves it out of `held_back` (errata E4); the model's features leave it out.
  - **Management.** The decision's `management_levels` reach the signal's flat `management_levels`, read only by Omega's management mode (`brokerKeyLevels` with `spread`, as zones with `source: "liquidity"`, `kind: "zone"`); `/api/key-levels` (Nexus) shows them.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 9766e732… → b4077f53… (`brokerKeyLevels`, `managementFields`); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged.
- **Lens B's effects, recorded and scored (Track B, B19, 2026-11-26; owner 2026-11-22; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §7.2, §15).** Two new `lens_readings` kinds, reported only and never in the floor: `lensFloorStep` counts `FLOOR_KINDS` (`sweep`, `block`, `maker`).
  - **skip.** The limit council's entry skips whose rule names `liquidity_sweep_against` (`recordSkipReadings`, the last 14 days). One `skip:<issued_ms>:B|S` per signal and side, scored as a block from `ledger_omega4` (a v4 loss avoided is a hit).
  - **hold.** The stop moves Omega made to a lens B level (`lensHoldStep`): `omega_intents` `modify_sl`/`modify_sltp`, done and applied, `level_source: "liquidity"`, the last 3 days. One reading per ticket and stop price (`holdReadingId`: a hash, so no price). Detail `{ what: "ladder" | "trail" }`: "trail" is the SL+ key-level stop move, never the trailing TP (named so because the lens report refuses "sl" and "stop"). `scoreLevelHold` reads the next eight closed 15m bars of the broker's M1 archive: a close more than 0.1 × ATR(14, 15m) beyond the stop is a miss; eight bars without one is a hit.
  - **The ladder's tag.** The ladder's lock carries `level_source: "liquidity"` only when its rung is lens B's exact edge and no other level shares that edge (`manageIntents`; the only engine change). With the lens off, or no lens B rung, it is byte for byte today's.
  - **Switch.** `LENS_LEVEL_READINGS` (group `lens`, default "on"; "off" stops both kinds' recording and scoring), on the FS1 panel. The Nexus liquidity view carries each skip's `outcome` and `effects.level_moves` (action `ladder_lock` or `key_level_lock`, the SL+ move; no price; null when off).
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` b4077f53… → 20e78488… (`manageIntents`); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged.
- **Same-side re-entry guard** (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; `reentryGate` in `src/omega-engine.mjs`, the latest exit from `lastWeakeningExit` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`). After a Position Manager close for weakening (`pm_take_profit_weakening`) or a cut loss (`pm_cut_loss`) in the last 6 hours, a new entry on the same side waits only until the next M5 candle closes after the exit (`reentry_cooldown_bars`, 1 closed M5 candle, 0 to 12; note `reentry_cooldown` with the time it frees). Owner 2026-11-18 (confirmed: "Setuju keduanya"): the 0.5×ATR pullback and the confidence bypass were removed. Exits by TP, the ladder or the stop are not weakening exits; the other side is never blocked. The 1m re-entry after a profit-lock stop (RE1, above) is apart from it: it is never offered after these exits, and the cooldown applies to a re-entry too.
- **TP ladder** (owner 2026-11-18, relayed and confirmed: "a, langsung live, termasuk agent juga bisa dinamis mengatur TP sebagian secara progresive"; every mode, Scalp included; `ladderPlan` in `src/omega-engine.mjs`, setting `tp_ladder`, on by default). It replaces the single TP1 scale-out of Omega's own positions; the other management above is unchanged.
  - **Rungs.** TP1..TPn are the near edges of the key levels wholly between the entry and the take-profit, then the take-profit itself. Without such a level, a third and two thirds of the way to the take-profit, then the take-profit.
  - **SL lock, per mode** (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; `ladderStep`, `LADDER_CONFIRM_TF`). The step is the mode the position opened in (recorded on its open intent with `r_dist`, its stop distance at entry). Scalp, or an unknown mode, uses `ladder_lock_pips` (30, a pip 0.1); intraday uses `ladder_r_intraday` (0.5) × R; swing uses `ladder_r_swing` (0.3) × R. One step past the entry, the stop goes to break-even plus the buffer (TP0); one step past TPx, it goes to TPx (owner 2026-11-18, in the owner's own words: "TP1+1 langkah geser ke TP1, TP2+1 langkah, geser ke TP2, dan seterusnya") (`ms:<ticket>:lad:<cents>`, reason `ladder_lock_tp<n>`). The trigger is the mode's last CLOSED broker candle, built from the M1 corpus (scalp M5, intraday M15, swing H1): its close must be beyond the level and the candle in the trade's direction (no reversal candle). With no such candle, or no R for an R mode, no new lock. The lock wins only when it is tighter than a stop already planned in the same sync, is never looser than the stop, and keeps the broker's stop distance. "The stop" is the tightest of the position's snapshot and the `modify_sl` intents of the last `STOP_COMMAND_MS` (5 minutes) still pending or sent, or done with an ok result (`commandedStops`, `withCommandedStop`): a snapshot taken before a modification landed never loosens it (2026-11-19, ticket 362785677: three modifications in seven seconds, 3719.19, 3719.23, 3719.19). The break-even lock is taken once: a stop already past the entry is not moved again at TP0, whose buffer follows the spread and the M5 ATR. Since SLG1 (2026-11-23) every ladder lock waits for the profit-lock gate, and TP0 is the gate's first lock (see the Position Manager section above).
  - **Partials.** At each rung reached, one partial close of the current volume, at most `ladder_max_partials` (3) a position and one per sync: `pl:<ticket>:<n>`, reason `ladder_tp<n>_<pct>pct`. The share follows the Decision Engine's power behind the position (fresh 35 minutes): strong `partial_min_pct` (25), weak or reversed `partial_max_pct` (50), otherwise between. The volume is floored to the broker's step and must leave at least the minimum open, so a 0.01 position gets the lock only. OmegaExecutor's `partial_close` (since 1.0) runs it; no EA change.
  - **Exposure only goes down:** no add, no wider stop. Under platform ADR-0020 Amendment 4 (merged 2026-11-18), the take-profit may move outward as often as the key levels allow (the trailing TP by key level, the extended target), but only while the stop already locks a profit at or beyond TP1 (`ladderTp1Locked`: the current stop or one planned in the same sync). Before that, an outward move is dropped. Knobs: `partial_min_pct` 10 to 50, `partial_max_pct` 25 to 75 (min not above max), `ladder_lock_pips` 5 to 100, `ladder_max_partials` 0 to 5.
- **Intents:** ids of at most 31 characters (the MT5 order comment); `pending` → `sent` (first returned) → `done` or `failed` by the bridge's result, or `expired` after 2 minutes (a late result still lands). An open is never retried. A close or kill still needed after its attempt ended gets the next attempt id (`<id>:<n>`, at most 10). The bridge never executes an id twice.
  - **Reconciled from the terminal** (a result line that never arrived): an open from the open position carrying its id, a stop or target change from the position showing it (`reconcileFilledOpens`, `reconcileModifies`, `reconciled: true`), and since CA1 (2026-11-25, `src/close-reconcile.mjs`) a close from its deal. Measured 2026-11-18 to 2026-11-25: 10 of 28 close intents expired with no result although the ticket's deal closed 0.3 to 1.2 s after the intent, reason `expert`. A close (`pending`, `sent` or `expired`, no result; never a `failed` or `done` one, so the `:1` retries created after the deal stay failed) becomes `done` only when all hold: the deal is the ticket's closing deal (its last exit deal in `ea_deals`, the position gone from the terminal's report, the exit deals covering the opened volume where the open intent records it: a partial close never counts); its reason is `expert` (sl, tp, stop-out and client closes never); its time is at or after the intent's `created_ms` and at most 5 s after it; one deal one intent (of the close, `partial_close` and `close_all` intents whose window holds the deal only the earliest may take it, and only a close with no result; a deal already taken is never used again). The result carries `reconciled_from: "ea_deals"`, `deal_ticket`, `executed_ms` (the deal's time), `fill_price` (its exit price), `reconciled: true`; the audit row is `intent_reconciled` (actor `engine`), never an EA acknowledgement. A later EA result does not change a done intent. **Where:** the bridge sync, when a deal lands or a close is about to expire, before the TTL expiry (a reconciled Position Manager close gets no "not applied" line); and once, a 30-day backfill from the 5-minute ledger step behind KV `omega:close_reconcile:backfill:v1` (pages of 500, the marker written only once the window is read; the open tickets from the stored terminal state). `position_actions` is unchanged: a Position Manager action is `applied` when it is planned into an intent, and the eight tied to these closes already read `applied`. Readers that change: B5's breaker (it waits up to 30 minutes for the intent to read done; reconciled, the close is credited to its own line instead of falling back), the same-side re-entry guard (`lastWeakeningExit`: a reconciled `pm_take_profit_weakening` or `pm_cut_loss` close starts its cooldown), the open intents the sync returns (a reconciled close is no longer delivered, so calm mode can start sooner), the profile council's and Call-for-Councils packs, Nexus analytics and the Omega page (they show done). `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` unchanged (no hashed function changed). The EA and the bridge are unchanged.
  - **Lost acks at the source: the results-file race** (owner 2026-11-26: "Ya, bridge saja"; omega-bridge 1.7.1, no EA change; docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md "Results-file race"). The EA opens `omega/results.txt` (creating it), then writes; a native rename inside that window moved the file under the EA's handle (Wine's locks do not stop it), so the line landed in a `.done` file already read. Since 1.7.1 the bridge renames `results.txt` only after 400 ms without a write (a watcher event wakes a sync only after 400 ms of quiet; a tick waits for it, at most 1 s), reads again every `.done` file modified in the last 60 s, reads complete lines only (an incomplete last line keeps its file read and unpruned for up to 10 minutes, then `result_partial_line_dropped`), and sends each intent id once (reported ids kept 30 minutes, at most 5000, persisted in the state dir as `reported-results.json`, so a restarted bridge re-sends nothing). A line found only on a re-read is logged `result_recovered`. Server side, a result lifts the "EA never answered" limit fault (`clearNoAnswerFault`) only when its intent was still pending, sent or expired at receipt (`RESULT_OPEN_STATUSES`): a late line for a done or failed intent clears nothing. CA1 stays: it still covers a write that never happens (the EA's FileOpen failing). The Worker is unchanged and checks no minimum bridge version.
- **Page** (`nexus.cryptophys.work/omega/` and `omega.cryptophys.work/`): Broker connection, Engine (settings, Arm live), Bridge & terminal, Intents, Engine events. Every URL the page fetches or loads is relative. The static page at `omega.cryptophys.work/` is Omega's own fallback console (owner 2026-11-22, mockup approved): a status strip with the Nexus header's Omega codes from `./api/status` (`public/omega-status.js`, a plain-JS copy of `lib/headerStatus.ts` kept equal by `test/omega-page-status.test.mjs`), the EA heartbeat and the clock in UTC plus local time; a menu of its own sections (a drawer on phones); two absolute links to Nexus (`https://nexus.cryptophys.work/#/omega`, `#/`), its only absolute URLs (the old `../#/` links looped back to Omega itself there); a banner on a failed read; reads only while the tab is visible.
- **Engine form, Default** (owner 2026-11-20: "Follow the active profile"; `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui/src/lib/engineDefaults.ts`). Setup → Engine's Default button fills the form only: risk, max positions and max daily loss from the connected account's profile (the overview's `accounts[].profile_limits`: `target_risk_pct`, `max_positions`, `daily_loss_pct` of `PROFILES`), max spread and signal age from `OMEGA_DEFAULTS` (the overview's `engine_defaults`); Conservative fills 2 / 2 / 6 / 60 / 10. No connected account, or a value the profile does not set (Custom): `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`. Save writes it through `POST /omega/api/settings` (audited); Default never touches `enabled`, `live_armed`, Arm or the profile. Under each field above what the profile applies, a note ("5% set, 2% applied (Conservative cap)"; the smaller of the two, as `effectiveLimits`).
- **Change audit on Telegram** (owner 2026-11-20: "harus ada notif Telegram karena buat mengetahui dan audit"; `src/change-notify.mjs`). Every audit row of a change that affects trading (`omega_events`: settings, the profile and its ceiling, agent mode and baseline, the trading mode, delegation, a hand modify or close, the connection, the bridge symbol) becomes a private line to the owner's chat: who (owner, an agent, Aether), old → new, the time; never an email, login, server or account reference. A switch saved several times in a row is one line per switch per minute with the value it ended on (KV `audit:notify:v1`: the cursor, the settings last told, the held changes). The sweep runs on the 5-minute ledger cron, on every 15-minute sweep while the market is closed, and right after every settings save; its first run starts at the newest row and sends nothing. Every `omega_events` action is in one list of `src/change-notify.mjs`: told by the sweep (`AUDIT_ACTIONS`), told where it happens (`TOLD_ELSEWHERE`), or not a change (`NOT_A_CHANGE`); a test fails on a new one. A line Telegram does not take keeps the cursor for the next sweep; after three sweeps in a row the cursor moves on and the health alert `audit_telegram_failed` says so. Identity keys and price keys are dropped by exact name. The EA control (pause, kill, resume) is told at once by `setControl`. The arm, probation and lens-floor lines stay where they were.
- **Entry council, first decision** (`src/limit-council.mjs` `firstCouncilLine`): the first decision in council mode is told once (KV `limit_council:first_council:v1`), with its side, mode, choice, rule and reason.
- **Fail closed on limit results** (owner 2026-11-20, the demo test skipped; `recordLimitFault` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`). A `place_limit`, `modify_order` or `delete_order` intent the EA never answered (it expires with no result) or answered as placed without a ticket, created after the owner's last save, while `entry_order` is limit or council, opens the limit fault: `omega_settings.limit_fault` (read with the settings the sync reads anyway: no query per sync), one `limit_fail_closed` audit row and one Telegram line, once per episode (the stop's own deletions that expire add nothing). While it is open the sync runs with `entry_order` market (resting Omega limits are deleted, `entry_order_market`) and the plan notes `limit_fail_closed`. The two faults differ (owner 2026-11-21, "Split"): **no ticket** → market entries until the owner saves the settings (`owner_saved_ms`; an Aether policy write or rollback does not clear it); **never answered** → the plan runs as paused (no new entry, no loosening; closes and protection still go out; a kill stays a kill) until the EA answers any intent again, or, when the EA was silent itself at the fault, until its heartbeat is fresh again at 1.21 or later (the next limit is the probe) (`clearNoAnswerFault`, one `limit_fault_cleared` row and one Telegram line), or the owner saves. While open, the health alert `limit_fail_closed` shows it with its age. A `wrangler secret put` creates a deployment with no message, which the deploy drift guard reads as drift and answers with a redeploy of `main` (harmless: secrets survive a deploy).

### Feature switches (FS1: one owner control surface; owner 2026-11-25, relayed by the orchestrator: "supaya bisa dikontrol perlu panel khusus pengaturan on off untuk semua fitur ini"; `src/feature-switches.mjs`, `src/feature-switch-store.mjs`)
- **What.** Every owner-facing feature switch on one panel (Nexus, Settings › Feature switches), changed at run time without a deploy. No behaviour change at merge: no override exists until the owner sets one, and with none every run gets env itself.
- **Registry** (`src/feature-switches.mjs`, pure). One entry per switch: `key`, `label`, `description` (one line, with any tooltip note), `group`, `worker` (`ai` | `omega`), `values` (the values the panel may set, or `{ type: "tokens", tokens }` for a token list), `default_value` (the code default), `config_value` (wrangler.jsonc's, pinned by the test against the file), `era_switch` (in `ERA_SWITCHES`: a parity era starts on change), `restarts_mode_evidence` (`TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` and every `ZONE_*` switch with `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES`: the rule version moves, so the mode selector's evidence restarts), `emergency_stop`, `read_only` and `write` (`feature-switch`: the override store; `omega-settings`: the Omega settings page's own write path). Each entry reads a raw value with exactly its accessor's rule (test FS-2 pins every one).
- **Override store.** KV `feature-switch:override:v1` (`SIGNAL_CACHE`): `{ KEY: { value, actor, at } }`, `value` in the env form (what wrangler.jsonc would hold: `ZONE_AIM_OFF` keeps the off-list, `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED` `true`/`false`), `actor` a role word (`owner`), never an email. Written only by the AI Worker, through the owner's POST. An override the panel could not have written (an unknown or read-only key, an Omega setting, a config-only value such as `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` `record`, a non-canonical text) is ignored with one log line (`feature_switch_override_ignored`).
- **Resolution.** Owner override, then the env (wrangler) value, then the code default. `withFeatureSwitches` resolves it once per run onto a copy of env at every entry point of the AI Worker: `fetch` (after the CORS preflight), `queue`, `scheduled` (after the idle ledger step's early return, which still reads nothing) and the RPC entrypoints `LocalComputationIngress`, `PublicProjection` and `OmegaGateway` (each method starts with `this.fs()`; `this.env` becomes the copy, env itself is never written). With no valid override the run gets env itself, the same object; the copy is kept while the store does not change, so the per-binding memos keep working. The store is read at most every 30 s per isolate (`FEATURE_SWITCH_MEMO_MS`; KV itself spreads in about a minute); the isolate that wrote sees the change at once. An unreadable store falls back to env (one log line, `feature_switch_store_unreadable`). `AGENT_VETO_RULE` is never copied: its override is SV1b's owner force, read by `resolveAgentVetoRule` before the automatic revert and the env value (owner force > auto-revert > env, test FS-10). The copy also carries `FEATURE_SWITCH_OVERRIDDEN` (an enumerable symbol, never in JSON: the keys the override set), so `ROOM_BREAKOUT_WAIVE` (RM1b), copied like any env row, knows an override from the config for its own order owner override > auto-revert > env (test RW-8). No other Worker reads any of these keys.
- **API.** `GET /api/feature-switches` on the AI Worker's read API (through Nexus' `/api/*` proxy, behind Access; `viewer.can_control` false there) and `GET /omega/api/feature-switches` on the Omega Worker (any signed-in person reads; `viewer.can_control` true only for a person whose Access user id is `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB`, checked by Omega and again by the AI Worker). Payload `{ version: "cryptophys.feature-switches.v1", at, viewer: { can_control }, groups: [{ id, label }], switches: [{ key, label, description, group, worker, values, value, source, config_value, default_value, set_by, set_at, era_switch, restarts_mode_evidence, emergency_stop, read_only, write }] }`; a token list's `values` is `{ type: "tokens", tokens, on }` and its `value` the on-list; `source` is `override`, `config` or `default`; `set_by` a role word or null. The `AGENT_VETO_RULE` and `ROOM_BREAKOUT_WAIVE` rows also carry `auto_revert` (`{ since, until }` while the automatic revert holds and no override is set, else null; the `value` is then the one in force). Z5: the `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` row carries `zone_hold` (`{ since, until, by }` while the zone breaker holds zone_ahead on the decision side, else null) and the `ZONE_AIM_OFF` row `zone_hold` as a map of the tokens held (`{ tp, stop, factor }`, each `{ since, until, by }`; null when none), from one KV read of `zone-priority:breaker:v1` (unreadable: null; a hold on another rule version than the overrides resolve to: not shown). Their `value` stays the owner's: the hold is decision-side only.
- **POST, owner only.** `POST /omega/api/feature-switches` (a person, same origin, JSON; `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB` set and equal to the Access user id; the route pattern of the council toggle) → `OmegaGateway.setFeatureSwitch`, which checks the owner again (`setFeatureSwitch`, no new auth path). Body `{ key, value }` to set or `{ key, reset: true }` to go back to the config (`AGENT_VETO_RULE` `auto` is a reset). Token lists take the on-list. Reply `{ ok: true, switch, changed }` or `{ ok: false, error }`, error `unknown_key`, `invalid_value`, `read_only`, `owner_only` (or `unavailable` when the store cannot be read). The same value again changes nothing and tells nothing.
  - **A feature-switch row:** the override written, one audit row in `omega_events` (action `feature_switch`, actor `owner`, detail `{ key, from, to, source_from, source_to, reset }`: the Omega event; classified as told where it happens in the change audit) and one Telegram line ("XAUUSD SYSTEM · Feature switch <label> (<KEY>) set by the owner: <from> (<source>) → <to> (<source>).").
  - **An Omega settings row** (`profit_lock_gate`, `change_thresholds`, `pm_fast_1m`, `reentry_1m`, `limit_supersede`, `cd_gate`, `zone_mgmt`, `zone_sd`): forwarded to `writeSettings`, the Omega settings page's own path (`settingsFrom`'s validation, the `settings` audit row told once by the change audit's sweep, `updated_ms`). One store: D1 `omega_settings`, never the KV override. Its era starts exactly as the page's save starts it (test FS-9). Reset goes to `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`, source `default`. Ruling 3: `omega_settings.key_meta` (added by `ensureSchema`) keeps each setting's last `{ by, at }` (role word) on every save, the page's and Aether's included; `updated_ms` stays the row's time for the era.
- **The switches** (E: emergency stop; R: restarts the mode evidence; X: era switch; RO: read-only):
  - zones: `ZONE_PRIORITY` (on/off; E, R), `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` (trend/all; `record`, `off` config-only; R), `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES` (on/off; E, R), `ZONE_AIM_OFF` (tokens tp, stop, factor, the panel's on-list; R), `ZONE_SOURCES_OFF` (tokens prophet_zone, prophet_best, whale, gabfx, gmi_flag, gmi_ob; not in the config; R), `ZONE_AIM_TP` (label/qualified, qualified only after the owner's TP1-KZ amendment; R), `ZONE_READINGS` (on/off; Z5's readings, counterfactual tables, breaker and `GET /api/zones`; no mode-evidence restart, not an emergency stop), `SD_LEVELS` (off/record/signal; Z6's box levels; production "signal", code default "off");
  - plans: `ENTRY_ZONE_MODE` (edge/on/off), `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` (on/off; R), `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` (on/off), `GRID_ADAPT_MODE` (live/off), `ROOM_BREAKOUT_WAIVE` (on/off; RM1b, with its own automatic revert: the row carries `auto_revert`; in plans because it decides whether a plan may enter, while the zones group is the Z-series typing whose switches all restart the mode evidence), `PM_LINES_OFF` (tokens: the Position Manager lines, the panel's on-list; not in the config; B5);
  - agents: `AGENT_VETO_RULE` (two_sided/classic), `CFC_MODE` (off/suggest/on);
  - modes: `MODE_SHADOW_EVIDENCE`, `MODE_PROBATION` (on/off);
  - lens: `LIQUIDITY_WITNESS` (on/off), `LENS_ROOM_LINE` (conditions/brief), `CALENDAR_CONTRARIAN` (on/off);
  - knowledge: `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED` (on/off, the variable true/false; E), `KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_POSTS` (on/off; needs the arm), `METRIC_REBUTTAL` (on/off, anything but off is on and it needs `METRIC_SHADOW`; RO: retired by the owner 2026-11-19), `OWNER_CORRECTIONS` (on/off, default off, not in wrangler.jsonc; HAIC H3f: the owner's corrections recorded through `POST /omega/api/correction` and sent as nexus-teacher); `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER` (on/off, default off, not in wrangler.jsonc; SB1: the news shadow comparison on Beam, see "Shadow model router" in §3); `MODEL_GBM_CONNECT` (on/off, default off, not in wrangler.jsonc; MA1 (b): the decision model may advise the council once its gate passes, see "Baseline model" in §3);
  - omega: `profit_lock_gate` (on/legacy; X), `change_thresholds` (tokens conf, tpw, limit, skip, stance, or none; X), `pm_fast_1m`, `reentry_1m`, `limit_supersede`, `cd_gate` (on/off; X; `cd_gate` with its own automatic revert to off, CDG), `zone_mgmt` (off/omega, default off; X; Z3a, owner-only: no automatic rule writes it), `zone_sd` (on/off, default on; X; Z6, owner-only).
- **The panel** (Nexus, Settings › Feature switches, `#/switches`, the last navigation group; `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui/src/pages/Switches.tsx`, `lib/featureSwitches.ts`; the owner-reviewed mockup). It polls `GET /omega/api/feature-switches` every 60 s and shows each row as its label and key, its control on the same line (a toggle for on/off in the ok cyan, a segmented control for any other value list, token toggles for a token list), a source chip (override amber, config plain, default grey) and the era, ev and emergency-stop marks; the description, config, default, set at (UTC and GMT+7), set by (a role word) and the automatic revert are in each row's ⓘ tooltip. Controls only when `viewer.can_control`, never on a read-only row; every change confirms with what changes (`KEY: from → to`, "Starts a new parity era.", "Restarts mode evidence."), an emergency stop needs its key typed, and an override has its reset (Omega settings: reset to default). Its one write is `POST /omega/api/feature-switches`; each error code shows on its row, and a saved feature-switch row's tooltip notes that it takes effect within ~30 s. A value in force the panel cannot set (a `config_only` value such as `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` `record` from wrangler.jsonc) shows as text beside the settable values, its tooltip saying why. The Omega page is unchanged.
- **Not on the panel:** secrets and bindings, `OMEGA_ENGINE_ENABLED`, the kill switch (`EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED`, Omega `killed`), the strategy arms and their ceiling (`STRATEGY_ARM_MODE`, `STRATEGY_COUNCIL_ARM`), the `LIVE` arm, the account profiles, the model ids and call budgets, the data-source settings, and the Omega settings that stay on the Engine panel (`entry_order`, `pm_loosen_enabled`, the lens switches, risk and limits).
- **Hashes.** No `ENGINE_CODE` function changes: `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (V13, V14) and the parity era are unchanged.

### Omega's policy from Aether (B-12, issue #227; owner 2026-11-11 "All")
- Hourly, the AI Worker pulls `AETHER.omegaPolicy()`: the doctrine-in-force values of `omega.risk_pct`, `omega.max_positions`, `omega.max_daily_loss_pct`, `omega.max_spread_points` and `omega.max_signal_age_min`. It stores them in KV `omega:policy` (`src/omega-policy.mjs`).
- **Off by default.** `applyOmegaPolicy` applies the pack only while `OMEGA_POLICY_AUTO` is `"true"` or the page's `policy_auto` is on.
- **Applying.** Each value is clamped to Omega's hard bounds (`OMEGA_POLICY_BOUNDS`). The change is audited in `omega_events` as `settings`, actor `aether`, and reported to Telegram. An unchanged pack does nothing.
- **Rollback.** After 50 Omega trades under a policy, it is reverted (actor `aether-rollback`, Telegram) when its profit factor is below 1 and below the 50 trades before it. Its doctrine sha is then never applied again.
- **The owner's switches.** The policy write and the rollback carry as stored, and never write, `entry_order`, `pm_loosen_enabled`, `profit_lock_gate`, `change_thresholds`, `pm_fast_1m`, `reentry_1m`, `limit_supersede`, `cd_gate`, `zone_mgmt` (Z3a) and `zone_sd` (Z6): each is deleted from the body before `writeSettings`, so `settingsFrom` keeps the stored value.
- **Trade source.** Trades are told apart by `ea_deals.source` (`omega`, `manual`, `external`, `ea`; a column added 2026-11-11 from the bridge's deals).

### Corpus Oracle (owner 2026-11-11: "persiapkan engine baru bernama corpus oracle yang akan melakukan feeding data ke local lane dari live mt5 terminal sesuai dengan broker, pair dan user active dari omega"; `src/corpus-oracle.mjs`, docs/RFC_CORPUS_ORACLE.md)
- **Phase 1, the corpus** (`CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE = "shadow"` since 2026-11-11, owner: "Nyalakan, host sudah #243", collecting for the MT5 fallback of `docs/RFC_MT5_FALLBACK.md`; `"off"` ignores the bars). The Omega executor writes the broker's closed M1 bars of the symbol it trades (`omega/corpus.json`, UTC, no login), the bridge sends those after the server's cursor with each sync (`corpus`), and the AI Worker keeps a bar only from Omega's active broker (the terminal's server equals the stored connection's), pair (the executor's symbol) and user (the stored connection, its version recorded): D1 `corpus_bars` (14 days), `corpus_sources`; the sync answers `corpus_cursor_ms`.
- **Measured** at `GET /api/corpus/status`: freshness, missing minutes, complete 15m bars and the basis against the TradingView 15m series (mean offset broker minus TradingView, its spread, largest offset, return correlation).
- **Phase 2, the broker shadow lane** (2026-11-11). The pull adapter runs the 15m engines once more on the broker's M15 bars at each published 15m close (`broker-shadow.mjs`, `BROKER_SHADOW_MODE`). It sends only the field-by-field agreement with TradingView's records (`recordBrokerShadow`, D1 `broker_shadow`), shown under `shadow` on `GET /api/corpus/status`. It registers no identity and feeds no parity, room, decision or signal. It needs 400 broker M15 bars and is skipped while the MT5 fallback runs.
- **Feeds the MT5 fallback only** (since 2026-11-11, `docs/RFC_MT5_FALLBACK.md`, owner: trade as usual on the broker's raw price). While the TradingView gateway is down and the market trades, the pull adapter reads the active source's bars (`LocalComputationIngress.corpusBars`, `readCorpusBars`) and merges them into `OANDA:XAUUSD|1m` (`src: "mt5"`, `fallback.since_sec`); 5m, 15m and 1H aggregate from them (`FEED_FALLBACK_MODE = "auto"`, `market-data-ingestion/src/mt5-fallback.mjs`). Every local record, the 5m context and every decision built on them carry `feed: "mt5_fallback"`; parity skips those records (`fallback_skipped`, no approval earned or lost); the decision adds a caution and keeps its guardrails; the `market_data_fallback` alert and Telegram report the start and the end. When the gateway answers, native pulls over the fallback range (`healPlan`, once per 5 minutes) overwrite the broker bars and the derived series re-aggregate. Since 2026-11-11 (owner: "input untuk AI Worker bisa sama persis tidak ada regresi") the 4H and daily series are rebuilt too, on the grid of their last native bar (OANDA's session start, 22:00 UTC in winter; `aggregateCandles` `anchor`), and only for a bucket the 1m series covers from its start; the weekly bar waits for TradingView. **Stage 2** (2026-11-11, owner: no regression in the AI Worker's input): OmegaExecutor 1.10 to 1.12 also wrote (since 1.13 off by default, `CorpusMacroEnabled`, owner 2026-11-12: "data makro dimatikan di mt5": the broker supplies XAUUSD only) the broker's M1 bars of silver, oil and the dollar index's six pairs (`omega/corpus_macro.json`, inputs `CorpusMacroSymbols`, alternatives with `|`, the gold symbol's suffix tried first) and the USD economic calendar of MQL5 (`omega/calendar.json`, every 5 minutes); bridge 1.1.0 sends them after per-base cursors (`corpus_macro`, `calendar`; dropped first when a sync is too large); the AI Worker keeps them only from Omega's active broker and user (`ingestCorpusMacro`, sources `mt5macro:<server>:<BASE>`; `ingestMt5Calendar`, D1 `mt5_calendar`, 30 days).
