During a fallback the pull adapter rebuilds TVC:USOIL 5m/15m/1H, OANDA:XAGUSD 4H and TVC:DXY 4H (the ICE formula from the six pairs, per minute where all six have a bar) on their own grids, for covered buckets only (`applyMacroFallback`, `corpusBars` with a base); when TradingView's calendar fails, the econ step takes the released values from the MQL5 calendar (`applyMt5Actuals`: the waiting row of the same minute, so the series' history stays one; **CG1, 2026-11-27**: only when the names agree (`titlesAgree`: shared words at least `TITLE_MIN_JACCARD` 0.6 and the same qualifiers, core, private, U6, real, control, import, export), the period words agree (`periodOf`: m/m, y/y, q/q) and the scale agrees (`sameScale`: within `MT5_SCALE_MAX_RATIO` 10, or both under 1); otherwise the event is stored under its own title with ` MQL5` and its own series. Replay of 2026-11-05..25: the old kind rule put 19 of 28 values on the wrong row, CG1 puts 10 on the right row and 0 on a wrong one). `ingestMt5Calendar` rewrites an event only when a value changed. US10Y, the USDT market cap and news have no broker source. **The owner's switch** (War Room card "Data source · MT5 fallback", owner 2026-11-11): `GET`/`POST /omega/api/feed-fallback` (Cloudflare Access; a change needs a person, the same origin and a reason) → `OmegaGateway.feedFallback`/`setFeedFallback` (`src/feed.mjs`) → KV `market-data:v2:feed-fallback-mode` (`auto` or `off`), which the pull adapter reads before each fallback step (`resolveFallbackMode`; unset means its `FEED_FALLBACK_MODE`, `auto`). Each change is audited in D1 `feed_fallback_events` and sent to Telegram. Off: while TradingView is down, prices stop and decisions hold WAIT; a fallback already running keeps its bars until TradingView heals them. Otherwise no room, decision, plan or signal reads the corpus. Phase 2 (a reviewed PR) computes the local engines on the broker bars as shadow identities; they reach the decision lane only by earning parity (doctrine `parity_trust_only`).

### Positioning & Liquidity analyst (owner 2026-11-20: "Sekarang breakglass urgent jadi langsung live", "Setuju", "rekomendasi"; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md; `src/positioning-lens.mjs`, `src/liquidity-lens.mjs`, `src/lens-policy.mjs`, `src/lens-steps.mjs`)
- **One agent, two lenses, live in every role on merge** (breakglass: the owner's word is the `promotion_by_owner` act; CI, review, every hard guard, the automatic floor, "never opens alone" and "baseline: insufficient" stay). No model call. Never a price toward Aether, the kernel, the rooms or a public channel.
- **Rollback switches.** Omega settings `positioning_enabled`, `liquidity_enabled`, default on, owner only (Omega page, Setup → Engine); Aether never writes them. Off: the lens contributes nothing. Both are in `ERA_SWITCHES`; the signature compares with the default, so switching a lens off starts a new parity era.
- **Weights.** Per lens and mode, `positioning.de_weight_<mode>` (0.02/0.03/0.05/0.03) and `liquidity.council_weight_<mode>` (0.4/0.3/0.2/0.3), with the thresholds of RFC §8, start at the anchor and are clamped to their bounds. Aether's values arrive with the hourly policy pull (`AETHER.lensPolicy()` when offered, else the `omegaPolicy()` pack's lens keys) into KV `lens:policy:v1`. Lens B's weight stays at its anchor while its baseline is insufficient.
- **Score and floor.** D1 `lens_readings` (lens A: 4H readings scored on 6 bars; lens B: sweeps, spread-spike blocks scored from v4, maker choices from the limit ledger). Below `score_floor` over `score_window` (at least 20 decisive readings) the lens drops to the floor of its bound in every mode (KV `lens:floor:v1`), with `omega_events` `lens_weight_floor` and one line to the owner's private chat; only a weight under another `doctrine_sha` from Aether lifts it.
- **Lens A, CFTC COT** (`publicreporting.cftc.gov`, disaggregated `72hh-3qpy`, legacy `6dca-aqww` as the fallback, code `088691`, never mixed in one percentile). D1 `cot_reports` (lazy, 5 years); backfill 260 weeks once (KV `cot:backfill:v1`); then fetched only while a report is due (Friday 15:30 New York, Monday in a federal-holiday week), at most once an hour and 30 a week, in the sweep and the closed-market heartbeat. Point in time: `cotAt(t)` reads `released_ms <= t`. Facts: net, the week's change, the 156-week percentile, the change's z, open interest, crowding, divergence; stale after 14 days. The Decision Engine's `POSITIONING` witness (lean within ±0.6, no hourly decay) at its mode's weight, the other witnesses × (1 − w), outside the witness tuner (its replay keeps the recorded weight). A line in the 4H and 1D rooms' brief, outside the quorum; crowding and divergence in the mode council's pack and a profile upgrade's evidence. `cot_stale` health alert.
- **Lens B, broker M1 liquidity.** D1 `broker_bars_1m` (key `account_ref`, source, minute; `acct_<16 hex>` only, never the account number) from the corpus ingest, 120 days. The first one-time backfill (KV `liquidity:backfill:v1`, 2026-11-20) did not copy the corpus live: every batch bound 104 parameters, above D1's 100, and rolled back, so 2,419 of the 3,083 connection-90 `corpus_bars` minutes never reached the archive; since B2 (#530, 2026-11-22) it is backfilled from `corpus_bars` once per identity (account_ref, source and connection versions: the active one and every version already archived, each copied under its own version; KV `liquidity:backfill:v2:<identity>`; 7 rows a statement, 91 of D1's 100 bound parameters; rows added rebuild the baseline and replay the added minutes' sweeps once, driven by the stored marker, `liquidity:replay:v2:<identity>`; the identity carries a short hash of the versions; a failure is retried at the next step and logs one `liquidity_backfill_failed` per identity; the first backfill replay is expected to floor lens B, RFC §7.2-§7.3). Baseline per account and New York hour of the week over 8 weeks, reset on a new account, server, symbol or digits (`liquidity_baseline_reset`); "insufficient" below 4 weeks, with a rolling fallback; rollover quarantine, pause and session-open minutes excluded. Facts (5-minute ledger step, D1 `liquidity_readings`, KV `liquidity:state:v1`): the spike percentile, thin, the spread ratio, a reclaimed sweep, equal-high/low pools, high-volume nodes. Uses: the signal's `liquidity_*` fields; pools, sweep extremes and nodes in `brokerKeyLevels` with `source: "liquidity"` (so A8's `key_level_outside` covers pools); the entry gate `liquidity_spread_spike` after `spread`; `modify_sl`/`modify_tp`/`modify_sltp` deferred up to 10 minutes during a spike (closes, kill, the fill guard, the daily limit, any forced close never); the entry council's `liquidity_thin`, `liquidity_sweep_with`, `liquidity_sweep_against`; the Decision Engine council's per-side sweep evidence and, since B15, its council-only `LIQUIDITY` card (never counted; see "Decision Engine"); the Position Manager's `liquidity_sweep_against` (cut reason and A8 target nearer, by council only); a line in the 15m and 1H rooms' brief and, since B16, its conditions for those rooms' risk desk and moderator (below). `liquidity_archive_stale` health alert. Liquidity never opens, sizes or prices a position.
- **Lens B's conditions in the 15m and 1H rooms** (Track B, B16, 2026-11-23; `liquidityConditions`, `liquidityConditionsOn`, `sessionLiquidity` in `src/liquidity-lens.mjs`; `roomContextLine`, `roomConditionsLine`, `lensRoomLine` in `src/lens-steps.mjs`). `liquidityConditions` turns lens B's facts into one plain-words line: the spread against its norm for this New York hour of the week (widened or normal, with the mode's `spread_widen`), the depth (the last 15 minutes' tick-volume percentile, thin or normal, with the mode's `thin_pctl`), the minute's tick volume (spike or not), the session's liquidity at the facts' minute (overlap high, one session normal, between sessions thin, the daily pause, closed; the minute's baseline exclusion) and where price sits against lens B's levels (inside a high-volume node or not; nodes and untaken equal-high/low pools above, below and at price, as counts). No sweep clause (the sweep is the LIQUIDITY card's, B15) and no number shaped like a price (`PRICE_SHAPED`, `\b\d{3,5}(\.\d+)?\b`; a line that would carry one is refused). The line goes into the existing prompts, no model call is added: the 15m War Room (`src/war-room.mjs`) and the 1H room (`src/timeframe-threads.mjs`) give it to the Risk Manager's opening data (`liquidity_conditions`, also in that opening's shadow and metric-shadow copies) and to the moderator (`LIQUIDITY_CONDITIONS_LABEL`, before the bus); the stored brief carries it in `context.conditions`, and a retried bar reads it from there. Only while lens B is on, above its floor, its facts available and its state at most 15 minutes old (the gates of `liquidityEvidence` and the LIQUIDITY card); otherwise, and in the 4H and D1 rooms (gated by chart), today's behaviour. Context outside the quorum and the crowd, never a vote. Switch `LENS_ROOM_LINE` (`conditions`, the default; `brief`: the brief's line only, the rooms' prompts and briefs byte for byte as before). Outside `ENGINE_CODE`: `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` hold.
- **Report and page.** `cryptophys.lens-report.v1` daily through `AETHER.ingestLensReport` when offered (no price key at any depth). Doctrine facts: `positioning.*` and `liquidity.*` in the Nexus seat (Aether keeps the Omega seat to `omega.*`). `lens_at_floor` health alert. `GET /api/lens`; Nexus Liquidity page (`#/liquidity`, Track C2) panel "Positioning & Liquidity analyst" (`components/LensPanel.tsx`: the pure `LensView`, from the page's one `/api/lens` read every 120 s, which also feeds its score panel; the polled `LensPanel` wrapper and its Strategy-page mount are gone; states as chips: on ok, switched off off, at floor late, the CFTC report fresh ok / stale late, the baseline sufficient ok / insufficient late; "No lens state yet" until the ledger step writes it; fixture `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/lens.json`, PL21b).
- **The Nexus liquidity view** (Track B, B8; owner 2026-11-22: "integrasi juga tanpa shadow terkait chart pattern dan liquidity, ketiganya dibuat panel visualisasi sendiri terpisah dalam Intelligence"). `GET /api/liquidity` (`src/liquidity-view.mjs`, `cryptophys.liquidity-view.v1`; the contract of the Nexus Liquidity page is `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/liquidity.json`, with `liquidity-unavailable.json` for no recent broker bar), proxied by Nexus like every `GET /api/*` (no-store; any other method 405). Nexus only, behind Cloudflare Access: of the Track B read routes only this one and the later B9 and B14 carry prices, each labelled by scale (`low`/`high` and `price.broker` the broker's, `low_feed`/`high_feed` and `price.feed` the feed's, `feed = broker − feed_basis`, null without a known basis). Keys: `state` (`at_ms`, `fresh`, `mode`, `on` (the signal's `liquidity_on`), `baseline`, `backfill` {`status` done/backfilled/failed/unavailable}), `scale` (`feed_basis`, `feed_basis_known`, `basis_at`: `feedBasis`, now `src/feed-basis.mjs`, on lens B's archive of the broker bars and the feed's last two hours of 5m bars), `price`, `atr_m15` (the feed's 15m ATR(14)), `facts`, `line`, `levels` (lens B's, with `in_key_zone` against the Key Level Agent's candidate zones), `readings` (24 h), `sweeps` (the newest 50 with `outcome` hit/miss/neutral/pending, `scored` (in the score's window) and `replay`; none while the facts are unavailable), `score`, `effects` (`blocks`, `makers`, `skips` (the entry council's `liquidity_sweep_against`), `deferrals` (the plan's `liquidity_modify_deferred`, no outcome), `pm_actions` (actions citing `liquidity_sweep_against`)), `baseline` (`hours`: the 168 New York hours of the week), `management` (today's management level set, `brokerKeyLevels` over the latest decision's key zones and lens B's levels while it is on). `card` and `quorum_with_liquidity` (B15) come from the latest decision's `decision.liquidity` (the LIQUIDITY card: `at_ms` (when it was made), `state_at_ms`, `bar_close`, `stale` (older than 15 minutes), `counts` false, `weight`, `lens_weight`, `lean`, `side`, `sweep` (`sell_side`/`buy_side`/null), `sweep_age_min`, `lean_reason`, `agreeing`, `summary`; the quorum with liquidity: `score`, `bias`, the three shares, `share_needed_above`, `quorum_min`, `long_met`, `short_met`, `met`), null when that decision has none; `conditions` (B16) is `liquidityConditions` of the latest lens B state at the mode's knobs (the line the 15m and 1H risk desks and moderators read, with its parts: `spread` {`ratio`, `widen`, `state`}, `depth` {`vol_pctl_15m`, `thin_pctl`, `state`}, `activity` {`vol_pctl`, `spike_pctl`, `state`}, `session` {`names`, `overlap`, `liquidity`, `excluded`}, `levels` (counts and `inside_node`), `baseline`, `baseline_weeks`, `mode`, `line`), with `at_ms`, `stale`, `in_rooms` (whether the rooms read it now: `LENS_ROOM_LINE` `conditions` and lens B on, above its floor and fresh) and `room_line`; null without available facts; no price; `effects.level_moves` (B17/B19) is null until those merge. Read-only: SELECTs and KV reads (a table not created yet reads as empty), never `lensStatus` (it creates `lens_readings`); the strategy memo it shares with the later views is `readStrategy` (its own idempotent schema steps, as `GET /api/strategy`), kept 60 s per isolate (`src/intel-memo.mjs`). No account number, login, server, `account_ref`, source or identity in the answer. 500 `liquidity_unavailable` on a D1 error. Switch `INTEL_VIEWS` (`on`; `off` answers 404 `{status: "not_found", reason: "intel_views_off"}`).
- **The Nexus chart patterns view** (Track B, B9; owner 2026-11-22, as B8). `GET /api/chart-patterns` (`src/chart-patterns-view.mjs`, `cryptophys.chart-patterns-view.v1`; the contract of the Nexus Chart patterns page (C3) is `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/chart-patterns.json`), in `INTEL_VIEW_PATHS` beside the liquidity view and proxied by Nexus like every `GET /api/*` (no-store; any other method 405). Nexus only, behind Cloudflare Access; every price is the feed's (TradingView OANDA:XAUUSD closed bars, `scale.prices` "feed"), no broker price. Keys: `mode` (`CHART_PATTERNS_MODE`), `calibration_mode` (`PATTERN_CALIBRATION_MODE`), `scale`, `price` and `atr_h1` (the strategy memo's), `decision` (`decision_key`, `bar_close`, `trade_agent` (the trade's agent), `council_strategy` (the strategy arm's pick, `selection.agent`; null without one): from KV `decision-engine:latest`, else one `engine_decisions` SELECT in try/catch), `charts` (`15`, `60`, `240`, `D`: `label`, `interval_s` and `ohlcv`, the detector's 60 closed bars of the feed in `/api/ohlcv`'s candle shape, so `start_bar`/`end_bar` index them), `patterns` (the Key Level Agent's `key_levels.patterns` from the strategy memo, else detected on the same bars without event patterns; `[]` when `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` is off: every `detectPatterns` field with the drawing fields `line_from`/`line_to`/`upper_from`/`lower_from`, `target_up`/`target_down`, `event`, `record` {`chart` (`by_tf`), `pooled`}, `zone` (the strongest key zone holding the pattern's levels) and `plan` (the latest decision's pattern agent plan for that pattern and chart, `tp1`/`tp2` as the plan has them, and its EZ1 entry zone `entry_low`/`entry_high` on the feed scale, null without one)), `far_targets` (every measured target, a forming pattern's both, the nearest first, with its distance in ATR(1H)), `calibration` (`version`, `computed_at`, `base_rate`, `resolved`, `pending`, `patterns`, `by_tf`, `open`), `seat` (the Projection Agent's seat, `agent:projection` in `outcome_scores` over 30 days of this scoring version: `rooms` M15/H1/H4/D1 with the directional and WAIT counts, Brier and reputation; `judged_on` "bar", today's 4-bar 0.5 ATR stance scoring; `scoring`) and `history` (`?history=N`, 0 by default, at most 24: the newest decisions' stored `chart_patterns`). TP1-KZ2: a pattern's measured target is only ever the far target (`far_target`, which is `detectPatterns`' `target`, `target_up`/`target_down`, `far_targets`); no price key is called `target`. `held`, `breakout_bars_ago`, `bars_since_breakout`, `horizon_bars`, `bars_left` (A10/A12), `seat.horizon_bars` (A12) and `plan.reach` (a later PR) are null until those merge. The Nexus Chart patterns page (`#/patterns`, Track C3) reads it. Read-only: SELECTs and KV reads (a table not created yet reads as empty), never `readLatestDecision`, `readDecisions`, `readAllocationEvents` or `scorecards` (they run DDL); the strategy memo is `readStrategyMemo` (`src/intel-memo.mjs`). No account number, login, server or identity in the answer. 500 `chart_patterns_unavailable` on a D1 error. Switch `INTEL_VIEWS` (`off` answers 404 `{status: "not_found", reason: "intel_views_off"}`).
- **The Nexus ORB view** (Track B, B14; orchestrator 2026-11-26). `GET /api/orb` (`src/orb-view.mjs`, `cryptophys.orb-view.v1`; the contract of the Nexus ORB page, C4, is `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/orb.json`), in `INTEL_VIEW_PATHS` beside `/api/liquidity` and `/api/chart-patterns` (Nexus only, behind Access; `INTEL_VIEWS` "off" answers 404; any error 500 `orb_unavailable`); never on a public route. Every price is the feed's (`scale.prices` "feed"). It reads the strategy memo, the feed's 15m series, `decision-engine:latest` (else the newest `engine_decisions` row), the cached calendar, `plan-learning:state:v1` and D1 `plan_setups` (SELECT only; a missing table reads as empty) and never re-implements a rule: `openingRanges`, `orbMode`, `orbLevelsMode`, `rangeBreakout` (`src/opening-range.mjs`, shared with A9's reading), `orbWidthStatus` (`src/strategy-agents.mjs`, now the agent's own width check), `AGENT_RULES`, `suspendedPatterns`, `sessionOpenMs`. Keys: `mode`, `levels_mode`, `rules`, `price`, `atr_h1`, `atr_m15`, `market { open, next_range }`, `decision { decision_key, bar_close, session_range }`, `overlap { long, short }` (the session whose plan the agent gives each side), `sessions.{london,new_york}` (`variant`, `date_ny`, the window's times, `phase` forming/live/over, `bars_built`, `bars_left`, `status` (the module's codes plus `too_narrow`/`too_wide`) and `status_detail` in words, `high`/`low`/`mid`/`width`/`width_atr`, `breakout { side, bar_ms, bars_since, state none/held/retesting/back_inside/invalidated }`, `invalidated { long, short }`, `events_in_range`, `edges_in_zones`, `plans.{long,short}` with `entry_type`, `lapse_ms`, `role`, `picked`), `records.{london,new_york}` (the plan learner's per-session record, `held_by_breaker`) and `recent` (the last 10 `plan_setups` rows of the agent with the control arm's exit and R). A session whose window is over is shown as it stood at its last bar. `/api/strategy-agents` lists `orb_breakout`'s `variants` (`agentCatalog`); its per-session records are its record's `patterns` and `suspended_patterns` names a held session. The Nexus Opening range page (`#/orb`, Track C4) reads it.
- **v4.** The stored decisions hold `POSITIONING` as computed live, so v4 replays it; v4 has no live spread, so a v4-only trade whose signal the spread-spike gate held live is an expected difference in `omegaParity`.

### Room quorum weighting, tuned automatically per timeframe (owner decisions 2026-11-09, `src/weighting.mjs`, `src/weighting-replay.mjs`, `src/market-regime.mjs`)
- **Owner decisions of 2026-11-09:** "nanti aether yang akan membuat tuning persamaan pembobotan berdasarkan hasil learning, masing-masing agent akan diberi score pembobotan sendiri"; then auto-tuning without an operator ("Otomatis + pengaman"), adapting to volatility and market dynamics ("Per regime + shrinkage … berlaku masing-masing timeframe independent"). This replaces the first release's activation by approval in the Console.
- **Every timeframe has its own weighting**: the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 rooms each build their crowd line (weights and quorum share) from their own active point, their own agents' records on that timeframe and the regime of their own bar. The crowd records `weighting`, `quorum_share` and `regime`; the moderator's validation (`entry_without_quorum`) and the H1 decision (`quorumOf`) read the same quorum.
- **Every room is scored** (`outcome_scores`, `agent:<id>`): the 15m and 1H rooms as before, and since 2026-11-09 the 4H (`240`, thread `tf:H4`) and daily (`1D`, thread `tf:D1`) rooms on their own bars, four candles ahead. On the daily chart a weekend is not a gap (`DAILY_MAX_GAP_MS`, 3.5 days).
- **The regime** of a closed bar, from that timeframe's own bars (`src/market-regime.mjs`): the 14-bar ATR as a share of the close, ranked against the previous 100 bars (low, normal, high tercile), and the 20-bar efficiency ratio (trending at 0.35 or more, else ranging): six regimes such as `high-trending`.
- **The equation** (`cryptophys.weighting.v3`), per agent and timeframe, from its scored calls within `days`:
  - decay d = 0.5 ^ (age in days / `half_life_days`), 1 without a half-life;
  - global accuracy acc_g = (Σ d·hit + prior/2) / (Σ d·decisive + prior);
  - regime accuracy acc = (Σ d·hit in the bar's regime + k·acc_g) / (Σ d·decisive in that regime + k), k = `regime_prior` (shrinkage toward the agent's own record); acc = acc_g without a `regime_prior`;
  - calibration = Σ d·(0.25 − Brier) / (0.25 · (Σ d + prior));
  - weight = clamp(2 · acc · (1 + `calibration_beta` · calibration), `weight_range`);
  - the quorum: more than `quorum_share` of the weight on the matching bias, plus `high_vol_quorum_bonus` in a high-volatility regime.
  - The default point (prior 10, weights 0.25 to 2, 30 days, no half-life, `calibration_beta` 0, quorum 0.5, no regime, no bonus) is exactly the P7 reputation weight.
- **The reviewed grid** (`WEIGHTING_GRID`): prior 5, 10, 20; weights 0.25–2, 0.5–1.5, 0.1–3; days 14, 30, 60; half-life none, 7, 14; `calibration_beta` 0, 0.5, 1; `quorum_share` 0.5, 0.55, 0.6; `regime_prior` none, 10, 30; `high_vol_quorum_bonus` 0, 0.05, 0.1. A change of the grid is a reviewed PR.
- **The replay** (read-only, no model call; the 15m and 1H rooms every hour, the 4H room every four hours, the daily room once a day): every concluded room of the timeframe in its window (15m 10 days, 1H 30, 4H 90, D1 365), each agent's final bias and its weight at the room's time (point in time: only calls whose horizon had closed, in the bar's regime). The replayed decision takes the room's direction from the moderator's bias and is BUY or SELL when more than the point's quorum share (in that regime) holds it, else WAIT; it is scored like every stance. Arms: the active point and every grid point one parameter away (16 candidates). Statistics as every shadow arm: paired one-sided sign test, Holm across the month's tests, 200 rooms for discovery, the newest 100 as a holdout.
- **Automatic activation** (`applyWeighting`, `autoDecision`): the candidate rated `better_than_active` on discovery AND holdout with the lowest Holm-adjusted p (then the larger gain) becomes active by itself, at most once every 7 days per timeframe, never a point that was rolled back before. The state lives in D1 `weighting_states` (one row per timeframe; the first release's 1H row in `weighting_state` is read until it is rewritten).
- **Rollback:** the reverse shadow compares the previous point with the active one on the rooms since the activation. After 100 rooms a previous point right significantly more often (sign test, p < 0.05) is restored automatically.
- **Telegram:** every activation and rollback is one message to the owner (`weightingMessage`: the timeframe, the change in words, the replay's accuracy against the active point and the holdout; no price).
- **Aether audits:** the report of each timeframe (`mode: "auto"`, its `timeframe` and regimes) goes to `KnowledgeIngress.ingestWeightingReport` when it changed. Aether no longer proposes weighting points; approval is no longer needed.
- **Kill switch:** `WEIGHTING_MODE = "default"` makes every room use the default point and stops every activation and rollback.
- **Read:** `GET /api/weighting?timeframe=15|60|240|1D` (the active point, each agent's weight, the regimes of the window, the replay, the reverse shadow, the history).

### Crowd line, reputation and scorecards (RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P7, owner go 2026-11-07)
- **Owner decisions of 2026-11-07:** the RFC's proposed values are accepted. That covers the scoring definition (horizon 4 candles, 0.5 × ATR, the v3 WAIT rule), the stuck-state thresholds (8 candles, more than 3 × ATR), and Supertrend first then Order Block (P6).
- **Room agents scored:**
  - Outcome scoring now covers the agents of the rooms that meet today: the Live War Room 15m (timeframe `15`) and the 1H War Room (timeframe `60`).
  - The subject is `agent:<id>`. The stance scored is the agent's final one: its Consensus Discussion answer, else its opening. The moderator's conclusion is scored as `agent:moderator`. A re-convened session (`#hud`) wins.
  - Each bar is scored once, with its own run marker (`outcome_runs` timeframe `<tf>:agents`), so bars already scored for the chain still get their agents.
  - Scoring starts with the bars after this deploy. The retired per-H1 HUD agents and the legacy 15m room keep their old subjects.
  - **Source (ADR-0015 P2b reader 1, owner 2026-11-16):** the room statements come from the run documents in `room_runs` (`ROOM_RUNS_SHADOW` and `ROOM_RUNS_READ` both `on`; `docs/RFC_ROOM_CELLS.md`). Scoring first derives the runs concluded since the last hourly pass. Bars older than the 48 h derive window still come from `agent_thread_messages`. `ROOM_RUNS_READ` `off` returns scoring to the messages. The hourly comparison (`room_runs_compare`) keeps reading the messages, so a document that drifts from its run shows up there.
  - **Other readers (ADR-0015 P2b readers 2-6, owner 2026-11-16):** the weighting replay, the compliance checks and counts, the neuron allocation, the decision cards and the kernel reports read concluded runs from the `room_runs` v2 documents. Each reader has its own flag (`ROOM_RUNS_READ_REPLAY`, `_COMPLIANCE`, `_ALLOCATION`, `_CARDS`, `_KERNEL`); `off` returns that reader to the messages. Every reader falls back to `agent_thread_messages` until the one-time v2 backfill marker is set. Runs in progress, and the rooms without documents, still come from the messages.
- **Reputation** (`src/reputation.mjs`):
  - It is computed per agent and chart over 30 days.
  - Decisive calls are BUY/SELL hits and misses, plus WAIT hits and missed moves.
  - The accuracy is shrunk toward 50 % with 10 pseudo-observations, so a new agent weighs 1 and earns its weight with its sample.
  - Weight = 2 × the shrunk accuracy, kept between 0.25 and 2.
  - In the 1H room, open H1 stuck states put a caution flag on the agents that read the stuck field (the 1H Desk, Prophet, R37 Funnel, Decision Matrix).
- **Crowd line** (deterministic, one per room and bar):
  - the stances and biases of every agent that spoke;
  - the same biases weighted by reputation;
  - a disagreement index (the normalised entropy of the weighted biases: 0 unanimous, 1 an even three-way split);
  - the agent families whose weighted lean goes against the crowd.
  - The families are a table in `src/reputation.mjs` until P2 manifests carry them.
- **Who uses it:**
  - The moderator of every room (15m, 1H, 4H, D1) receives it. If its bias differs from the weighted crowd, it says so in the summary; this is never a validation error.
  - The H1 Reasoner receives it in `CONSENSUS.crowd`. A weighted crowd against the room, or a disagreement above 0.7, lowers the Reasoner's confidence; it never reverses the room's bias on its own.
- **API:**
  - Every room read (`GET /api/war-room/latest`, `GET /api/threads/timeframe`) carries `crowd` and `reputation`, keyed by agent.
  - `GET /api/scorecards` adds `reputation.rooms` (`15m`, `1H`): name, family, decisive calls, accuracy, hit rate, WAIT accuracy, Brier and crowd weight.
- **Nexus:**
  - Each post shows its agent's record: accuracy, sample, crowd weight, and a CAUTION chip with the stuck state.
  - Each room shows a crowd line card under its conclusion: head-count and weighted bias bars, the disagreement, the families against the crowd, and whether it differs from the conclusion.
  - A new **Scorecards** page (`#/scorecards`) lists the room agents of each chart, the H1 decision, the indicators and the open stuck states.
- No limit on who speaks (owner decision 6, 2026-11-05): every active agent still speaks. The crowd line informs; it does not silence anyone.

### Shadow indicator versions (RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P3)
- Code: `src/shadow-versions.mjs`. Table `shadow_outputs` (migration `0008_shadow_outputs.sql`; also ensured at runtime).
- **What runs.** Once an hour, in the first parity sweep after the H1 close, every version is replayed over the pull adapter's cached H1 bars (`SIGNAL_CACHE`).
  - **Control** for each engine family (`prophet_r37@active`, `prophet_r38@active`): the active, parity-approved code with its default parameters.
  - **Candidates**: the same pure engine functions with different parameters. The engine files and their provenance hashes do not change.
- **Storage.** An output is stored only with at least 300 bars of history behind it. The first computation is final.
- **Scoring.** Every output is scored by the outcome scoring as `shadow:<version>` (`source = "shadow"`) once its horizon has closed.
- **Isolation.** Nothing here reaches a room, a prompt, the evidence or a decision.
- **Comparison is like for like.** A candidate is compared with its control only on bars scored for both.
  - **Accuracy:** right calls over decided calls. A directional hit and a WAIT hit are right; a miss and a missed opportunity are wrong; a directional neutral is undecided.
  - **Criteria:** a higher score that is significant, meaning a one-sided exact sign test with p < 0.05 on the bars where the two versions disagree and exactly one is right; no more stuck time (candles inside stuck episodes, § above: ≥ 8 candles with range > 3 × ATR); and a minimum sample of 200 bars. Brier does not apply, because indicator directions carry no confidence. Reference parity applies only to a Pine candidate (RFC decision 1).
  - **Discovery, Holm and holdout (Aether A8, RFC_AETHER §9.3, O12):**
    - **Holdout:** the newest 100 shared bars. The discovery test (the 200-bar minimum, the sign test) uses only the bars before them.
    - **Holm:** the discovery p-value is Holm–Bonferroni adjusted across the family's tests of the same calendar month. That set is the live candidates with a full discovery sample, plus the candidates stopped that month with their recorded p.
    - **Verdicts:**

      | Verdict | Meaning |
      |---|---|
      | `collecting` | The discovery sample is not full yet. |
      | `not_better` | The discovery test did not show a significant improvement. |
      | `holdout_failed` | Better in discovery, but worse than the control on the holdout. |
      | `better_than_active: ready for owner review` | Better in discovery and no worse on the holdout. |
  - **Control check:** the control is also checked against the approved local records of the last 24 candles (`matches_approved_local_records`).
- **Read model.** `GET /api/shadow-versions?days=30` (cached for 5 minutes) and the Nexus page `/shadow`.
- **Tuning view (Aether A8, `src/tuning.mjs`).** `GET /api/tuning` lists every `TUNING_GRID` point:
  - **running:** with its verdict, discovery test (Holm-adjusted) and holdout;
  - **stopped:** with the reason;
  - **untested.**

  It is read-only and starts nothing. A point starts only when a person approves it in Aether (the calibrator proposes the next untested point) or through a reviewed PR here. The Nexus `/shadow` page shows it as the "Tuning grid" panel.
- **Crowd replay (Aether A8 part 2, `src/tuning-replay.mjs`).** `/api/tuning` also carries `replays.crowd`, a deterministic replay of the reputation parameters behind the 1H room's weighted crowd line. It is cached for 5 minutes, stores nothing and reaches no room.
  - **Control:** the active parameters (prior 10, weights 0.25–2, 30 days, neutral band 0.1).
  - **Candidates (reviewed grid, at most 3):** `crowd@prior20`, `crowd@weight0.5-1.5`, `crowd@band0.2`.
  - **How a room is replayed:**
    - each agent's reputation is computed point in time, counting only scores whose 4-bar horizon had closed before the room met;
    - its bias is its final scored stance, the same rule for every arm;
    - the crowd stance is scored with the outcome of that stance on that candle.
  - **Statistics:** the same as the shadow versions (sign test, Holm, discovery minimum, holdout). It also reports the weighted crowd against the moderator on the same rooms.
  - **Promotion:** a better set is a proposal for an owner PR to `src/reputation.mjs`.
- **Strategy inputs (Aether A8 part 3, owner go 2026-11-08, `src/strategy-inputs.mjs`).** These records feed the replay of the strategy-engine thresholds.
  - **What is stored.** Once per closed H1 bar, at the first sweep 40 or more minutes after the close, the sweep stores what `buildStrategy` reads in D1 `strategy_inputs` (migration `0014`):
    - each room's agents (status, stance, bias, confidence) and conclusion;
    - the News Daily TLDR stance, the HUD snapshot fields and the confirmations;
    - the H1 chain decision and the closed bars.
  - **Format.** The inputs are slimmed to the fields the engine uses and gzip-compressed. A test proves the slim record gives exactly the strategy of the full inputs. The live decision and state are stored beside them.
  - **Outcomes.** Once the 4-hour horizon closes, each stance (BUY, SELL, WAIT) is scored once on the 15m bars and stored. The yardstick is 16 bars with doubled thresholds, about 0.5 and 1 H1 ATR. A replay therefore needs no old bars.
  - **Retention.** Kept 60 days.
  - **Isolation.** The table is never sent to the platform or to Aether, and it changes nothing in a room or a decision. `/api/tuning` shows the counts under `strategy_inputs`.
- **Strategy replay (Aether A8 part 4, `src/strategy-replay.mjs`).** `buildStrategy` takes its thresholds as `params` (default `STRATEGY_PARAMS`, the live values).
  - **What runs.** Each sweep computes, a few hours at a time, each arm's decision on every stored hour. The results are kept in D1 `strategy_replays` (migration `0015`), and replays of pruned hours are dropped.
  - **Arms:**

    | Arm | What it changes |
    |---|---|
    | `strategy@active` (control) | nothing: the live thresholds |
    | `strategy@rrr1.2` | the minimum reward to the final target |
    | `strategy@conf0.3` | the minimum confidence |
    | `strategy@near0.5` | the ATR distance that counts as "at a key zone" |

  - **Scoring.** Each decision is scored with the hour's stored outcome, using the shared statistics (sign test, Holm, discovery minimum, holdout).
  - **Drift check.** The control is checked against the live decision stored at capture. A mismatch is reported as `drift`.
  - **Where to see it.** `/api/tuning` shows `replays.strategy`. A better threshold is a proposal for an owner PR to `src/strategy-engine.mjs`.
- **Shadow knowledge arm (Aether A7, `src/shadow-knowledge.mjs`).** It is off unless `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED = "true"`; production set it on from 2026-11-12 (owner: "mode production harus active semua") and **off again on 2026-11-19** (owner: "Hentikan seluruh arm"): none of its 11 notes was better than its control on the sign test with Holm, chronos_matrix on 15m was worse (9 vs 26), and its A/A arm is stopped with it. Its stored rows stay; switching it on again (for the indicator-adoption shadow arms) is a config PR. **Reopened 2026-11-24, self-judging (KA1; the owner, relayed by the orchestrator: "buka arm, biarkan system menilai dirinya sendiri")**: `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED = "true"`, the same cap (32 calls an hour, 6 items), the kill switch and the neuron breaker; `METRIC_REBUTTAL` and `MODEL_ARENA` stay off.
  - **Self-judging** (`runShadowClosures`, hourly right after the promotion below; only with the arm on). A subject (agent, item, chart) is final once its discovery slice has `SHADOW_MIN_SAMPLE` (200) scored pairs, i.e. 300 with the holdout. An item is judged on its first final subject (a promotion likewise takes the first better one): `better` goes live through the promotion (unchanged); `not_better`, `holdout_failed` and `worse` (the control significantly better: the reverse one-sided sign test on the discovery slice, Holm-adjusted over the same family) close it, as a `learned_live` row with status `retired`, reason `shadow:<token>` and the evidence in `verdict_json` (`cryptophys.knowledge-closure.v1`: per subject rows, scored, note and control hits, the changed bars per slice, p and p_holm both ways, the agent's A/A changed/total on that chart; counts and p only). `promotedKeys` then skips it, so its slot frees and that version is never tested or promoted again (a new version is a new test). Below the sample an item stays in the queue. The owner gets one Telegram line per closed item; `/api/tuning` `learned_live` shows the row with its `closure`. Nothing goes to a room, the bus, a decision, Aether or the platform.
  - **Idle items (KA3, `planIdleClosures`, in the same hourly `runShadowClosures` run, after KA1).** An item whose agent no longer opens in its room (benched by the neuron allocation, gone from the roster, or its openings fail) gets no shadow call and would hold its place in the queue for good. Once it has been idle for 72 hours of the arm running, it closes as a `learned_live` row with status `retired`, reason `shadow:not_tested`, through the same `closeShadowItems`, with the evidence in `verdict_json` (`cryptophys.knowledge-closure.v1`, `rule` `untestable.agent_inactive`: rows, scored, the window, the idle start and the counted hours; counts and ids only). `promotedKeys` then skips it: it leaves the queue, and frees its slot when it held one (an agent still in the roster whose openings fail; a benched agent's item already holds none, since `planShadowCalls` gives slots only to items with an agent in the roster). KA2 posts it once as `not_tested` with rule `untestable.agent_inactive`, the agent in `arm`, its rows, scored pairs, window and charts. The rule, read from the arm's own call log (the stored rows), not from market hours, because the log also covers the arm switch, the shadow kill switch, the neuron breaker, the hourly budget and a room that did not meet:
    - **Idle since** the item's newest `shadow_agent_outputs` row (any subject, any status), or when the pack first carried it if it has no row in the 60-day window.
    - **An hour counts** when, after that, the arm stored a note row, on a bar of a room the item can be tested in (its charts; the council apart from the rooms), for an item that ranks after it in the queue (first seen, then id, the order `shadowItems` serves). On that bar the arm was on, the room met, the slots and the hourly budget reached past this item, and it still got no call: none of its agents sat in the roster or answered. An hour with the arm off or the market closed stores no row and never counts. An earlier-ranked queue-mate proves nothing (it may have taken the last slot or the budget), so it never counts either. An item that ranks last therefore never closes as idle.
    - **Never closed:** a version `learned_live` already has (live or retired); an item KA1 can judge (a final outcome in the plan, or a subject with 300 answered rows waiting to be scored); an item KA2 already posts as untestable (moderator only, Omega, the 4H or daily chart only); an item not in the stored pack (it holds no slot).
    - **Known limits (orchestrator, 2026-11-25).** An item that ranks last among the items its room can test never closes this way, since no later-ranked item can show the arm reached past it. A slot held by an earlier item whose agent failed its opening leaves no row, so that hour is not seen. The closure is final for that version: if a benched agent returns to live, the item is tested again only as a new version (KA2's post carries `item_version`, so the platform knows which version closed; Aether's `agent_inactive` skip lapses after 14 days, so the curator can propose it again).
    - **Production on 2026-11-25 (read-only study):** `k-caution-supertrend-60-new-york@1` stopped because the neuron allocation benched supertrend in the 1H room (2026-11-17 09:11Z, 45% of 107; the last row is the 08:00 bar), and `k-role-aegis-footprint-data-scope@1` stopped on 15m because aegis_footprint was benched in the 15m War Room (2026-11-18 03:12Z, 38% of 60). Both still answer every fourth bar in their benched turn, outside the knowledge arm. Aegis's 1H rows go on (the newest is the 2026-11-25 00:00 bar), so it is not idle. Supertrend had 56 counted hours, so it closes after 16 more hours of the 1H room running. Two never-tested items would close at once: `k-role-htf-calibration-talk@1` (120 hours) and `k-role-interstellar-eyes-data-scope@1` (87 hours; interstellar_eyes has been benched in both rooms since 2026-11-16). `k-caution-news-60-late@1` has no rows because six earlier items fill the 1H room's slots, and it stays in the queue.
  - **The #492 items, once.** The first run settles the items tested until 2026-11-19 by the same rule on their rows stored before 2026-11-20 (`SHADOW_SETTLE`; the rows are kept, nothing is re-run) and writes KV `shadow-knowledge:settle:v1` in `SIGNAL_CACHE` (only the Worker writes it; it keeps the report: items, live, retired, collecting, closed). On 2026-11-24's rows: 11 items, 1 final (`k-role-chronos-matrix-calibration-talk@1` on the 15m War Room: 304 discovery bars, the note better on 3 changed bars and the control on 9; not better), 10 still collecting.
  - **Verdicts to the platform (KA2, `src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs`; platform PF3, capability `knowledge.verdict`, live 2026-11-24).** Hourly, right after `runShadowClosures`, the kernel agent `shadow-knowledge` (tier 1, no seat, `knowledge.verdict` only) posts each item's verdict for Aether's curator, one call per item on each verdict change, oldest final first: a KA1 closure posts its own token as the verdict (`not_better`, `holdout_failed` or `worse`; the reason in plain words, the test in `evidence.rule`: `sign_holm.better`, `holdout.no_worse`, `sign_holm.worse`); a promoted note posts `live`, a live note that retired `retired`; an item still collecting posts `collecting` at most once a UTC day (and once per `window_end`), with its rows and scored pairs; a pack text item no shadow call can ever test posts `not_tested` once (`untestable.moderator_only`, `untestable.audience_omega`, `untestable.chart_4h_only`), and so does an idle item KA3 closed (`untestable.agent_inactive`, with its rows and scored pairs). `inconclusive` is not used. The payload is exactly the platform's schema (`item_id`, `item_version`, `verdict`, `reason`, `evidence` with only `arm` (the agent), `window_start`/`window_end`, `rows`, `scored`, `treat_hits`, `control_hits`, `aa_changed`, `aa_total`, `p_value`, `p_holm` (for `worse`, the reverse test's), `charts`, `rule`; no null, no price), checked before every call. Each item's evidence is its deciding subject (the one with the closing verdict, or the one with the most scored pairs while collecting); the subject windows come from the closure plan, never written into `learned_live`.
    - **Marker.** D1 `knowledge_verdict_posts` (item, version, verdict, window_end; status `posted`, `pending`, `failed`, `invalid`, `superseded` or `not_found`), made by `ensureSchema` (no migration), and `knowledge_verdict_strikes`.
    - **Errors.** `knowledge_proposal_not_found` marks the item, never retried; a not yet registered agent stops the run with nothing marked, and the next run tries again (one log line a run, no Telegram); a denial stops the run and backs the agent off 55 minutes in `platform_backoff`, then one `whoami` decides; a kernel failure or a transport error stops the run, at most 5 attempts; a timeout (the call's own 20 s, `KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_TIMEOUT_MS`; each call takes about 5 s on the platform; the other kernel calls keep `PLATFORM_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS`) is unknown, not failed: it stops the run too and leaves the row pending, counting an attempt but no strike (the platform answers a repeat of the same proposal, verdict and `window_end` with `deduplicated: true`, marked posted); at most 12 calls a run and 3 refused or failed calls an hour (the kernel proposes a suspension at 5).
    - **Retries (KA2 fix, 2026-11-25).** A pending row is tried again no sooner than `retry_gap_ms` (50 minutes, `KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_RULES`) after its own `updated_at`. A pending row the current plan no longer makes (a `collecting` row whose `window_end` a later run moved on from, or a final verdict the plan no longer produces) is retried from its stored `payload_json`, after the current entries and oldest first, through the same gates (12 a run, 3 strikes an hour, the agent backoff, the payload check, not registered, `knowledge_proposal_not_found`, the denial); a stored payload that no longer parses or is not the row's own is marked `invalid`. Once a row's attempts reach 5 (`max_attempts`, timeouts included) it is marked `failed` with `attempts_exhausted:<last reason>` (e.g. `attempts_exhausted:timeout`) and never retried, so no row stays `pending`. **Superseded** (orchestrator ruling): an orphan `collecting` row is marked `superseded`, final, with no call, no attempt and no strike, once the same item@version has a posted `collecting` row with a later `window_end` (from an earlier run, or posted earlier in the same run) or a posted final verdict; the reason names the newer row (`superseded:<window_end>` or `superseded:<verdict>`). So the platform never receives an older `collecting` window after a newer one. No call is made, so the retry gap does not hold it back; a `superseded` row is done and never retried. A final-verdict orphan is never superseded and is retried as above. No schema change.
    - **Switch.** `KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_POSTS` (`on` in wrangler.jsonc; the code default `off`) and `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED`; off, nothing is read, written or called. Nothing reaches a room, the bus, a decision, a prompt or public output.
  - **Which items.** At most 6 text items of the learned pack (caution or role reminder; approved, or undecided under `KNOWLEDGE_AUTO_SHADOW`) are tested at once, oldest first seen first (2 until 2026-11-16; owner: "opsi A", because six curator items had queued behind the two oldest for weeks).
  - **Which agents.** An agent qualifies when it is in the item's audience, sat in the room and answered there: the 1H room, and since 2026-11-13 (owner: "1-3") the 15m War Room (subjects end in `@15`) and the Decision Engine council (consumer `council`: one extra council call with the note, put through the same guardrails, against the stance decided on that bar).
  - **The call.** That agent makes one extra opening call on the same bar, with the same data and prompt plus the note line.
  - **A/A noise check (O13).** While the first item runs, the same prompt is also asked once more without the note.
  - **Timing and budget.** The calls run after the room and its decision (the H1 decision, or the Decision Engine's for the 15m War Room and the council), never before them. They share `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (default 32 since 2026-11-16, 16 before; at most 48). The shadow kill switch and the daily neuron breaker bound it as before.
  - **Storage.** Answers go in D1 `shadow_agent_outputs` (migration `0016`), with the agent's real opening stance as the control. Both are scored there on their chart's bars (1H, or 15m for the War Room and the council), never in `outcome_scores`, so no scorecard or reputation sees them. Rows are kept 60 days.
  - **Isolation.** The answers are never shown in a room, on the bus, in the crowd line, to the moderator or the Reasoner, or in a decision.
  - **Where to see it.** `/api/tuning` shows `replays.knowledge`, with the verdicts and the noise rate.
  - **Which items** (since 2026-11-13, Aether `KNOWLEDGE_AUTO_SHADOW`): the approved items and the text proposals no person has decided yet (review `auto`); a rejection removes one from the pack at once. Promoted versions (live or retired) and versions closed in shadow (KA1 and KA3, above) are no longer tested.
  - **Promotion, autonomous** (owner 2026-11-13: "Apakah menyambungkan ini masih manual? Jika iya, buat agar autonomous"; `src/learned-live.mjs`). Hourly, after the pack pull:
    - a text item whose verdict reads `better_than_active` (discovery better than the agent's own opening, Holm-adjusted p < 0.05, the newest 100 bars no worse) goes **live for exactly what was tested**: the tested agent(s), on the chart(s) tested (1H, or 15m for the War Room and the council), never the moderator or another chart (D1 `learned_live`, with the verdict);
    - a live item **retires** when Aether's pack no longer carries it (a person rejected it), or when the agent's scored statements on that chart since the promotion (for the council, its own decisions scored on the 15m bars) are significantly worse than its control accuracy in the test (one-sided binomial p < 0.05, from 40 decided calls). A retired version never returns by itself;
    - each change goes to the owner on Telegram; `/api/tuning` shows `learned_live`. The live items join the bundled pack in every prompt that reads learned notes (`effectiveLearnedPack`). Switch `LEARNED_AUTO_PROMOTION` (`on` by default; `off` retires every live item). The bundled pack below still changes only by an owner PR.
- **Metric opening promoted for Order Blocks (owner 2026-11-19: "Promosi Order Blocks sekarang"; `METRIC_LIVE_AGENTS` in `src/metric-shadow.mjs`).**
  - **What changes.** Order Blocks' live 15m War Room opening is the metric prompt tested in shadow (`metricMessages`, schema `METRIC_OPENING_SCHEMA`, usage source `war_room_opening_metric`).
  - **How the answer is used.** `metricToOpening` turns it into a room opening: the bias from the weighted implications (above 0.1 bullish, below -0.1 bearish), a plain claim built from the readings with no metric ids, one plain line per metric, and the table kept as `metric_table`. The room's own `validateOpening` then applies.
  - **The evidence.** Order Blocks was the one agent better than its prose control in both slices: discovery 36 vs 20 (sign test p 0.022) and holdout 24 vs 16. Its discovery slice had 175 bars, short of the usual 200; the owner promoted it anyway.
  - **What stays.** Every other agent keeps the prose opening. The metric shadow no longer runs for a promoted agent.
  - **Switch.** `METRIC_LIVE` "off" puts every agent back on prose; a comma list of agent ids replaces the set.
- **Shadow kill switch (owner 2026-11-14: "berikan kill switch untuk mode shadow jika ternyata biaya membengkak"; `src/shadow-switch.mjs`).** One switch for every shadow path that calls a model: the metric-format arm (`src/metric-shadow.mjs`), the learned notes under test (`runShadowCalls`: 15m War Room, 1H room, council) and a benched agent's turn beside its room (`gateShadowTurns` on `rosterSplit`).
  - **Off** means none of them calls a model. The live rooms, the quorum and the decision are untouched. A benched agent stays benched and its record pauses until shadow is on again.
  - **Who turns it off.**
    - The owner, from the card "Shadow mode · kill switch" (War Room right column and the Neuron allocation page), via `GET`/`POST /omega/api/shadow-mode` (Cloudflare Access; a person, the same origin, a reason) → `OmegaGateway.shadowMode`/`setShadowMode` → KV `shadow-mode:switch:v1` (`on` or `off`; unset is `on`).
    - The cost breaker, hourly in the sweep (`checkShadowBudget`): it turns the switch off when today's (UTC) shadow neurons pass `SHADOW_NEURONS_PER_DAY_MAX` (default 25,000, about USD 0.28 at the paid rate). It stays off until a person turns it on.
    - `SHADOW_MODE = "off"` (a Worker variable) holds it off whatever the switch says.
  - **The meter.** Every shadow call is metered apart in `ai_usage_daily` as `shadow:<schema name>` (`callBusModel` `meterSource`; a room step `shadow:<agent>` is metered the same way). The breaker and the card read only those rows.
  - **Audit.** Each change is stored in D1 `shadow_switch_events` and sent to Telegram.
- **Model arena, AI Neuron B (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut B"; `src/model-arena.mjs`).** Shadow only: it changes no room, council or decision.
  - **What.** On every 8th 15m bar (`ARENA_EVERY`; bar index % 8 = 2, so never a metric rebuttal bar), after the decision, the council (`decision_council`) and the 15m War Room conclusion (`war_room_conclusion`) are asked again, with the live step's first messages, of the challenger `ARENA_MODEL` (default `@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017`), once, at `ARENA_REASONING_EFFORT` "low", with no correction or retry. The council's answer goes through the same guardrails (`decide`) as the live one; the conclusion is checked by the room's own validation.
  - **Stored.** D1 `model_arena_outputs` (one row per step and bar): the challenger's status, stance, latency and tokens (cached included) beside the live step's model, status (`ok` at the first try, `ok_after_retry`), stance and latency. Both stances are scored on the same 15m outcome in the sweep (`scoreModelArena`; a WAIT that let a 2:1 trade go by counts as a miss); rows older than 90 days are dropped.
  - **Cost.** Metered as `shadow:arena:<schema>`, so it counts against the shadow kill switch's budget. It stops for the day at its own cap `ARENA_NEURONS_PER_DAY_MAX` (default 6,000) and once all shadow calls reach 80% of `SHADOW_NEURONS_PER_DAY_MAX`, so it never trips the breaker by itself. `MODEL_ARENA = "off"` and the kill switch turn it off; production sets it off since 2026-11-19 (owner: "1+2+3 hentikan semua"; 37 and 36 scored bars, the arena's stance never differed decisively from the live one).
  - **Read.** `GET /api/model-arena` and the Nexus Neuron allocation page ("Model arena"): validity at the first try, agreement, hit rate on both sides, latency, tokens and neurons per call. The verdict stays "collecting" below 60 scored pairs per step; the challenger goes live only by an owner decision.
- **Shadow model router: the news comparison on Beam (SB1; owner 2026-11-25: "semua shadow pakai beam", then, after the read-only survey found that only one source can route safely, "news_compare saja"; `src/model-router.mjs`, a small copy of CTA's router, cryptophys-cta `src/agent/router.mjs` at b2a30f3).** Shadow only, behind the FS1 switch `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER` (`off` by default, owner-flipped; not in wrangler.jsonc). Off, the router is never called: the comparison is byte for byte as before (`test/shadow-model-router.test.mjs` SB1-1).
  - **What routes.** Only the news shadow comparison's two daily readings (`runNewsShadowCompare`, the TradingView set and the free set, switched together), selected by call site: that call site passes its own allow-list `MODEL_ROUTER_ALLOW = ["news_compare"]` and its site name; never by the meter source. `metric_opening`, the note arm and the benched seats (`shadow:war_room_opening`, `shadow:tf_room_opening`), `decision_council`, every other shadow source and every live caller keep their Workers AI models and never reach the router.
  - **The call.** OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions at `MODEL_BASE_URL` (default `https://api.reflection.ai/openai/v1`), model `MODEL_ID` (default `Beam-1.0-501B-A23B`), key in the AI Worker secret `REFLECTION_API_KEY` (set by the owner through the platform; no key, no call). `reasoning_effort` "low" and a hard `max_completion_tokens` of 2,000 (Beam's reasoning counts as output: about 2 calls a day use at most 4,000 of the 20,000 a day). The answer goes through the same parse (`parseModelObject`) and validation (`cleanReading`, then `agreement`) as the Workers AI answer.
  - **Falls back** to the Workers AI call of today, unchanged, for that call, with a reason code (a `model_router_fallback` console line and a row in D1 `model_router_events`; never the prompt or the answer): a 25 s timeout or a network error; a breaker that opens after 3 failures (timeout, network error, 5xx) in a 15-minute window aligned to the quarter hour, for the rest of that window; a 429; Beam's own limits (100k input tokens and 20k output tokens a UTC day, this call's cap reserved; 20 requests a day; 2 a minute); a paid sign (a 402, a billing or credit error, or an answer reporting a cost above 0); the kernel's `model_router.status` not "on" (60 s cache per isolate; a refused, failed or unknown capability call reads as not "on"); no `reflection-data-retention` header, or "contributor"; an invalid answer.
  - **Paid signs** also turn routing off org-wide: one `model_router.report_paid` call, and KV `model-router:paid:v1` (`SIGNAL_CACHE`) holds routing off here until a kernel status read made after it says "on" again. If the `report_paid` call itself failed, the org-wide switch-off is not in force, so a kernel "on" does not lift the hold: each later call reports again (`paid_hold`) until the report lands.
  - **Kernel answers (platform 982971c).** `model_router.status` routes only on `result.state.state` "on" with `result.state.mode` "free_only" (never `paid_capped`, where only the kernel counts spend); `effective` is not read. `model_router.report_paid` is sent as `{ signal }` only: `http_402`, `billing_error`, `cost_field`, `plan_change` (mapped, not detected here) or `reported`; the model, host and source stay in `model_router_events`.
  - **Rate limits and paid signs, in the platform's order (AIR1 B1).** A 402 is paid (`http_402`). A 429 is never paid, whatever its text says (a free-quota answer reads "upgrade your plan" or "credits"): it falls back as `rate_limited` and backs off (KV `model-router:rate-limited:v1`, `rate_limit_hold`) until the next 00:00 UTC when the text says day, daily or today, else for Retry-After seconds, else 60 s. Billing or credit text is a paid sign (`billing_error`) only on another 4xx, never on a 429 or a 5xx (a 5xx is `http_5xx`). A cost above 0 counts only on a 2xx (`cost_field`).
  - **Key.** news_compare needs its own `REFLECTION_API_KEY` secret on `xauusd-ai-trading-assistant` (the owner sets it through the platform). Without it every reading falls back as `no_key`, i.e. Workers AI.
  - **Kernel.** Both capabilities are called like every other kernel capability (`kernelSyscall` over `AGENTS`) as the agent `xauusd-model-router` (platform AIR1, 982971c; `XAUUSD_CEILING` grants it the two; `organism.json` declares it for the census).
  - **Counters and the shadow breaker.** Beam calls are metered in `ai_usage_daily` under the call's source (`shadow:news_compare`) with the Beam model name, tokens and calls. Beam has no Workers AI neuron rate, so `shadowSpendToday` reads those rows as Beam's own counters (`beam`: calls, tokens in and out, the limits and the limit reached), not as an unpriced pass; `checkShadowBudget` reports them hourly (and a `model_router_limit` line at a limit), and the router's day limits are that reading. The request counts (a day and a minute) and the breaker come from `model_router_events` (rows kept 90 days). Every fallback is a Workers AI call priced in neurons as before.
  - **The switch date.** The owner's flips are FS1's own audit rows (`omega_events` `feature_switch`, key `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER`, from, to, ms); each reading's effective route (routed, or the fallback reason) and day are in `model_router_events`. The 14-day agreement window of `GET /api/news/shadow-compare` splits at them; the read model itself is unchanged.
  - **Memo.** No memo hash covers the news comparison (the room memo hashes room steps only), so none moves at the switch.
- **Bundled learned pack (Aether A9, `src/learned-knowledge.mjs`).** The source is `knowledge/learned/pack.json`, bundled as `src/learned-knowledge.generated.mjs` by `scripts/build-learned-knowledge.mjs --write`, like KB v4. It is empty today.
  - **How an item gets in.** The script's `--add <export> --tuning <tuning> --items id@v` copies an item only when its shadow verdict is `better_than_active`. That change, in an owner PR, is the promotion (K9).
  - **Test gates.** Tests fail when the generated module is stale or an item breaks the rules:
    - plain words, no 3+ digit number and no order wording;
    - its numbers in its evidence;
    - provenance with at least 10 cards and the canon hash;
    - a shadow verdict.
  - **Selection is deterministic.** An item applies when the agent is in its audience, the chart matches and the situation matches the bar (the session, for a caution; a role reminder always applies).
  - **Budgets.**

    | Consumer | Notes | Characters |
    |---|---|---|
    | Agents | at most 2 | at most 400 |
    | Moderator | at most 3 | at most 600 |

  - **Where it goes.** The block is placed after the agent's own data in the 15m War Room (since 2026-11-13, owner: "strengthen autonomous learning & knowledge looping"), 1H, 4H and D1 room openings and conclusions, and after the rules of the Decision Engine council (consumer id `council`, chart 15, the moderator's budget). The room brief records `learned_pack` (version, sha and the notes each agent saw).
  - **Switch.** It is off unless `LEARNED_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED = "true"` (on in production since 2026-11-12; the bundled pack is empty until an owner PR adds an item); turning it off restores today's prompts. KB v4 always takes precedence. The H1 Reasoner does not read the notes.
- **P5 review (owner decision 2026-11-07):**
  - `prophet_r37@latch12` and `prophet_r38@latch8` are stopped; they are listed under `retired` in the view.
  - `prophet_r37@latch8` runs until 30 decisive pairs or 2026-12-07.
  - The GMI candidates (`gmi_v12_70@latch8`, `@latch12`) stopped at their review (owner 2026-11-14): over 116 shared bars neither changed GMI's stance once, so neither could be shown better. They are listed under `retired`; the GMI control keeps running.
  - Each running candidate carries a `review` with its date, rule, decisive pairs and `due`.
- **Candidates (P5, 2026-11-05):** `prophet_r37@latch8`, `prophet_r37@latch12` and `prophet_r38@latch8`. The ±3 structure latch expires after 8 or 12 closed H1 bars instead of 50. They are local-only: owner decision 1 (2026-11-05) is that a local-only version is enough, so an improvement is proven by outcome first.
- **GMI "Prophet risk hold" candidates** (`gmi_v12_70@latch8`, `gmi_v12_70@latch12`; stopped 2026-11-14, see above). These tested the latch where it acts on the chain; the re-gate below is how any GMI candidate runs.
  - **How they work.** GMI needs M1, M15 and higher-timeframe bars, so it is not replayed. Each bar starts from the approved local GMI record of that candle (`localcalc:<key>:gmi`, kept 14 days, last 72 bars tried). `src/gmi-regate.mjs` recomputes only the two points the Prophet structure feeds: `mtf_state` in the initial verdict cascade, and the R10 gate with its risk hold (subtype 1).
  - **Exactness.** With the active structure, the re-gate reproduces GMI's comparable fields exactly (`test/gmi-regate.test.mjs`). In production the control is checked against the approved records.
  - **Sample.** Bars accumulate from the local records forward, so the 200-bar sample takes about a week.
- **Candidates approved in Aether (phase A7, owner go 2026-11-07):** `src/aether-pack.mjs`.
  - **The pull.** Once an hour, just before the shadow versions run, the AI Worker pulls Aether's learned pack (`AETHER` → `KnowledgeIngress.learnedPack()`). D1 `aether_learned_pack` (migration `0013`) keeps the last good pack.
  - **What becomes a candidate.** An approved `param_candidate` item becomes a candidate version (`<family>@latch<value>`, with `origin.source = "aether"`) only when all of these hold:
    - its family, parameter and value are in the reviewed `TUNING_GRID` (`stateMaxAge` 8, 12, 16 or 24 for `prophet_r37` and `gmi_v12_70`);
    - it is not already running and was not stopped;
    - its family runs fewer than 3 candidates.
  - **Review.** It is reviewed after its 200-bar sample or 30 decisive pairs, at the latest 30 days after it was first pulled.
  - **Text items** (caution, role reminder) are stored and listed only. They reach no prompt.
  - **Where to see it.** `GET /api/aether/status` shows the pack under `learned_pack`, with its hash, items, the shadow candidates it started and the items it ignored, and why. Nexus `/shadow` marks those candidates "From Aether".
- **Promotion** is never automatic. The verdict `better_than_active: ready for owner review` is a proposal; the owner approves a separate promotion PR.

## 4. Sources and knowledge
- **Pine sources:** `pinescript/ssot/`. Verified against the installed TradingView indicators; see `docs/PINE_SOURCE_VERIFICATION.md`. Never edit these files.
- **Agent knowledge base:** v4, generated from Pine with line citations and bundled into the AI Worker. See `docs/KNOWLEDGE_BASE_V4.md`.
- **Models:** the values in `wrangler.jsonc` are authoritative. Reasoning-model token budgets are `MAX_TOKENS` in `src/model-text.mjs`.

## 5. Deployment rules (mandatory for every human and agent)
1. Production changes reach production **only** by merging a PR into `main`. The workflows in §1 then deploy it.
2. **Never** run `wrangler deploy`, `wrangler delete` or `wrangler secret put` against production by hand or from a VPS. **Never** create a new Worker outside this repository. A manual deploy on 2026-11-03 replaced the pull adapter with a stub and stopped the local lane for about 6 hours. On 2026-11-21 an old checkout on the trading VPS uploaded an old build of this Worker twice (00:26 and 11:43 UTC; no ledger cron, no owner-only arm gate, 30 bindings missing) until `deploy-worker.yml` restored `main` at 14:46. The VPS runs the bridge and the EA only: no wrangler login, no `wrangler deploy`. **Deploy drift guard** (`.github/workflows/deploy-drift-guard.yml`, `scripts/deploy-drift-check.mjs`): every 15 minutes the live Worker must report a commit on `main` that contains the last commit touching the Worker, with `wrangler.jsonc`'s crons and an active deployment made by `deploy-worker.yml` (message `commit <sha>`); a drift fails the run and re-runs `deploy-worker.yml` on `main` (owner 2026-11-21: "Yes, auto-restore"; the repository variable `DRIFT_AUTO_RESTORE=off` leaves the alert only).
3. Work from the latest `main`. Branches cut from an old base must not be merged or deployed.
4. A PR is mergeable only when CI is green (the required checks below, and every other check on the PR) and the change is described against this SSOT.
7. **Main ruleset (owner 2026-11-15, "kerjakan hardening branch protection xauusd").** `main` is protected by the repository ruleset declared in `ops/branch-ruleset.json`:
   - no deletion, no force push, linear history (squash or rebase merges);
   - a pull request is required, with 0 approvals: the gate is the checks, not a human review;
   - required checks, which must pass on a branch that is up to date with `main`:
     - `evidence-unit-tests` (`test-evidence-fusion.yml`: every unit test, including the DNA/organism tests, the type-check and the bundle dry-run);
     - `preview-e2e` (`deploy-preview-worker.yml`; skipped, and so passing, when a PR touches no preview path);
     - `protection-guard` (`branch-protection-guard.yml`);
     - `typecheck` (`typecheck.yml`: `tsc --noEmit` for the AI Worker and for the Nexus UI; cut-over option (D), 2026-11-15);
   - no bypass actor. "Workers Builds: …" checks are dashboard-managed and never required. The repository has no linter, so no lint check exists.

   `protection-guard` (on every PR, on `main` and daily) fails when the rules GitHub applies to `main` differ from `ops/branch-ruleset.json`, or when a required check is no longer exactly one job of a workflow that runs on every pull request (a renamed job or a path filter). To change a required check: edit `ops/branch-ruleset.json` and the workflow in one PR. Then the owner applies the same change in Settings → Rules → Rulesets, because this repository's automation cannot write rulesets.
5. A topology change requires editing this file, `AGENTS.md` and `test/ssot-topology.test.mjs` in the same PR, and the owner's explicit approval.
6. **Ops dispatch (owner request 2026-11-09, backlog item 17).** The automation operator's GitHub access cannot call `workflow_dispatch` or re-run a job. `.github/workflows/ops-dispatch.yml` runs on a push to the operator branch `claude/vibrant-mccarthy-dq5xs7` whose head commit adds exactly one `ops/requests/<UTC timestamp>-<name>.json` (`{"workflow": "deploy-worker.yml"}`), and dispatches that workflow on `main`, with no inputs, then follows it to completion. Allow-list: `deploy-worker.yml`, `deploy-nexus-dashboard.yml`, `deploy-market-data-ingestion.yml`, `deploy-fundamental-news-ingestion.yml` and the read-only `inspect-ai-worker.yml`; never `set-webhook-secret.yml`. Rule 1 holds: a deploy is still the deploy workflow on `main`, the same run the owner starts with "Run workflow". A branch reset onto `main` never replays a request (`test/ops-dispatch.test.mjs`).

8. **One deploy path per production Worker (cut-over option (D), owner 2026-11-15).** Production reaches Cloudflare only through the tagged deploy workflow of its Worker, on `main`:

   | Worker config | Workflow |
   |---|---|
   | `wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-worker.yml` |
   | `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-nexus-dashboard.yml` |
   | `workers/xauusd-omega/wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-omega.yml` |
   | `fundamental-news-ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-fundamental-news-ingestion.yml` |
   | `market-data-ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` | `deploy-market-data-ingestion.yml` |

   - Each deploy passes `--tag <sha12>`, `--message "commit <sha>"`, `DEPLOY_COMMIT` and `DEPLOYED_AT`.
   - The only untagged deploy is the isolated, ephemeral preview Worker of `deploy-preview-worker.yml`: it has its own `--name` and uses `wrangler.preview.jsonc`.
   - There is no `wrangler versions deploy`, no `wrangler rollback` in a workflow, and no deploy script in a `package.json`.
   - `test/deploy-paths.test.mjs` enforces all of the above.

   **Workers Builds (Cloudflare Git integration) is owner-managed in the Cloudflare dashboard.** No repository file can check it.
   - It is connected for `xauusd-ai-trading-assistant` and `xauusd-fundamental-news-ingestion`.
   - Production deploy command: `npx wrangler versions upload`. A production build uploads a version and never promotes it. The owner set this on 2026-11-15 for both Workers.
   - Preview builds stay on; the News root directory is `/fundamental-news-ingestion`.
   - Any change to these settings is the owner's, and must keep production builds versions-only.
9. **Platform cut-over: option (D), hybrid (owner decision 2026-11-15).** xauusd is not deployed by the Cryptophys platform.
   - **Deploys:** xauusd keeps its own CI and its own deploy workflows (rules 1 and 8). The platform never runs a deploy for these five Workers. The ADR-0013 external ceiling is unchanged.
   - **Gates:** xauusd adopts the platform's gates:
     - version provenance: tags, `/health` commit and DNA `deployed_commit`;
     - main protection with `protection-guard` (rule 7);
     - the required `typecheck`;
     - an npm `dependency-audit` on every PR and daily: since 2026-11-16 a high or critical advisory, or an audit that gave no report, fails the job, and it is a required check on `main` since 2026-11-16 (BACKLOG B-14: the owner added it to the ruleset; `ops/branch-ruleset.json` declares it);
     - one tagged deploy path per Worker, with Workers Builds production set to versions-only (rule 8).
   - **What the platform does:**
     - reads the DNA (`organism.json` and the OrganismDna RPC), `/health` and the versions API;
     - keeps its engine census, including `register_engine` and `retire_engine`;
     - may report drift.
   - **What the platform does not do:** it changes no xauusd code, configuration or deploy.
   - **Changing the cut-over:** a different option needs the owner's decision and a PR that edits this rule.
## 6. Owner-only actions
- TradingView OAuth re-authorization at `https://news.cryptophys.work/oauth/start`.
- Rotation of `WEBHOOK_SECRET` (in the AI Worker and the Pine "CTA Webhook Secret" input).
- TradingView alerts: **retired (S3, 2026-11-27)**. The owner deletes the 25 alerts that point to `https://ai.cryptophys.work/webhook` (Prophet R38, Prophet R37, GMI v12.70 and the two HUD comparators on 5m, 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D) once S3 is deployed and verified; `POST /webhook` answers 410 meanwhile.
